# Build decisions

Append-only log of decisions made where `docs/DESIGN.md` was ambiguous or had to change during the build. Add entries under your work package heading: date, the decision, and the DESIGN section it touches.

## WP0

- 2026-09-27: The repo is public and hosted on GitHub Pages (owner's choice, to keep hosting free). DESIGN B1 and C2/WP0 updated accordingly.
- 2026-09-27 (contracts): B15 `export declare function createSim / replayMatch / createBot` became the type aliases `CreateSim`, `ReplayMatch` (`src/contracts/sim.ts`) and `CreateBot` (`src/contracts/bot.ts`). A declared function in a real `.ts` module has no runtime value, so under `verbatimModuleSyntax` a value import of it would silently be `undefined`. Implementations export `const createSim: CreateSim = ...` (WP2) and `const createBot: CreateBot = ...` (WP3). Signatures are unchanged. (DESIGN B15, B10)
- 2026-09-27 (contracts): The combined B15 blocks "observation.ts and bot.ts" and "session.ts and platform.ts" are split into the separate files B14 lists. `src/contracts/index.ts` is a type-only barrel (`export type * from ...`); every contract module has zero runtime exports (a test checks this). Pixi and signals types are referenced only as type-level `import('pixi.js')` / `import('@preact/signals')`, as in B15, so the "contracts import nothing" rule (B2) holds at runtime. (DESIGN B2, B14, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (contracts): `Observation.units[].p` and `telegraphs[].p` use the same unit as `SimState` positions (milli-lu, B3), measured from the observer's gate. B15 only says "p relative to the observer". (DESIGN B3, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (fakes): `src/contracts/fakes/**` are test and dev helpers only; production code must not import them. `fakes/art.ts` imports `pixi.js` at runtime (C2/WP0 task 5 asks for Pixi Graphics rectangles), with a single `eslint-disable-next-line ageborn/layers` on that import. (DESIGN B2, C2/WP0)
- 2026-09-27 (fakes): The fake content uses the A5 table units (lu, ms, whole HP, gold), not compiled units; it is shape-correct `CompiledContent` for UI, render and wiring tests, never balance. Because `ages` and `formats` are total records, all five `AgeDef`s exist, but every fake format spans only Stone and Medieval and only those have cards. The fake economy uses `mountCosts: [0, 150, 350, 700]` (mount 1 free, A2.3) and `emoteCooldownMs: 3000` (DESIGN gives no value; WP1 must pick the real one). The fake `SaveDoc` reuses the Medieval loadout for ages without fake cards. The fake sim's canned stream uses B3 integer units (milli-lu, centi-HP, milli-gold); units do not walk on their own. (DESIGN A2.3, B3, C2/WP0)
- 2026-09-27 (scaffold): Toolchain pins (B1). TypeScript 6.0.3, not 7.0: typescript-eslint 8.70.1 supports `typescript >=4.8.4 <6.1.0`. Vite 8.3.1 with @preact/preset-vite 2.10.6 (peer range includes Vite 8; dev and build verified). Vitest 5.0.2, ESLint 10.11.0 (typescript-eslint supports it), @playwright/test 1.56.1 because its bundled Chromium is revision 1194, the one preinstalled in cloud sessions. @types/node 22.x to match the Node 22 runtime.
- 2026-09-27 (scaffold): Layer rules (B2) are enforced by a small local ESLint rule `ageborn/layers` in `eslint.config.js` that resolves `@/` and relative specifiers to their src layer, instead of `no-restricted-imports` string patterns (which cannot tell `contracts/fakes -> ../content` from a real `content` import). `LAYER_RULES` is exported for the tests/integrity import-graph test. Package allowances not spelled out in B2: `valibot` in content and save, `fflate` in save, `@preact/signals` in capsule. Test files (`*.test.ts(x)`, `*.bench.ts`, `test/` folders) are exempt from layer and determinism rules, since for example AI tests must run the sim. `import('pixi.js')` type annotations are allowed (contracts use them for view types).
- 2026-09-27 (scaffold): Determinism lint bans float literals in sim, ai, meta, core and content, except `src/content/raw/**`, which mirrors the Part A tables exactly (compile.ts converts to integers, B4).
- 2026-09-27 (scaffold): sfc32 follows PractRand (`tmp = a + b + counter++`), seeded with four xmur3 words of `"${seed}"`, no warm-up discards. Draws return uint32; `randInt` uses rejection sampling (no modulo bias). Core offers integer `isqrt` and a degree-based `sinDegBp`/`cosDegBp` lookup table since `Math.sin/sqrt` are banned in the pure layers.
- 2026-09-27 (scaffold): i18n files are `src/i18n/<namespace>.<locale>.json`, discovered with `import.meta.glob`. Nested objects flatten to dot keys; keys are global across files (each WP prefixes its own), duplicates warn in dev and the first file (alphabetical) wins. Missing keys fall back to EN, then to the key itself. Placeholders are `{name}`.
- 2026-09-27 (scaffold): Dev pages (`src/dev/<name>/page.tsx`) default-export a Preact component and may export `title`; `?dev=1#<name>` opens one. Dev pages are internal tools and exempt from the i18n rule. The game name "Ageborn" is a brand constant, not a string key.
- 2026-09-27 (scaffold): `npm run size` (tools/size.ts) counts index.html plus the entry script, modulepreload chunks and stylesheets referenced from dist/index.html, gzipped; fails above 3 MB, warns when all of dist exceeds 8 MB. Playwright runs against `vite preview` at /ageborn/ (Chromium only for now); `PW_CHROMIUM_PATH` overrides the browser binary.
- 2026-09-27 (raw content): Layout of `src/content/raw`. Each age file exports one `RawAgeTables` (`{ age, units, turrets }`, DESIGN table order); `powers.ts` exports `powers: PowerDef[]`; `economy.ts` exports `economy: EconomyRules` plus four raw-only tables: `ageScale` (A2.2 P and base HP, A2.4 thresholds; WP1's `ages.ts` adds palette, visual and music ids), `formats` (A2.10 as `FormatDef`, for WP1's `formats.ts` and the WP2 shim), `damageMods` (the A2.6 role-default mod lists, shared by the age files) and `battle` (A2.1-A2.11 and A5.1 numbers with no `EconomyRules` field: lane length, windup percents, train time per group, Brace/air knockback resist, mark ×1.2, heal pulse, modernise credit, final-age XP cap 1,200, Siege decay step, Stampede fallback p = 200, default projectile speeds). `types.ts` holds the raw-only types and `index.ts` aggregates everything as `raw: RawContent`. Units stay in table units (lu, lu/s, ms, whole HP and damage, gold, bp); `windupPct` stays a percent as in the contract. (DESIGN B4, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (raw content): Contract fields not in the Part A tables are filled by convention: `visualId` `unit.<slug>` / `turret.<slug>` / `power.<slug>` (A14.1); `nameKey`/`descKey` `card.<slug>.name` / `card.<slug>.desc` for units, turrets and powers (WP1 writes those keys in `content.en.json`); unit `sfx` from the A14.2 defaults (spawn by role group, `die_mech` for the mech tag else `die_bio`, so the Balloon Admiral dies with `die_bio`); power `sfx` from the A13 `pw_*` ids; `strongVs`/`weakVs` empty until WP1's counter matrix fills them; `trainMs` and `pop` from the A2.7 group tables. Every attack sets `windupPct` explicitly (melee 40, ranged 50, turret 0; "melee" = no projectile), so rider and secondary attacks are unambiguous. (DESIGN A2.7, A13, A14.1, A14.2, B4)
- 2026-09-27 (raw content): Tags. Every non-air unit also gets the `ground` tag (the table's Tags column only lists `air` for fliers), so each unit has exactly one of `ground`/`air`. The tutorial Training Dummy (listed under A5.6) is a Stone card (`age: 'stone'`, in `stone.ts`, `hidden: true`) so it never triggers the underdog bounty in the Stone-only tutorial; it is an Infantry-group unit with the Blunt role default, `dmgType` blunt. (DESIGN A2.6, A5.6)
- 2026-09-27 (raw content): Projectile speeds not in the A5.1 default list: projectiles whose card note says "arc" (Rock Tosser, Trebuchet, Bronze Cannon, Howitzer, Plasma Mortar, Grenadier lob) fly at the arc/lob speed 450 with `arc: true`, even when the A14.2 visual is a shell or cannonball; bee, bolt and goose use the arrow speed 650; log, bomb and gravity orb 450 (no arc); grapeshot 1,500 (musket class); chainshot and flak 1,200 (shell class). Instant effects: `fx.tongue`, `fx.pitch_pour`, `fx.beam_rail`, `fx.beam_laser`, `fx.arc_chain`. (DESIGN A5.1, A14.2)
- 2026-09-27 (raw content): Ability encodings. Aura statuses use `durationMs: 0`, meaning "while inside the aura" (recomputed each tick, B3 step 6). `resist.bp` is the share of damage resisted (Footman 2500 = 25% less), matching how knockback resist is expressed. Corsair's Boarding Hook is `firstHitBonus` with `multBp: 10000` and `knockback: -20`. Battering Ram: `damage: 10` (vs units), `vsBaseDamage: 160`. Balloon Admiral: `range: 40` (the ±40 lu drop window, same as `bomber.dropWindow`) and explicit `vsBaseDamage: 110`. Grumpy Toad uses `priority: 'backline'` plus `drag`; its "else the second-frontmost small or medium ground enemy" fallback is a sim rule. Gravity Well's `onHit` slow goes to every enemy inside its `pull` radius, not only the (at most 4) damaged ones. Repair Drone has `attacks: []` (Hits: none). Searchlight mark: `magnitudeBp: 2000`. (DESIGN A2.7, A5.2-A5.6)
- 2026-09-27 (raw content): Power encodings. Arrow Storm and Broadside state no jitter; A2.9 says jitter is 0 only for line patterns, so every even barrage uses the one stated value, ±20 lu (Meteor Shower); Carpet Bomber (line) uses 0. Orbital Lance `sweep.width` is the full beam width, 40 (±20 lu), consistent with Smoke Screen's `cloud.width` 350 being a full width. Nanite Surge: regen `magnitudeBp: 4000` is the share of max HP healed over the 4 s; the 150 shield is `amount` with `magnitudeBp: 0`. Stampede's start (frontmost unit or p = 200) and Paratroopers' p ≤ 1,050 clamp come from `battle.stampedeFallbackP` and `economy.powerZoneClamp`. (DESIGN A2.9, A5.7)
- 2026-09-27 (raw content): `economy.mountCosts` is `[0, 150, 350, 700]` (index i = mount i + 1; mount 1 free), as in the fakes. `economy.emoteCooldownMs` is 3,000 ms: DESIGN gives no player emote cooldown (only the bot's 20 s reply rule in A7.2). (DESIGN A2.3, A7.2)
- 2026-09-27 (raw content): `tests/fixtures/content` is a byte-for-byte copy of `src/content/raw` (plus a 3-line "frozen" header) taken in Phase 0. `src/content/raw/raw.test.ts` runs the same DESIGN-typed table checks (24 units, 11 turrets, 8 powers, collection shape, group prices, windups, speeds, sounds, economy, formats) against both. When tuning changes a raw number, update DESIGN and that test row, never the fixture. (DESIGN C2/WP0 task 8, B13)
- 2026-09-27 (verification): The fake content (`src/contracts/fakes/content.ts`) now follows DESIGN where it differed: Tuskback carries no Blunt mods (A2.6 gives them to Infantry melee only), the Pumpkin Head skin is Rare (A5.8), fake powers use the A13 sound ids `pw_stampede` / `pw_arrows`, and the fake skin id is the bare slug `pumpkin_head` (the `SkinDef.id` / `SideConfig.skins` value), with `@` used only in the visual id `unit.bonker@pumpkin_head`, matching `src/core/ids.ts` `skinnedVisualId`. (DESIGN A2.6, A5.8, A13, A14.1, B5)
- 2026-09-27 (verification): `tests/integrity/lint-rules.test.ts` lints small virtual files through the real `eslint.config.js` to prove the layer rule (for example `pixi.js`, `preact` or `render` imported from `src/sim`, Node built-ins in `core`) and the determinism rules (`Math.random`, `Math.sin`, `Date`, float literals, `for...in` in sim, ai, meta, core and content) fire, and that allowed imports, `render` and `content/raw` floats pass. It sits in WP12's `tests/integrity` because B13 lists the layer check as an integrity test; WP12 owns it from Phase 1. (DESIGN B2, B13)
- 2026-09-27 (verification): `.gitignore` also covers `dist-ssr/`, `*.tsbuildinfo`, `blob-report/`, `playwright/.cache/`, `.eslintcache`, `*.local` and `.claude/settings.local.json` (the last was only ignored by the container's global git config).

## WP1

- 2026-09-27 (units): `CompiledContent` card, age, format and economy fields stay in DESIGN's table units (ms, lu, lu/s, whole HP and damage, gold, bp), exactly as the contract field names, `src/content/raw` and the fakes use them; only `ticks` holds tick values. This answers `docs/requests/wp2-content-units.md` (done). B4's integer conversion lives in an extra, precomputed `content.int` view (centi-HP, milli-lu, milli-lu per tick, ticks, milli-gold, bounties per card); it is not in the frozen contract, so the sim converts on its own and tools may read `int`. The compiler derives `pop` and `trainMs` from the role-group tables (A2.7); `validateRaw` reports a raw row that disagrees instead of hiding it. (DESIGN B3, B4, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (hash): `contentHash` = FNV-1a over the canonical JSON of the battle slice only: ages, formats, economy, `battle` rules, units, turrets, powers, daily modifier effects and `ticks`, with presentation fields (visual, effect and sound ids, string keys, `strongVs`/`weakVs`) stripped. Skins, meta tables, strings and the counter matrix never mark replays "from an older version". `compileContent` also compiles `tests/fixtures/content` for golden replays. (DESIGN B3 Replays, B4)
- 2026-09-27 (typed tables): The contract's `unknown` slots are typed in `src/content/types.ts` (`Content extends CompiledContent`). Code that may import `content` gets typed access with `import { content } from '@/content'` or `asContent(c)` for a `CompiledContent` that came through a contract; `asContent` throws on the fakes, whose meta tables are null. `content.order` gives display order (collectable units in DESIGN table order, hidden units apart), `content.battle` the raw-only A2 numbers. Codex rules live in `quests.codex` (A6.7), ladder and matchmaking constants in `arenas.ladder` (A6.3, A6.8), Conquest in `generals.conquest` (A6.10), Daily Challenge rules in `dailyModifiers.challenge` (A9.1). (DESIGN B4, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (counters): `content.counters[a][b]` is M in [0, 1] as B4 defines it (stored as integer bp in `generated/counters.json`, so values have 4-decimal precision; `Math.round(m * 10000)` gives bp). M[a][b] + M[b][a] = 1 exactly and M[a][a] = 0.5; only the 35 collectable units have rows (no Training Dummy). "Strong vs" / "Weak vs" are the top / bottom 3 of the same or adjacent age as B4 says, but only opponents with M > 0.5 count as strong and M < 0.5 as weak, so a card never shows "Strong vs" something it loses to. (DESIGN A2.6, B4)
- 2026-09-27 (counter duels): The real sim (WP2) is built in parallel and has no duel entry point, so the matrix comes from a compact deterministic duel model in `src/content/counters/duel.ts` that follows A2.7 (edge ranges, soft single file with overtaking, symmetric closing, two-phase impacts, damage pipeline steps without phases or buffs, area rules, first-hit, Brace, resist, auras, heals, shields, Roar, called strikes, EMP, Time Stop, pounce, riders, death explosions). Duel-only rules: no bases, so an idle unit walks toward the nearest enemy its first attack can hit (either direction) and the bomber hovers over the nearest ground enemy; targets are re-picked at each attack start. Equal gold: both armies cost 450-1,100 gold, smallest gold gap first (≤ 5%), then closest to 700. Each pair runs both ways and the results are averaged; a duel ends when a side is wiped, after 90 s, or after 10 s without movement, attacks or damage. Abilities trigger on pre-step state and stuns apply together, so every mirror duel is exactly even. Staleness: the file stores the hash of every input (units, economy, battle, engine version, duel rules) and `src/content/test/counters.test.ts` fails when it differs; bump `DUEL_ENGINE_VERSION` when duel logic changes. Regenerate with `npx tsx tools/counters.ts` (about 10 s). Switching to the real sim later is requested in `docs/requests/wp1-counter-duel-harness.md`. (DESIGN B4, B12)
- 2026-09-27 (emote cooldown): `economy.emoteCooldownMs` stays 3,000 ms (WP0's placeholder is now the decision). All v1 opponents are AI and bot replies are already limited to once per 20 s (A7.2), so the cooldown only stops accidental double taps and spam of the emote bubble; 3 s is long enough for one bubble to read and short enough to answer a bot's GG. (DESIGN A5.8, A7.2)
- 2026-09-27 (generals): DESIGN names only signatures, so the personal War Plans are ours: A3-valid (every card from its age, no duplicates, 5 units, 2 turrets, a power per age), matching each signature (Boomsworth: Trebuchet, Bronze Cannon, Howitzer, Grenadier and barrage powers; Tempest: damaging powers; Moss: Rare/Epic turrets; Kettle: Royal Decree and cheap turrets). Only The Warden brings Legendaries (all five, `legendaryLevel` 9), per A6.8; ladder matchmaking (WP7) still applies the A6.8 rarity allowance. Old Grogg has a Stone-only plan (Training Dummy, Tuskback), `scripted`, `baseStartBp` 5,000 and `neverEvolves`; Echo has no plan (`mirror`). Grogg and Echo carry the Balanced weights (all 50) so `BotProfile.weights` is always filled. Rook's counter weight ×1.5 is `counterWeightBp` 15,000; The Warden's 1-in-5 Arena 8 ladder chance is `arenas.list[7].wardenChanceBp` 2,000. Procedural Commanders may copy the personalities of the 8 ladder Generals (`commanderPersonalities`). (DESIGN A6.8, A6.10, A7.4)
- 2026-09-27 (capsules): Stack guarantees are an ordered rarity list per tier (`guaranteed`), which reproduces DESIGN's "Expected copies" column and the 9.1 copies / 227 Amber bag averages exactly (tests recompute them). Climb start per capsule kind: Win and Clay meter from Clay, Daily from Bronze, Road, Age, Codex, Conquest and Age Unlock fixed (no climb). Only Age Unlock Capsules skip pity and script counting (A6.5). Conquest milestone capsules use kind `conquest`; the star-3 reward is an Age Capsule. (DESIGN A6.4, A6.5, A10)
- 2026-09-27 (cosmetics): DESIGN names no frames, so the eight Codex frames are Bark, Bone, Bronze, Iron, Brass, Steel, Chrome and Aeon. A new profile has the Tar Pit banner, the Recruit title and frame `none` (no frame until Codex 5). Banners, frames and emotes carry no visual ids because A14.1 lists none; the UI/visuals can key them by id. Profile names use the prefixes Chief, Warlord, Captain, Marshal, Elder and Scout plus 4 digits ("Chief-4821", A6.1); AI Commander names are built from syllables and always start with "AI · " (A7.4). (DESIGN A5.8, A6.1, A7.4)
- 2026-09-27 (strings): Content owns these i18n namespaces in `src/i18n/content.en.json`: `card`, `skin`, `age`, `format`, `rarity`, `role`, `group`, `tag`, `foil`, `capsuleTier`, `capsuleKind`, `arena`, `general`, `quest`, `modifier`, `banner`, `frame`, `title`, `emote` (other string files should not use them). Key helpers are in `src/content/keys.ts`. Quest names use one placeholder, `{n}` = the quest's `target` (`t(q.nameKey, { n: q.target })`); time-based quest names spell their times out. The file holds exactly the keys the content references (a test checks both directions). (DESIGN B4 Strings)
- 2026-09-27 (validation): `src/content/schema.ts` (Valibot strict objects plus semantic checks) runs in tests and is meant for dev boot (`docs/requests/wp1-dev-boot-validation.md`); `index.ts` does not import it, so production ships no schema. The collection shape (35 + 20 cards, 25/15/10/5 by rarity, per-age slots, 10 powers, 12 skins) is enforced there too. (DESIGN B4 Validation)
- 2026-09-27 (dev page): WP1's dev page is `src/dev/content/page.tsx` (`?dev=1#content`): validation status, content hash, card tables with Strong/Weak vs, the counter heatmap and a live duel inspector. WP1 owns `src/dev/content/**`. (DESIGN C1 dev pages)
- 2026-09-28 (review): Schema checks added in the WP1 review, each the plainest reading of DESIGN: a Conquest board tier (A6.10) must lie inside that General's own tier range (A7.4); scripted onboarding cards are revealed as NEW (A6.5), so none may be a starter Common (A3), and a script capsule holds no more guaranteed cards than it has stacks; every age's Anti-armor Rare must arrive by the script or by an arena's Age Unlock Capsule (A3). Strings that spell out table numbers (quest times, title unlocks, disclosures, modifier and card descriptions) are checked against the tables in `src/content/test/strings.test.ts`, so a balance change that touches one fails until the text follows. `roadAmber` truncates (B3 integers). (DESIGN A3, A6.3, A6.5, A6.10, A7.4)

## WP2

- 2026-09-27 (content units): The sim reads `CompiledContent` in table units (ms, lu, lu/s, whole HP, gold, bp) and converts once per content object into its runtime tables (`src/sim/rules.ts`, cached in a `WeakMap`): ticks, milli-lu, milli-lu per tick, centi-HP at spawn, milli-gold. WP1 confirmed the convention (see WP1 "units"); the sim does not read `content.int`. Tests and golden replays compile `tests/fixtures/content` with the local shim `src/sim/shim.ts` (`compileForSim`); `src/sim/test/realContent.test.ts` checks that WP1's `content` yields the same sim rules as the shim. Values the contract has no field for are rules in code: Brace and air knockback resist 100%, the final-age XP cap = `economy.overchargeXp` (both 1,200), the modernise credit = `economy.sellRefundBp` (both 50%), Stampede's fallback start p = 200 (`STAMPEDE_FALLBACK_P`), mid-lane = the centre of `powerZoneClamp`. (DESIGN A2.4, A2.7, A2.8, A5.7, B3, B4)
- 2026-09-27 (units of state and events): As B3: x in milli-lu, HP/shields/damage in centi, gold/XP in milli, charge in ppm. `Observation.me.gold` is milli-gold (state units, like the fake sim); `xpBp` is XP as bp of the current threshold (final age: of the 1,200 Overcharge amount). `died.bountyGold` / `bountyXp` are the bounty before the XP cap; `goldEarned` / `xpEarned` carry what was credited. `hit.damage` is the full pipeline result (overkill included), `shieldAbsorbed` the part the shields took; `hit.heavy` = damage ≥ 15% of the victim's max HP (A12). `hit.x` is the target's x. `targetId` 0 means "no target" (ids start at 1). (DESIGN B3, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (source ids): Turrets and powers have no entity id. Turret hits and projectiles use `sourceId` / `from` = −10 − (side × 4 + mount) (`turretSourceId`, exported by `@/sim`); `turretFired` precedes its `projectileFired` in the same tick. Power hits use `sourceId` −1 with `castId` set; Last Stand hits −2 with `sourceCard` `last_stand`. For kills by powers and Last Stand `died.killerId` is null; for turret kills it is the turret source id. Called strikes (Radio Operator) are projectiles with visual `proj.shell` and `sourceKind` `ability`. (DESIGN A14.2, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (event cadence): Passive gold and XP accrue every tick but are reported once per second as one `goldEarned` / `xpEarned` (reason `passive`), to keep the stream small. Sell refunds and cancel refunds add gold without an event (the HUD reads `state`). `turretSold` and `turretReplaced` fire when the 1 s sale or modernise starts (the turret stops firing at once, the view plays the clip); the mount frees (sale) or `turretBuilt` fires (modernise) 1 s later. The Orbital Lance emits one `powerImpact` per tick of its sweep (index = tick of the sweep); Stampede one per aurochs at launch; Smoke Screen, Royal Decree, Nanite Surge and Paratroopers one at the telegraph end. `prevX` is set at the start of each tick (the position the previous tick ended at), which is what B3 step 17's interpolation bookkeeping needs. (DESIGN B3, B6)
- 2026-09-27 (distances): All "within N lu" rules between units use edge distance (A2.7: all ranges are edge distances): attack ranges, auras, heal radius, Roar, EMP, Time Stop, called-strike search, chain hops. Splash radii and barrage impacts use centre distance ("whose centre lies within the radius"). Cleave, pierce and follow-behind reach is measured from the primary's centre to the other unit's near edge, on the side away from the attacker. Line and gate-zone turrets measure edge distance from their own gate line; turret range likewise. The Orbital Lance touches a unit when the beam is within 20 lu of its edge. "Allies" never include the source (auras, heals, Roar). (DESIGN A2.6, A2.7, A5)
- 2026-09-27 (movement): Soft single file is resolved front to back per side against the planned positions of the allies ahead; the first capping ally ahead may be joined side by side (front rank) and every later one keeps (wA + wB) × 0.3. An ally caps a mover when it moved this tick or its longest range ≤ the mover's; others are passed. Enemy blocking uses pre-move positions; when the nearest enemy ahead also wants to advance, each side may take at most floor(gap / 2) (symmetric resolution). Retreats (Hold) never back into an enemy behind. Knockback, pulls and the Toad drag all use the knockback resist (Brace and air 100%, large and huge 50%) and cancel pending windups; leaping units ignore them. The Toad drag stops at the target side's frontmost ground unit and short of the dragging side's nearest ground unit ahead. Units standing still without a target report mode `hold`. (DESIGN A2.7)
- 2026-09-27 (targeting): Units keep a target within range + 20 lu leash; attacks start only in range. The base counts as a lower priority class than any unit, so at the 1 s re-check a unit hitting the base switches to an enemy unit in range. Turrets pick afresh for every shot (0% windup), `front` = nearest their gate. Grumpy Toad: nearest enemy ranged or support ground unit in range, else the second-closest small or medium ground enemy (the only one when there is one). Gravity Well aims at the densest point of its candidates' span on the 10 lu grid; the primary is the enemy nearest that point. Power auto-aim scans p 150-1,050 in 10 lu steps for the most summed enemy card cost within half the zone (ties: the p nearest the caster), mid-lane when nobody scores. (DESIGN A2.7, A2.8, A2.9, A5.2, A5.6)
- 2026-09-27 (abilities and powers): Stunned or leaping units use no abilities (auras continue). Pounce's "blocked" = an enemy ground unit ahead within its attack range; the leap takes 0.5 s, cancels windups, and the next attack 0 bites ×2. Roar needs attack 0's target in range. The Battering Ram targets the base when in range, else only enemy ground units ahead within its range. Stampede ignores the aim and starts at the caster's frontmost ground unit (p = 200 without one); Paratroopers ignore the aim and land where the cast computed (150 lu beyond the enemy front at cast time). Royal Decree and Nanite Surge apply at the telegraph end to every own unit alive then; the Nanite shield scales with the loadout multiplier, its 40% regen does not (it is already proportional to max HP) and heals on the shared 10-tick grid. Barrage, Stampede, Lance, Last Stand and death explosions are exempt from the area rule. Smoke Screen misses are rolled with the sim RNG when a projectile (or instant effect) is fired from or into an enemy cloud; missed shots still fly and emit `projectileFired` but deal nothing. Legendaries receive 50% of heals and regen. (DESIGN A2.7, A2.9, A5)
- 2026-09-27 (training): `training.trays` restricts the learner (side 0) only, so the scripted opponent trains from its full loadout; `unlockSlot` adds a slot to the event side's tray for its current age, and an age missing from `trays` is unrestricted. Training pauses during Ascension (spawns of already finished items still happen). A finished item that does not fit waits and later items keep training; each finished item spawns as soon as it fits, in queue order. `cancelTrain` refunds exactly what was paid (`QueueItem.paid`, milli-gold). Scouted lists record a card when it is first played: trained unit spawned, turret build or modernise started, power cast. (DESIGN A2.7, A3, A8)
- 2026-09-27 (numbers): Every intermediate stays a safe integer (< 2^53) with truncation after each bp step; B3's ±2^31 bound cannot hold for centi-HP × bp (a Legendary's 560,000 centi × 14,500 bp at L10), so it is not enforced. Rejection reasons are stable codes listed in `src/sim/commands.ts`. `buildReplay` refuses a sim touched by the dev helpers (`src/sim/debug.ts`). `SIM_VERSION` (replay.ts) is bumped with every behaviour change, together with re-recording the golden replays (`UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 npx vitest run src/sim/test/golden.test.ts`). (DESIGN B3, B13)
- 2026-09-27 (tests and benchmark): Golden replays are recorded from deterministic scripted players (`src/sim/test/helpers.ts`) on the frozen fixture content, cover all four formats, the six daily modifiers, alternate powers, Epics, Legendaries and every turret rarity, and are loaded with `import.meta.glob` (src has no Node types). The fast-check invariants run 20 random matches (random formats, plans, levels, strategies plus random valid and invalid commands) and check every tick. Vitest 5 runs benchmarks through its module runner, which wraps every cross-module call in a tracked getter (about 5× on this hot loop), so `src/sim/test/sim.bench.ts` bundles the sim with esbuild (as the game ships it) and measures that: worst-case Full War ≈ 105 ms, a full-length golden Full War ≈ 92 ms, budget 400 ms. `src/sim/test/perf.test.ts` is a loose regression guard (< 1.5 s under the runner). (DESIGN B3, B13)
- 2026-09-27 (duel harness): `runDuel(content, a, b, countA, countB, { maxTicks })` in `src/sim/duel.ts` (exported from `@/sim`) answers `docs/requests/wp1-counter-duel-harness.md`: level-1 groups start 300 lu apart around mid-lane on the real rules (no turrets, powers, phases or Last Stand); it returns each side's HP left in bp. Units that find nothing to hit keep advancing along the lane (the sim has no "turn around"), so pairs that can never trade run out the clock. Mirror duels are exactly even. (DESIGN B4)
- 2026-09-28 (review: content is data): The battle numbers without a contract field are now read from the content instead of code (CLAUDE.md "Content is data"), superseding the last sentence of "content units" above. `rules.ts` `battleOf(content)` reads WP1's `content.battle` (the shim passes the raw `battle` table through the same way) for mid-lane, the default windups, the Brace and air knockback resists, the modernise credit (`moderniseCreditBp`), the final-age XP cap (`finalAgeXpCap`), the Siege decay step and Stampede's fallback start; content without the table (the contract fakes) and malformed fields fall back to the DESIGN values (`DEFAULT_BATTLE`). Daily Challenge effects are read from `content.dailyModifiers.list[id].effect` (`modifiers.ts`); content without a modifier table (the frozen fixture, the fakes) uses the A9.1 table in the sim, so golden replays never move when a modifier is tuned. Mark stays the status magnitude on the card (Searchlight 2,000 bp). Lane length and default projectile speeds stay out of scope (every card carries its own speed; the lane is `LANE_MLU` in core). No behaviour change: golden replays were identical before the re-record below. (DESIGN A2.1-A2.11, A9.1, B4)
- 2026-09-28 (review: splash primary): A splash projectile is aimed at a point (the target's x at fire time, A2.7), so its primary is the enemy whose centre is nearest the impact (A2.6), not the unit it was fired at; before, the fired-at unit stayed primary while inside the radius. Melee splash (Mammoth Matriarch) keeps its target as the primary. (DESIGN A2.6, A2.7)
- 2026-09-28 (review: base target): The enemy base is never a sticky target, superseding "(targeting)" above: it is a candidate only while no unit candidate exists (A2.7), so a unit hitting the base turns to an enemy unit the tick one is in range (a defender spawning at its gate), instead of at the next 1 s re-check. Unit targets keep the stickiness rules. (DESIGN A2.7)
- 2026-09-28 (review: emotes): An `emote` command with an id outside the six `EmoteId`s is rejected (`badCommand`), so the event stream only carries known emotes. (DESIGN A2.12, B15)
- 2026-09-28 (review: version): The two behaviour changes above bump `SIM_VERSION` to 1.1.0; the 10 golden replays were re-recorded. The tutorial's match 1 beats move (Future 1701 → 1731, Grogg falls 2210 → 2282 ticks); WP11 is asked to re-pin `MATCH1_TIMING` (`docs/requests/wp2-tutorial-retime.md`). (DESIGN A8, B13)
- 2026-09-28 (review: third rank): A2.7 says the soft single file lets Infantry (range 16) hit from the third position and reach units (range ≥ 55) too. That only holds when the front rank stands at contact; under A2.7's own movement rule ("advances unless it has a valid target in range") the front rank stops at 12.5-16 lu (Infantry) or 56.5-60 lu (reach) from the enemy, so the third rank is 26-30 lu (Infantry) or 76-80 lu (Spear Hunter) away and never hits: only two melee units per side fight at a time. The sim keeps the literal movement rule; the choice is raised with the integration lead for Phase 3 in `docs/requests/wp2-third-rank-reach.md`. (DESIGN A2.7, A2.14)

## WP5

- 2026-09-27 (render API): `BattleView` (`src/render/battleView.ts`) takes `{ sim, art, audio, mySide?, settings?, feel?, isMobile?, arena?, seed?, onPresetChange? }`, where `sim` is anything with read-only `state` and `config` (a live sim or a replay). The session calls `view.onEvents(events)` after every `sim.step`, `view.render(alpha, frameMs)` once per frame, and adds no time to its accumulator while `view.simFrozen` is true (global freezes, A12). `render/loop.ts` `FixedStepClock` holds the B6 accumulator arithmetic. The view raises `ViewEvent`s (`view.on`) for the HUD: mount taps, emotes, denied commands, evolves, coins, match end. `ui` may not import `render` (B2), so the HUD takes the view through the structural `HudViewBridge` in `ui/hud/bridge.ts`, which `BattleView` satisfies. (DESIGN B2, B6)
- 2026-09-27 (feel config): `feel.config.json` satisfies the frozen `FeelConfig` and adds render-only fields (`RenderFeelConfig` in `render/feelConfig.ts`): per rule `flashTarget` (victim/screen/base), `flashAlpha`, `traumaGapMs` (base hits: trauma at most once per 0.5 s), `jitterPx` (heavy-hit victim jitter), `slowMo` and `globalExempt` (base destroyed); and a `tuning` block for numbers A12 states outside its table (ghost hold/drain, number spread and merge window, coin travel and pitch climb, sound gaps, music intensity increments, reduce-motion factors, seam drift, camera follow and zoom, death linger, telegraph length, 1-in-3 mech explosions). Rule keys are event keys the mapper derives (`hit.light`, `hit.heavy`, `death.unit|heavy|legendary|mech`, `power.impact`, `evolve.own|enemy`, `lastStand.fire`, `base.destroyed`, ...). Per-event ids use placeholders resolved by the mapper: `$spark`, `$hitSound`, `$attackSound`, `$dieSound`, `$spawnSound`, `$powerSound`, `$fanfare`. (DESIGN A12, B6)
- 2026-09-27 (event units): The view converts every sim position to lu, including `powerTelegraph.zone`, which the real sim emits in milli-lu like `x` (500 lu = 500,000). The canned fake stream still says `zone: 500`; requested a fix in `docs/requests/wp5-fake-telegraph-zone.md`. (DESIGN B3)
- 2026-09-27 (camera and layout): World y is lu with 0 on the ground line, which sits at 80% of the lane band's height. The fit scale is `min(band width / 1,560, band height / 330)`, so on very wide, short screens tall units and bases stay in the band and the world is centred. Pinch zoom (screens < 900 CSS px) keeps the ground line in place and grows upward; the follow target is the midpoint of the two ground front lines (a side with no ground units counts its gate). Air units fly at y = −118 lu (alternating −14 lu per air unit) above the depth rows. (DESIGN A2.1)
- 2026-09-27 (juice details): Coins that fly to the gold counter = bounty / 25 gold, clamped to 1-4. Hits from the same source on the same target within 380 ms merge into one number (a barrage reads as a few totals). The music intensity layer is a view-side estimate: each hit, heavy hit, death, base hit, power and own evolve adds a tuned amount, it decays 0.2/s and is pushed to audio at 4 Hz. Own evolves transpose +2, +2, +1, +1. Level trims: bronze at levels 4-6, silver 7-9, gold 10. Melee swing sounds play 80 ms before the impact frame. (DESIGN A12, A13)
- 2026-09-27 (presets): Auto starts High on desktop and Lite on mobile, drops to Lite when the average frame over 3 s exceeds 20 ms, never climbs back during a match, and ignores the first 1.5 s of frames (texture uploads and lazy bakes are not the steady frame rate). The particle cap is the preset's cap, never above the device cap in `feel.config.json` (600 mobile, 1,500 desktop). The view applies the particle cap, shadows and Legendary auras; the host applies `view.resolution(dpr)` (DPR ≤ 2, 1 in Lite). (DESIGN B6, B16)
- 2026-09-27 (HUD input): A press the HUD can already tell is invalid (unaffordable card, uncharged power, Evolve not ready, ...) gets the denied feedback locally with `ui_deny`; everything else is sent and a sim `commandRejected` flashes the element that last sent that command type (the view plays `ui_deny`). Mouse presses do not move focus onto HUD buttons, so Space always casts the power; a Tab-focused button keeps Space/Enter. Held keys do not repeat. A long press of 450 ms cancels a card's last queued instance. The power button turns into a drag after 12 px, only when charged and aimable (Stampede, Royal Decree, Nanite Surge and Paratroopers ignore the aim, WP2); releasing a drag off the lane cancels. The mount popover asks for a second tap to sell; Shift + click on a mount sells at once and the "+" mount buys at once (A2.12). The emote button honours `economy.emoteCooldownMs`. (DESIGN A2.9, A2.12, A9.2)
- 2026-09-27 (HUD layout and look): The HUD root must cover the canvas box exactly (view-local and HUD-local coordinates are the same). It is a CSS size container: the top bar is 12% of its height, the tray 24%, and cards are 88 px when the HUD is at least 900 px wide, 72 px below. HUD chrome icons (coin, age glyphs, role glyphs, horn, emotes, pause/speed) are inline SVG in `ui/hud/icons.tsx`, one module a later art pass can replace; card, turret and power pictures come from `ArtProvider.portrait` through the `portrait` prop, with role glyphs as the fallback. `ui` keeps its own copy of the A11 team colours per preset because it may not import `render` or `visuals`. On short screens only your own name row is dropped; the opponent's AI chip and label always show (A7.1). The portrait "Rotate your device" overlay is WP9's (C2/WP9). (DESIGN A7.1, A9.2, A11, B2)
- 2026-09-27 (dev pages): `?dev=1#sandbox` has three tabs: the battle view (fake stream + fake art for the Phase 1 DoD; a real-sim match you can play against a WP3 AI General or a dev autoplayer, or watch bot vs bot; the 80-unit stress scene; fake or procedural art), the HUD state gallery, and WP2's sim panel. It shows fps, frame and CPU time, WebGL draw calls, particles and the freeze budget. `?dev=1#feel` tunes every feel number live, fires single A12 events into the view, keeps edits in localStorage and copies the JSON for `feel.config.json`. The dev pages load WP4 and WP3 through `import.meta.glob` with loose typing and fall back to the fakes, so they keep working while those packages change. (DESIGN A9 screen 16, A12, C2/WP5)
- 2026-09-27 (review: effect options): `ArtProvider.createEffect` / `EffectView.playAt` options are untyped in the contract, so the render uses the art's documented option names (WP4 `src/visuals/effects/recipes.ts`): `side`, `dir` (+1 faces right), `radius`, `zone`, `width`, `height`, `distance`, `speed`, `durationMs` (length of looping effects), `toX`/`toY`. Screen effects (Overdrive frame, Siege vignette) are played at the canvas's top-left corner with `width`/`height` in CSS px. The telegraph decoration gets `zone` and `durationMs`; ability rings (EMP, Time Stop, Roar) get the ability's `radius`; the call-strike marker lasts the strike delay; Legendary auras get a `radius` of 55% of the figure's height and sit on its hit centre. A real-sim test checks that every sized effect played in a Full War got its size. (DESIGN A11, A12, B5)
- 2026-09-27 (review: per-power presets): `power.<slug>` is the HUD icon, not an effect, so power impacts now play A12's "per-power preset" from `feel.config.json`: `power.fx.<powerId>` (else `power.fx.<effect kind>`) on every impact and `power.fx.<powerId>.first` (else `.<kind>.first`) on the first. Meteor Shower → `fx.meteor`, Arrow Storm → `fx.arrow_rain`, Broadside → `fx.cannonball_rain`, Carpet Bomber → `fx.plane_bomber` once over the zone centre plus `fx.explosion_m` per bomb, Stampede → `fx.aurochs` per runner, Orbital Lance → one `fx.orbital_beam` sweep (its per-tick impacts add no effect or shake), Smoke Screen → `fx.smoke_cloud`, Paratroopers → one `fx.parachute` per trooper, Royal Decree / Nanite Surge → `fx.decree_glow` / `fx.nanite_swarm` on every unit of the caster (rule field `fxTarget: 'sideUnits'`). Sizes and durations come from the power's data, the zone centre from the cast's telegraph. (DESIGN A12, A14.1)
- 2026-09-27 (review: status effects): Status effects (mark, dizzy, Time Stop ripple, heal glyph, shield) follow their unit, loop for the status duration, and end when the unit dies (A12 checklist 10). The lasting shield bubble is the art's (`UnitPose.shieldBp`, B5), so `status.shield` is a 500 ms "shield up" pop (`tuning.shieldPopMs`). The Roar (Ursa's `periodicShieldAura`) shows `fx.roar_ring`. (DESIGN A11, A12)
- 2026-09-27 (review: splash rings, decay, sounds): A splash or pull attack shows one `fx.splash_ring` / `fx.gravity_swirl` per impact (first hit of that source in the tick), sized by its radius; called strikes too. Siege decay (`baseDamaged` with `sourceId: null`) only crumbles (2 debris): no base hit clip, shake, flash or `base_hit`, and its number is not Important, so the 0.5%/s decay does not read as an attack. `evolve_ready` (A13 "single soft chime") plays once when your Evolve becomes available; `xp_tick` plays when your kill's XP sparkles reach the bar (throttled like coins). Muted AI emotes are dropped before mapping, so they make no sound either. Delayed sounds keep their throttle. (DESIGN A2.10, A12, A13)

## WP11

- 2026-09-27 (session): `BattleSessionImpl` (`src/app/session.ts`) follows the B6 loop with WP5's `FixedStepClock` and `HudModelBuilder`. Human commands are accepted only while running and only for the player's side; bot commands for another side are dropped and counted (A7.1). All commands of a tick are stamped `sim.tick + 1` with a per-side seq and recorded sorted by (side, seq), so the session's `ReplayDoc` equals the sim's own `buildReplay` (tested). The render loop runs from construction (the title is the live battlefield); ticks run only after `start()`. The visibility pause pauses on hidden and stays paused on return (the player resumes; C5 #20). The HUD signal refreshes at 15 Hz on the frame clock (2 ms slack, so every 4th frame at 60 fps qualifies). `fastForward(ticks)` is the dev fast-forward of B13 e2e step 6. Match stats come from WP2's `createStatsTracker`. (DESIGN A7.1, B3, B6, B11)
- 2026-09-27 (Pixi scheduler): the session and replay loops run on the Pixi ticker through one high-priority listener that runs the callbacks requested before the frame (`pixiHost.ts`). Registering a Pixi `addOnce` listener from inside a ticker callback runs it again in the same tick, which froze the page. (DESIGN B6)
- 2026-09-27 (services): `services.ts` builds every service from a `ServiceChoice` through dynamic-import loaders, so fakes that are not chosen are never downloaded. Default: real content, sim, bots (WP3 `createBot`) and art (WP4); audio, save and meta use the recording fake, the in-memory store and no meta until WP6, WP8 and WP7 are wired (Phase 2 adds their loaders; a chosen but missing implementation falls back with a warning). `?svc=fake` or per service (`?sim=fake`, `?art=fake`, `?bots=fallback`) overrides; a fake sim forces fake content. `fallbackBot.ts` is an honest stand-in bot (A7.3 decision interval and snapshot delay per tier, observation only) kept for dev autopilots and tests. (DESIGN B11, C2/WP11)
- 2026-09-27 (tutorial retiming): with the Tutorial thresholds (250/300/350/400 XP) a dummy kill is 50 XP and 1% of Grogg's base 12 XP, so the real pace is faster than the A8 draft whatever Grogg sends; slowing XP would need rule changes. The scripted run (autopilot tapping every 1.5 s from 0:02.5, following each prompt) gives: first kill 0:15, Pebbler 0:17, Tuskback and 150 gold 0:23-0:24, Evolve ready 0:31, Medieval 0:36, Arrow Storm 0:42, Gunpowder 0:48, Modern 1:06, Future 1:25, Grogg falls 1:51 (A8 draft: 0:17 ... 3:00). The A8 order holds. Grogg sends two Training Dummies before his Tuskback, then one every 8 s (skipped while 3 of his units live), and his gold is granted by the script one tick before each send. The Arrow Storm is filled by `setPowerPpm` at 0:42 because the natural charge (halved on evolve) would finish only in Gunpowder. `MATCH1_TIMING` pins the measured ticks; `tutorial/test/retime.test.ts` fails when content tuning moves a beat by more than 3 s. (DESIGN A8)
- 2026-09-27 (director): beats of match 1 show strictly in A8 order, each at most once. An action done early (for example the turret built while an earlier prompt is up) completes its beat silently once the beat's trigger has held; a beat whose text names a card of one age (`onlyInAge`) is skipped when the player leaves that age; a shown beat retires after its timeout. Shown/done/timeout/skipped go to the event log (drop-off instrumentation). Adaptive hints (A8 table, patterns in `tutorial/hints.ts`) fire only when no beat is on screen, at most once per 30 s, at most 3 times each (counts in `SaveDoc.tutorial.hintsShown`). The "Heavies stop Bonkers" hint only fires when Bonkers die to Heavies and a Spear Hunter is in the loadout, because the text names those cards. (DESIGN A8, C5 #2)
- 2026-09-27 (Old Grogg): the app plays Grogg with `tutorial/grogg.ts` (a `BotController` over `tutorial/scripts.ts` data), not WP3's `ScriptedController`; see `docs/requests/wp11-grogg-brain.md`. The match 1 player loadouts are the starter commons per age (Infantry, Ranged, Heavy in slots 0-2, both Common turrets, the default power) with trays Stone `[0]` (Pebbler slot 1 unlocked at 0:17) and `[0, 1]` in every later age. (DESIGN A3, A7.4, A8, B10)
- 2026-09-27 (staged unlocks): match n = `matchesPlayed + 1`. Matches 1-3 set `manualLastStand: [false, true]` and `stanceEnabled: [false, true]` (bots keep both), match 4 only `manualLastStand`, match 5 on no training flags. War Plan and Skirmish open after match 3. The onboarding step lives in `SaveDoc.tutorial.step` (0 match 1, 1 capsule 1, 2 match 2, 3 capsule 2, 4 Home); a lost match 1 is replayed, match 2 moves on either way. Without meta (Phase 1) the capsule steps complete at once and the step is also kept in memory. (DESIGN A2.11, A3, A8)
- 2026-09-27 (match setup): the opponent side of an `OpponentSpec` is used as is but always flagged `isBot`, labeled with the display name. Bot profiles take the General's weights (Balanced when unknown) and the A6.8 new-player mistake bonus for the first 20 matches. Match 2's Pip plays the Arena 1 bot level with cards above the Arena 1 rarity allowance removed. The bot RNG seed is `fnv1a32("${matchSeed}:${side}")` (A7.2). Quick Battle (C3) is Short War (or the chosen format) vs Captain Kettle at tier III with no training flags or hints; `?quick=short|standard|full` opens it directly. (DESIGN A6.8, A7.2, A8, C3)
- 2026-09-27 (end flow): after the sim ends, the view keeps animating for 2.5 s, then `finishMatch` pushes the replay into the store's ring, applies meta (or only counts the match without meta), merges hint counts and the onboarding step, and saves immediately (capsules are rolled at grant time, B8). The result screen stages rewards one per 0.9 s; a tap reveals the next at once. (DESIGN A9 #7, B8, B11)
- 2026-09-27 (replays): the `ReplayPlayer` re-simulates with `createSim` + the recorded commands by tick on the same frame clock (1x/2x/4x, restart re-creates sim and view, no seek) and reports `verified` (outcome and final hash). A replay whose `contentHash` or `simVersion` differs from the build is `incompatible` ("from an older version"): a changed sim version can change outcomes too. (DESIGN A9 #14, B3)
- 2026-09-27 (strings): WP11's strings live in `src/i18n/tutorial.en.json`, the only string file WP11 owns, under the prefixes `tutorial.` (prompts, at most 8 words, tested), `app.` (Phase 1 shell) and `replay.`. (DESIGN A8, B4 Strings)
- 2026-09-27 (Phase 1 shell): until WP9's screens are wired, the app renders its own title (the live battlefield with one Play button; after onboarding: Quick Battle with a format picker and this session's replays), battle (WP5's HUD, the tutorial bubble and a small pause panel), result and replay screens under `src/app/ui` and `src/app/screens/replay`, with its own rotate overlay. `?dev=1&autopilot=1` boots the game with the autopilot and presses Play; `window.__agebornDev.fastForward` exists with `?dev=1`. Dev boot runs WP1's content validation (`docs/requests/wp1-dev-boot-validation.md`). (DESIGN A8, A9, B11, B13)
- 2026-09-28 (review: retry): a lost onboarding match offers Retry on the result screen (A8 "a loss still gives rewards plus a retry"). After match 1 Retry is the only button (the step stays at match 1). After match 2 the loss has already paid out and moved on to capsule 2, so Retry is one more Short War vs Pip (mode `tutorial`, match script 2) and Next still goes on; the retry never moves the step back. (DESIGN A8)
- 2026-09-28 (review: opponents): bot profiles are built with WP3's `botProfile`; a procedural AI Commander (`commander:<personality>:<card>`, meta) plays with its personality General's weights and opening plus its favourite card (A7.4). A missing save counts as a first launch for the A6.8 new-player mistake bonus. Meta names named Generals by string key, so `setupForMode` resolves the name through i18n before it becomes `SideConfig.label` (the HUD nameplate), and the app screens resolve names the same way (`app/names.ts`). Skirmish "Standard levels" also puts every card of the player at the content's `standardLevel` (L7). The player's label before a save exists comes from i18n (`app.you`). (DESIGN A6.8, A7.4, B4)
- 2026-09-28 (review: emotes, end flow): the session relays the other side's emotes to bots that implement `hearEmote` (WP3's reply rule, A7.2), like WP3's `BotMatch`. `finishMatch` runs as soon as the sim ends (meta and the immediate save come before the 2.5 s end animation, B8); a failure there is logged and the result still shows, and a replay that cannot be stored never blocks the save. (DESIGN A7.2, B8)

## WP10

- 2026-09-27 (inputs): The show takes outcomes only from `CapsuleReveal` / `WardrobeReveal`. Presentation lookups (a card's age, visual id and name key; a skin's rarity and target; which capsule kinds climb) come from an injected `CapsuleCatalog` that `createCatalog(content)` builds from `CompiledContent` (a contract type, so `capsule` never imports `content`, B2). Copies bars come from an injected `ProgressLookup`; `progressFromCollections(before, after, upgradeCopies, maxLevel)` diffs the collection around `meta.openCapsule`, so the bar is exact whatever rule the meta uses for a new card's first copy. (C2/WP10, B2, A10 step 7)
- 2026-09-27 (climb): The strike pattern is derived from `startTier` and `tier` (the rolled result is the source of truth); `climbs` and `strikeClimbs` are cross-checked and a mismatch is logged in dev only. The drum never shows a tier above the rolled one. Kinds with `climbFrom: null` start at the burst (A6.4). `cap_climb_N` is the note of the tier reached (Bronze 1 ... Aeon 4), so each climb is a step higher and reaching Aeon always sounds highest. "Tap!" appears from 60% of the charge, and a tap from then on queues the first strike. (A10 steps 2-3, A13)
- 2026-09-27 (timing): arrival 0.5 s (thud at 0.22 s); charge 1.5 s; strikes 0.6 s after a tap or 1.5 s idle (the hammer lands at 90 ms); burst 0.3 s; fan ≤ 1 s; a 0.3 s pre-signal for every card; flips 0.15 / 0.4 / 0.8 s (a Legendary card lands face up in 0.4 s after its walkout; a Legendary skin flips in 0.8 s); foil sweep 0.5 s (Holo 1 s); NEW stamp with silhouette fill 0.45 s; NEW Epic mini-walkout 2 s; first-ever Legendary walkout 9 s, repeat 3 s; duplicates 0.5 s per stack; crate 0.5 s + reel 5.5 s + winner 1.6 s. `checkPlan` enforces the A10 limits on every plan; the bench test runs it on every bench case. (A10)
- 2026-09-27 (sounds): A13 has no tier stinger ids, so the burst layers `cap_climb_<final tier>` at −3 dB after `cap_burst` (none for Clay). Other ids used: `card_flip` (quiet and pitched down for the deal), `ui_confirm` (NEW stamp), `spawn_heavy` and `swing_whoosh` (mini-walkout), `spawn_legendary` (walkout drop). `pitchBp` is a playback-rate multiplier in bp (10,000 = unchanged), as in WP5. Music ducks −6 dB for walkouts and mini-walkouts; the screen switches to `music.capsule` unless `playMusic` is false. (A13)
- 2026-09-27 (input and skip): Tap fires a waiting strike, queues the next during a strike, and hurries other reveal steps to 4×; a press held ≥ 240 ms fast-forwards at 3×; Skip (button, Esc or S) jumps to the next step that may not be skipped (a first-ever Legendary walkout, which also ignores fast-forward and taps) or to the summary. Skipped steps settle silently: no cues, no flashes. The Skip button shows on every other step, so nothing runs longer than 10 s without a skip. (A10 Rules)
- 2026-09-27 (open all): "Open all" merges the capsules by card (copies and Dust add up, the best foil wins, NEW if any stack was new), plays a volley of bursts (0.15 s per capsule, 0.6-2 s), then only the Epic-or-better reveals with their walkouts, then one summary; there is no duplicates step. Pity before is the first capsule's, after is the last's. (A10 Rules)
- 2026-09-27 (Dust and skins): Dust shown = `contents.dust` (capsule-level, e.g. the Jade +100) + every `stack.dust` (max-level conversion). An Aeon's bonus `contents.skin` is its own card stamped "SKIN"; the reveal cannot tell a duplicate skin, so it is never stamped NEW. Both readings are in `docs/requests/wp10-meta-reveal-data.md` for WP7 to confirm. (A6.4)
- 2026-09-27 (pity and odds): Pity lines read "within N" with N = rule − since (at least 1), using the field names of `content.capsules.pity`, so the app passes that object as is. Wardrobe screens take the save's pity counters as a prop (the reveal carries none). The capsule screen shows an Odds button only when the app passes `onShowOdds` (WP9's odds overview); it owns no odds tables. (A6.5, A9 screen 8)
- 2026-09-27 (reel): The stop point maps `stopOffsetBp` onto the middle 84% of the winning tile (8% clear of each edge) so it always reads as inside. Tick cues closer than 40 ms (the mixer's retrigger gap) are dropped; pitch falls from ×1.10 to ×0.92. The view always draws the crate's skin at tile 45 and never a rarer tile at 46 (`reelTilesForView`); `checkReel` reports reveal data that would need that fix. With the flag off, the crate's single card rises out of the crate, pre-signals and flips. (A10.1)
- 2026-09-27 (summary): Items list rarest first, new before owned. "Equip now" shows per new card (and per new crate skin, `onEquipSkin`); "Upgrade" jumps to the rarest ready card, then the lowest level, then reveal order; "Open next (N)" shows when `pendingCount > 0`. (A10 step 8)
- 2026-09-27 (stage): The stage is authored at 1280 × 720 and scaled to fit (contain); the room backdrop and screen flash cover the whole canvas. It mounts into the app's persistent Pixi `Application` and removes itself on unmount (B6). Cards fan in one arc up to 7, then in rows (tested to fit up to 24). Reduce motion: no shake, flashes at 40%, a third of the particles. (A10, A12, B6)
- 2026-09-27 (bench): `src/dev/capsuleBench` (`?dev=1#capsuleBench/<case>`, `&art=fake` for the fake art provider) plays 40 cases; `window.__capsuleBench.advance(ms)` steps the show frame by frame for screenshots. (C2/WP10 DoD)
- 2026-09-28 (review: intro walkout): A8 gives capsule 1's "Spear Hunter NEW (short walkout)", a Rare. The simplest reading: in the onboarding script's first capsule (`scriptIndex === 1`, 1-based as the meta writes it) every NEW card below Legendary gets the 2 s mini-walkout that NEW Epics get (banner "NEW CARD"; the meta fills the other stacks with owned Commons, so that is Spear Hunter alone). Other capsules keep the A10 rule: NEW Epics only. (A8, A10 step 5)
- 2026-09-28 (review: first Legendary): a NEW Legendary always gets the full, unskippable walkout, even when `firstLegendaryReveal` does not list it (a NEW card is by definition revealed for the first time); the list still marks the others. (A10 step 6)
- 2026-09-28 (review: walkout views): walkouts stage turret cards with `ArtProvider.createTurret` (`build` for the pop, `fire` for the act, aimed along the lane) and units with `createUnit` and their real role group as the ring glyph; `CardInfo` carries `view` and `group` from content. Using `createUnit` for a turret id only worked by chance with the procedural tier. (B5, A10 step 5)
- 2026-09-28 (review: input and honesty): a press taps at once (the hammer lands on touch, not on release) and a press held 240 ms also fast-forwards. `CapsuleScreen` takes `newCardProtection` (default true); false hides the "New card within N" line once the pool has no unowned card (A6.5 "while unowned cards exist"). The in-game Reduce motion setting stills the DOM pulses like the OS preference. (A10 Input, A6.5, A12)

## WP9

- 2026-09-27 (injection): Screens never import meta, save, visuals or app code (B2). The app gives the `ScreenHost` one `UiEnv`: the save signal, the typed `Content`, `t`, `now`, the router, `UiServices` (queries such as `validatePlan`, `autoFill`, `previewOpponent`, `matchHistory`, and every action) and `ArtProvider.portrait` for card art. `src/ui/screens/fixtures/services.ts` is a local reference implementation used by the dev page and tests; wiring notes in `docs/requests/wp9-app-wiring.md`. (DESIGN B2, B9, B11)
- 2026-09-27 (router): `src/ui/router.ts` is a signal-based stack with screen ids for all A9 screens, including the ones other packages render (boot, battle, capsule, replay: app slots). An entry can be an overlay, so Pause renders over a still-mounted battle and Settings over Pause. `MatchRequest` has `{ mode: 'tutorial'; match: 1 | 2 }` for the onboarding matches (answers `docs/requests/wp11-router-tutorial-route.md`); `ResultInfo.request` is what "Next battle" starts. Escape goes back (Pause: resume; Result: Home; Home, VS and app screens: nothing). Browser history is not used. (DESIGN A8, A9, B11)
- 2026-09-27 (save reading): `arenaIndex` is 0-based into `arenas.list`. `capsules.bag` holds the Win Capsule tiers left in the current bag as tier indices (empty = a fresh 30/40/20/7/3 bag). `conquest.milestonesClaimed` holds star thresholds (9, 18, 27). `cosmetics.owned` holds banner and title ids. Trophy Road nodes are claimable up to the best trophies. WP7 owns these fields; if its encoding differs, only `components/oddsModel.ts` and `screens/model/progress.ts` change. (DESIGN A6.3, A6.5, A6.10, B8)
- 2026-09-27 (unlocks): War Plan and Skirmish open when `matchesPlayed >= 3` (A3 "after match 3"); Conquest when the arena's 1-based index reaches `generals.conquest.unlockArena`; the ladder format picker shows when the arena offers more than one ladder format. Locked items stay visible with the unlock condition. The Home "Capsules" entry is the capsule tray on Home. (DESIGN A3, A6.3, A9)
- 2026-09-27 (War Plan): the averages use units and turrets (powers have no level); "the next format" is the longest ladder format of the current arena. Edits keep a loadout legal by construction: a card already in the loadout moves (the displaced card takes its old slot), unowned cards cannot be picked, the power slot never empties. Presets B and C are created from the active plan the first time they are edited or used. Advisor messages use `ui.advisor.*` keys with `{age}` (`docs/requests/wp9-advisor-keys.md`). (DESIGN A3)
- 2026-09-27 (opponents): Generals are named from the content strings (`GeneralDef.nameKey`); other opponents show `displayName` as given (translated when it is a string key), so procedural commanders keep their "AI · " prefix. Every opponent name carries the robot icon and "AI" chip; VS and the Conquest card add "AI General" and "Plays by the same rules as you"; Settings > About and the Profile history say "All opponents in this version are AI."; Mode select and About carry "Opponent difficulty adapts to your recent results." (DESIGN A7.1)
- 2026-09-27 (result): the recap shows time, units trained, units defeated, turret kills, base damage, evolves and the MVP card; `MatchStats` has no time per age (`docs/requests/wp9-match-stats-age-times.md`). Rewards appear one per 650 ms (the first after 900 ms); a tap reveals the next one, "Tap to skip" reveals all, reduce motion shows all at once. (DESIGN A9 #7)
- 2026-09-27 (VS and pause): VS begins the battle after 2.0 s or on a tap/Enter/Space, exactly once. Pause hides Retreat when the format has none (Tutorial, `retreatAfterMs: null`) and shows it inert with "Retreat unlocks at 1:00" before then; Retreat and Quit Skirmish ask for confirmation. (DESIGN A2.10, A9 #4, A9 #6)
- 2026-09-27 (settings): the backup reminder shows when `lastExportAt` (or, before any export, `createdAt`) is more than 5 days old, so a new profile is not nagged at once. Reset needs two confirmations. Danish is listed but disabled until v1.1. (DESIGN A9 #15, B8)
- 2026-09-27 (card art): card art is `ArtProvider.portrait` when injected, else an age-tinted plate with the role glyph. Unowned cards are true silhouettes: a plate-free portrait (`plate: false`, which WP4's provider supports ahead of the contract, `docs/requests/wp4-portrait-plate-contract.md`) darkened by CSS on a neutral plate. The card detail "animated idle on a stage" is a CSS idle on the portrait under a spotlight; a live rig can be slotted in later by the app. (DESIGN A9 #10, #11, B5)
- 2026-09-27 (look and feel): one theme (`src/ui/theme.css`, `--ui-*` tokens, `ui-` classes) plus a stylesheet per screen: ink outlines, lipped buttons that sink on press, rarity colours (A10) on frames only, eased transitions everywhere, all animation off under the reduce-motion setting and `prefers-reduced-motion`. Team colours follow the colourblind preset (`data-team` on the root). Modals render at the UI root through `preact/compat`'s `createPortal`, so panel transforms and filters never clip them. Touch targets are at least 48 px (checked with a Playwright audit of every screen at 1280×720 and 844×390). Home is self-contained (`screens/home/`, pieces in `parts.tsx`) so an illustrated village can replace it. (DESIGN A11, C2/WP9)
- 2026-09-27 (tests): Vitest runs in Node without jsdom, so `src/ui/screens/test/dom.ts` is a small DOM (elements, text, attributes, events with bubbling, focus, simple selectors) that Preact renders into. A pseudo-locale test wraps every translated string and fails on any visible letters outside it (no hard-coded UI text); a strings test checks that every `ui.*` key used exists and every key in `ui.en.json` is used. UI code writes keys as whole string literals for this reason. (DESIGN C2/WP9 DoD)
- 2026-09-27 (dev page): WP9's page is `src/dev/screens/page.tsx` (`?dev=1#screens/<variant>/<new|mid|maxed>/<WxH>`), a new folder no other package uses; WP9 owns `src/dev/screens/**`. (DESIGN C1 dev pages)
- 2026-09-28 (review: minimum to play): a War Plan may have empty slots, so Mode select, the Conquest board and Result "Next battle" check the active plan against the request's format before asking for an opponent (`screens/shared/MatchStarter.tsx`). Advisor `error` findings (below 3 units or no turret in an age the format uses, or an illegal card) block with a "War Plan not ready" dialog that opens the builder at the first problem age; warnings never block. Onboarding matches are not checked (the app builds them). (DESIGN A3)
- 2026-09-28 (review: meta alignment): every `PlanIssue` code `meta.validatePlan` reports has a `ui.advisor.*` string, including the legality errors (`badShape`, `unknownCard`, `wrongAge`, `notOwned`, `duplicate`, `badPower`); an unknown future key falls back to a generic line. Since `meta.setWarPlan` refuses gaps, editing preset C before B first stores B as a copy of the active plan. `UiServices.markSeen(card)` (`meta.markSeen`) clears a NEW badge when the card detail opens. `screens/test/realMeta.test.tsx` renders the screens against a real `meta.newSave`, real advisor findings, real opponents for every mode and real reward lists. (DESIGN A3, A6.4, A9 #9-#11)
- 2026-09-28 (review: smaller rules): the Skirmish note on an age (A3) shows while the player owns no Anti-armor unit of that age, not when the loadout merely has 3 units. The Daily Capsule row reads as locked until 2 capsules are opened (`pity.opened`), matching A6.3. VS shows a procedural commander's personality from its `commander:<General>:<card>` id (A7.4). The Home road bar shows the next node's reward (A9 #2), and its countdowns tick every second. "Open capsule" on Result shows only while that capsule is still unopened. The Skirmish General picker carries the AI chip on every portrait (A7.1). Failed actions map meta reasons to text (`amber`, `dust`, else a generic line). (DESIGN A3, A6.3, A7.1, A7.4, A9)

## WP4

- 2026-09-27 (structure): the procedural tier is data all the way down: SVG part libraries per age (`src/visuals/parts/<age>.ts`, plus `shared`, `icons`, `skins`), rig builders (`rigs/`: biped, quadruped, rider, vehicle, walker, flyer, turret, plus a base rig), puppets per age (`puppets/<age>.ts`), keyframe clips with procedural helpers (`clips/`), and a manifest generated from the library (`manifest.ts`) with `OVERRIDES` as the one swap point. Nothing in the data imports Pixi, so the bake, portraits, handoff sheets and the Node checks read the same SVG data. Parts may be shared across ages (the Cuirassier rides the Medieval horse parts with its own palette). (DESIGN B5, A11)
- 2026-09-27 (body width): A11 "visual width stays within 1.4× the collision width" is checked on the rest pose's rasterised silhouette without weapons, held gear (shields, tools), arms, rotors, banners and pennants (slots tagged `weapon` or marked `noWidth`), plus one outline width (3.7 lu) because the outline is drawn half outside the fill. A11 asks for oversized, role-readable weapons, so they may stick out; the body may not. (DESIGN A11 Scale)
- 2026-09-27 (heights): a unit's `heightLu` and head anchor are measured from the drawn rest pose (the top of the hat, plume, flag or rotor), not declared, so the health bar (`anchors.head.y − 12` in render) never overlaps the art. Scale bands checked by tests: small/medium ground units 54-90 lu, heavies 90-125, Legendaries 150-225; Epics and air units vary. (DESIGN A11, B6)
- 2026-09-27 (air): flyers are authored with the origin at their lowest point, like every unit; the battle view lifts air units to flight altitude (WP5 `AIR_ALTITUDE_LU`), and their ground ring rides along under them. (DESIGN A11 Rigs, B6)
- 2026-09-27 (colour rule scope): the rule is checked on units, turrets, projectiles, skins and lane effects (A11's list) by rasterising the SVG sources with team layers painted neutral grey, so only non-team paint counts, outlines and cel shadows included. Bases are exempt (not in A11's list), as are screen and UI cues: the overdrive frame, the siege vignette, coins and XP sparkles (`exemptColorRule`). For effects, each sprite, particle, falling object and chain is rasterised with its tint and weighted by how many copies the recipe spawns. (DESIGN A11)
- 2026-09-27 (team readability): besides the A11 cues, every unit shows at least 7% of its silhouette in team colour at rest (a test). Modern soldiers get team armbands, helmets team bands. High contrast stripes only the opponent's banner-type team layers, so the pattern itself tells the sides apart. (DESIGN A11 Team readability)
- 2026-09-27 (skins): a skin keeps its base's bones, anchors, clips, team zones and height; it may add bones (the Toy Soldier's wind-up key) and point the idle flourish at one (the key twirls), add overlays, replace parts with same-outline parts, recolour non-team zones, go translucent down to 0.7 (Ghost Corsair) and set an aura (`snow` also puffs frosty breath). The silhouette test compares skin and base at rest and at the attack impact pose, IoU ≥ 0.85 (lowest today 0.891, Tin Can). The Crystal Spire base skin carries the base's mounts. (DESIGN A5.8)
- 2026-09-27 (bows): a bow string is two halves hanging from the bow tips (`stringU`, `stringL` bones); the bow clip rotates them so the nock comes back without stretching strokes. Rider rigs prefix colliding rider bone and slot ids with `rider.` so every id is unique. Turrets pick their fire clip (`turret.fire`, `.pour` for the Pitch Cauldron, `.sling` for the Trebuchet). (DESIGN A11 Clip contract)
- 2026-09-27 (atlas tier): the sprite-sheet tier is implemented for units and one-shot effects, not only stubbed, so "units swap one at a time by editing their manifest entry" holds today. Format: the `art/blender` pipeline's PixiJS spritesheet (frames anchored at the feet, `<clip>` and `<clip>_team` animations drawn as a tinted underlay, `meta.ageborn` with `pxPerLu`, anchors and per-clip timing); `atlasVisualDef(json, url)` builds the manifest entry. The attack's contact frame is time-warped onto the sim's impact tick like the keyframe tier; missing clips fall back (spawn/stun/victory → idle frames with code motion, ability → attack). Rings, glyphs and trims stay the shared procedural parts. Atlas turrets, bases and backdrops, and the Spine tier, draw placeholders until needed. The provider asks each adapter `canDraw(what, def)`; an entry that cannot be drawn (tier not built, sheet not loaded) becomes a placeholder with one warning. Verified in the gallery with the bonker, destrier_knight and pulse_trooper sheets. (DESIGN B5 Later tiers)
- 2026-09-27 (portraits): `portrait()` also takes `plate?: boolean` (default true); `false` leaves the background transparent for silhouettes (answers `docs/requests/wp9-transparent-portraits.md`; the contract field is requested in `docs/requests/wp4-portrait-plate-contract.md`). Ground Legendaries crop to the upper body on cards; flyers show whole. (DESIGN B5 Portraits)
- 2026-09-27 (gallery): `src/dev/gallery` is addressable by hash (`?dev=1#gallery/section=units&age=stone&clip=attack&preset=highContrast&t=300`); `t=<ms>` freezes every animation at that time for screenshots, and results are published on `window.__galleryInfo` (`checks`, `bake`, `portraits`) for e2e (`docs/requests/wp4-gallery-e2e.md`). Sections: units, turrets, world, effects, portraits, checks, handoff (SVG sheets, single or zipped), bake. (DESIGN B5 Art gallery)
- 2026-09-27 (bake budget): measured in headless Chromium on the build container: ages 0-1 bake in 150 ms at DPR 1, 113 ms at DPR 2 and 96 ms in Lite (budget 400 ms); ages 2-4 take about 125 ms of CPU in 8 ms idle slices. 754 parts, 1,854 textures, 3 atlas pages of 2,048² at DPR 1 (8 at DPR 2). (DESIGN B16)
- 2026-09-27 (review: heights): the scale test now uses A11's bands exactly for ground units: heavies 100-120 lu and Legendaries 170-220 lu (this replaces the 90-125 and 150-225 bands above; small and medium roles keep 54-90 for "~68"). To fit: the Knight's rider sits 3 lu lower (122 → 119), the Tankette's flag is on a 6 lu taller whip antenna (94 → 100), the Behemoth is 3% larger overall (166 → 170). Air units and Epics stay unbanded. (DESIGN A11 Scale)
- 2026-09-27 (review: pennants): "pennant on heavies" is enforced by a test on every Heavy and ground Legendary and their skins: a flag, pennant, banner or pennoned lance slot with a banner-type team layer. Added to the Cuirassier, Ursa Paladin, Walker Mech and Chrono Titan; on the two riders it trails behind (mirrored slot) so the rider does not hide it. The Matriarch's death prop is its pennant, not a rider: the riders jump off as live Pebblers (sim), so a rider lying on the ground would read as a dead Pebbler. (DESIGN A11 Team readability, A5.2)
- 2026-09-27 (review: base skins): `createBase({ age, skin })` applies a base skin to whichever age has an entry for it (`base.future@crystal_spire`) and draws plain bases for the other ages without a warning, so a match that starts in the Stone Age shows the Crystal Spire after evolving into Future. The battle view must pass the side's base skin at creation (`docs/requests/wp4-base-skin-render.md`). (DESIGN A5.8, A14.1)
- 2026-09-27 (review: portraits): portraits follow the provider's colourblind preset (part of the cache key), and a visual moved to a tier that cannot draw portraits yet (sprite sheets, Spine) keeps the procedural portrait of the same visual id instead of a placeholder capsule, so a manifest swap never changes card art; `?art=` still forces a tier. The placeholder portrait honours `plate: false`. (DESIGN B5 Portraits, Later tiers)
- 2026-09-27 (review: seam): the split-age cross-fade draws the sky's lower strip of each pair opaque (drawing both halves translucent let whatever is behind the backdrop show through as a dark column), strips are 6 lu (12 lu banded visibly between very different skies), and the 30% desaturation is a neutral grey haze in strips whose alpha rises as sin(πt) from 0 at the seam edges to 30% at the seam, replacing a hard-edged 30% ellipse that read as a pillar of fog. (DESIGN A11 Split-age lane)
- 2026-09-27 (review: walk cadence): A11's "0.5 s cycle at 80 lu/s" and A12 #2 "no foot sliding beyond 3 px" cannot both hold with ±25° swings on short cartoon legs (a 68 lu biped's leg is 15.8 lu, a 27 lu stride). The MUST wins: the cycle is driven by ground distance with stride = 4 × leg × sin(amplitude), so infantry step at about 0.33 s per cycle at 80 lu/s. (DESIGN A11 Clip contract, A12)
- 2026-09-27 (review: muzzle flash): the turret muzzle flash shows for exactly one rendered frame (it showed for two), as A11 and A12 state; the smoke puff is the feel layer's `fx.muzzle`. (DESIGN A11 Turret clips, A12)
- 2026-09-27 (review: Legendary aura): the white Legendary aura (A12 VFX list) is drawn once, by the battle view (`fx.legendary_aura` for Legendary units and Legendary skins, following the graphics preset live, for every art tier); the procedural unit view drew its own glow as well, which doubled it and ignored a switch to Lite. Unit views keep only skin auras (snow, ghost, neon). The gallery adds the aura the way the battle view does. (DESIGN A12, B5, B6)
- 2026-09-27 (review: spawn and build): action clips cross-fade in over 70 ms, which made the spawn pop start at full size and dip before popping, and a turret build start on the mount before jumping up. `ClipDef.blendInMs` (0 for `common.spawn` and `turret.build`) starts them at once, so spawn really goes 0 → 1.15 → 1 in 180 ms. (DESIGN A11 Clip contract)
- 2026-09-27 (review: bake scale): the default atlas bake scale is the largest world scale the screen can show (landscape width / 1,560 lu, ×1.6 pinch zoom under 900 CSS px, clamped to 0.6-1.25 px/lu) times the capped DPR, instead of a fixed 1.25 px/lu: measured in headless Chromium with every age baked: a DPR 2 phone (844 × 390) bakes 8.0 Mpx on 5 atlas pages instead of 16.1 Mpx on 8, a 1,280 px desktop at DPR 1 2.1 Mpx on 2 pages instead of 4.4 Mpx on 3 (sprites drawn 1:1), and nothing bakes larger than before. The bake panel measures the worst case (1.25 px/lu); the gallery bakes at 1.6 px/lu for its review zoom. (DESIGN A2.1, B5, B16)
- 2026-09-27 (review: muzzles): every shooter has a muzzle bone at its weapon (a test), including the Mammoth Matriarch, whose muzzle is the front rider's throwing hand because its riders throw the rocks (A5.2, A14.2). Every turret has one too. (DESIGN B5 anchors)
- 2026-09-27 (review: effect size options): answers `docs/requests/wp5-effect-options.md`. #1: every world effect takes `scale` (sizes the whole effect) and `small: 1` (0.6×), so the enemy's evolve pillar the battle view plays with `{ small: 1 }` is smaller as A12 asks; a pooled effect view resets the size on each `playAt`. #2: done, see "review: Legendary aura". The option names in `effects/recipes.ts` stay as listed there. (DESIGN A12)

## WP12

- 2026-09-27 (tools layout): `tools/sim-cli.ts` is the one entry point (`npx tsx tools/sim-cli.ts <balance|exploits|economy|drops|replay-verify|csv|match>`); each B12 tool is a library module with a `run*` function and its own report sections (`balance.ts`, `exploits.ts`, `economy.ts`, `drops.ts`, `replayVerify.ts`, `csv.ts`), shared code lives in `tools/lib/` (driver, metrics, plans, runner, stats, scenarios, raw-source patcher) and tests in `tools/test/`. Every tool writes `reports/<tool>.json` and `.md` (`--out` to change) and exits 1 when a DESIGN target fails, unless `--no-gate`. `--mode smoke|full` picks the A2.14 sizes. npm script names are requested in `docs/requests/wp12-package-scripts.md`, CI stages in `docs/requests/wp12-ci.md`. (DESIGN B12, C2/WP12)
- 2026-09-27 (contracts only): the tools drive the sim through `createSim`/`Sim` and bots through `CreateBot`/`BotController`, with their own copy of the B6 loop (`tools/lib/driver.ts`: observation ring per seat, commands stamped `tick + 1` with a per-side seq). `src/ai` and `src/meta` are loaded at run time through a computed URL; without `src/ai` the scripted `balanced` proxy stands in and every report says so, without `src/meta` the economy and drops tools write a skipped report and exit 0. The bot profile comes from the AI's `botProfile` when exported, else a Balanced profile built by hand. (DESIGN B10, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (workers): worker threads do not inherit the tsx loader, so `tools/lib/worker-boot.mjs` calls `register()` from `tsx/esm/api` and then imports the TypeScript worker. Results return in job order, so a run is identical for any worker count (a test compares 0 and 2 workers). Default workers: cores − 1. (DESIGN B12)
- 2026-09-27 (balance matrix): cards in their age's A2.14 baseline (3 Commons, AA Rare, Support Rare, Common turrets, default power) have no separate test plan; they are the control, measured by the Balanced mirror, and listed as "control" in the report. Every other card (per age: Epic, Legendary, Rare turret, Epic turret, alternate power; 25 today) gets `pairs` seeds × 2 matches (test plan on each side, same seed), all in Full War. The win-rate delta CI uses the pair means (the two matches of a seed are not independent). Match length, Final Bell, evolves, first mover and turret share come from a Balanced mirror in Full War and Short War (full 1,000, smoke 200 matches each). The median targets use ±30 s (A2.14 gives no tolerance for "median 7:00" and "4:30"); evolve times are the `ascendStart` ticks (the Evolve press), medians over matches that reached that evolve; "Later evolves ±20 s of A2.4" uses 2:05, 3:20 and 4:50. (DESIGN A2.4, A2.14, B12)
- 2026-09-27 (gating): range targets judge the point estimate; the per-card target passes only when the whole 95% CI lies within ±3 (full) or ±6 (smoke). A statistical check with fewer than 30 samples (pairs or matches) cannot pass, so tiny debug runs never look green. (DESIGN A2.14)
- 2026-09-27 (scenarios): "base time to kill" is measured on the real sim from the army's first base hit to the base's destruction: 60 pop of the age's Infantry, Ranged and Heavy commons (round-robin) at L1, spawned with the sim's dev helpers, against a full same-age base evolved there with the Evolve command, with no defenders, no turrets, the clock off and the defender's Last Stand spent.
