"Power damage per unit in zone" uses the A2.9 coverage formula (barrage `damage × count × 2 × radius / zone`, sweep its damage, stampede damage × hit cap) against the age's L1 Infantry and Heavy commons; it reproduces the A5.7 "Per unit" column. (DESIGN A2.9, A2.14, A5.7)
- 2026-09-27 (proxies): the eight B12 exploit proxies are deterministic `BotController`s in `tools/proxies.ts` with a 300 ms reaction delay (6 ticks, the A7.3 floor) that decide every 0.5 s, see only `Observation` and play A3-valid plans derived from content by role and ability (mass splash: splash and call-strike units plus splash turrets; heal stacking: every healer). Treasury greed buys nothing else before Treasury 3 unless threatened; the turtle saves for 4 mounts and turrets and holds; XP bank evolves only at ≥ 145% of the threshold; power-on-evolve casts right before each Evolve. "Every scripted strategy reaches Future between 4:00 and 6:15" is checked per proxy (median `ageUp` to Future). (DESIGN A2.14, B12)
- 2026-09-27 (economy and drops): both run through the `Meta` contract only (details and assumptions in `docs/requests/wp12-meta-entry.md`). Drops opens capsules on fresh saves moved to the last arena (full pool, random Legendaries) with the script done, 4 Win Capsules to 1 Daily; chi-square (p > 0.01, C4.5) for Daily tiers, foils per stack, the Aeon skin chance and the stack rarity roll, the latter only on stacks no guarantee or pity could touch (sinceEpic ≤ 7, sinceLegendary ≤ 23, new-card protection not due; Jade excluded because its Rare-to-Legendary conversion cannot be told apart). Bag groups of 100, guarantees, and the Epic/Legendary/new-card pity boundaries are exact checks. The economy player plays ladder matches at 60% until 4 wins used a charge each day, claims the Daily Capsule and road nodes, opens everything and upgrades the active War Plan first; Amber income is every rise of the balance plus what upgrades spent; months are 30.44 days; per-day averages use days 11-120. (DESIGN A6.4, A6.5, A6.9, C4.5)
- 2026-09-27 (CSV): `content:csv` exports only literal numbers of the raw unit, turret and power objects (shared references such as `damageMods.blunt` stay in code), one column per dotted path (`attacks.0.damage`, `abilities.firstHitBonus.multBp`, `effect.count`), rows in age order. Import rewrites exactly the changed literals in `src/content/raw/*.ts` through the TypeScript AST, so formatting and comments stay; unknown cards, non-numbers and added or removed fields are refused and nothing is written. (DESIGN B4 Spreadsheets)
- 2026-09-27 (replay verify): a replay is re-simulated on the content whose hash it carries: the game content or the frozen fixture content compiled by the sim's shim (the golden replays); any other hash is reported as "from an older version" and fails. (DESIGN B3)
- 2026-09-27 (integrity): the layer test imports `LAYER_RULES` from eslint.config.js and checks every import edge of production code (static, `import type`, `import()`, `import.meta.glob`), that every src folder is a layer, that the table matches the B2 table in DESIGN.md, that the layer graph is acyclic, and that the production entry never statically reaches `src/contracts/fakes` or test code. The one allowed exception is the WP0 fake ArtProvider's `pixi.js` import. The string test checks literal and template keys of every `t(...)` / `x.t(...)` call and every `*Key` string in the compiled content against the EN files, duplicates across files, and Danish files (skipped until v1.1). The hard-coded-string test scans ui, capsule, app, tutorial and render for JSX text, JSX child literals, user-visible attributes and Pixi `text`; dev pages anywhere (`src/dev/**`, `src/<layer>/dev/**`, as `docs/requests/wp11-dev-page.md` asks) and fixtures are exempt, as are strings without letters, single key glyphs and the brand name. ID checks resolve content and feel-config ids against the visual manifest (WP4) and, once WP6 ships them, the sound and music manifests (skipped with the reason until then; shapes in `docs/requests/wp12-audio-manifests.md`), and parse the A13, A14.1 and A14.3 lists from DESIGN.md. A hard-rules test enforces "no real money" (no payment or ads SDK, no payment API), "our own IP" (no "Age of War" outside tests) and offline v1 (no http(s) URL strings in production code). (DESIGN B2, B4, B13, CLAUDE.md)
- 2026-09-27 (e2e): specs live in `tests/e2e` and run against the production build at `/ageborn/` (root config for CI; `tests/e2e/playwright.local.config.ts` builds into `node_modules/.cache/ageborn-e2e` and previews on port 5212 for parallel local work). `helpers.ts` `FLOWS` marks each B13 flow ready or pending with the packages it needs; pending flows skip with that reason and `E2E_ALL=1` runs them anyway. Ready now: boot (no console errors, assets under the base, title with Play and the AI chip, dev list), tutorial match 1 on the autopilot (`?dev=1&autopilot=1`, `window.__agebornDev.fastForward`), the visibility pause (C5 #20), a Quick Battle ended by fast-forward (`?dev=1&autopilot=1&quick=short`, B13 step 6 until Skirmish is wired, per `docs/requests/wp11-e2e-hooks.md`) and the gallery's art checks and bake budget (`docs/requests/wp4-gallery-e2e.md`). Pending (Phase 2b): capsule 1 opens, reload keeps state, Home renders, Skirmish via Home. Gallery screenshot baselines stay v1.x (B13). (DESIGN B13)
- 2026-09-28 (review: gating): every statistical check needs 30 samples, as the gating entry says: the Balanced mirror's median length, 5:00-9:00 share and Final Bell rate need 30 matches, each evolve median 30 evolves, and each exploit proxy's "reaches Future" median 30 matches that got there (before, one sample could pass them). The 5:00-9:00 share is reported for Full War only. `sim-cli` refuses unknown flags, so a typo cannot start a default-sized run. (DESIGN A2.14, B12)
- 2026-09-28 (review: economy quests): A6.9's engaged player completes 3 quests a day, but quest claiming is not in the `Meta` contract; `tools/economy.ts` uses the meta package's `claimQuest` and `rerollQuest` when exported (WP7 `MetaRules` does), claims every finished quest after the day's matches and upgrades (twice, for "Upgrade 2 cards"), and spends the free reroll on the oldest quest still open. The synthetic match stats follow the format (A2.4 final-age times 2:05 / 3:20 / 4:50, A2.14 lengths), skip the Treasury in a third of the matches and use Last Stand in every loss and one win in five, so ladder play can finish most of the A6.7 pool. Quests per day is reported (info). (DESIGN A6.7, A6.9)
- 2026-09-28 (review: integrity): `tests/integrity/determinism.test.ts` backs up the lint in `npm test`: no disable directive in sim, ai, meta, core or content may switch off `no-restricted-globals/properties/syntax` or `ageborn/layers` (or all rules), and their production code may not call `Math.random`, `Date.now`, `new Date`, `performance.now` or `crypto` randomness. The string test also checks every key-shaped `*Key` property literal or template in production code (keys that reach `t()` indirectly). The hard-coded-string scan also reads both branches of conditionals, `||` / `??` / `&&` / `+` operands, the static text of templates, and `textContent` / `innerText` / `title` / `setAttribute('title' ...)` assignments; Pixi's `label` is a debug name and exempt. (DESIGN B2, B4, B13)
- 2026-09-28 (review: cross-engine determinism): `tests/e2e/determinism.spec.ts` bundles `tests/e2e/determinism/entry.ts` (the sim, the frozen fixture content and the golden replays) with Vite's build API into one IIFE, runs it in Node and in the browser under test, and requires identical per-second hashes, final hashes, tick counts and results for every golden replay (C4.3, risk table "golden replays in Chromium and WebKit from Phase 1"). It compares engines, not the recorded hashes (`src/sim/test/golden.test.ts` owns those), so it stays meaningful while WP2 re-records. The WebKit project itself is requested in `docs/requests/wp12-ci.md`. The tool test for `replay-verify` likewise checks that the tool picks the fixture content and reports the sim's own verdict. (DESIGN B13, C4.3)

## WP3

- 2026-09-27 (package): `src/ai` follows B14 (`createBot`, `controller`, `brain`, `scoring`, `counters`, `personalities`, `tiers`, `openings`, `estimate`, `mistakes`, `scripted`) plus `book.ts` (the published card numbers in observation units), `view.ts` (the decision-time picture), `ledger.ts` (own pending commands), `memory.ts`, `emotes.ts`, `actions.ts` and `harness.ts` (`BotMatch` / `runHeadless`: the session's observation ring and stamping without rendering, for tests, tools and the dev viewer; the caller passes the `Sim`, since `ai` may not import `sim`). All scores are integer bp (10,000 = 1.0), money milli-gold and positions milli-lu, like `Observation`. (DESIGN B2, B10, B14)
- 2026-09-27 (content access): `ai` may not import `src/content` (B2), so it reads `CompiledContent.generals` (untyped in the contract) with a runtime shape check for a General's personality, weights, counter weight, signature cards and flags; content without the table (the fakes) and unknown ids get the plain Balanced brain. `botProfile(content, { generalId, tier, mistakeBonusBp, personalityOf, favoriteCard, openings, stanceLocked, autoLastStand })` builds a `BotProfile`. Procedural Commanders copy a ladder General via `personalityOf` (the profile's `generalId` becomes that General). The plain Balanced brain of A2.14 is Echo's (`BALANCED_BRAIN_ID = 'echo'`, personality `mirror`); Pip's `balanced` personality adds his Heavy lean. (DESIGN A7.4, B2, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (profile strings): `BotProfile.openings` carries the opening build in a small step language (`train:<group>`, `turret`, `mount`, `treasury`, `a|b` alternatives, a seeded swap of two later steps) plus directives: `favorite:<card>` (Commander's favourite card), `rule:noStance` and `rule:autoLastStand` (training matches). For the scripted Old Grogg the same list holds his script (`at <ms> train <card>`, `every <ms> from <ms> until <ms> ...`, `max-alive <n> <card>`). An empty list means the personality's default opening. (DESIGN A7.2 Openings, A7.4, A8)
- 2026-09-27 (timing): the controller's clock is `obs.tick + snapshotDelayTicks` (the session hands it `ring.at(delay)`, the oldest observation while the ring fills); a command runs one tick later. The brain runs every decision interval while the action cap (every command in the last 200 ticks, emotes included) has room. After rounding, the decision interval is kept above the snapshot delay so a bot always sees its previous decision's effect. Tiers accept fractions (resolved to 1/100 tier); counts (actions, Treasury max) truncate toward the lower tier; Max turrets is 1 only at tier 0. (DESIGN A7.3, B6, B10)
- 2026-09-27 (legal commands): the observation is delayed, so the controller keeps a ledger of its own commands until the observation shows them: pending gold, queue, Treasury, mounts, turrets (busy for the 1 s build), stance cooldown, a power just cast, an Evolve in flight (age-dependent commands wait while the ageUp may land before they run). The observation has no "final age" flag: Evolve counts as available when `xpBp` > 10,000, or = 10,000 with charge below 100% (in the final age XP converts to charge until the ring is full, so it never exceeds 10,000 there). The ledger learns from commands that had no effect (a locked stance, an automatic-only Last Stand, an Evolve in the final age), and the brain leaves a manual Last Stand to the automatic trigger when twice the worst base-HP burst of the last 5 s over its reaction time could reach 10% first. Result: 0 rejected commands in the 500-match fuzz. Daily modifiers are not observable; only Heavy Metal changes prices and only downward, so the bot's list prices stay safe. (DESIGN A2.4, A2.11, A7.1, B3)
- 2026-09-27 (scoring): the A7.2 table is implemented as written, with these readings. f_role counts own alive plus queued units of the group. "Counter depth 0 means weighted random choice": the counter term is a flat 0.5 and the chosen train is drawn from the train candidates weighted by score. Remembered composition = enemy cards seen in the last 45 s (peak copies); "predicts next enemy age" (X) blends 30% of the counter term against the Commons of the foe's next age once the foe's XP is ≥ 85%. Power zones: barrage and sweep scan their zone, the smoke cloud its width, the stampede runs from the own front, buffs count the own army when it is within 300 lu of the enemy, paratroopers the enemy value within 450 lu behind the enemy front. A full power is also fired just before an own Evolve (score 1.3) when its zone holds ≥ 100 gold, so the 50% carry cap wastes nothing (A2.13); Tempest also fires into the 5 s after the foe evolves. Stance is a candidate scored 1.5, at most one change per 8 s; m_hold scales the 0.7 × foeArmy test and a failed push gate holds only with a Hold weight ≥ 20. Last Stand scores 2.0. (DESIGN A7.2, A7.3)
- 2026-09-27 (push gate, banking): D = enemy value within 500 lu of their gate + 300 per enemy turret (the foe gold estimate is not added: with it, two equal starts already failed the gate). The gate applies only while no enemy is on the bot's half. "Otherwise it banks" is read strongly: the bot stops training (it does not feed units into the turrets one by one) until its gold can lift its army over the gate in one wave, then spends it all at once; meanwhile it banks for Treasury (below its tier max, before 3:00), prefers range ≥ 250 (+0.3) and holds where the tier allows. The attack clock raises train scores 10% per 5 s (capped ×3) and also relaxes the gate 0.1 per 5 s down to parity, and an army within 6 pop of the cap always passes, so two banking bots cannot stall a match. From Overdrive the gate is 1.0, in Siege off. Tier ordering depended on this discipline: without it VII vs III fell to about 60%. (DESIGN A7.2, A2.10)
- 2026-09-27 (saving goals and float): saving goals are Treasury (when the push gate fails, or when the lane within 600 lu of the gate is quiet and the level still pays back by 6:00, in the first 3:00, up to the tier max; Ledger rushes 3 levels by 2:30 whatever its tier) and "Bank 350 for a Legendary" (m_legendary ≥ 1.0 and no Legendary alive or queued). While a goal is active, trains that would dip below it take the −0.8 f_save and must still score ≥ 0.5 to act, the float target rises to the goal, and the goal's own action gets +1.5 once affordable. Without a goal any positive score acts. The A7.3 gold float target is a hysteresis: the bot starts training at the target and trains until it cannot afford its cheapest card (urgent pressure ≥ 0.5, a wave or Kettle's all-in override it). Turrets: a bot wants 1 + age index × m_turret turrets (tier max applies); under pressure ≥ 0.25 a wanted turret or mount gets +0.8, and rebuilding tiers (V+) get +0.8 for a Modernise when calm. These wants never pause training. (DESIGN A7.2, A7.3)
- 2026-09-27 (mistakes): a mistake replaces an action the bot meant to take (the roll happens only when there is a best action, or when banking holds back a train, which is when "over-commit" happens), so the error rate follows what the bot does, not how often it looks. The bot picks uniformly among the mistakes the situation allows and floats gold when none does; a float-gold mistake leaves the tray untouched for 3 s. Over-commit = train while the gate says bank, or Charge while holding. The profile's `mistakeBonusBp` adds to the tier rate (capped at 90%). No mistake sells turrets or wastes gold on nothing. (DESIGN A7.2 Choice, A6.8)
- 2026-09-27 (personalities): content holds the numbers, `personalities.ts` the rules. Pip leans on Heavies (+0.15); Kettle leans on Infantry (+0.25) and goes all-in from 80% XP (trains +0.3, ignores float and goals, Evolve waits while units can still join the push); Moss opens with a turret and uses a 2.0 push gate before Overdrive; Ledger rushes Treasury 3 by 2:30 and evolves ignoring safety (m_greed 1.45); Boomsworth's signature cards get +0.25 (turrets ×2 value); Rook's counter weight ×1.5 comes from content; Tempest fires into evolve moments; The Warden's Legendary weight saves 350 for a Legendary. Echo mirrors the player's plan with the Balanced brain. (DESIGN A7.4)
- 2026-09-27 (emotes): at most one own emote per match (GG when the own base is ≤ 10%, Salute when the foe's is ≤ 20% and below the bot's, each moment with a 50% chance), and replies to the player with 50% chance after 1-2 s, at most one per 20 s, mirroring GG/Salute/Thumbs up and answering others with Salute or Thumbs up. Emotes use a separate RNG stream so they never change the bot's battle decisions. The observation carries no emotes, so `AiBotController.hearEmote` receives them (the match driver relays them); the contract field is requested in `docs/requests/wp3-observation-emotes.md`. (DESIGN A7.2)
- 2026-09-27 (Old Grogg): `createBot` gives `grogg` (a `scripted` General) the `ScriptedController`; its default script mirrors WP11's retimed `tutorial/scripts.ts` schedule (see `docs/requests/wp11-grogg-brain.md`), while the app keeps its own `GroggBrain`. A due send waits until it is legal and affordable, never queues up twice, and a `max-alive` send is dropped while enough of Grogg's units live. (DESIGN A7.4, A8, B10)
- 2026-09-27 (tests): tier ordering runs Balanced (Echo) vs Balanced on the A2.14 baseline plan at L1 in Full Wars (the A2.14 reference format), alternating sides with mirrored seeds; the fuzz runs random Generals, tiers 0-X, own or random plans (thin trays included), levels 1-10, formats and daily modifiers with both sides bots. `npm test` runs samples (100 per pair, 60 fuzz matches; the tier bar there is target − 2 SE); `AI_FULL=1` runs the DoD sizes (400 per pair against the exact targets, 500 fuzz matches). Results at DoD size on the current content: X vs I 96%, VII vs III 91.75%, I vs 0 67.5%, fuzz 0 rejected of 71,587 commands. On Short War the same pairs give about 96%, 87% and 65-69%: tiers 0 and I differ only in small A7.3 steps (mistakes 45/35%, 2/3 actions per 10 s, float 450/400), so I vs 0 is the thin one and content tuning can move it; the test will say so. (DESIGN B13, C2/WP3)
- 2026-09-27 (dev page): WP3 owns `src/dev/botViewer/**` (`?dev=1#botViewer`): pick Generals and tiers per side, format, seed and level; the lane is drawn on a plain canvas from the sim state (no WP5 dependency); each side shows its economy, the foe gold estimate against the truth, the push-gate numbers and the last decisions with their top scores; a headless series reports wins, draws, Final Bells and rejected commands. (DESIGN C2/WP3)
- 2026-09-28 (review, safe-window evolve): the A7.3 "safe window" of tiers VII and X was computed but unused. A safe window now means no enemy ground unit can walk to within 300 lu of the own gate before the Ascension ends: each foe is moved on at its card speed over snapshot delay + 1 tick + the 2.5 s Ascension. Tiers below VII keep the A7.2 check of where the enemy stands now, so they sometimes evolve into a push. The delay column (≤ 2 s at VII, ≤ 0.5 s at X) stays the reaction time after Evolve becomes available; m_greed ≥ 1.3 still skips the check. (DESIGN A7.2 Evolve, A7.3)
- 2026-09-28 (review, Moss): A7.3 allows Hold from tier V, but Mama Moss (tiers II-IV) has Hold as her signature (A7.4). Like Ledger's Treasury rush, the signature wins: the turtle personality may Hold at any tier. (DESIGN A7.3, A7.4)
- 2026-09-28 (review, rules): counter depth 0 draws its random train only among trains that clear the same bar as the best action, so a saving goal still pauses the trains f_save holds back. The scripted brain (Old Grogg) follows the same A7 rules as the utility brain: due actions wait while the tier's action cap is full, and script emotes must be GG, Salute or Thumbs up and spend the bot's one own emote of the match. (DESIGN A7.2, A7.3, B10)
- 2026-09-28 (review, gold estimator): the estimator now treats summons as free on both sides (Vanguard after an ageUp, Paratroopers after a paradrop telegraph, riders after their mount falls: no spend, no bounty), pays 30% for own losses during a damaging foe power or the foe's Last Stand, adds the +50% underdog bounty when the victim's card age is above the foe's age and the foe's XP bar is not full, and refunds 50% when a foe turret disappears (sold). Over whole Full Wars the error against the foe's gold plus queue fell from about 450 to under 200 gold; it remains display-only (bot viewer), not a scoring input. (DESIGN A2.3, A7.1)
- 2026-09-28 (review, tests): tier ordering now uses truly mirrored seeds (each seed twice, tier A once on each side). DoD sizes after the review: X vs I 97.25%, VII vs III 90.25%, I vs 0 68.75%, fuzz 0 rejected of 71,631 commands. (DESIGN B13, C2/WP3)

## WP6

- 2026-09-27 (manifest): `src/audio/sounds.ts` keeps B7's `{ kind: 'zzfx', variants: number[][] }` (one ZzFX call per variant, arrays paste to and from the ZzFX designer) and `{ kind: 'file', src }`, and adds `{ kind: 'zzfxMix', variants: { atMs, params }[][] }` for jingles, fanfares and layered impacts (several ZzFX calls summed). Every entry also carries mix data: `bus` (`ui` for the A13 UI group, `sfx` otherwise), a pre-render `group`, and optional `gainDb`, `maxVoices` (default 4), `gapMs` (default 40), `pitchVarBp` (default 800 = ±8%) and `volVarDb` (default 3). Musical sounds (fanfares, jingles, rarity reveals, chimes, capsule climbs, the Royal Decree fanfare, the Siege bell) use `pitchVarBp: 0` so they stay in tune with the music and each other. All 112 A13 ids have 3-5 variants (hits 4, heavy hit and base hit 3, as A12). The evolve fanfares, victory/defeat jingles and capsule climbs quote the "Dawn March" motif. (DESIGN A12, A13, B7)
- 2026-09-27 (pre-render): effects render at 32 kHz (16 kHz bandwidth, `SFX_SAMPLE_RATE`), not ZzFX's 44.1 kHz: the retro sounds have almost nothing above 16 kHz and it keeps the boot render well inside B16's 300 ms. Boot renders the groups `ui`, `battle` (what every battle needs from its first seconds), `stone` and `medieval` (B7 "UI, Stone and Medieval"); `match` (Legendary spawns, large explosion, base crumble and destruction, Treasury, evolve riser, all evolve fanfares, enemy evolve, Last Stand, Overdrive horn, Siege bell, jingles), the later ages and `capsule` render in idle slices right after boot, and anything not ready renders on first use. A short warm-up pass runs every ZzFX branch before the boot render (the JIT otherwise deoptimises on each new wave shape, about doubling a cold render). Measured in Chromium: 126-134 ms for 49 sounds / 162 variants (7 MB) on a quiet machine, 233 ms with the machine fully loaded by parallel agents. The unit test measures CPU time (so parallel test files do not count) plus a machine-independent work-size bound; samples move into AudioBuffers on first use and the bank drops its copy. (DESIGN B7, B16)
- 2026-09-27 (vendored ZzFX): `src/audio/vendor/zzfx.ts` is upstream 1.3.2 `buildSamples` (MIT header kept) as a pure function with no module-level AudioContext. Output is bit-identical to a verbatim port (tested) except for noise: above an argument of 10^6, `sin(t**3)` (noise shape) and `sin(i**5)` (noise parameter) are effectively random phases whose exact evaluation is very slow, so a seeded generator and a sine table supply the random phase (same loudness, tested; reproducible per parameter list). (DESIGN B1, B7)
- 2026-09-27 (mixer): buses master, music, sfx, ui (A13); music passes a duck gain. Settings volumes (0..1) use a square-law taper times a fixed trim (master 0.7, music 0.55, sfx 0.9, ui 0.8). The master chain is a hard-knee 20:1 limiter (threshold -10 dB, attack 0, release 150 ms) and a soft-knee safety clipper (linear to 0.8, ceiling 0.98). DoD check (soundboard, offline in Chromium): 40 hits starting on the same sample with no voice limits, each its loudest variant at +3 dB, peak 4.32 (+12.7 dBFS) raw and 0.90 (-0.9 dBFS) through the chain; with the real voice limits the same 40 plays give 8 voices and about -11 dBFS. (DESIGN A13, C2/WP6)
- 2026-09-27 (voices and priority): at most 4 voices per id and a 40 ms retrigger gap (A13), plus a global cap of 32 effect voices. `play`'s `priority`: higher wins; UI-bus sounds get +10. A new voice at a full cap steals the oldest voice of the lowest priority not above its own, else it is dropped; a retrigger inside the gap is dropped unless it outranks the play that started the gap. Player-caused sounds should pass `priority: 1` (requested from WP5 in `docs/requests/wp6-audio-wiring.md`). Each play picks a random variant (never the same twice in a row), pitch ±8% and volume ±3 dB; `pitchBp` multiplies the rate (10000 = unchanged), `volumeDb` adds, `pan` places the sound. Effects played before the first gesture are dropped. (DESIGN A13, B15)
- 2026-09-27 (ducking): `music.duck(db, ms)` lowers the music bus by |db| (either sign means down; WP5 and WP10 send -6) for `ms`, with a 50 ms attack and a 350 ms release; overlapping ducks merge into the deepest depth and the latest end. (DESIGN A12, A13)
- 2026-09-27 (music engine): a look-ahead step sequencer (25 ms timer, 200 ms ahead) plays scores with synthesised instruments (oscillators, a shared noise buffer, envelopes, filters, vibrato; `src/audio/instruments.ts`). `music.transpose(n)` sets the total transposition (WP5 sends 2, 4, 5, 6 after each own evolve, A13 +2/+2/+1/+1; `evolveTranspose` gives the same numbers); drums are not transposed. Layers: `intensity` = a harmony line a diatonic third below the melody plus extra percussion, `overdrive` = double-time percussion and +8 BPM × the layer level, `siege` = a heartbeat bass on the chord roots; tracks of a silent layer are not scheduled. Layers and key carry over from a battle cue to the next battle cue (an evolve) or a stinger; any other change, and `stop()`, resets them, so every battle starts in the home key. Two battle arrangements switch on the same step (the evolve crossfade continues the melody). A throttled tab skips missed steps instead of bursting; the context is suspended while the page is hidden. Cue levels are trimmed per cue (`gainDb`). (DESIGN A13, A14.3)
- 2026-09-27 (theme): "Dawn March" is ours: 16 bars in C major at 110 BPM, AA'BA'' with a dotted, stepwise march motif (C-G-C-E), one chord per bar (`src/audio/scores/dawnMarch.ts`). Arrangements: Stone (breathy square flute, hide drums, toms, shaker, drone), Medieval (horn, plucked lute arpeggios, tabor), Gunpowder (fife an octave up, snare march, bass drum, tuba oom-pah), Modern (brass lead and stabs, synth bass, kit), Future (lead, 16th arpeggios, pad, sidechain pump on the beat, four-on-the-floor); Menu is the slow version at 84 BPM (soft flute, pad, bell); the capsule room is an 8-bar A-minor anticipation loop at 100 BPM that quotes the motif in minor; the stingers (2 bars and a beat) play the motif as a brass fanfare (victory) and a gentle descent home to C (defeat). (DESIGN A13)
- 2026-09-27 (unlock and vibration): `unlock()` creates the context inside the first gesture, calls `resume()` and plays a one-sample silent buffer synchronously (iOS); after an interruption the next gesture or the page becoming visible resumes it. Without Web Audio the game stays silent instead of failing. A13's `navigator.vibrate` on climbs and Legendaries stays in WP10's capsule stage (it already honours `settings.vibrate`); the `AudioService` contract has no vibration. (DESIGN A13, B11, C5 #44)
- 2026-09-27 (dev page): WP6 owns `src/dev/soundboard/**` (`?dev=1#soundboard`): every id by group (random play through the service, exact variants, ZzFX array copy), every cue, layer sliders, the evolve key changes, ducking, bus volumes, a live peak meter and clip counter, the boot render time, and offline checks that render every id and cue through the mixer and run the 40-hit limiter test. `window.__soundboard` exposes the service and results for headless checks. (DESIGN A9 screen 16, C2/WP6)

## WP8

- 2026-09-27 (slots): Each slot holds the JSON envelope `{ v, writtenAt, checksum, payload }` where `payload` is the doc's JSON text and `checksum` the FNV-1a 32 (`@/core` `fnv1a32`) of exactly that text, so any change is caught before parsing. `writtenAt` = max(clock, previous newest + 1): writes stay ordered even when the device clock goes back. The newest valid `writtenAt` wins (tie: slot A). Writes go to the slot that did not load, so a failed or cut-off write can only hit the older copy. (DESIGN B8 Keys, Load order)
- 2026-09-27 (write timing): `save()` validates and serializes at once, then writes 2 s after the first unsaved change; later saves in that window replace the payload without postponing it, so at most 2 s of progress is at risk. `{ immediate: true }` and `flush()` write now; the browser hooks flush on `visibilitychange` (hidden) and `pagehide`, and `load()` flushes first. A failed write stays pending and is retried by the next flush. The promise resolves after the write attempt and never rejects; outcomes go through `problem` / `onProblem`. (DESIGN B8 Writes)
- 2026-09-27 (validation): The schema checks the contract types exactly (type-parity tests with `expectTypeOf(...).branded`) plus finite numbers, non-negative integer counts, 5/2 loadout slots, all five ages per War Plan, at least one plan, `activePlan` in range and uint32 RNG words; it does not check ids against content. Unknown keys are stripped. Settings fall back field by field to `DEFAULT_SETTINGS`, so a bad slider value never costs a profile. The exact version is checked by `validateSaveDoc(doc, version)`, after migrations. `save()` writes the validated output and refuses a doc that fails (problem `invalidDoc`, last good save kept), so disk only ever holds loadable docs; the invariants are listed for WP7 in `docs/requests/wp8-meta-save-invariants.md`. (DESIGN B8 step 4, B13 Schemas)
- 2026-09-27 (load outcomes): `load()` returns the doc or null and records a `LoadReport`: `empty` (first run), `loaded`, `recovered` (a newer copy was rejected, or the other slot is damaged, so progress may be missing) or `unreadable` (nothing loads: null, and the app creates the fresh save and shows the B8 banner from `loadReport.notice`). Before anything can overwrite them, rejected copies are set aside in `ageborn.backup.unreadable` (all present slots when nothing loads, else the rejected ones with a valid checksum; the first set aside is kept). A slot written by a newer build (`tooNew`, e.g. an old cached page after a deploy) blocks all writes and reports the ongoing `tooNew` problem until the page is reloaded or the player resets, so the newer save survives. (DESIGN B8 step 5)
- 2026-09-27 (migrations): `src/save/migrations/vN.ts` describes save version N and holds the step that produces it (`up` = `m[N-1]`); v1 is the base and has none. `SAVE_VERSIONS` lists them and `SAVE_VERSION` is the last. Each step gets a deep copy and must return `v: N`. Before each step the store writes `ageborn.backup.pre-v<N>` (the envelope of the doc before the step producing N; the first backup is kept). `load()` itself writes no slot; the next save writes the new version. Every version needs a frozen `src/save/test/fixtures/v<N>.json` (a test enforces it) and `v1.code.txt` freezes an export code that must keep importing. (DESIGN B8 step 3, B13 Save)
- 2026-09-27 (export codes): `ageborn1.` + base64url (no padding) of zlib-deflated JSON at level 9 (a realistic save: 7.8 KB JSON → 2.6 KB code). `1` is the code format, not the save version. fflate's `unzlibSync` does not check the Adler-32, so the import does; a mistyped or truncated code is always rejected. Import strips whitespace, accepts a capitalised prefix, standard base64 and plain JSON (hand-made backups), caps codes at 1 M characters and inflated JSON at 4 MiB, then migrates and validates. Import never writes by itself. `.ageborn` files (`ageborn-YYYY-MM-DD.ageborn`, `text/plain`) hold the same code plus a newline, so a file and a pasted code import the same way. (DESIGN B8 Export/import)
- 2026-09-27 (quota): The replay ring (20) and the event log (500) persist the newest items that fit and halve on quota errors; a save write that hits the quota first takes their space (replays, then the log; never the backups), and only then reports `quota`. Every problem is a `SaveNotice` with an i18n key (`save.problem.*`, `save.import.*`); the save layer cannot import i18n, so the string file is requested in `docs/requests/wp8-strings.md` and the display in `docs/requests/wp8-app-wiring.md`. Blocked storage (access or probe write throws anything but a quota error) runs on an in-memory stand-in with the ongoing `unavailable` problem; a full storage is still used. (DESIGN B8 Durability)
- 2026-09-27 (replays and event log): `ReplayDocSchema` lives in `src/save/replaySchema.ts` (B13 lists ReplayDoc among the schema/type pairs; WP8 reads replays back); all ten WP2 golden replays validate unchanged, and a missing `training` reads back as null. `EventLogStore` uses the record shape and JSON array of WP11's `EventLog` on the same key (tested in both directions). `reset()` removes both slots, every `ageborn.backup.*` key and the replays, and keeps the event log (instrumentation, not progress) unless `{ eventLog: true }`. (DESIGN A8, B3, B8)
- 2026-09-27 (durability): `persist()` checks `persisted()` first, then asks, and never throws (`persisted` / `granted` / `denied` / `unsupported`); `isFirstWin(prev, next)` tells the app when to call it. `backupReminderDue` uses the same rule as WP9 (`lastExportAt ?? createdAt`, more than 5 days); `markExported` stamps `lastExportAt`. Two tabs writing at once are not coordinated in v1: the last writer wins. Valibot and fflate ship in the save chunk because validation runs in production (about 11 KB gzipped with the dev page). (DESIGN B8 Durability)
- 2026-09-27 (dev page): WP8's page is `src/dev/save/page.tsx` (`?dev=1#save`), a new folder no other package uses; WP8 owns `src/dev/save/**`. It runs on a sandbox in-memory storage (the real save is only read, on request) and drives every case: debounced and immediate writes, alternation, damaged, invalid and newer-version slots, the quota message, export/import, replays and the event log; `window.__saveDev` exposes the sandbox. (DESIGN C1 dev pages)
- 2026-09-28 (review: kept copies): `ageborn.backup.unreadable` now holds `{ copies: [{ at, source, raw }] }`: up to 4 distinct rejected slot texts, newest last, each kept once; when the list is full or storage has no room the oldest copies go first. This supersedes "the first set aside is kept" in "(load outcomes)", which lost a later rejection (say, both slots refused by a later build) once anything had been set aside before. Unknown content under the key is kept as one copy. `readUnreadableCopies` reads it for tools. (DESIGN B8 step 5)
- 2026-09-28 (review: recovered): `recovered` now means a copy that was or may have been newer was rejected, superseding "or the other slot is damaged" in "(load outcomes)". A slot whose payload fails its checksum still shows its envelope `writtenAt`; when that is older than the loaded copy nothing was lost, so the load is `loaded` with no notice (the old wording told the player "Your last save was damaged" when it was not). Unparsable text has no time and still gives `recovered`. (DESIGN B8 step 5)
- 2026-09-28 (review: robustness): `save()` before `load()` also refuses to write over a slot from a newer build (`tooNew`). A storage that holds no keys and refuses even the 1-character probe with a quota error (old Safari private browsing) counts as blocked (`unavailable`, memory stand-in) instead of "full". The rings store JSON copies of pushed items, and before writing re-read their key when another writer changed it, so a quota `shrink()` of `ageborn.eventlog` never writes a stale list over the app `EventLog`'s newer entries. Plain-JSON import tolerates a byte-order mark. `src/save/test/strings.test.ts` checks that every notice and import key has EN text (in `src/i18n` once the file lands, else in `docs/requests/wp8-strings.md`) and that the unreadable banner uses B8's wording. (DESIGN A8, B8)

## WP7

- 2026-09-28 (API and purity): `src/meta` exports `meta` (bound to the game content) and `createMeta(content)`, both a `MetaRules`: the frozen `Meta` contract plus helpers the app and the screens need (`claimDailyCapsule`, `claimQuest`, `rerollQuest`, `grantWardrobe`, `dailyModifier`, `conquestBoard`, `setWarPlan`, `setActivePlan`, `equipSkin`, `markSeen`). `openCapsule`, `openWardrobe` and `tickTimers` take no content in the contract, so they use the bound content. Every function returns a new save and never mutates its input (a test runs them on deep-frozen saves). Failure reasons are stable codes (listed in `docs/requests/wp7-app-wiring.md`). (DESIGN B9, B15)
- 2026-09-28 (local time): daily timers reset at local 04:00 without `Date`: the injected clock may carry `offsetMs(t)` (`LocalClock` in `src/meta/time.ts`); a plain `Clock` means UTC days. Calendar keys use integer civil-date formulas: `dayKey` `YYYY-MM-DD` and `daily.dayKey` of the game day (03:59 belongs to the previous day), `weekKey` the ISO week (weekly quest resets Monday 04:00), the Daily Challenge seed `YYYYMMDD` of the game day. A clock moved backwards only restarts the current charge period and the Daily Capsule timer; nothing earned is taken away. (DESIGN A6.3, A6.7, A9.1, B2)
- 2026-09-28 (pity timing): contents are rolled at grant (A6.4) but `SaveDoc.pity` counts *opened* capsules since the last hit (A6.5 "count every opened capsule"; the reveal's `pityBefore`/`pityAfter` are the counters around the opening). A roll uses a conservative n: opened count + every pending counted capsule taken as a miss + 1. With nothing pending (the usual case) that is exactly A6.5; with a pile pending, pity triggers no later than the rule needs in any order the player opens them, so "guaranteed within N" on screen is always true (tested by opening piles oldest-first and newest-first). While rolling, cards and skins inside unopened capsules and crates count as owned, so a NEW card is never promised twice; `isNew` and the max-level Dust are decided again at reveal. Wardrobe pity works the same way over pending crates. (DESIGN A6.4, A6.5, A10)
- 2026-09-28 (roll details): pity upgrades a stack and then keeps it (treated as guaranteed) so a later rule never undoes an earlier one; Epic pity counts literal Epic stacks (a Legendary does not reset it), wardrobe pity counts "Epic or better" as A6.5 says. New-card protection first marks the lowest-rarity stack (ties last, guaranteed stacks allowed, the rarity unchanged) whose rarity still has unowned cards; otherwise the lowest non-guaranteed stack becomes the lowest rarity with unowned cards. A stack with no card left to pick (a one-age Age Capsule, a second Legendary stack when the last unowned Legendary is taken) falls back to the next lower rarity rather than repeating a card or giving a duplicate Legendary. Stacks are stored rarest last (A10 step 5). (DESIGN A6.4, A6.5)
- 2026-09-28 (bag): `capsules.bag` holds the Win Capsule tiers left in the current bag as sorted tier indices (WP9's reading); a draw takes a uniform element and removes it (drawing without replacement), `[]` means a fresh bag. Only `win` capsules outside the script use it. (DESIGN A6.4)
- 2026-09-28 (script): the first five counted capsules of a save follow A6.5 whatever their kind (a Daily or Clay-meter capsule can be scripted capsule 3); `scriptIndex` is the 1-based capsule number. The scripted cards (and capsule 4's random unowned Epic) replace the tier's guarantees; the other stacks are Common stacks of owned cards, first from the active plan's first-age loadout (the cards just played), then other owned Commons, so every NEW card, the first Epic and the first Legendary arrive exactly as scripted; the tier's Amber, bonus Dust and Aeon skin chance still apply; no pity upgrades. Capsule 1 also carries a Bonker stack (`SCRIPT_EXTRA_CARDS`) so A8's forced Bonker upgrade after capsule 2 is always affordable. Age Unlock Capsules never take a script step. (DESIGN A6.5, A8)
- 2026-09-28 (capsule kinds): Age Capsules roll 4 stacks with Silver copies and Silver Amber (300), ≥ 1 Epic, pity applies, from one age (the caller's `age`; default: the drop-pool age with the most cards not owned yet, ties to the earliest). Age Unlock Capsules show as Silver (A6.3 gives no tier), hold the age's Anti-armor Rare ×1 and its 3 common units ×4 with no foils and no Amber. Road, Codex and Conquest capsules have fixed tiers. The Aeon bonus skin goes into `contents.skin` (Wardrobe odds, no wardrobe pity, unowned first); `PendingCrate.source: 'aeon'` is unused. Quest crates use the `weekly` source. (DESIGN A6.4)
- 2026-09-28 (copies and Dust): a new card starts at L1 holding all of its stack's copies (crafting an unowned card likewise gives L1 with 1 copy). Copies of a card at L10 turn into Dust at reveal; reaching L10 by an upgrade turns the leftover copies into Dust at once, so a maxed card never holds copies. A card at L10 cannot be crafted. (DESIGN A6.4 step 6, A6.6)
- 2026-09-28 (charges): the first 10 Win Capsules from matches (the two tutorial capsules included) are free; then each ladder win with a capsule uses a charge. `chargesUpdatedAt` is the start of the current 6 h period; spending a charge from a full bank restarts it. The Clay meter step reports the pips reached (1-3); the third makes a `meter` capsule and empties the meter. (DESIGN A6.3)
- 2026-09-28 (Daily Capsule): opening the second counted capsule sets `dailyBank` to 1 and the flag `meta.dailyUnlocked`; the next `tickTimers` starts `dailyNextAt` (opening has no clock). Each later local 04:00 adds one, up to 3. `grantCapsule('daily')` takes one from the bank when there is one (the economy model and `claimDailyCapsule` rely on it) and still grants when the bank is empty (the drop tool uses it). (DESIGN A6.3)
- 2026-09-28 (results): tutorial results (A8 matches 1-2) grant the next scripted capsule win or lose plus the ladder Amber for the result, with no trophies, charges or MMR. Skirmish pays 5 Amber per win; the Daily Challenge's first win of the day an Age Capsule, later wins 20 Amber; Conquest pays stars and milestones; only ladder matches move trophies, MMR, the loss streak and the Clay meter. Every mode counts in `matchesPlayed`, the profile stats (wins and losses by the opponent's tier) and quests. Reward steps come in the A9 #7 order: trophies, Amber, capsule or Clay pip, stars, quests, arenas, titles. The loss streak resets on a win or a draw. `stats.trainedByCard` stays empty: `MatchStats` has no per-card counts (`docs/requests/wp7-matchstats-trained-by-card.md`). (DESIGN A6.1, A6.3, A6.10, A9.1)
- 2026-09-28 (arenas and road): the arena follows the trophies and never goes down; gate rewards come from the Trophy Road "Gate N" nodes (banners, capsules, the two Age Unlock Capsules, the Crystal Spire; a duplicate skin becomes Dust), while Conquest (Arena 3) and The Warden (Arena 8) follow the arena itself. Nodes are claimable up to the *best* trophies; `roadClaimed` holds node trophy values. (DESIGN A6.3)
- 2026-09-28 (quests): at each reset claimed quests leave, and up to 3 new quests per day passed join (never more than 6 unclaimed); a finished unclaimed weekly waits for its claim. New quests are drawn with a seed of the save's `createdAt` and the day, so the capsule stream is never touched. A reroll replaces an open unfinished quest. Quest facts: "before" times are strict (`<`); "Destroy a base before 6:00" needs a win by `baseDestroyed`; the Legendary quest counts a Legendary in the active plan within the format's ages. Titles live in `cosmetics.owned` next to banners and frames; the Evolver title uses `stats.futureReached`. (DESIGN A5.8, A6.7)
- 2026-09-28 (Codex): a save starts at Codex Level 1; level = 1 + floor(points / 15), so the full collection (1,215 points) reaches 82 (81 level-ups, A6.7's "about 81 levels"). Rewards per level as A6.7; frames and the Collector/Curator titles as A5.8. Upgrades, which have no clock, stamp any capsule or crate they grant with the latest time the save knows. (DESIGN A6.7)
- 2026-09-28 (MMR): Elo uses a 41-entry bp table of 1 / (1 + 10^(−d/400)) every 25 rating points with linear interpolation (within 3 bp, 0.01 MMR at K = 32), because meta has no `Math.pow`; changes round half away from zero. Only ladder matches move MMR. (DESIGN A6.8, B2)
- 2026-09-28 (opponents): ladder and Daily opponents are named Generals 30% of the time when one's tier range holds the tier (`GENERAL_SHARE_BP`; DESIGN gives no share), else procedural AI Commanders that copy a ladder General's personality and plan under the arena's rarity allowance, with a seeded name, favourite card and a −1/0/+1 level roll; their `generalId` is `commander:<personality>:<favourite>` (`commanderInfo`). Cards above the allowance are replaced by allowed cards of the same age. The Legendary rule is per age: the bot gets an age's Legendary only when the player's loadout for that age holds one, at the player's level of it (replacing its Epic, else its last unit slot). The Warden (1 in 5 Arena 8 matches, never a warm-up) brings all five Legendaries at L9 with its disclosure. The first ladder match is Captain Kettle. Daily opponents use the ladder tier without loss protection. Skirmish Generals use their full personal plan at the arena level (every card at L7 with Standard levels); Echo mirrors the active plan and levels. Specs are deterministic in the save (seeded by `createdAt`, `matchesPlayed` and the mode), so the Home preview is the match. `pickOpponent` does not refuse a locked Conquest General; the board (`conquestBoard`) gates it. (DESIGN A6.8, A6.10, A7.4, A8, A9.1)
- 2026-09-28 (advisor): error codes `badShape`, `unknownCard`, `wrongAge`, `notOwned`, `duplicate`, `badPower`, `tooFewUnits`, `noTurret`; warnings `onlyThreeUnits`, `noAntiArmor`, `noAir` (from Gunpowder on; turrets and riders count), `noSplash` (plan-wide, reported on the format's first age; splash, cleave, chain, pierce, line, gate-zone and follow-behind attacks all count as area damage, so Pebbler's ricochet does). Message keys are `ui.advisor.<code>` (all present in WP9's `ui.en.json`). Auto-fill orders units in content order and keeps the chosen power when it is owned. (DESIGN A3)
- 2026-09-28 (pacing note for Phase 3): `tools/sim-cli.ts economy` through the real rules passes the per-capsule and per-day checks and the copy/Amber finish gap (25 days), but finishes Commons, Epics and Legendaries faster and the focused plan at L7 slower than A6.9, and owns all 5 Legendaries in about 6 days (A6.9: ~2 weeks). The main cause is literal A6.5: once only Legendaries are unowned, new-card protection upgrades a stack to Legendary every 5 capsules. This is a tuning question for Phase 3 (numbers or the protection's scope), not a rule change WP7 made. (DESIGN A6.5, A6.9)
- 2026-09-28 (dev page): WP7's page is `src/dev/meta/page.tsx` (`?dev=1#meta`), a new folder no other package uses; WP7 owns `src/dev/meta/**`. It drives a save through the real rules with a controllable clock (matches in every mode, capsules and crates with their reveals, upgrades, crafting, quests, road, advisor, opponent preview, a 10,000-capsule sample). (DESIGN C1 dev pages)

## WP6 (review)

- 2026-09-28 (retrigger gap): the 40 ms retrigger gap is absolute (A13 "40 ms minimum retrigger gap", and `coin_gain` "throttled to 1 per 40 ms"); a higher `priority` no longer starts a same-id voice inside it (it would only flam with the voice just started). Priority decides the voice caps: a player-caused sound (`priority: 1`) steals the opponent's voices at a full cap and is never stolen by them. No sound may loosen A13's limits: `maxVoices` ≤ 4 and `gapMs` ≥ 40 (tested); `reel_tick`'s 25 ms gap is gone, since WP10's reel already spaces its ticks 40 ms apart. Supersedes the gap clause of the WP6 "voices and priority" entry. (DESIGN A13)
- 2026-09-28 (per-play variation): every sound varies by A13's pitch ±8% and volume ±3 dB (A12 checklist "Correct SFX with variation"). Only sounds another rule needs at a steady pitch use `pitchVarBp: 0`, and they keep the ±3 dB volume spread: musical sounds (jingles, fanfares, rarity reveals, chimes, capsule climbs, plus `spawn_legendary` and `turret_upgrade`, which carry a note), timed sounds (the random pitch is a playback-rate change, so it changes the length: `power_telegraph` for the 1.0 s telegraph, `evolve_riser`, `cap_riser`, `last_stand_charge`) and caller-pitched sounds (`coin_gain` climbs, `copy_tick` climbs, the falling `reel_tick`). The ad hoc ±2-4% spreads and the ±1 dB volume on musical sounds are gone (tested: an override is 0 or nothing). (DESIGN A12, A13)
- 2026-09-28 (unlock on touch screens): on a touch screen the tap's `pointerdown` (and `touchstart`) carries no user activation under the HTML rules, so an unlock attempted there can leave the context suspended, and a context that never ran fires no `statechange`. `keepAlive` now arms its retry listeners (`pointerdown`, `pointerup`, `mousedown`, `touchend`, `click`, `keydown`) as soon as the context exists and not running, retries resume plus the silent blip on every gesture, and disarms only once the context reports `running`; gestures while the page is hidden do not resume it. `unlock()` resolves when the context actually runs (or after the 3 s safety timeout), not when `resume()` settles. (DESIGN A13, B11, C5 #44)
- 2026-09-28 (defeat stinger): A13 wants both stingers "on the motif"; the defeat stinger now opens with the motif (C - G C in the theme's rhythm) and sinks to A over A minor where the theme rises to E, then walks down to C (gentle, not mocking). (DESIGN A13)
- 2026-09-28 (robustness): the service turns non-finite numbers from callers (`pitchBp`, `volumeDb`, `pan`, fade times, layer levels, transposition, duck) into their defaults instead of letting the AudioParam setters throw inside the caller's frame. The vendored ZzFX picks the wave by range as upstream does, so fractional or negative shape values from the designer match upstream too (tested against the verbatim port). (DESIGN B7, B1)

## WP7 (review)

- 2026-09-28 (Age Capsule dialog): A6.4 says an Age Capsule's age is "picked in a dialog when granted", but the Daily Challenge first win and Conquest star 3 grant theirs inside `applyMatchResult`, which had no way to take the age. `MetaRules.applyMatchResult` now takes an optional fifth argument `{ age }` (still assignable to the frozen `Meta` signature), and `MetaRules.ageCapsuleDue(save, input, content, clock)` tells the app, before it applies a result, whether that result grants a non-scripted Age Capsule, so it can ask first. Without an age the default age is used as before. (DESIGN A6.4, A6.10, A9.1)
- 2026-09-28 (Conquest gating): a Conquest result against a General that is not open on the board (before Arena 3, or before the previous General is beaten once) pays no stars and no milestones. Meta owns the rule, so it no longer relies on the board screen alone. (DESIGN A6.10)
- 2026-09-28 (scripted Age Capsule): when an Age Capsule is one of the five scripted capsules, its contents follow the A6.5 script and are not from one age, so its `age` is null (it used to carry the picked age while holding Stone cards) and `ageCapsuleDue` asks for no age. (DESIGN A6.4, A6.5)
- 2026-09-28 (NEW stays in its capsule): a pile of unopened capsules opened newest-first could break new-card protection (a reviewer probe reached 8 capsules without a new card on the counter). Two causes, both fixed. (1) A later roll could pick another pending capsule's NEW card as a plain copy; opened first, it took the NEW away from the capsule that carried the protection. A roll now leaves cards another pending capsule reveals as NEW alone while their rarity has any other card (`RollContext.reserved`; the rarity odds are unchanged, only which card of the rarity). (2) Opening counted a miss whenever the collection lacked a pool card, even when that card was already inside an unopened capsule, while the roll treats such a card as covered. Opening now uses the roll's view: a card inside an unopened capsule needs no protection. With nothing pending (the drop tool, the usual case) nothing changes; with piles, `sinceNewCard` stays within 0-4 in any opening order and every reveal shows exactly the NEW stamps rolled at grant (unless the player crafted the card meanwhile). (DESIGN A6.4, A6.5)

## Owner decisions, 2026-09-28

The owner accepted all recommendations:
- A15: the Wardrobe reel is dropped (card-flip reveal everywhere); the safe defaults apply to all players; the 12-item A15 v1 slice goes into Phase 2b (cut from the bottom if needed).
- A16: the new top rarity tier is prestige variants of Legendary cards with no extra power; the home village comes in v1.1; online ranked shows both trophies (progress) and a visible rating.
- 3D sprite art: decided after the owner has played the first battle.

## Phase 2a (battle integration)

- 2026-09-28 (start screen): until WP9's Home is wired (Phase 2b) the app opens on one start screen with Quick Battle (format picker Short/Standard/Full, Short by default, vs AI Captain Kettle tier III) and the waiting onboarding match behind a second button. After onboarding the waiting match is the training match vs Old Grogg, so the title is always a live battlefield (A8 0:00). `AppController.training()` starts match 1 at any step. A fresh profile still reaches match 1 in one tap. (DESIGN A8, C3)
- 2026-09-28 (result and pause buttons): the result screen always offers Home, and Play again unless Retry is shown (Play again after a tutorial match replays that match). The pause panel offers Quit in every mode and Restart in Quick Battle. (DESIGN C3 "restart button")
- 2026-09-28 (music cues): the controller sets `music.menu` on the title, the first age's cue when a battle starts, and `music.stop(600)` when a running battle is left without a result; the view keeps the evolve, layer and stinger cues. (DESIGN A14.3)
- 2026-09-28 (sound priority): the event mapper marks sounds caused by the player's side (`actingSide`: the event's side, the acting unit or turret, the killer) with `priority: 1`. (DESIGN A13)
- 2026-09-28 (dev autopilot): outside the tutorial `?dev=1&autopilot=1` plays the player's side with WP3's Balanced brain at tier V (it uses every control: turrets, Modernise, evolve, powers, stance, Last Stand); `?bots=fallback` keeps the simple stand-in. `?dev=1&game=1` boots the game with `window.__agebornDev` but no autopilot, for scripted human-like tests. (DESIGN B13)
- 2026-09-28 (audio unlock events): the first-gesture unlock listens for `pointerup`, `touchend`, `click` and `keydown`; a touch `pointerdown` carries no user activation. (DESIGN B11, C5 #44)
- 2026-09-28 (rules playtest: ARMY FULL tap): a tap on a card showing ARMY FULL now sends the train command instead of a HUD deny. ARMY FULL only means one instance of that card waits for room; queueing another is legal (A2.7), and the sim's rejection still flashes the card when gold or the queue runs out. (DESIGN A2.7, A9.2, C5 #10)
- 2026-09-28 (rules playtest: modifiers in the HUD): the Daily Challenge modifier rules moved from `src/sim/modifiers.ts` to `src/core/modifiers.ts` (the sim module re-exports them, values unchanged, golden replays unchanged), so the battle HUD (render may not import the sim, B2) reads them too. The HUD's Evolve readiness and XP bar (Fast Forward), card prices and affordability (Heavy Metal), income (Gold Rush) and Siege mark (Sudden Siege) now match the sim. (DESIGN A9.1, A9.2, B2)

## Player experience (usability audit of the first battle)

- 2026-09-28 (pointer events): full-screen UI layers never take pointer events (`#uiHost`, `.ab-root`, `.ab-layer`, `.hud`); only panels, bubbles for hints and buttons opt back in. Taps on the lane now reach the canvas (mount taps, pinch zoom, double tap). Tested in `tests/e2e/ux.spec.ts`. (DESIGN A2.1, A9.2)
- 2026-09-28 (base placement): render places each base root on its gate and lets the art mirror itself, as the WP4 base adapters expect; `mountPoints()` are world points. This put side 0's base fully on screen and fixed side 1's facing and mount points. Request `docs/requests/wp4-baseview-root.md`. (DESIGN A2.1, B5)
- 2026-09-28 (tutorial): a beat can `jumpQueue`: "Evolve!" shows as soon as Evolve is ready, even while "Build a Rock Tosser" still waits (the turret step then retires with Stone). "Kills earn gold" stays 5 s. The Pebbler beat asks for an action ("Tap to send a Pebbler") and waits up to 30 s, as does the turret beat. Every beat prompt shows a 48 px animated hand on its target; the bubble is placed above, below, right or left so it never covers the target; mount targets get a 64 px round ring, and once the mount's menu is open the prompt points at the Rock Tosser option. Retimed beats still pass `retime.test.ts`. (DESIGN A8)
- 2026-09-28 (countdown): Quick Battles and other non-tutorial matches open with "3-2-1 Fight!" (0.7 s per number); the session stays 'ready' (no ticks, no commands) until "Fight!". Pause or dev fast-forward end the countdown. The tutorial has none. (audit #21)
- 2026-09-28 (Quick Battle unlocks): Quick Battle keeps the staged unlocks of `stagedTraining(n)` (no stance flag before match 4, no Last Stand button before match 5); it still has no tutorial script. The Scouted chip shows from match 3. (DESIGN A8, A9.2)
- 2026-09-28 (HUD readability): the XP bar reads "XP 180/250"; Evolve is a round 56 px button (48 px on phones) at the end of the XP bar: gold, steady glow, a slow bob of its parts (never a flash, A2.4), the next age's icon and "Evolve!". Treasury reads "+Income · 200". The centre shows "Overdrive in 2:59" under the clock and a front-line strip (your colour against theirs); the training match has no clock, only the strip. Base hits kick that side's HP panel. "Blocked at their gate. Evolve or use your power." shows when your front stood at their gate 5 s without hurting the base (at most once per 45 s). Phase starts and your evolves get a big centred banner ("Overdrive! Gold ×2", "Medieval Age! New units!"). Keyboard badges show only after the first key press (remembered in localStorage). (DESIGN A9.2, audit #4, #7, #11, #22, #24)
- 2026-09-28 (tray): empty slots are hidden (one padlock slot while the match still has a scripted unlock); cards carry a name ribbon, a gold 22 px queue badge, a training fill rising from the bottom over a portrait that stays in colour, and flip in after an evolve. A card you cannot afford fills toward affordable and shows "4s" (at the current income); a denied tap shakes it and floats "Need 23"; a train floats "-50" from the gold. Hover (desktop) or the "i" corner (touch) shows the card's name, role, text, health, cost and what it is good against. The power button shows its name, and "READY" with a burst and bob when full. (DESIGN A9.2, audit #8, #9, #12, #13, #15)
- 2026-09-28 (moments): your evolve freezes 220 ms, then the camera pushes in (1.3x) on your base for the rebuild and eases out; a destroyed base gets a 1.45x push that holds into the result. Pushes never show past the world's ends and are off with Reduce motion. In crowds (> 10 units) only each side's 3 front units and recently hit units show health bars; every ground unit stands on a team-coloured disc. (DESIGN A12, audit #5, #17, #18)
- 2026-09-28 (start and result screens): a new profile (step `match1`) sees one big gold "Play" that starts the training match; Quick Battle is a small secondary button until then, and formats say "up to 6 min". The result keeps the battle's last frame behind a dimmed, outcome-tinted scrim (the battle is disposed when the player leaves the result), shows a big banner, one story line ("You toppled Old Grogg in 1:59"), one gold main button (Continue, Retry or Play again), and Replay and Home as icon buttons. (DESIGN A8, A9 #7, audit #10, #16)

## Design merge 2026-09-28

- 2026-09-28: A15 (`design-engagement.md`) and A16 (`design-depth.md`) are merged into DESIGN as A15 and A16 at the end of Part A, with the owner decisions applied (no reel, card flip everywhere and A10.1 deleted; safe defaults for all; A15 v1 slice in Phase 2b; Mythic = Ascended forms of Legendaries with no extra power; village in v1.1; ranked shows trophies and a visible Glicko-2 rating), the listed edits to A1, A2.7, A2.10, A2.14, A3, A5.8, A6.1-A6.9, A7.1-A7.3, A8, A9, A9.1, A10, A13, B8, B9, B11, B12, B14, B15 (new B15.1), C2, C3, C5, D1, D2 and the appendix, engagement rule 10 (MMR and bot tuning target win rate only, never session length or retention) recorded, and eight `docs/requests/a15-*.md` files written. (DESIGN A15, A16)

## Quality pass

- 2026-09-28 (size budget): the initial download limit stays 3 MB gzip (`npm run size` fails above it). The total budget of the deployed build rises from 8 MB to 16 MB gzip (warning only), because the 3D-rendered unit, turret and base sprite sheets (`dist/art`, about 4 MB of PNG for 36 units) and the audio (`dist/audio`) now ship with the game. Both load lazily after the initial chunk and are reported separately by `tools/size.ts`. (DESIGN B16)
- 2026-09-28 (unit sheets): every unit (35 plus the Training Dummy) draws from its 3D sheet in `public/art/units/<age>/<slug>.json` (manifest entries in `src/visuals/manifest.units.ts`, built from `unitSheets.gen.ts`, which `node art/blender/gen_unit_manifest.mjs` writes and a unit test keeps in sync). Stone sheets are awaited by the boot preload (every match starts in Stone, about 0.8 MB); the other ages stream in the background and also load on first use. A unit whose sheet has not arrived or failed is drawn with its procedural puppet, which also stays the tier for skins without a sheet, card portraits and `?art=procedural`. Anchors come from the sheets: the static muzzle is the attack clip's per-frame muzzle at the impact frame; melee units keep the chest-height default. (DESIGN B5, B16)
- 2026-09-28 (atlas unit motion): walk clips play at `authored cycle × naturalSpeedLuPerS / sim speed` (clamped to 0.5x-2x) so feet do not slide; the speed is read back from the battle view's walk request (500 ms at 80 lu/s convention). The die clip hands off to view-owned dust and KO-star puffs at the sheet's `fx` times and hides the body at `hideUnitAtMs`. The atlas view also shows the stun stars, time-stop clock, shield bubble, spawn dust, ground shadow, hitstop jitter and the procedural flash curve, so every tier shows the same cues. (DESIGN A11, A12, B5)
- 2026-09-28 (UX verify pass): the "Old turret? Tap it to modernise." hint now points at the old turret's mount (mount 0 or 1) instead of the screen centre, and stays quiet while Evolve is ready, because evolving first is the better move (it fired in the training match with Evolve lit). After a tap on a unit card the desktop hover info stays hidden until the pointer leaves the card, and the hover delay is 450 ms, so the info card no longer sits over the battlefield while a player trains units. On a card with a queue badge the "12s" wait pill moves down so the two do not overlap on phone. (DESIGN A8, A9.2)
- 2026-09-28 (world sheets): all 20 turrets and the 5 bases draw from 3D sheets rendered by `art/blender/world` (same shading, outline and tinted team layer as the units) into `public/art/turrets/<age>/<slug>.{png,json}` and `public/art/bases/<age>.{png,json}` (about 0.95 MB in total at 1.5x). Manifest entries live in `src/visuals/manifest.world.ts`; `WorldAtlas` loads them per age with the unit sheets' preload (Stone and Medieval at boot, the rest in idle time, and on first use). A turret or base whose sheet has not arrived draws its procedural puppet (provider fallback, as for units). Turret sheets split a static `mount` frame from a rotating head (`idle`, `fire` with a per-frame muzzle anchor), so aiming stays code motion; `build` and `destroyed` are whole-turret clips, with the 1 s drop-in, recoil squash, one-frame muzzle flash and sell sink as code motion on top. Base sheets hold one body frame per crumble stage, looping flags, and Treasury props per level; torch glow, damage smoke, hit flash and debris, the evolve morph, Last Stand horn and collapse stay code motion. Base `mountsLu` are the procedural puppets' mount points (the Blender ledges are placed by inverse projection, and a test checks the rendered trackers land on them), so mount taps and turret placement did not move. A base skin (e.g. `base.future@crystal_spire`) still draws procedurally when the match starts in that age, but a base that evolves into it keeps the plain Future sheet. (DESIGN A2.8, A11, B5, B16)
- 2026-09-28 (backdrop lighting): the far and mid backdrop layers get a baked lit rim along every top edge, a soft top-to-foot shade and atmospheric haze at their foot (`src/visuals/backdrops/lighting.ts`), the sky has a deeper zenith, a horizon glow, soft sun rays and faint cel-shaded cloud banks, drifting clouds are depth-layered (bigger, higher and faster when near), and every age gets flapping bird flocks (not Future) and slow mist banks. The backdrop palettes are slightly clearer (bluer skies, greener near trees) so the scene reads as depth rather than grey; backdrops stay out of the A11 colour rule. All of it is baked once per age, so the per-frame cost is unchanged apart from a few more ambient sprites. (DESIGN A11, B16)
- 2026-09-28 (effect polish): explosions add a warm fireball, a white-hot bloom, a delayed second shock ring, a ground scorch and a lingering smoke column; hit sparks, the effective spark and muzzle flashes add a short additive bloom; the dust poof adds a ring and side puffs; the evolve pillar adds a glow, an additive core and rising sparkles. Tints stay pale (A11 colour rule). (DESIGN A12)

## Phase 2b: modes, replays and AI depth (A16.3, A16.5, A16.6, A16.14)

- 2026-09-28 (A16.3 rules 1-4 in `src/ai/brain.ts`): rule 1 compares f_counter only (the counter term, gold ignored) of the best tray card with the best affordable one; the counter goal replaces a Treasury or Legendary goal, reuses the Legendary goal's f_save exemption, and lapses after 8 s, when an enemy unit is within 300 lu of the gate, when the card leaves the tray, or once it is affordable (then the card simply wins on score). Rule 2: below tier VII the evolve delay applies with no safety check; from VII the bot evolves at once in a safe window and after the cap (2 s at VII, 0.5 s at X) anyway, except that XP at exactly 100% (which can also mean the final age, `evolveVisible`) still waits for the cap, so no Evolve is ever rejected (fuzz: 0 rejected). Rule 3: `monoFactorBp` over the visible enemy army value by role group; it multiplies the counter term and divides the diversity term and the gold float target. Rule 4: no Legendary goal while the push gate fails. (DESIGN A7.2, A7.3, A16.3)
- 2026-09-28 (exploit proxies, `tools/proxies.ts`, `tools/exploits.ts`): new `random_spam` and the mono family at m = 100% (`mono_heavy` in the default set, `mono_ranged` and `mono_antiair` on request); anti-air = any non-air unit that hits air. Exploit runs play Short and Full War (`--formats`), report the Final Bell share per row, and gate cheapest spam ≤ 20%, random spam ≤ 15%, mono ≤ 35%, the turtle 35-45% with ≤ 50% of its matches at the Bell. Draws count as half a win, as before. The mixing sweep (m = 0-80%), bait-and-switch, save-and-counter and the rules-sanity rows are not built yet. (DESIGN A2.14, A16.5)
- 2026-09-28 (trickle detector, `src/app/trickle.ts`): checked only on the player's own non-summoned spawns; army value = summed card cost of live units on each side. The Result tip key is `app.tip.trickle` (WP9's Result shows it in the summary row through `ResultInfo.tipKey`; the onboarding Result shows it under the stats). The in-battle hint for onboarding matches is requested from WP11 (`docs/requests/modes-trickle-hint.md`). (DESIGN A16.6)
- 2026-09-28 (wall prototype): a separate dev page `?dev=1#walls` (`src/dev/walls/`) instead of a toggle in WP5's sandbox, so no WP5 file changes; it draws the lane in SVG, has pads 240/360/460 for either side (legal only with no enemy ground unit within 120 lu, 25 s recharge), free placement, HP ×1/×2 and AI seats. The wall is the A16.14 `palisade` clone of Tuskback. (DESIGN A16.14)
- 2026-09-28 (replay viewer): broadcast plate ("REPLAY", both names, AI chip, timeline), icon controls in one pill, and an end card that shows whether the re-simulation matched the recording (outcome and final hash). (DESIGN A9 #14, B3)

## Phase 2b: onboarding and Daily Challenge (onboarding-daily track)

- 2026-09-28 (contract amendment, A15.18 / B15.1): applied `a15-wp0-contracts.md` in one change: `SaveDoc.daily` is `{ dayKey, bank }`; optional `pickOpponent` `daily.difficulty`, `OpponentSpec.standardLevels`, `MatchResultInput.feats`, `RewardStep { kind: 'feat' }`, `Settings.breakReminder` and `quickReveal`; the JSDoc meaning changes (charges 28, Supply allowance 7, quest queue 21, War Chest in `QuestState.weekly`, `winsByTier`, `flags['feat.<id>']`). Save schema: `daily.bank` and the two optional settings (defaults: break reminder on, quick reveal off, vibration off). SaveDoc stays v1. (DESIGN A15.18, B15.1)
- 2026-09-28 (match 1 pacing, `docs/requests/wp1-tutorial-pacing.md`): kept A8's "Stone to Future" and slowed it with content numbers only: Tutorial XP thresholds 680 / 690 / 520 / 700 and Old Grogg's base at 90% (was 250 / 300 / 350 / 400 and 50%). Grogg now sends three dummies before his Tuskback (0:33), the Rock Tosser gold comes at 0:38 and the Arrow Storm is filled at 1:04. Measured run: first kill 0:15, Evolve ready 0:52, Medieval 0:55, Arrow Storm 1:04, Gunpowder 1:36, Modern 2:07, Future 2:40, Grogg falls 2:59 (A8 targets 0:55 / 1:30 / 2:00 / 2:35 / 3:00). DESIGN A8 now says "his base at 90%". The content hash snapshot moved to `1ea324ad`; the frozen fixture keeps the old thresholds. (DESIGN A8)
- 2026-09-28 (adaptive hints): each hint shows at most once per match (C5 #2) and at most 3 times per profile (A8); in-battle hints run only in matches 1-5 (`STAGES.hintsUntilMatch`), the detectors keep running. The trickle hint "Save gold, then send them together." is fed by the app's trickle detector through `TutorialDirector.reportPattern`. (DESIGN A8, A16.6)
- 2026-09-28 (Daily Challenge 2.0): `dailySeed = xmur3('daily' + YYYYMMDD)` seeds one sfc32 stream that draws, in order, the modifier, the General (from `dailyModifiers.challenge.generals`, the 8 ladder Generals) and the match seed; so everyone on a date and difficulty gets the same opponent spec (the A6.8 Legendary rule may add the player's own Legendaries). The General plays its personal plan with the ladder rarity allowance at Epic, every card at L7 (`standardLevels`). Default difficulty: nearest tier to the skill tier, ties to the easier one. The bank gains one per 04:00 passed since `daily.dayKey` (never on a clock moved back), capped at 7. (DESIGN A9.1, A15.7)
- 2026-09-28 (rewards by format): `arenas.ladder.winByFormat` holds the A15.8 rows from 400 trophies; below, `ladder.win` applies to every format. (DESIGN A15.8)
- 2026-09-28 (peak rank): `winsByTier` counts Ladder, Daily and Conquest wins only; a Ladder or Conquest win counts only when the active plan's average level over the format's ages is at most the opponent's level + 1 (a plan with no cards counts). (DESIGN A15.9, A16.7)
- 2026-09-28 (quests): the queue keeps claimed quests out (a claim removes the quest; old saves' claimed entries leave at the next 04:00); only indices 0-2 progress, claim and reroll. The weighted draw avoids repeating an id within any 3 neighbours while the pool allows. The War Chest is `content.quests.weekly` (`war_chest`, `countingWins`, target 20). (DESIGN A6.7, A15.4, A15.5)
- 2026-09-28 (feats): the tracker (`src/meta/feats.ts`) is fed by the session each tick and never in the tutorial; Arrow Storm kills are attributed to the last power cast that hit the victim; Old Guard counts living units (summons included) per age of the card; Underdog uses the largest age gap seen during the match. Four feat titles have the unlock kind `feat`, whose "unlock" text says only "Find a hidden feat". (DESIGN A15.10)
- 2026-09-28 (Rookie AI disclosure): `pickOpponent` adds `app.disclosure.rookie` ("Rookie AI: makes extra mistakes while you learn") to the disclosures while the A6.8 new-player bonus applies (first 20 matches). (DESIGN A15.3)
- 2026-09-28 (stopping well): `src/app/stopping.ts` `StoppingCues` holds the session counters (active play sampled each second by `attachActivity`; a new session after 20 min hidden) and picks at most one card per Result: tilt (3rd Ladder loss in a row; Watch opens the loss with the most base damage dealt), then break (first Result after each 60 min, when the setting is on), then wrap (the Ladder win that used the last charge, or 30 min with 3 matches). Healthy-play signals go to the local event log as `health` entries. `NonePlatform.features.reelReveal` is false. (DESIGN A15.6, A15.20, A15.3)
- 2026-09-28 (onboarding Result): the app's onboarding Result shows only what was just earned (Amber, the "Starter Capsule", titles and feats by name); quest progress waits for Home. (DESIGN A8, A15.3, A15.13)

## Quality pass: 3D unit sheets v3 (art director review)

- 2026-09-28 (sheet density): every unit, Legendaries included, is rendered once at 2.46 px/lu and shipped twice: `<slug>.hd.json` (2.46 px/lu) and `<slug>.json` (1.23 px/lu, an exact 2:1 premultiplied downsample of the same frames). The atlas tier loads the HD sheet when the battle draws more than 1.3 device px per lu (`wantsHdSheets`: DPR 2 desktops, 1440p+, phones at DPR 3) and falls back to the plain sheet if the HD one is missing. Lite quality always uses the plain sheet. Both are 256-colour palette PNGs. (DESIGN B5, B16)
- 2026-09-28 (clip timing, `art/blender/ageborn_art/retime.py`): idle 8 frames at 115 ms (one smooth loop), hit 5 frames (~310 ms), die 10 frames (~700 ms: knockback, airborne arc, bounce, slump, then the poof and KO stars); heavies die in 12 frames (~950 ms) without flight. Attack timing by role: light melee 3 wind-up / 2 hold / 3 recovery (690 ms authored), heavy melee 5 wind-up, 3 held impact frames and 4 recovery (13 frames; knight, cuirassier, mammoth, tuskback, paladin, walker, titan, ram), ranged keep their frames plus a recoil kick after the shot. New frames are blends of each unit's authored poses, so the 36 unit modules were not rewritten. The game still time-scales attacks so the impact frame lands on the sim's impact tick. (DESIGN A11, A12, B5)
- 2026-09-28 (outline and view): unit outline is 2.5 px at 1x (about 3 lu) in the nearest fill colour x 0.55 (value capped at 0.50), replacing x 0.40 / cap 0.38; Cycles pixel filter width 1.0; bipeds are yawed 10 degrees toward the camera instead of 18 so facing reads at game size. Base and turret renders keep the previous settings (the v3 path is opt-in, `run_unit(..., v3=True)`). (DESIGN A11)
- 2026-09-28 (card portraits): units with a sheet get a rendered 256 px card still (`public/art/portraits/<slug>.png` plus a grey `_team` layer tinted at runtime), composited on the same age plate and foil frame as the procedural portraits; skins and missing stills keep the procedural portrait. (DESIGN A5.8, B5)
- 2026-09-28 (view polish): projectiles and their tails draw 1.5x larger (visual only); the shield bubble is a filled light-team-tint disc with a thick rim; the team ground ring sits behind the contact shadow at 60% alpha, 1.2x wider and flatter; the backdrop mid-ground layer is tinted about 13% darker so grey and white units stand out. (DESIGN A11, A12)

## Phase 2b: capsules (capsules track)

- 2026-09-28 (no reel, WP10/WP7): the reel is gone (A15.3). `src/capsule/reel.ts`, `reelMath.ts` and their tests are deleted; `planWardrobeShow` always plays the card flip: crate arrival, a 0.7 s crate open (the crate rattles harder, warm light leaks from its seams in a neutral colour, then the lid bursts off), then the one skin card rises, pre-signals its rarity and flips. `meta.openWardrobe` returns `reelTiles: []` and `stopOffsetBp: 0`; `winnerIndex` stays 45 for the contract. `WardrobeScreenProps.reelReveal` is removed. (A10, A15.3)
- 2026-09-28 (quick reveal, WP10): `Settings.quickReveal` reaches the show as `ShowSettings.quickReveal`; `planCapsuleShow` then skips arrival, charge and strikes and starts at the burst (the burst is the "fixed" one of Trophy Road capsules). Pre-signals, walkouts and skips are unchanged. `ShowSettings.vibrate` now defaults to false. (A10 Rules, A15.6)
- 2026-09-28 (honesty copy, WP10): a pill under the capsule names it: "Starter Capsule · contents set to get you started" for scripted capsules (shown as a title and a smaller line), "Supply Capsule" for kind `daily`, else the kind's content name. The honesty line shows at the bottom until the burst on the first capsule of a save (`scriptIndex === 1` or `pityBefore.opened === 0`). The show owns its odds panel (`OddsPanel`): the honesty line first, then "Set contents" when every capsule on screen is scripted, else the app's odds sheet (WP9's `OddsSheet`, passed in as `oddsSheet`; its own honest line is hidden there). The show holds still while the panel is open. (A6.4, A15.3, C5 #27)
- 2026-09-28 (app wiring, WP11 capsule parts): `src/app/capsules/capsuleFlow.ts` opens capsules with `meta.openCapsule`/`openWardrobe`, commits the save with `immediate`, then stores a `ShowRecord` (reveals, copies bars, new-card protection flag, onboarding step) under local storage key `ageborn.capsuleShow`, and only then starts the show. A reload finds the record and plays the same result again; closing the summary clears it (C5 #29). AppRoot renders only the capsule host while a show runs (it draws over the whole canvas), so both the onboarding capsules and the meta screens use the same host; `createMetaUi` gets `openCapsules`/`openWardrobe`, so the router's `capsule` route stays unused. Open next opens the oldest waiting capsule; Open all (shown when 2 or more wait) opens every waiting capsule in one show. Upgrade closes the show and opens the card's detail. (B8, A10, docs/requests/wp10-app-wiring.md)
- 2026-09-28 (onboarding capsules, WP11): at the `capsule1`/`capsule2` steps with meta, `showTitle` shows an empty title and AppRoot opens the waiting scripted capsule; `controller.finishCapsuleStep()` completes the step when the summary closes. The first scripted capsule equips its NEW cards (`meta.equipNow`, A8 and C5 #5 "Spear Hunter NEW, auto-equipped"); the summary then shows them as Equipped. Open next and Open all are hidden during onboarding. (A8)
- 2026-09-28 (Supply Capsule, WP7): `src/meta/supply.ts`. After a finished match is counted, `matchesPlayed % 3 === 0` in any mode but the tutorial turns one banked allowance into a Supply Capsule (a `capsule` reward step); none banked, nothing happens. The allowance cap is 7 (`accrueDaily`). Opening the second counted capsule grants the first Supply Capsule at once without using an allowance (so it is scripted capsule 3, as the old claimed Daily Capsule was) and starts the allowance at 0. Numbers come from `content.capsules.supply` (`matchesPerCapsule`, `allowanceMax`) once WP1 adds it, else the A15.4 values 3 and 7. `claimDailyCapsule` stays for tools. (A6.3, A15.4)
- 2026-09-28 (War Chest, WP7): `src/meta/warChest.ts`. `quests.weekly` is the chest: its `target` is wins per chest and its `rewards` are what it grants.
