A counting win (Ladder or Daily win; a Conquest win that earns a star or beats a General of tier ≥ skill tier − 2, skill tier = clamp(roundDiv(MMR − 870, 100), 0, 10)) adds 1; at the target the chest grants its crate (source `weekly`) and Age Capsule at once and restarts at 0. `refreshQuests` no longer resets the weekly slot, match progress no longer bumps it, and `claimQuest('weekly')` is refused (`warChest`). `ageCapsuleDue` now also answers for ladder results, so the app can ask for the chest's Age Capsule age. (A15.5, A15.9)

- 2026-09-28 (services, WP11): `DEFAULT_CHOICE` builds the real save store (`createBrowserSaveStore`) and the real meta rules; `?save=memory` and `?meta=none` keep the stand-ins. `systemClock` also gives meta the local time zone (`offsetMs`), so daily timers reset at local 04:00. Boot runs `meta.tickTimers` on a loaded save. The controller tests that cover the no-meta onboarding path build `{ save: 'memory', meta: 'none' }` explicitly. ## World art review pass (turrets, bases, backdrops, effects), 2026-09-28

- Turret mounts are the same four points on every 3D base (`WORLD_BASE_MOUNTS_LU`: (-6, 46),
  (-50, 112), (-8, 178), (-56, 246) lu), modelled as real platforms and returned by
  `AtlasBaseView.mountPoints()`. One layout for all ages means a base morph never moves a turret or
  a tap target. The procedural fallback bases keep their old mounts.
- Turret sheets are 1.7x the authored models (max about 72 lu); manifest anchors scale with them.
- World sheets use the unit v3 outline, filter and 2:1 supersampling.
- Backdrop far and mid layers and arena grounds are pre-rendered WebP images streamed on first use;
  the code-painted layers stay as the fallback. Sun rays halved, painted haze at the layer foot cut 30%.
- `FxRecipe.maxInstances` caps multi-count emits (fx.debris: 12) and `SpriteSpec.jitter` varies a
  sprite per play (explosion lobes).

## A17 step 1: the long lane in the sim, content, AI and tools (SIM_VERSION 2.0.0), 2026-09-28

- 2026-09-28 (lane): `LANE_MLU` is 2,000,000 (A17.2); `battle.laneLength` 2,000, `battle.midLane` 1,000, `economy.powerZoneClamp` [150, 1,850] and the Paratroopers fallback p = 1,000 follow it, in `src/content/raw` and in the frozen fixture `tests/fixtures/content` (the golden replays exercise the new rules). A content schema check asserts `laneLength × 1,000 = LANE_MLU`, `midLane = L / 2` and the clamp `[150, L − 150]` (A17.15 rule 1). Home rules keep their size (spawn 20, hold line 320, turret cap 480, Last Stand 450, Stampede fallback 200, `soloMaxP` 200). The tutorial view helpers, the bot viewer and the contract fake sim use the new lane; render still draws a 1,200 lu world until WP5 takes the lane from core (`docs/requests/wp5-a17-lane-from-core.md`). (DESIGN A17.2, A17.3, A17.15)
- 2026-09-28 (march speed): new `EconomyRules.marchSpeedBp` 12,500: unit speed in milli-lu per tick = trunc(speed × 50 × marchSpeedBp / 10,000), applied once in `src/sim/rules.ts` (Bonker 70 lu/s → 4,375 mlu per tick). The hold retreat (70%) follows. Knockback, pulls, leaps, projectiles and power runners are unchanged. The AI's safe-evolve forecast (`book.ts` speed) and WP1's counter duel engine (`DUEL_ENGINE_VERSION` 2, which also takes the lane from core) apply it too. (DESIGN A17.2, A17.15 rule 2)
- 2026-09-28 (Siege forced march): new `EconomyRules.siege.moveSpeedBp` 12,000, applied in `movement.ts` after slows and speed buffs while the phase is Siege, to ground and air units. (DESIGN A17.3, A17.15 rule 3)
- 2026-09-28 (formats in step 1): the formats and XP thresholds stay as A2.10 and A2.4 (Short 3 ages, Standard 4, Full 5, today's clocks); A17.8's 8-age formats and thresholds need the three new ages and arrive with step 2. The step 1 tool targets are therefore A2.14's for 5 ages plus the A16.5 Bell gates and the new A17.14 rows. (DESIGN A17.16)
- 2026-09-28 (stalls, A16.4 step 1 measured on the 2,000 lu lane): the tools got `--patch file.json` (a JSON content override for `balance` and `exploits`, workers inherit it through `AGEBORN_PATCH`) and the levers were measured as content fields on the tier VII Balanced mirror and the proxies (100 matches per row and format unless noted; Short / Full Final Bell share):
  - lane and march only (old AI): mirror 46% / 20%; mono Heavy wins 53% / 52%; turtle 50% / 22% with 99% / 100% of its matches at the Bell;
  - L5 Siege lethality (+40% damage taken): no measurable change (34% / 19% with the siege crowd off);
  - L6 the rope (loser 1%/s): mirror 32% / 10%, but the turtle drops to 0% (as A16.27 C1.5 predicted), so it fails the 35-45% band;
  - L3 melee closes to contact (with the siege crowd): mirror 28% / 15%, mono Heavy 73% / 59%;
  - L4 three-wide front: mirror 13% / 7%, mono Heavy 64% / 53% (old counter matrix);
  - a siege crowd (new, below): alone 50% / 32%; with L4 11% / 6%, turtle Bell 82% / 94%;
  - a gate crowd in every phase: base time to kill 166 s → 43 s (A2.14's 40-60 s), but Full War median 4:53-5:16 (A16.4 wants 6:00-8:00), so it is Siege only;
  - Siege decay 0.75%/s instead of 0.5%/s: no change.
- 2026-09-28 (stalls, adopted: two sim changes as A16.4 step 2 allows): (1) **L4, a three-wide front**: new `EconomyRules.frontWidth` 3, the first three units of a file stand side by side (A2.7 said two); (2) **the siege crowd**: new `EconomyRules.siege.gateCrowdLu` 60, in Siege a unit may stand level with the ally ahead once that ally is within 60 lu of the enemy gate, so an army at the gate hits the base with every unit instead of queuing in single file (the A2.7 file made the base unkillable while the defender kept spawning). L3, L5 and L6 are not built. Chosen by A16.4's rule: L4 cut the mirror Bell most and kept cheapest spam at 13% / 1% and random spam at 25% / 13% (without it 25% / 8% and 37% / 18%); the siege crowd lowers the turtle's and Heavy plus Ranged's Bell share. Content without the new fields (old fixtures, hand-built test content) falls back to these DESIGN values (`DEFAULT_*` in `rules.ts`). The counter matrix is regenerated (the duel engine models the three-wide front; e.g. Spear Hunter vs Tuskback 68% → 51%, still an answer: has-an-answer holds for every non-Legendary card). DESIGN A2.7 ("two-wide front") and A2.10 (Siege effects) need the merge. (DESIGN A16.4, A2.7, A2.10)
- 2026-09-28 (AI on the long lane, A17.13 retune): (1) Treasury only while no enemy is on the bot's own half (was "within 600 lu of the gate", half of the old lane; 24% of the new one let the bot buy Treasury with a Heavy wave at mid-lane): together with (2), mono Heavy vs tier VII 53% → 34% / 31%. (2) The push gate counts enemy units in their gate zone only after the 30 s opening, so freshly spawned units walking out do not make both bots Hold at the start: first clash 0:38 → 0:13 (A17.14 wants 0:11-0:16). (3) Hold without turrets against a one-type army (A16.3 rule 3 factor ×2, an 80% role-group share) worth 450+ gold while weaker, so the bot falls back to its hold line instead of trickling units across 1,000+ lu into Heavy spam: mono Heavy 56% → 42% (Short). A plain "hold without turrets when weaker" also helped mono Heavy (to 17%) but raised the mirror Bell to 58% / 40%, so it was dropped. A "save for a turret when enemies are at the gate" goal did not help and was dropped. (DESIGN A7.2, A7.3, A16.3, A17.13)
- 2026-09-28 (tool targets): `balance` gates the Final Bell at ≤ 10% Short and ≤ 5% Full (A16.5 v1 gate; was < 3%), adds the A17.14 first clash (median 0:11-0:16, gated) and the contact point between the turret covers (share of seconds, reported), and reports the turret share of kills only (A16.5). `MatchSummary` carries `firstClashTick` and `contact` (sampled once a second in `playJob`). (DESIGN A2.14, A16.5, A17.14)
- 2026-09-28 (version): `SIM_VERSION` 2.0.0; the 10 golden replays are re-recorded; `contentHash` snapshot 4b5e2fde, fixture f63c08d8 → 53d42a0d. Tutorial match 1 moves (Grogg falls at 2:29 instead of 2:59, one beat completes before it is shown): `docs/requests/wp11-a17-match1-retime.md`. (DESIGN B3, B13, A8)
- 2026-09-28 (measured result, release rule for the Bell rows): after the changes, tier VII Balanced mirror, 200 matches per format: Final Bell 31.0% Short / 24.5% Full (before A17: 30% / 16%; lane only: 46% / 20%; lane with the AI fixes: 60% / 33%), Full median 8:12, 70% of Full Wars in 5:00-9:00, Short median 4:57, first clash 0:13, contact between the turret covers 69% / 63% of the time, first evolve 1:37, first mover 45.8% [39.0, 52.7]. Proxies vs tier VII (200 per format, Short / Full): turtle 39.0% / 23.5% with 87% / 97% at the Bell, cheapest spam 12.8% / 0.8%, random spam 25.0% / 13.0%, mono Heavy 46.8% / 45.0%, Heavy plus mass Ranged 19.0% / 17.8% (Bell 68% / 81%), others ≤ 26%. Base time to kill 126 s (Stone) and 105 s (Future) outside Siege. The Bell gates (10% / 5%), the turtle's Full War band, the base time to kill and mono Heavy (≤ 35%; 24% / 17% before A17) stay open for Phase 3; per A16.5's release rule the Bell values are recorded here and go to the owner. ## A17 step 1: the scrolling camera, minimap and off-screen UI (WP5 with small WP4, WP9, WP11 edits), 2026-09-28

- 2026-09-28 (lane from core): `render/layout.ts` takes `LANE_LU` from core (`LANE_MLU / 1000` = 2,000), so the world is 2,360 lu (x -180 to 2,180) and gates, `pToX` and `xToP` follow (request `wp5-a17-lane-from-core.md` done). Device classes per A17.7: phone (height < 500: top bar 10%, min 36 px / band 68% / tray the rest, s = band / 290), tablet (width < 1,280: 12/64/24, s = min(w / 1,100, band / 330)), desktop (s = min(w / 1,400, band / 330)). On 844 × 390 infantry is 62 px, on 1280 × 720 also 62 px. The HUD's CSS follows the phone split with a container query (`@container hud (max-height: 499px)`). (DESIGN A17.7)
- 2026-09-28 (camera): `render/camera.ts` is rewritten to A17.4: zoom [0.8, 1.25] on every device (pinch, Ctrl + wheel), clamps with a 40 px rubber band (200 ms spring back), drag 1:1 after 10 px, swipe momentum (last 100 ms, ≤ 3,000 lu/s, e^(-4t), stop < 20 lu/s), wheel, ← / → (1,000 / 2,000 lu/s, 150 ms ramp), desktop edge scroll (fine pointer, 32 px, 300 → 1,200 lu/s; setting), H / Home and J / End, minimap tap (300 ms ease) and scrub. Auto-follow: ±8% dead zone, then an exact critically damped spring (350 ms half-life, capped at 900 lu/s × game speed); manual input yields at once, follow resumes after 5 s without camera input (not while a pointer is down, a mount popover is open, a power is dragged, the minimap is scrubbed or a tutorial beat holds it) with a 600 ms ease; the front button resumes it at once (350 ms). "Auto camera" Off: never resumes; the front button jumps once. Reduce motion: no momentum or rubber band, 150 ms eases, 200 ms half-life. A destroyed base locks the camera (500 ms pan, then the push); the own-evolve push runs only with the base in view, else the minimap base flashes gold. (DESIGN A17.4, A12)
- 2026-09-28 (follow focus, one deviation): A17.4's focus and framing are implemented as written (`seam.ts` `followFocus`, `framingCenter`, priority moments for your power zone ≤ 3.5 s, an enemy zone over your units, your Last Stand 1.5 s at your gate), except "only enemy units: the enemy front": while that front is still on the enemy's half the focus is the opening view. Otherwise the bot's first spawn at its gate flew the camera across the whole lane in the first second, away from the player's base and tray actions. Once the enemy crosses the middle the camera goes to it. (DESIGN A17.4)
- 2026-09-28 (minimap and badges): the minimap strip (`ui/hud/Minimap.tsx`) replaces the front-line strip (still the fallback when a bridge has no `minimap()`). It draws on a canvas at up to 30 Hz (A17.5 asks ≥ 10 Hz; 30 keeps the camera window smooth; ≤ 80 dots): territory, turret-cover brackets, gates, power zones (pulsing; your drag preview in white), fronts, unit dots (circles yours, diamonds theirs, 3/4/6 px phone and 4/5/7 px desktop, white ring for Legendaries, air row above), camera window. Bases are DOM medallions with an HP ring (red flash on hits, gold flash on an unseen evolve). The fronts are drawn under the dots so the frontmost unit stays visible (A17.5 lists them after the dots). The strip sits just under the top bar (10 px on desktop, 6 px on phones), where the front-line strip was; on phones the side panels shrink to 30 px and 26% of the width so the strip and its 32 px buttons clear the Evolve button. A17.7's "20 px row over a 16 px minimap row inside a 39 px top bar" was not reachable without cutting the Evolve button, so the strip overlaps the top 6-22 px of the phone's lane band, which is sky. Off-screen badges (`EdgeBadges`): base under attack (until 3 s after the last hit, `alert_base` at most once per 10 s), power incoming (countdown ring during the telegraph; card portrait), enemy Legendary spawned off-screen (4 s, hides once in view); 44 px (40 on phones), 35% down the band, ≤ 3 per edge, newest first; a tap jumps the camera there. (DESIGN A17.5)
- 2026-09-28 (acting off-screen): the mount popover holds the camera, follows its mount while the camera moves and closes once the mount leaves the screen. The base button carries a hammer badge while an owned mount is empty and the cheapest loadout turret is affordable. A power drag within 48 px of the band's left or right edge edge-scrolls the camera (up to 1,200 lu/s, touch too) and the preview stays under the finger (`previewedP`); a power can be dropped on the minimap (`minimapDropX` in `Tray.tsx`). Tutorial beats that point at a mount bring the base into view and hold the camera (`TutorialBubble.tsx`); match 2 adds one beat, "Drag the field or tap the map", pointing at the minimap at 0:20 for 5 s (new prompt target `minimap`). (DESIGN A17.6, A8)
- 2026-09-28 (settings): `Settings.autoCamera` and `Settings.edgeScroll` (optional, default true when missing, so no save migration) with toggles in Settings > Gameplay; `ViewSettings` carries them. (DESIGN A17.4, A17.16)
- 2026-09-28 (depth rows): the three-wide front shows its front rank at y = -12, 0, +12; the siege crowd (own p ≥ L - 90, after the front rank) spreads over seven rows (-20, 8, -6, 20, -26, 14, 0), each later round 4 lu further back (request of the sim agent). (DESIGN A2.1, A16.4)
- 2026-09-28 (rendering): units, auras and projectiles more than 150 lu outside the view are hidden (state still updates), bars and shadows skip them, and particles anchored off-screen are not emitted (base anchors and looping effects excepted). Seam per A17.3: start 1,000, clamp [700, 1,300], 300 lu blend, 30 lu/s drift, 2.0 s wipe (render `seam.ts` and visuals `WORLD`). (DESIGN A17.3, A17.7)
- 2026-09-28 (backdrop parallax, WP4 files): `ProceduralBackdropView.setView(left, width, above)` (duck-typed; the contract is unchanged) places the sky, far and mid layers at 0.05 / 0.25 / 0.55 of the camera movement, lowering a factor only when the 1,720 lu art is too narrow for it at that view width (never an edge on screen; a layer narrower than the view is stretched). Each layer's age split is re-cut every move so its seam stays at the ground seam's screen x. On short views (phones show ~275 lu above the ground) the far and mid layers shrink toward the ground line so the peaks stay in view. The ground gets a mirrored copy (and mirrored ambient) to cover the 2,000 lu lane. Lite now keeps the mid layer (it is the layer that shows the parallax; without it the phone lane was an empty plain), only its ambient life stays off. The atlas bake scale follows the new world scale (`screenWorldPxPerLu`). (DESIGN A17.7, B5)
- 2026-09-28 (not done in this step): sound panning and off-view fading (A17.7, WP6), the sharper 1.8 px/lu sheet tier (WP4), the spectator follow for replays and the minimap in the replay viewer (the replay HUD gets no view bridge yet), and the A17.14 camera e2e (WP12).

- 2026-09-28 (ages): `AgeId` gains `bronze`, `industrial`, `cosmic`; `AGE_ORDER` is Stone, Bronze, Medieval, Gunpowder, Industrial, Modern, Future, Cosmic with A17.8's P, base HP and thresholds (550 / 500 / 900 / 700 / 800 / 1,200 / 1,300 / none) in `raw/economy.ts` `ageScale`. ## A17 step 1 review fixes (render, HUD, sim, tools, tutorial), 2026-09-28

- 2026-09-28 (format ages in the HUD): `render/hudModel.ts` `ageOrder(config)` and `ui/hud/model.ts` `ageIds(config)` return the format's own age list (A17.15 rule 4: `ageIndex` is a position in the format), falling back to every age in index order for an unknown format. The tutorial skips Bronze, so its position 1 read as Bronze and the tray was empty after the first evolve. The battle view's "outdated turret" mark compares `AgeDef.index` of both ages. Test: `ui/hud/test/formatAges.test.ts`. (DESIGN A17.15, `docs/requests/wp5-a17-eight-ages.md` part 1)
- 2026-09-28 (stalls, the falling gate): new optional `EconomyRules.gateFall` `{ lu: 120, hpBp: 10000 }`: in Overdrive and Siege, a unit killed by an enemy unit, turret or ability within 120 lu of its own gate costs its base its max HP, dealt as base damage by the killer (Siege's ×2 applies; the killer earns base-damage XP; summoned units and power or Last Stand kills never count; never in the tutorial format). A decided match with the loser spawning into a parked army now ends instead of feeding bounties. Content without the field (the golden fixture) has it off, so the goldens are unchanged; `SIM_VERSION` 2.2.0 for the live content. Measured on the 8-age tree, tier V Balanced mirror, 200 matches per format (before → after): Final Bell Full 21.0% → 11.5%, Standard 16.0% → 10.0%, Short 9.0% → 9.0%; Full median 9:14 → 8:39 (target 8:30 ± 0:30, now passes), 46% → 55.5% of Full Wars in 6:45-10:15 (target 80%); Standard median 7:20 → 6:59, window 79% → 85.5%. Proxies vs tier VII (200 per format, Short / Full): turtle 16.3% / 8.3% with 74% / 98% at the Bell (review: 29% / 11%, 76% / 99%), Heavy plus mass Ranged 5.5% / 12.5% with 64% / 75% at the Bell (83% / 91%), mono Heavy 43.0% / 31.5%, random spam 25.0% / 17.0%, cheapest spam 6.8% / 0.0%. Variants measured and not adopted (100-200 matches): the falling gate in every phase (Full Bell 8%, but Full median 6:59 and random spam Full 23%), at half strength in regulation (median 7:14), 200 lu or ×2 HP (median 5:51-6:51); a gate crowd outside Siege when the defender has no ground unit within 480 lu (below). (DESIGN A16.4, A16.5, A17.14)
- 2026-09-28 (stalls, the open gate, built but off): new optional `EconomyRules.openGateLu`: while a side has no ground unit within that distance of its own gate, attackers close up at it as in the siege crowd in every phase. At 480 (or 100) lu it brings the base time-to-kill scenario from 104-128 s to 40-70 s (A2.14 wants 40-60 s) but shortens the Full War median to 7:19 and its window share to 34.5%, so the game content leaves it off; Phase 3 decides with the owner (a shorter Full War against the base time-to-kill row). Tests: `sim/test/gateFall.test.ts`. (DESIGN A16.4, A2.14)
- 2026-09-28 (stalls, what is still open): the Full War Bell (11.5%, gate ≤ 5%) and the turtle band (35-45%) still fail. The turtle proxy only ever holds, so its wins can come only from the Bell; A16.5's "35-45% and ≤ 50% at the Bell" asks it to win about 70% of its Bells and lose the rest outright, which no rule tried here does. In Full War the tier VII bot does not attack a 4-turret turtle until Siege (the push gate), so 98% of turtle matches reach the Bell; that is an AI change (WP3). The first evolve (1:12 vs 0:52) and Cosmic (5:43 vs 6:30) come from the 8-age content, not the stall rules. Per A16.5's release rule these go to the owner. (DESIGN A16.5)
- 2026-09-28 (tools): the balance mirror adds Standard War (median 6:30 ± 0:30 and 80% in 5:00-8:00 gated, A17.14; its Bell reported, as A16.5 gates Short and Full only); `writeCardsCsv` creates its `--out` folder. (DESIGN A17.14, B12)
- 2026-09-28 (pause): the app's pause panel is a compact card under the minimap (142 px from the top on desktop, 86 px on phones, one row there), with no scrim; the lane and the minimap stay live, so a paused player can drag, swipe or tap the map to scout (A17.4). WP9's meta Pause screen is requested (`docs/requests/wp9-a17-pause-scout.md`). (DESIGN A17.4)

## Owner feedback 2026-09-28 (evening), direction for A18

- The single-player heart of the game is a **War Path**: a level map (Candy Crush style) of battles you beat one by one against ever cooler AI opponents. It must be obvious from the very first minute: the War Path is the centerpiece of Home, the training match is level 1 on it, and the next level is always one tap away. Other modes (Quick Battle, Daily, ladder) sit around it.
- Harder and longer: difficulty levels in single player, slower evolving and longer ages, matches that last longer, and an AI that punishes a player who only sends a few units.
- More ages (inspired by the classic sequels), and in-battle upgrades with real choices: troop stats and abilities, turret range, income.
- The Charge/Hold stance is available from the start.
- Troops must look and move much cooler (an art style exploration is running; the owner picks a direction).
- Online multiplayer is the final goal; single player and the architecture must lead there.
- **Card class and counters visible (owner, 2026-09-28 evening):** every unit card (battle tray, War Plan, Collection, card detail, capsule reveal) shows its class with a clear role icon and label (for example Infantry, Ranged, Heavy, Anti-armor, Siege, Support, Air, and Legendary), using the existing roles and tags (A2.6). The card detail and a long-press/hover tooltip show "Strong vs" and "Weak vs" from the compiled counter lists, and the War Plan builder shows a simple counter triangle legend. Colours are colourblind-safe and never rely on colour alone.
- **Age Power targeting (owner, 2026-09-28 evening):** using the special must feel obvious: the player drags the power from its button out onto the battlefield. Drag is the primary, taught interaction; tap starts an aiming mode (then tap the field) instead of firing blind. Clear affordances: the ready button invites dragging, a large ghost of the power's area follows the finger with a valid/invalid tint and the units it would hit highlighted, the camera edge-scrolls while dragging, dropping on the minimap works, releasing over the HUD cancels.
- **More classes, more historical ages, six troops (owner, 2026-09-28 late):**
  - Add a **flying class** and an **underground class** as real unit classes (air units already exist per A2.6; make "Air" a full class with its own counters, and add "Underground" tunnelers that bypass the front and fortifications, with their own counters), shown with class icons like the others.
  - Brainstorm and then implement **more historical ages**. The owner explicitly wants Bronze Age (exists), **Ancient Egypt**, **Ancient Greece** and other historical eras (for example Rome, Vikings/Norse, Samurai/feudal Japan, Renaissance, Napoleonic, World War eras). A18 must propose the full age list and the order in which to build them.
  - **Six troops per battle:** a loadout has 6 unit slots (was 5), and the battle tray shows 6 cards.
  - **Equipping cards must be intuitive:** War Plan becomes a clear deck builder (drag or tap a card into a slot, see instantly which cards are equipped per age, filters by class, counters and advisor visible).
- **Art realism (owner):** the current look is "too crude / not realistic enough". The art style exploration must include a more realistic direction; the owner chooses.
- **Stationary class (owner, 2026-09-28 late):** add a stationary class of structures the player places on the lane (the A16 fortifications idea: walls, barricades, traps, bunkers, shields per age), as real cards with a class icon, placed by dragging onto your own half of the lane. A18 must specify rules, anti-turtle limits and AI use, and schedule it early.
- **Capsule opening (owner):** must be much more satisfying (a capsule-show polish track is running).
- **Many cosmetic collections (owner, 2026-09-28 late):** besides troops and troop skins, players collect **emotes**, **quotes** (curated one-liners), **base flags**, **base skins**, **base decorations/attributes** and **national flags** for the base (Denmark, England, USA and many more, drawn in our own style; country flags only, no political or hate symbols; the player's pick is never inferred from location). All are earned (capsules, Wardrobe Crate, Trophy Road, War Path, feats, events), have rarities, show in Collection with completion counts, and are equipped in the Customize screen; the chosen base cosmetics and flag show in battle for both sides and on the VS screen. A18 must add the content tables, drop tables and the Customize screen design.
- **Art direction decided (owner, 2026-09-28 late): ultra realistic.** The target is an ultra-realistic look (realistic proportions and anatomy, physically based materials, realistic lighting and weighty, natural motion), not the stylized chunky cartoon of A11. The heroic, gritty and toon explorations were stopped; the realistic exploration continues and becomes the base for restyling all units, turrets, bases and backdrops. Readability rules still apply at in-game size (silhouette, team colour on large parts, outline or rim light). Free CC0 photoscanned materials and models (Poly Haven, ambientCG, Quaternius) would help a lot but their hosts are blocked by the environment's network policy until the owner allows them.
- **Owner delegation (2026-09-28 late):** the owner asked the orchestrator to plan the work and continue through the night without asking for permission. Open owner decisions are taken by following the recommendations in the design documents and logged here as "Decided by orchestrator (owner delegated)". Plan: `docs/night-plan.md`.

## Owner feedback 2026-09-28: Home as the hub, early unlocks, difficulty

- 2026-09-28 (Home from match 2 on): after the training match and capsule 1 the player lands on Home (not the title). While onboarding step `match2` is next, Home's Battle button says "Next: Pip Quickstep · AI" and starts onboarding match 2 through VS (`MatchRequest { mode: 'tutorial', match: 2 }`); the app still plays its onboarding battle, result, capsule 2 and the forced upgrade, then returns to Home. `bootRoute` sends a reload at step `match2` to Home. (DESIGN A8, A9 flow; `app/onboarding.ts`, `app/metaUi.ts`, `ui/screens/home/parts.tsx`)
- 2026-09-28 (entries always visible): Home's bottom row is War Plan, Collection, Capsules, Customize, Trophy Road and Conquest. Capsules opens a sheet with the capsule tray (with an "Open Wardrobe Crate" button when only crates wait), so it works on phones too. War Plan, Customize, Skirmish and Quick Battle open after match 1 (`WAR_PLAN_UNLOCK_MATCHES` 1, `STAGES.warPlanAfterMatch` / `skirmishAfterMatch` 1; A8 had "after match 3"); a locked entry says "Unlocks after the training match". (DESIGN A3, A8)
- 2026-09-28 (first-time pointers): one short line (at most 8 words) sits above one unopened entry at a time, in the order War Plan, Collection, Capsules, Customize, Trophy Road, and goes away for good once that entry is opened (`SaveDoc.flags['ui-pointer.<entry>']`, set through the new `UiServices.setUiFlags`, which only writes `ui-` keys). (DESIGN A8)
- 2026-09-28 (Customize screen): new WP9 route `customize` (tabs Troops, Bases, Banner & title, Emotes), numbered 19 after the A9 table. It reuses the Collection's skin tiles (equip, craft with Dust, crate only) and the profile look fields (banner, frame, title, with how each locked one unlocks); emotes are listed (all are available). Base skins show an empty state while the content has none. Nothing can be bought. (DESIGN A5.8, A6.1, A9)
- 2026-09-28 (welcome crate): winning the training match also grants one Wardrobe Crate (`PendingCrate.source` gains `'welcome'`, contracts and the save schema; still SaveDoc v1), so a new player can open something from Home and equip a skin in Customize right away. It is rolled at grant time like every crate (B8) and staged on the result. (DESIGN A6.5, A10.1)
- 2026-09-28 (stance and Last Stand): the Charge/Hold flag is there from match 1 with one hint; the manual Last Stand button from match 2 (A8 had matches 4 and 5). (DESIGN A2.10, A2.11, A8)
- 2026-09-28 (A7 difficulty): Quick Battle (a new first card on Mode select) and Skirmish pick one of five difficulties, each an AI tier: Easy II, Normal IV, Hard VI, Expert VIII, Legendary X (`generals.difficulty`). The picker shows the tier and the AI badge. Quick Battle is a Short War Skirmish (5 Amber per win, no trophies or capsules) vs the first ladder General whose tier range holds the tier (Easy Pip, Normal Kettle, Hard Ledger, Expert Twins, Legendary the Warden). The Skirmish tier slider is replaced by the same picker. Default Normal; the last pick is remembered in `SaveDoc.flags['ui-difficulty.<id>']` and shared by both. The title's dev Quick Battle panel has the same picker. (DESIGN A7, A9 #3)
- 2026-09-28 (A6.8 Rookie AI): the new-player mistake bonus applies only in the onboarding matches (`arenas.ladder.newPlayer.matches` 2, was 20) and never in a match whose difficulty the player picked (Quick Battle, Skirmish); the "Rookie AI" disclosure follows it (A7.1, A15.3 unchanged). The ladder keeps its matchmaking. (DESIGN A6.8, A15.3)
- **A18 decisions D1-D8, decided by orchestrator (owner delegated), all per recommendation:** D1 slower ages yes; D2 match lengths about 7, 10 and 15 minutes; D3 the Treasury is replaced by the War Council's income choices; D4 new ages wait until the realistic restyle works on the Stone Age, then the target is 13 ages (including Japan); D5 Conquest folds into the War Path, keeping earned stars; D6 three stances (Charge, Hold at the flag, Fall back); D7 Legendary difficulty is smarter play only, no hidden or extra AI advantages; D8 accept that old replays stop playing after the SIM_VERSION 3.0.0 bump (results are kept).
- 2026-09-28 (AI strength, WP3/WP12): measured first with `tools/sim-cli.ts strength` (30 matches per tier × strategy × format at L7, draws half; adjacent tiers head to head, 30 per pair). Before: tiers VIII and X already beat every simple scripted strategy (cheapest spam, a few soldiers then evolve, balanced, rush) 88-100% in every format, and the 4-turret turtle 82-95% (mostly on Final Bell margins); tier II lost to the skilled Save-and-counter proxy (0-3% AI wins), so "too easy" comes from the tiers a new player met (0-III plus Rookie AI), not from the top tiers losing to simple play. The weak spots were the top: the tiers were not ordered (VIII vs VI 53-57%, X vs VIII 48-60% head to head), and a trace of tier X showed it trickling units into held turret lines (charge/hold flapping on the push gate), never buying Treasury against a passive foe (the push-gate wave spent the gold first), building no turrets without pressure, and almost never casting its power (the A7.3 threshold is Stone gold: 450-600 gold in one zone). (DESIGN A7.2, A7.3)
- 2026-09-28 (AI craft from tier VI, `TierParams`): four fair-play rules on the same delayed observation, off below tier VI so tiers 0-V are unchanged (A7.3 as written, the balance mirror at tier V too). `waveCommit`: a wave starts only with a 15% margin over the push gate and keeps charging until it has lost half its peak value (no trickles, no flapping). `econPlan`: a Treasury goal is not spent into a gate wave, and against a passive foe (no enemy ground unit on the bot's half for 20 s) the bot buys a level in regulation after 3:00 too while it pays back within 4:00 (levels 1-2). `powerArmyShareBp` (VI 50% → X 30%): the power also fires on a zone holding that share of the visible enemy army. `baseTurrets` (VI 1, VIII+ 2): from Bronze on the bot keeps that many turrets (mount included) on spare gold without waiting for pressure. D3 replaces the Treasury later; `econPlan` then moves to the War Council's income choices. Tried and dropped: holding a Siege lead for the Bell and wave-only Siege play (both raised Bell rates to 90-100% against the skilled proxy). (DESIGN A7.3, A18 D3, D7)
- 2026-09-28 (AI strength, after): the same run (`strength --matches 30 --pairs 30`): head to head VI vs IV 68/75/80% (Short/Standard/Full), VIII vs VI 73/67/78% (before 57/55/53%), X vs VIII 65/67/65% (before 60/57/48%); all ten adjacent pairs now reach the 60% bar (before 5 failed). Tiers VIII/X vs simple strategies: cheapest spam 93-100%, few then evolve 100%, balanced 83-100%, rush 93-100%, turtle 82-97% (Standard 82%, mostly Bell margins: no unit outranges a turret, so a pure 4-turret turtle can only be beaten on base damage in Siege); vs Save-and-counter 58-92% (Short 58-68% with ~90% Bells). Tier II unchanged (0-3% vs Save-and-counter). The mean over strategies is saturated at VI-X (85-96%), so "rises with every tier" fails by noise at VIII in Standard and X in Full. New tests: `ai/test/brain.test.ts` (upper-tier craft) and `ai/test/tierOrder.upper.test.ts` (X vs VI Standard 62.5%, VIII vs VI Full 60.8% at 60 matches). Open: the skilled proxy in Short War and the turtle are stalemates decided at the Bell; a real counter needs content (siege cards in the bot's deck, A16 fortifications) or the A16.4 open-gate rule, not the AI. (DESIGN A7.3, A16.5)

## Base upgrade moments (WP4 visuals, WP5 render feel), 2026-09-28

Owner: "The base upgrade animation must be much more satisfying."

- 2026-09-28 (evolve build-up): new render action `baseAscend` on `ascendStart` (both sides) calls the duck-typed `AtlasBaseView.ascend(ascendMs)`: the base trembles harder toward the beat (u², up to ~2 lu × age power; none with Reduce motion), 7-10 crack lights leak at its lights, ledges and centre, the windows burn up to 2.4×, the body glows in a quickening pulse, energy motes rise from a glowing footprint and dust trickles off; the last 12% squash down in anticipation. A build-up with no `ageUp` after it lets go 3 s past its end. (DESIGN A11 evolve, A12)
- 2026-09-28 (evolve beat): `morphTo` now slices the old body frame into a grid of shards (4-6 × 5-7 by age; 3 × 3 on Lite; one fading piece with Reduce motion) that burst away, biased away from the lane, while the new body assembles from 4-5 bands that drop in bottom-up, each landing with a squash, a white edge flash and corner dust; then the base settles with a spring, flags unfurl from their tops, the Treasury pops back, and a gleam, window pulse and star twinkles close it. A core glow, a ground shockwave, an air ring, sparks (pale warm; mint / lilac for Future / Cosmic) and rubble in the old age's palette mark the beat. Power per age 0.78 + 0.075 × index. Timings scale with the morph's `ms` (1,800 reference). If the next sheet is still loading the build-up holds at its peak (≤ 3 s), as before. The contract `BaseView` is unchanged; `ascend`, `mountBuilt` and `setMotion` are optional duck-typed hooks (the procedural fallback base ignores them). (A11, A12)
- 2026-09-28 (evolve camera): the own-evolve push holds `ascendMs + 1,400 ms` (was + 700) so it stays in through the beat and assembly (`EVOLVE_PUSH_AFTER_MS`). The existing global 100 ms hitstop, trauma 0.4 and 120 ms white flash on `ageUp` are the beat's camera side; no second push (a new `pushTo` restarts from zoom 1). (A12, A17.4)
- 2026-09-28 (Treasury): a live Treasury rise bursts 12 coins (6 Lite) out of the prop and rains 10 (5) back in, each absorbed with a small gold glint; the new level pops in with squash and stretch about its foot and a golden glint. Your own `treasuryUp` pops "+1.5/s" (new number kind `income`, one decimal plus "/s"; the number font gains "/" and "s"). The state a base view is created with never plays a moment (first update gate). (A12)
- 2026-09-28 (new slot): new render action `baseMount` on `mountBought` calls `mountBuilt(i)`: a ledge slides out of the wall with an overshoot, 6 blocks (4 Lite; metal plates with sparks from Industrial on) fly in on arcs and set with dust, a ring and twinkle close it, then the overlay fades into the modelled ledge. (A2.8)
- 2026-09-28 (turrets): the build drop is stretched over a growing shadow, lands with a squash that springs back, a white impact flash, dust, sparks and three bolts set one after another. Modernise: the old turret glows white, squashes and pulls up into light (no more sink); the new one (`modernisedIn()`, duck-typed; render calls it on `replace`) grows out of a light orb with a spring, ring, sparks and motes. Reduce motion keeps fades, drops squash and drop. Every turret and base gets `setMotion({ reduce, lite })` from `BattleView.applySettings` and on creation. (A2.8, A12)
- 2026-09-28 (budget): the moments add at most ~90 view-owned sprites per base for ~2 s (Bits cap 90, turret 40), all sharing the effect and sheet textures (batched); sliced shards and bands are sub-textures of the loaded sheets (no new uploads) and are freed at the end. (B16)
- 2026-09-28 (dev page): `?dev=1#baseup` is a base upgrade bench (evolve, Treasury, new slot, build, Modernise; `window.__baseup` steps and snaps frames deterministically).

## Card classes and counters visible (owner feedback 2026-09-28), 2026-09-28

- 2026-09-28 (class mapping): `src/core/cardClass.ts` `unitClass(u)` derives one player-facing class from content (no new data): any unit with the `air` tag is Air (Balloon Admiral, Gyrocopter, Repair Drone, Mothership); otherwise infantry and skirmisher → Infantry, ranged → Ranged, heavy and siegeHeavy → Heavy, antiArmor and antiMech → Anti-armor, siege and artillery → Siege, support → Support. Legendary is a crown marker on top of the class; turrets and powers show Turret and Age Power. Test: `src/ui/components/test/cardClass.test.ts` (every unit of all 8 ages maps to a class, every class is used, each age has at least 4 classes). (A2.6)
- 2026-09-28 (Strong vs / Weak vs as classes): `counterClasses` turns the compiled `strongVs` / `weakVs` into classes; a class named on both lists goes to the list with more of its cards and is dropped on a tie (12 of 56 units had such an overlap). The card lists stay on the card detail below the class rows. (B4)
- 2026-09-28 (icons): one glyph per class, distinct in shape (sword, bow, shield, spear through a plate, lit bomb, cross, wings; crown, tower, bolt) on a cel-shaded disc in Okabe-Ito based colours. The path data lives in core (stroke widths in tenths, B3 bans float literals there) so the UI (`ClassIcon.tsx`) and the capsule show (`capsule/classBadge.ts`, Pixi `Graphics.svg`) draw the same shapes. Labels are `ui.class.*`.
- 2026-09-28 (surfaces): the badge and label are on every `CardTile` (War Plan slots and grid, Collection, card detail counters, Pause scouted, Profile, Result), the battle tray cards, the HUD Scouted drop-down, the card detail stage and chips, the capsule card fan and the walkout banner. Card tiles show a hover (desktop, 350 ms) / long-press (touch, 450 ms, swallows the click) tip with class, Strong vs and Weak vs; the HUD card info shows the same rows. The War Plan shows a "Who beats whom" legend (the A2.6 triangle plus Air beats Infantry, Ranged beats Air, Siege beats Turret) and advisor warnings about a missing class show that class's icon. The legend is the design's triangle, not the counter matrix: the matrix is cross-age duels and does not show Anti-armor beating Heavy on average.
- 2026-09-28 (HUD power targeting): drag is the primary Age Power gesture. The input rules are a pure state machine (`src/ui/hud/powerAim.ts`, idle / pressed / dragging / aiming) that `PowerButton.tsx` feeds with pointer and key events. A press on the ready button becomes a drag after 12 px; a drop over the lane or the minimap fires with `p`, a drop over the tray or top bar (their boxes), a cancelled pointer or Escape cancels; a pointer released outside the window keeps the last lane point. A tap on a ready power no longer auto-aims: it enters aiming mode (the ghost starts over the enemy front, 35% of the zone beyond it; a field tap fires, a second button tap or Escape cancels); Space and a keyboard click on the focused button still auto-aim. Powers that pick their own spot use the same gestures and cast without `p`; their ghost shows where they act (Stampede: from the frontmost own ground unit, or p 200, for its distance; Paratroopers: 150 lu beyond the enemy front, 220 lu wide; Royal Decree and Nanite Surge have no ghost). The ghost is drawn by the view at world scale (team colour when valid, red with a cross when a drop would cancel) and rings the enemy units inside the zone (±¼ of their size; ground only unless the power hits air). A drop plays `ui_confirm` and an 18 ms vibration; pick-up `ui_click` and 8 ms; a cancel `ui_toggle`. The first-time hint ("Drag onto the battlefield!" plus an animated hand) shows once per profile (localStorage `ageborn.hud.powerDragHint`, also set by the first drag), not in the onboarding matches (`callouts` off), and waits while a tutorial bubble points at the power. Sim commands are unchanged. (DESIGN A2.9, A2.12, A9.2, A17.6; request `docs/requests/wp11-power-drag-hint.md`)

### Card classes: UX review fixes (2026-09-28)
- Class counter rows (tile tip, tray info, card detail icon rows) drop the unit's own class and any class that would contradict the War Plan "Who beats whom" legend. The raw duel lists compare a card with bigger same-class cards from adjacent ages, so 18 of 56 units read "Heavy ... Weak vs Heavy" and 6 contradicted the legend. The card-by-card lists on the detail screen are unchanged.
- Card detail no longer shows the raw role pill next to the class chip ("Heavy" + "Siege heavy" read as two clashing classes), nor a second "Turret"/"Age Power" pill.
- The Collection role filter is now a class filter ("All classes", Infantry ... Air, Turrets, Powers), so it matches the badges on the cards.

## Capsule opening polish (WP10), 2026-09-28

Owner: "the capsule opening animation must be much more satisfying". Everything stays within A10's honesty rules: the result is fixed before the show, the tier colour and the rarity glow are the only pre-signals, and a non-climb strike never teases a climb.

- 2026-09-28 (burst build): the burst step is now a build, a 70 ms freeze frame and the explosion. The build grows with the tier the climb has already shown (Clay 200, Bronze 260, Silver 360, Jade 560, Aeon 820 ms), plus a 360 ms pop; the A10 limit for the burst is 1.2 s (was 0.3 s). The drum swells, rattles harder and pours light from its cracks while motes are sucked in; Jade and Aeon also darken the room. The pop: white flash, three shockwave rings (a gold one for Aeon), radial light streaks, shards, the halves, embers that drift up through the card reveal (Bronze and up) and a confetti fountain (Jade, Aeon). `BurstStep.buildMs` marks the pop. (A10 step 4, A12)
- 2026-09-28 (strikes): every strike cracks the drum further (the charge leaves 30% of the cracks, each strike opens a quarter of the rest) and light beams pour out of the crack tips, whether it climbs or not. Every strike kicks the drum on a spring, throws warm white sparks (never the tier colour, so a non-climb never teases one), drops crumbs, and gets a short hitstop and zoom punch; the non-climb clunk rises in pitch strike by strike (+900 bp) under a soft `hit_heavy`. Climbs keep the A10 colour step, flash and +0.25 trauma. (A10 step 3)
- 2026-09-28 (arrival): the drum falls stretched from higher up and lands with a squash, trauma 0.42, a zoom punch, a dust cloud and ring along the floor, stone chips and a pedestal dip; `step_heavy` under `cap_thud`. (A10 step 1)
- 2026-09-28 (cards): cards fly out of the burst on high arcs with one full spin and sparkle trails and land with a pop and a floor ring. The honest pre-signal grows with the rarity (Common 260, Rare 380, Epic 640, Legendary 700 ms; A10 limit 0.8 s, was 0.3 s): Rare and up tremble harder and harder, Epic and Legendary draw streaks of rarity light in and darken and tint the room. The flip lands with a snap (white flash over the card, springy pop, a ring and sparks in the rarity colour, trauma, a short hitstop; Epic and Legendary flash the screen) and `flare_pop`; the copies badge counts up with ticks (Common 220, Rare 300, Epic and Legendary 360 ms, only with 2+ copies). The flip step lasts the flip plus the longest of foil and stamp, count-up and settle (Common 120 ... Epic 300 ms). The NEW stamp slams with a ring and sparks; UPGRADE READY pops with a green burst. (A10 steps 5, 7)
- 2026-09-28 (walkouts): the bass drop adds a 90 ms hitstop, a zoom punch and 650 ms of 0.3× slow motion for the effects (never with Reduce motion); god rays and a halo stand behind the unit from the drop on; the name banner slams down from 2.6× with a shake, dust and `upgrade_slam`. The NEW Epic mini-walkout gets rays behind the unit and a smaller name drop. (A10 steps 5, 6)
- 2026-09-28 (summary and Amber): summary cards drop into the collection with a spin and a bounce; NEW cards get a light sweep and a glow pulse; NEW and UPGRADE READY badges pop. The Amber counter now appears and pours after the pop, not when the burst step starts. (A10 steps 4, 8)
- 2026-09-28 (Reduce motion): no shake, zoom punch, card spins or slow motion; hitstops halved; particle counts cut to about a third; the summary animations finish at once. (A12)
- 2026-09-28 (sounds): dedicated ids are requested (`docs/requests/wp6-capsule-juice-sounds.md`); until then the plan layers existing ids, which the plan test now allows.
- 2026-09-28 (timing): a Bronze capsule with 3 cards plays in about 9.5 s with instant taps (was about 8.8 s); hold to fast-forward and Skip are unchanged.
- 2026-09-28 (review pass, centre stage): every fan card now comes forward to centre stage for its pre-signal and flip (spring, slight overshoot; Common 1.6×, Rare 1.72×, Epic 1.88×, Legendary 1.98× of the design card) while the rest of the fan dims to half, then flies back into its slot with a small landing pop and ring. Before, cards flipped in place at 0.94-1.2× and the reveal read small, especially with 5-6 cards. No step timings changed; the wardrobe card stays above its crate. With Reduce motion the move is a plain ease with no lean. (A10 step 5)
- 2026-09-28 (review pass, NEW silhouette): the NEW silhouette uses the plate-less portrait (`plate: false`, as the collection does), so it shows the unit's shape against the card's sky instead of a black box, and the colour fill starts a beat later with a bright line riding its front. (A10 step 5)
- 2026-09-28 (review pass, walkout): the Legendary hero stands 1.25× taller (UNIT_H 300 → 375 design px); the mini-walkout is unchanged.
- **Online server: Cloudflare Durable Objects instead of Colyseus (decided by orchestrator, owner delegated):** the local spike in `server/` works (lockstep relay, hash checks, reconnect, server re-sim, hostile-client tests). Colyseus needs an always-on Node host with no reliable free option. When the free daily limit is hit, players get an AI General match (no paid plan). Deployment waits for the owner's free Cloudflare account. See `docs/online-spike.md`.

### Age Power drag: UX review fixes (2026-09-28)
- Tap-to-aim brings the ghost on screen: when the camera is away from where the ghost starts (after a cancelled drag that edge-scrolled, a minimap jump), `powerAimStart` eases the camera there (`jumpTo`, stays Manual) unless the ghost's centre is already 120 lu inside the view. Before, a tap could show "Tap the battlefield to fire" with the ghost out of view. (A17.4, A17.6)
- Powers that pick their own spot say where they act, since their ghost does not follow the finger: the drag token and the aiming chip show "Charges from your front line" (Stampede, the default Stone Age power every new player drags first), "Lands behind the enemy front" (Paratroopers) or "Boosts all your troops" (Royal Decree, Nanite Surge). Strings `hud.powerAim.auto.*`.

### Base upgrade: game-feel review in a real battle (2026-09-28)
- Evolve shards now fly mostly up and only a little toward the back (`atlasBaseView.beat`): the base sits at the world's end, so shards biased backward left the screen at once in a real battle; up-and-over arcs stay in view and clear the lane as they fall.
- Turret build: the code landing squash is 5% when the sheet's `build` clip has its own landing frames (they already squash about 25%), 28% only for single-frame sheets; stacking both flattened turrets into a pancake.
- New slot: the sliding ledge gets a dark underside and a warm top edge so it reads against a grey wall.
- Open for the owner: the evolve push-in only runs when your base is in view (A17.4). In autopilot battles the camera usually follows the front, so the whole base upgrade happens off-screen and only the banner and the minimap flash show.

## Cosmetic collections (A18.9.4), 2026-09-29

- 2026-09-29 (content): the collection tables live in `src/content/raw/cosmetics.ts` (DESIGN A18.9.4), joined to `content.cosmetics.collections` with the drop tables, wheel sizes and defaults. 24 collected emotes (2 per age, 8 general; the six A5.8 emotes stay the free starters), 40 quotes (4 starters, at most 40 characters, checked for insults by a test), 15 base flags (2 starters), 50 national flags (country flags only, all from the Time Capsule pool so every flag is also craftable; none at the start; a player's flag is never inferred), 10 base skins (at least one per age) and 24 decorations (5 kinds; 2 starters). Sources: `start`, `capsule`, `crate`, `road` (a claimed Trophy Road node), `feat`, `arena`, `codexLevel`, `warPath` (shown as "coming later"). Strings are in the new `src/i18n/cosmetics.en.json` under `cosmetic.*`. (DESIGN A18.9.4, A16.28, A6.2)
- 2026-09-29 (keys and wire): an item's key is `<collection>.<id>` (`nationalFlag.gb_eng`), stored in `SaveDoc.cosmetics.owned` next to banner and title ids; the art id is `cosmetic.<collection>.<id>` (A14.4). `EmoteId` widens to the six starters plus `emote.<id>` and `quote.<id>`, so collected emotes and quotes ride the existing `emote` command (replays, relay, bot replies unchanged); the sim accepts exactly the starters plus the content's emote and quote ids (`rules.emotes`), the replay schema and the online room accept the same pattern. (DESIGN A2.12, A18.9.4, B15)
- 2026-09-29 (save v3): `cosmetics.equipped` (emote wheel ≤ 8, quote wheel ≤ 4, base flag, national flag or null, base skin per age, one decoration per anchor) and `rng.cosmetic`, a separate stream seeded from the capsule stream, so a cosmetic roll never changes a capsule's cards; `CapsuleContents.cosmetic` and `PendingCrate.cosmetic` are optional. Migration v3 adds the starter loadout (frozen in `migrations/v3.ts`). (DESIGN B8, A18.9.4)
- 2026-09-29 (drops, disclosed on the odds sheet): a Time Capsule holds one collection item with 8/12/20/35/60% by tier (Clay to Aeon; none in script and Age Unlock capsules), rarity Common 68%, Rare 26%, Epic 6% (no Legendary in the capsule pool); every Wardrobe Crate holds one item next to its skin, Rare 70%, Epic 25%, Legendary 5%. Unowned items of the rolled rarity first; a duplicate only when all are owned, paying 5/20/60/150 Dust. Capsule and crate items can be crafted for 40/150/500/1500 Dust. Road, feat, arena and Codex items are granted by state after a match result and a road claim (`syncEarnedCosmetics`). (DESIGN A6.4, A6.5, A15.3, A18.9.4)
- 2026-09-29 (lane look): `SideConfig.look` (presentation only; the sim ignores it) carries each side's base flag, national flag, base skins and decorations; the player's shows only owned items, an AI's is seeded by its General id and never has a national flag. `ArtProvider.createBaseDressing` (optional) attaches a dressing to the base view: one pole 44 lu in front of the gate with the base flag over the national flag (strip-mesh ripple, fixed at the hoist, harder flutter after hits; both sides fly with one wind so a flag is never mirrored), decorations at −150, −104 and +20 lu (never over mounts or the HUD HP bar), and the base skin as a body tint through the duck-typed `setSkinTint` (the team layer keeps its colour, A11), a trim-coloured pole cap and ambient particles; everything topples on a collapse; Reduce motion stills it. A base skin restyle is a tint plus ambience for now, not new sheets. (DESIGN A11, A18.9.4, B5)
- 2026-09-29 (UI): Customize has seven tabs (Troops, Bases, Flags, Decorations, Emotes, Quotes, Look) with compact icon tabs; the base tabs show a live mock-up of the base; every collection shows "N/M found"; the Collection's Skins tab shows the completion strip with a Customize button. The UI draws cosmetic art through the injected `CosmeticArtContext` (the app provides the visuals' SVG). The VS screen shows both sides' flags. The HUD wheel lists the equipped emotes and quotes (also on phones), quotes wait an extra 8 s (`quoteCooldownMs`), and "Mute opponent" hides the opponent's emotes and quotes for the match (on top of the Settings switch). After a capsule or crate show, a toast names the collection items it held (WP10 request for a proper reveal card). (DESIGN A9.2, A18.9.4)
- 2026-09-29 (review: cosmetics): checked against the owner's request, A15 honesty and A18.9.4; fixed what was wrong. Flags: South Korea had the taegeuk on the wrong diagonal, the trigram bars along instead of across their diagonal, and gam/ri swapped (now geon upper left, gam upper right, ri lower left, gon lower right, bars facing the centre); the Union Flag (also in Australia's and New Zealand's cantons) had the lower-hoist and upper-fly red diagonals centred on the white instead of counterchanged (now a pinwheel); Australia's Southern Cross stars have seven points; Portugal's shield has all five quinas. The other 45 flags were compared with a reference and are right. Tests hold Korea and the Union Flag. The Customize base preview and base-skin tiles showed a code-drawn castle for every age while the lane tints the age's real base, so the preview now draws the age's own base portrait with the skin's tint multiplied inside its silhouette and its particles on top (`cosmeticSvg(key, { layer: 'tint' | 'fx' })`, UI `BaseLook`); base-skin tints are stronger so a skin reads at lane size. Equipping now answers in the preview: a new flag is hoisted up the pole, a new decoration drops in with squash and stretch, a new skin re-enters with a gleam (Reduce motion stills all). Trophy Road nodes show the collection items they grant (with the item's kind), so the Collection's "Trophy Road at N trophies" is visible where it is claimed. National flags have a country search. (DESIGN A15.1, A18.9.4)

## A18 phases 0-3: contracts, content, sim, meta, save and tools (pacing, windows, War Council, stances, six slots), 2026-09-29

- 2026-09-29 (one contract bump): `SIM_VERSION` 3.0.0 and one golden re-record (10 replays on the frozen fixture, which now carries a frozen copy of the research table and exercises research, Hold flag and Fall back). Replays from 2.x load through `LegacyReplayDocSchema` so their results stay listed; they no longer play (D8). Fixture hash 786da415, live `contentHash` 67c9912c. (A18.13 phase 0, B3)
- 2026-09-29 (formats as windows): `FormatId` is an open string. Named `short` / `standard` / `full` are the 3-, 5- and 7-age windows from Stone; `short.<age>`, `standard.<age>`, `full.<age>` start later, and `w1` / `w2` / `w4.<age>` are the other lengths for the War Path. `content/raw/economy.ts` generates every window that fits (never past the last age) with the A18.3.4 clocks per length and a 60 s retreat; `FormatDef.kind` names the family. Thresholds follow the window position, not the age (`xpToNextOverride`). A window that starts later starts the base at that age's P and HP. `src/meta/formats.ts` holds the window helpers for meta and UI. Full War now ends at Future, so `futureReached` (the game's last age, Cosmic) needs a window that reaches Cosmic. (A18.3.4, A2.10)
- 2026-09-29 (XP and bounty, A18.3.2-3): passive 5 XP/s, kill XP 70%, own loss 50%, 8 XP per 1% base damage, bounty 50%, Overcharge and the final-age cap 1,650. Thresholds by position were tuned with the pacing run from 700 / 1,250 / 1,350 / 1,450 / 1,550 / 1,650 to **620 / 1,300 / 1,580 / 1,800 / 1,850 / 2,000**, which puts the tier V Balanced mirror's stays at 1:13 / 1:38 / 1:45 / 1:47 (Standard). The tutorial's overrides were retimed to [610, 580, 390, 900] so match 1 keeps its A8 beats (Grogg falls at tick 2,945).
- 2026-09-29 (War Council model): one research slot per side; a pick is an index into `content.research.picks` (28 v1 picks: Economy I-II, Defences I-II, Troops I-II for Infantry, Heavy, Anti-armor, Support and I for Ranged, Command I). Prices 150 / 300 / (500 | 450), times 10 / 14 / 18 s, cancel refunds 75%, underdog −20% (behind in age position, or 20+ points of base HP). Ranks open by window position: length 1 [0], 2 [0,1], 3 [0,1,2], 4-5 [0,1,3], 7 [0,2,4]. The two picks of a rank exclude each other. Unit effects are snapshot on the unit at spawn (A18.2 rule 2: research applies to units spawned after it completes); turret, economy and command effects are recomputed on completion and apply at once. Fire Arrows and Pierce (no damage-over-time status yet), every rank III and Command II-III wait for v1.1. (A18.5.1-5)
- 2026-09-29 (class mapping until the Air and Siege lines exist): skirmisher → Infantry, artillery and air → Ranged, siege and siege heavies → Heavy, anti-mech → Anti-armor; Epics and Legendaries by their base role (`research.classOfRole`). (A18.5.2)
- 2026-09-29 (caps, A18.2): every stacked bonus goes through `capSum(fixed, buff, cap) = min(max(cap, buff), fixed + buff)`, so research never pushes past a cap but an existing card value above it is kept; damage taken never below 35% (Mail's flat reduction included; a hit never drops below 1); turret range min(card + research, 560 lu). First-hit bonuses need a fresh engagement (`firstHitIdleMs` 4 s since the unit last fought).
- 2026-09-29 (stances, A18.4): Charge, Hold (flag p clamped to 320-800 lu, snapped to 20 lu, 1 s between flag moves) and Fall back (to p = 200 at full speed); stance cooldown 3 s. The Hold flag and research are in the observation (`holdP`, `research`, `ages`) for the AI and HUD.
- 2026-09-29 (six troop slots, A18.9): `Loadout.units` has 6 entries. Save v4 adds an empty sixth slot to every stored loadout; `meta` fills new sixth slots once, behind the `meta-troop-slot-6` flag (best owned card of a missing class, else empty). Treasury is gone as a control: the HUD's income button now researches the next Economy income pick, and "Win without Treasury" counts wins without Economy research (`usedTreasury` keeps its contract name). (A18.5.4, A18.9)
- 2026-09-29 (sideMods and victory rules for the War Path): `SideMods` scales base HP, adds a fifth mount and unit stats per side; `VictoryRule` `survive` (the defender wins at the tick) or `target` (a marked enemy turret; every base hit also damages the mark, reason `objective`). (A18.7 data hooks only; no War Path screens)
- 2026-09-29 (AI, A18.5.8): bots research from tier-based start times (90 / 60 / 45 / 30 s) with gaps of 90 / 65 / 50 / 45 s by tier, a random, hinted or counter pick policy by tier, and each General's research bias. The flag placement and a smarter Council policy are later phases.
- 2026-09-29 (meta pacing): quest "final age before" 3:00 / 6:45 / 11:30; "destroy a base before" 7:30; Evolver = the Full War's final age before 17:30, Speedrunner before 10:00; Lightspeed = Future before 9:00 in a Full War. The economy player model uses the measured evolve times. (A6.7, A5.8, A15.10)
- 2026-09-29 (tools, A18.12): `balance` gates Short / Standard every run and Full at gates, with the A18 medians (7:00 / 10:30 / 15:00), bands, Final Bell caps (10 / 8 / 5%), stays per position, the winner's evolve lead, research share (15-25%) and items (5-8); `exploits` adds `eco_greed` as the Economy-first proxy, `drill_rush`, `tech_turtle`, `flag_ball`, `fallback_turtle`, `stance_toggler` and the `no_research` / `few_then_evolve` rows.
- 2026-09-29 (pacing result, open): tier V Balanced mirror, 200 matches per format: Short median 8:10, Final Bell 46.5%; Standard 10:34, 67.5% in 8:30-12:30, Bell 24.5%; Full 14:52, 58.5% in 12:00-17:00, Bell 0%. Stays and research share are on target in Standard; Full stays 3-6 run 5-10 s long. The Short and Standard Bell rates miss A18.12 (10% / 8%); at tier VII they are 78% / 63%. Tried without success: no research, the A17 XP sources, a wider open gate; a faster Siege decay (0.75-1%/s) removes the Bell but shortens Standard to ~10:10. Left for the A16.5 release rule and the next balance pass (Siege decay or the Supply Cache), not tuned blind here.
- 2026-09-29 (Council badges): `ResearchPickDef.visualId` is optional until WP4 draws `research.<id>` badges (`docs/requests/wp4-council-badges.md`). Pick names and one-line effects are in `content.en.json` under `research.*`.

A tap flips to the line's two picks with "or" between them (at most two decisions on screen): badge, name, one-line effect, price (list price struck out under the discount), time, and a state tag (Owned, Not chosen, Opens in <age>, One research at a time, Need 30). First tap selects ("Tap again to research"), second starts; the badge flies on an arc into the button, "-150" floats from the gold. (A18.5.1, A12)

(A18.5.7)

## A18 phases 0-3: AI use of the War Council, stances and pacing (WP3, with WP12 tool fixes), 2026-09-29

- 2026-09-29 (research by tier, A18.5.8): tiers 0-I pick at random, weighted by the General's research style; II-IV score each pick's `aiHint` against what they see (pressure, visible enemy classes, busy or quiet lane); V+ use counter scoring (plus remembered composition from VII, Rook also the Scouted list, counters × his counter weight). Every tier adds the class's share of its own army (research follows the classes it fields) and deepens open lines. Push picks match on "my army is bigger" only for counter tiers; for II-IV only as a style (aggression ≥ 80). Matching them on the moment made tier IV buy Lightfoot and War Horns and lose 49% → 35% to the researching reference player. Enemy composition is shared by War Council class (`classOfRole`), so Legendaries count in their base class. (A18.5.2, A18.5.8)
- 2026-09-29 (research filters): no new item with less than 90 s to the Final Bell; Defences only with a turret up (or Mama Moss, whose style plans them); Ambush never at a tier that never Holds.
- 2026-09-29 (income timing follows the clocks): the Economy income picks are bought on a quiet lane before 3/5 of the Overdrive time and only if they pay back by a minute into Overdrive. That is Short 3:00 / 6:00 (unchanged), Standard 4:48 / 9:00 and Full 7:12 / 13:00. The bot reads the clocks from the public age window (`matchClock`). (A18.3.4)
- 2026-09-29 (research timing, A18.5.8 "uses enemy research"): V-VI ease the push gate × 0.8 for 20 s after their own Troops item lands. VII-X also strike (× 0.9) while the enemy's Troops item is past half, wait (× 1.15 for 15 s) after it lands, and do not evolve while that fresh wave is on their half (capped by the evolve-delay column). The timed gate never drops below 0.8 × D. An "evolve edge" gate (push while a whole age ahead) was tried and had no measurable effect, so it was dropped.
- 2026-09-29 (stances, A18.4.2): Fall back from tier V needs three things: the army is under 0.5 × the enemy's, the enemy front is past mid-lane and within 200 lu of the turret cover, and the match is not in Overdrive or Siege. The literal rule (anything past mid-lane) added 10 points of Final Bells to the tier V mirror (Standard 26% → 36%, 600 matches); the narrower rule gives 29.5% and keeps the win-rate gain (tier VI beats IV 82%, up from 75% without Fall back). The Hold flag moves from tier III (0-II never move it). When defending it sits just inside the turret cover (400 lu), or at the default without turrets. While gathering a wave it goes to the value-weighted centre of the own ground units, capped at mid-lane − 200 lu and 200 lu short of the nearest enemy. With War Horns it stays at p ≤ 480. A flag move is its own action (1 s cooldown, no stance cooldown). The over-commit mistake (Hold → Charge) is offered only after 15 s of holding.
- 2026-09-29 (thin army, A18.6): from tier IV (Normal) the bot pushes when its army is ≥ 1.5 × the enemy's, worth ≥ 250 and ≥ 300 per enemy turret, whatever the push gate says. Result: `few_then_evolve` wins 2.5% (Short) and 8% (Standard) vs Normal, and 0% vs Hard.
- 2026-09-29 (waves): a committed wave (`waveCommit`, tier VI+) also ends once it is worth less than the defence D it faces. Charging on below D fed the defender, and tier VI lost 65% of Standard Wars to tier V; now VI wins 55-64%.
- 2026-09-29 (Generals' styles): Rook reads the Scouted list (`researchScouted`). Ledger's Guildhall and Tempest's Reserve Charge are rank III (v1.1) and join their styles with those picks. Measured picks (Standard, 40 matches each): Kettle took Rush in 31, never Forage; Moss researched Defences first; Ledger took Granary and Market in 40 and 38; Boomsworth took Ranged Weapons in 33 and Signal Fires in 32; Tempest took Signal Fires in 40 of 40.
- 2026-09-29 (tools, match length cap): the headless drivers (`tools/lib/jobs.ts`, `tools/lib/driver.ts`, `src/ai/harness.ts`) stopped matches at 20,000 ticks (16:40), before Full War's Final Bell at 17:30. Those matches counted as draws and timeouts, not Bells. The cap is now 24,000. The earlier "Full War Final Bell 0%" was therefore wrong: with the cap fixed, the baseline AI reached the Bell in ~20% of tier V mirror Full Wars.
- 2026-09-29 (open, AI strength): above tier VI, head-to-head separation is weak. VIII beats VI 57-59% and X beats VIII 52-58%, with 78-92% of those matches at the Final Bell. Upper-tier mirrors stalemate in the final age of the window because the defender reinforces over a short walk. Ablations of every tier column (reaction, mistakes, counter depth, memory, safe evolve, float) found no single cause. The fix belongs with the Final Bell pacing work (Siege decay or the Supply Cache), not with more AI rules.

## A18 phases 0-3 review (rule check, tools, browser play), 2026-09-29

- 2026-09-29 (review verdict): checked every phase 0-3 rule of A18 against the code: XP sources, bounty, thresholds by window position, window clocks and later-start base HP, the one research slot, prices, times, 75% cancel refund, underdog discount, rank unlocks by window length, exclusive pairs, never-retroactive troop research, the A18.2 caps and the −35% damage-taken floor with Mail, the 560 lu turret cap, Economy income outside Overdrive, the three stances, flag clamp and snap, 3 s stance and 1 s flag cooldowns, engagement freshness, `sideMods`, victory rules, six tray slots, research hashing, AI tier columns and styles, HUD sheet and stance control. They match, with the gaps below, which were fixed.
- 2026-09-29 (fixed, six slots in the War Plan): the War Plan screen padded and trimmed every loadout to 5 unit slots (`ui/screens/model/plan.ts` `UNIT_SLOTS`), so the sixth troop could not be set and any edit dropped it. Now 6; on phones the six slots show as two rows of three (70 px cards), below 1,240 px wide as one row of 80 px cards. The screen fixtures and the tutorial's tray scan read six slots too. (A18.9)
- 2026-09-29 (fixed, research compatibility marks, A18.13 phase 3): the War Plan board shows the five War Council Troops lines under the unit slots, lit when the age's loadout holds a unit of that line (by `research.classOfRole`, so Epics and Legendaries count in their base class) and dimmed with a slash otherwise, each with an accessible label ("Heavy: no unit in this age"). (A18.5.2)
- 2026-09-29 (fixed, Field Care): "heals and shields +20%" now also raises the shield of a Support's shield aura (Starwarden); only heals were raised. Goldens unchanged (the fixture has no Support shield aura), so no re-record; a sim test holds it. (A18.5.2)
- 2026-09-29 (fixed, tutorial match 2): the income beat ends when the research starts (`researchStarted`), not 10 s later when it completes, and waits 20 s instead of 12 s for the three taps; text "Tap your gold, then Granary twice" (`docs/requests/done/wp11-council-tutorial.md`). (A18.5.4, A8)
- 2026-09-29 (Online M1, A18.10 "M1 with phase 3"): CI installs Firefox and runs the golden-replay determinism spec in it as a third engine (`playwright.config.ts` project `firefox`, determinism spec only; locally with `PW_FIREFOX=1`).
- 2026-09-29 (measured, tier V Balanced mirror, 200 per format, current AI): Short median 8:30, Final Bell 52%, research share 14.9%; Standard 11:37, 82% in 8:30-12:30, Bell 38.5%, stays 1:14 / 1:40 / 1:43 / 1:51 (all in band), 9 research items (target 5-8), share 16.1%; Full 14:50 median but only 22% in 12:00-17:00 (bimodal, 100-match sample: a third end at 4-8 min by a destroyed base, mostly with one side an age ahead in the 3rd-5th age; a quarter end at 17-17:30), Bell 20.5%. Items and share pull against each other (fewer items drop the share under 15%), so the AI research gap was left alone.
- 2026-09-29 (measured, Bell levers as content patches, Short / Standard Bell): Siege base damage ×3 50.5% / 37.5%, ×4 49% / 36%; Siege turret damage ×0.25 54.5% / 36%; falling gate ×3 42.5% / 33.5%; decay 0.75%/s 34.5% / 0% (Standard bases then always die of decay before the Bell, which only renames the Bell). Nothing that keeps the Bell meaningful moves it much: the stall is before the gate, not at it. Not adopted; it stays with the owner check and the Supply Cache (A18.3.5) or the A16.4 L1/L6 levers, and the A2.14 release rule applies.
- 2026-09-29 (measured, exploits vs tier VII, 100 per proxy and format, Short / Standard / Full): no_research 20 / 13 / 10% (≤ 30%), few_then_evolve vs Normal 2.5 / 8 / 5% and vs Hard 0 / 0 / 1%, flag_ball ≤ 16.5%, stance_toggler no gain, eco_greed and drill_rush ≤ 14.5%: pass. Mono Heavy 44 / 44 / 42% (≤ 35%) fails, as before these phases. The turtle, tech_turtle and fallback_turtle win 1.5-14.5% (band 35-45%, the safe side); fallback_turtle reaches the Bell in 63-70%.
- 2026-09-29 (browser play, production build): autopilot vs Normal won a Standard War at 11:52 by destroying the base in Siege. A scripted player through the HUD only (trains whatever it can afford, evolves at once, researches through the Council, uses Hold, the flag arrows and Fall back) beat Normal in Standard at 6:10, lost to Normal in Short at 6:41, and lost to Hard in Standard at 4:03 without scratching its base: Normal is beatable by steady play and Hard is a clear step up. No console errors on desktop 1280 × 720 or phone 844 × 390.

## UI-0 Foundations and UI-1 Blockers (ui-plan 6.2, 6.3), 2026-09-29

- 2026-09-29 (tokens): `src/ui/theme.css` defines every token of ui-plan 3.2-3.4 and 5.2 (`--ui-bg`, `--ui-surface-1..3`, `--ui-primary|progress|secondary|destructive(-light, -lip)`, status, reserved colours, `--ui-fs-*` and `--ui-lh-*`, `--ui-s1..s7`, `--ui-r-*`, `--ui-e1..e5`, `--ui-dur-*`, `--ui-ease-*`, the standard transforms). The integer source is `src/core/motion.ts` (`docs/requests/wp0-motion-tokens.md`); `tests/integrity/motion.test.ts` proves parity. The old names (`--ui-panel`, `--ui-gold`, `--ui-muted`, `--ui-dim` ...) stay one phase as aliases onto the new palette, so every screen moved to the neutral slate at once; `--ui-dim` now points at `--ui-text-3` (4.5:1). Violet and blue button faces are gone. (ui-plan 3.2, UA-09)
- 2026-09-29 (breakpoints): compact, short and large are container queries on the UI root (`container: ui-root / size` on `.ui-root` and the app's `.ab-root`), not media queries, so the dev galleries (a fixed frame inside a bigger window) and the real phone measure the same thing. (ui-plan 3.1)
- 2026-09-29 (reduce motion): the 1 ms rule is replaced. The setting or the OS preference switches the tokens (small, medium, large to 150 ms; press and release to 0; press scale, lift, bump, pop, axis offsets to none), so token-based motion becomes a fade with no scale; everything not yet on tokens runs once in 150 ms (never a loop, never a 1 ms jump). The ScreenHost reads `prefers-reduced-motion` too. (ui-plan 5.6, U14)
- 2026-09-29 (Button): one `Button` with `kind` (primary, progress, secondary, tertiary, destructive) and `size` (xl, l, m, s, icon). `variant` and the old sizes still map (gold = primary, green = progress, red = destructive, ghost = tertiary, others = secondary) for one phase. The pressed state is set on `pointerdown` (`data-pressed`), the release overshoots through `--ui-ease-back`; `data-primary` is set on an enabled gold primary (or any kind with `primary`), `data-pulse` with `pulse`, `data-clip-check` on the label. A disabled button with a `reason` stays tappable (`aria-disabled`) and pops the reason next to it with a shake and `ui_deny`. The pulse breathes the face through the `scale` property, so the hit box never moves (a moving box also broke Playwright's stability check). (ui-plan 3.6, U1, U3, MR-01, MR-03, MR-05)
- 2026-09-29 (shell): the router gains tabs (`TabId`, `switchTab`, `jump`, `leaveTabs`, `tab`): one kept stack per tab, back on a tab root goes to War Path, a cross-tab jump returns to its origin. `ScreenHost` takes an optional `shell` (`tabs`, `roots`, `bar`) and then draws the bottom `TabBar` on tab roots only, with keys 1-5. It is opt-in: no screen uses it yet, because Home keeps its own nav row until UI-2 rebuilds Home on it. Browser and Android back go through `bindHistory` (one trap entry; sheets register `pushBackHandler`; battle back pauses; at Home the first back shows "Press back again to leave"), wired in `MetaHost`. (ui-plan 2.2, U7)
- 2026-09-29 (stray motion): all 83 `cubic-bezier(` literals outside `theme.css` and `motion.css` were mapped to the six easing tokens (with a keyword fallback outside `src/ui/screens` and `src/ui/components`, so dev pages that do not load the theme still animate). The rule is a test, not an ESLint rule (CSS is not linted). (ui-plan 1.3, UA-23)
- 2026-09-29 (Card detail, UI-1): Upgrade lives in the fixed action bar, bottom-right, and takes two taps (`useConfirmSpend`, `src/ui/components/confirm.ts`): "Upgrade · 400 / Lv 5 → 6", then "Confirm · 400" (with "Leaves N" when it costs more than half the Amber), a tap elsewhere or Esc cancels, and the button stays blocked 600 ms after a spend. Disabled it says what is missing in its second line. Craft and Craft copy use the same two taps. The success toast is gone: MR-39 plays on the card (Amber flies in, 300 ms tremble, burst and hammer, the level flips, the stat rows tick with "+N" chips); a tap on the stage skips it. (ui-plan 4.4, U10, UA-03, UA-08)
- 2026-09-29 (Result, UI-1): one design for every mode (`ResultLayout`, `resultBar`), the primary chosen by `resultActions` in `ui/screens/model/result.ts` (the 4.9 table; War Path rows are ready for UI-2's `warPath` mode, Conquest follows them). The onboarding Result keeps the app's controller logic but draws this design (`src/app/ui/ResultScreen.tsx`; test ids `next`, `retry`, `play-again`, `home`, `watch-replay` kept). The stopping cards no longer carry their own Home button (Home is the bar's primary then). After a capsule opened from the Result, the Result continues its own path at once (Continue or Next battle) and the capsule summary's primary says so (`doneLabel`), so the summary never returns to the Result. (ui-plan 4.9, 2.5, UA-10)
- 2026-09-29 (capsule summary, UI-1): exactly one primary (`summaryButtons`: Open next while more wait, else Done, or the Result's path when opened from the Result), "Equipped" is a flat state pill, copies bars never read over-full ("Upgrade ready" instead of "5/2"), 44 px targets. (ui-plan 4.6, UA-18)
- 2026-09-29 (Pause, UI-1): Resume is the gold primary (56 tall on phones) at the bottom-right of an action bar; Settings beside it; Retreat is small and red at the far left (a locked Retreat says why on tap). VS keeps its 2 s auto-start, reads "Tap to start now", and Esc on VS cancels to Home. Mode select is one sideways-scrolling row of cards (no 4 + 1 grid, no clipped bodies): all 11 picker buttons pass the `elementFromPoint` hit test at 844 x 390, 844 x 340, 800 x 360 and 1280 x 720. (ui-plan 4.7, 4.8, UA-21, UA-01)
- 2026-09-29 (budget spec): `tests/e2e/ui-budget.spec.ts` measures settled states (no finite animation and no `[data-anim]` for 100 ms) at the four viewports. Strict (must pass): the component gallery (`?dev=1#components/<vp>`), Result (win, loss, no capsule), Card detail and Pause; it also holds the UA-01 hit test and the reduce-motion fade check. Home, Mode select, War Plan, Collection, Customize and Settings report their violations as annotations (the UI-2 to UI-4 backlog) until their phase makes them strict. Card names wrap between words and may use 8 px beyond the tile, never an ellipsis; the card class word and the AI chip are tags (11 px bold). (ui-plan 1.3)
