- 2026-09-29 (Level preview and Modes, S2a, S2b): the level sheet shows the General with the AI badge and tier, objective, ages, rules, the boss row, the star goals (the ★★/★★★ captions once beaten or from level 5), the first-clear reward, the difficulty (remembered per save, `setWarPathDifficulty`), a tip for what the level teaches, and "Edit army" (a cross-tab jump). Locked: "Beat level N first" and "Go to my level", no primary. The Modes panel has Quick Battle (difficulty inline), Ladder (level 6), Daily (level 7), Skirmish ("Set up" opens Mode select) and Conquest while it exists; "All options" opens the full Mode select.
- 2026-09-29 (Result): a War Path Result shows the level badge whose new stars stamp in, and the ★★ goal with whether it was met; a card reward row; the progress step is the region's stars; after 3 War Path losses in a row the secondary "Try Easy" sets Easy and retries (A18.7.4).

## UI-3 Battle HUD and UI-5b battle feel (ui-plan 4.7, 5.8, 6.5, 6.7b), 2026-09-29

- 2026-09-29 (HUD layout, UI-3): the HUD follows the 4.7 budget with size tokens on the `hud` container (desktop 56 / 128, phones 44 / 94; cards 88 × 116, 62 × 84 on phones, 72 × 96 from 900 px, 56 × 76 below 820 px). Top band: your block (age icon, HP 14 px with the percentage at 12 px, the XP bar with "XP 180/250" beside it; the "You" tag is gone), the clock (with "Overdrive in 0:45") and the emote button in the centre, the AI block (AI chip, name, HP, XP, age icon inside their power ring, horn, a small research ring on the medallion), then Scouted (a 72 × 44 chip, an eye and the count), pause and speed (36 px faces in 44 px hit areas). The minimap row hangs under the band (24 px strip, 44 px hit, 44 px base and front buttons). Tray: the left cluster (gold and income on one line, "Army 44/60" under it; the round Council and **Evolve** side by side, Evolve moved from the top bar to the left thumb, UA-05), six card slots (empty loadout slots are quiet sockets so nothing jumps between ages; the scripted first match keeps its own cards and padlock), the stance button, the power. The Fort slot is not drawn until forts ship. Everything is on the UI tokens (slate panels, gold = go, green = progress, red = denied). (ui-plan 4.7, 2.9 #4)
- 2026-09-29 (cards, UI-3): cost top-left, class icon top-right, the name at the bottom in up to two lines between words; a word wider than the card is condensed horizontally to fit (never an ellipsis; `fit.ts` does the same for the power name and the Council and Evolve tags). A clock-wipe training fill, the gold "affordable in 4s" fill, the queue badge (flips on change). **Train on release**: a card trains when a press ends within 450 ms and moved under 8 px; the pressed look is on `pointerdown`; the long-press ring starts after 150 ms and at 450 ms the card's tip opens and nothing trains. The tip (hover after 350 ms on desktop, a long-press on touch) shows class, Strong vs / Weak vs and, while the card has a queue, **"Cancel one"**: the touch alternative to A2.12's long-press cancel, which the long-press can no longer do (U10: a long-press never spends or refunds by surprise). Right-click and Backspace still cancel. The 24 px "i" corner is removed. A train pops the card (1.06), flashes it and floats "-50" from the gold. (ui-plan 4.7, MR-65, MR-07, UA-06, UA-24; DESIGN A2.12 cancel row needs the lead)
- 2026-09-29 (deny reasons, UI-3): every denied press says why next to its control for 1.5 s (MR-03): "Need 40 gold", "Queue full", "Legendary in field" (checked in the sim's order), "Need 120 more XP", "Charging: 64%", "Ready in 2s"; the sim's own rejections map through `simDenyReason`. The flash is red on the face, a ±4 px shake, `ui_deny` and a deny haptic. (MR-03, MR-67)
- 2026-09-29 (stance, UI-3): one 56 px button with the current stance's icon and name replaces the three-segment control; press-drag-release opens a flyout of the three stances stacked upward (48 px, with a one-line hint each), release on one to choose; a tap opens it for a tap; a tap elsewhere or Escape closes it. S and Shift+S unchanged; the 3 s wait shows as a sweep and an early choice says "Ready in 2s". (ui-plan 2.9 #5, MR-71)
- 2026-09-29 (one pulse, UI-3): the HUD breathes at most one thing (`hudPulse`): a tutorial target (the battle layer's `data-tut`), then Evolve, then the power, then the minimap hammer (a new mount). The others rest in a steady glow; affordable cards glow and never move; the Manual-camera Follow outline is steady. The power's READY tag (or its name while charging) sits inside the ring. Evolve stays dark and denies for 2 s after your evolve (UA-07). (U11, MR-68, MR-69, UA-14)
- 2026-09-29 (world framing, UI-3): the HUD reports its insets (`HudViewBridge.setHudInsets`: the top band with the minimap, the tray with the safe area); `screenLayout(w, h, insets)` puts the ground line at most 12 px above the tray (phones move it down to the tray) and shrinks the scale when needed so the tallest unit plus its pips (240 lu) stays under the top band; at 844 × 340 that zooms out instead of cropping. Camera pushes never lower the ground line. (ui-plan 3.1)
- 2026-09-29 (evolve staging, MR-80): your evolve frames your base from anywhere (`Camera.frameMoment`: a 500 ms pan and the push, at most 3 s, then follow picks the fight up again or a Manual camera eases back), unless you moved the camera in the last 2 s or your front is fighting within 300 lu of the camera centre; then the minimap base flashes gold. Any camera input (a drag, keys, a minimap press, a power drag, the base or front button) ends it at once. The evolve screen flash is warm white at 35% for 120 ms (was white 85%). (ui-plan 2.9 #9, #10)
- 2026-09-29 (reduce motion, HUD): the HUD mirrors the app root's `data-reduce-motion` onto its own root, so the Settings switch (not only the OS preference) turns its motion into fades and glows through the tokens.
- 2026-09-29 (battle feel, UI-5b): the rendered (atlas) units get realistic weight by mass class (light, medium, heavy from role and height): the spawn is a short drop and settle with a ground-toned dust ring (MR-100; the old 0 → 1.15 → 1 scale pop squashed the body), a hit flinches the body back 5 / 2.5 / 1 lu and returns with one small overshoot in 140-200 ms (MR-103; before, only a white flash), a death lies 600 ms after the fall, then sinks 3 lu and fades over 300 ms with dust (MR-105; the body used to vanish at the clip's end; `deathLingerMs` 900 → 1,700; a sheet's hand-off earlier than its clip's end still hides the body). KO stars read as cartoon over the realistic art and become a dust puff (the decision UI-5b owed A12). Death and spawn dust is multiplied toward the ground's tone at about 60% instead of a white cloud. Turrets keep the barrel kick; the body recoil and the landing settle are 3% (5.8: props at most 3%). No sim timing changed. Audit: `docs/research/battle-feel-audit.md`.

## UI-4 Army and Card detail (ui-plan 4.2, 4.4, 6.6), 2026-09-29

- 2026-09-29 (Army, S9): the `warPlan` route is now the Army tab screen (route id kept). Left: the selected age's loadout, always visible (four troop slots over two troops and two turrets, the Age Power beside them on phones; six troops in one row on wide desktops), with the War Council lines it can use and the advisor's first warning (tap for all, with the counter legend). Right: "This age" (the age's troops, turrets and powers) or "All cards" (the album of all 104 cards with "78/104"), a Filter panel (the 7 classes plus Turret and Power, rarity gems, owned only, sort by level, rarity, cost or ready to upgrade) and removable filter chips. Equipped grid cards carry a green check and a green underline, upgrade-ready cards the green arrow, unowned ones a silhouette and padlock. The Fort slot is not drawn until forts ship (as in the HUD). (ui-plan 4.2, U4)
- 2026-09-29 (Army gestures): tap a card = select it (it lifts, the slots it fits glow green, a small bar offers Use, Info and Upgrade when copies are ready); tap a glowing slot to place it. Tap a slot = select it (Info, Remove; the grid narrows to what fits, shown as a "Troop cards" chip); tap a card to place it there; tap another slot of the same kind to swap. A card drags after 8 px, on touch only when the move starts within 35 degrees of horizontal (a vertical swipe scrolls the grid); slots drag onto slots (swap) or onto the grid (remove). The shared behaviour is `src/ui/components/drag.ts` (ghost, valid targets, 24 px snap, return, click swallow, flights, sparks) for forts and the power later. A card of another age says "Bronze card: switch to Bronze" on its Use button; an unowned one "Not found yet". (ui-plan 4.2 gesture matrix, T5, T6)
- 2026-09-29 (Army rules, `model/plan.ts`): Use follows A3 "Equip now" (first empty slot, else the lowest-level card of the same class, else the lowest-level slot). Only reached ages show (the War Path regions so far, every age of the ladder formats once the ladder is open, all for a legacy save), plus a locked "More ages" that says how they open. Each age tab has a status mark: green check (full, no advice), amber "!" (advice or a gap), red "!" (not playable). Presets A/B/C appear after the first boss as a header button ("Rush ▾") with a panel; the army you look at is the army in use (choosing one also makes it active), which removes "which plan am I editing" confusion. One header Undo keeps every change of the visit and reverses them one at a time; Auto-fill shows one line ("Auto-filled 4 slots"). The per-slot red ×, the Skins button and the plan-average line are gone (skins live in Card detail and Customize). (ui-plan 4.2, U10, UA-11)
- 2026-09-29 (Army motion): select lifts 1.06 and pops the bar (MR-30); a placed card flies on an arc from the grid (pull-back 60, flight 300), lands with a 0.94 / 1.03 / 1 squash, a rim flash in its rarity colour and 6 sparks, `card_place` and a thump (MR-32); the card it replaces flies back to its grid spot (MR-34); swaps cross (MR-35); Auto-fill and Undo land slot by slot 60 ms apart (MR-36); the loadout flips in on an age change (MR-37); filters reorder with FLIP (MR-38); the average level counts (MR-20); NEW cards wobble once and Legendary frames get one light pass. Reduce motion: no flights, the slot cross-fades. (ui-plan 5.5)

New props `equipped` and `showName`. The card CSS moved from `theme.css` to `components/cardTile.css`. `AgeGlyph` gained Bronze, Industrial and Cosmic glyphs (they drew nothing before). Pause's Scouted row uses md tiles so long names fit. (ui-plan 3.5, 3.6)

The right column leads with Strong vs / Weak vs as class icons plus the counter legend's sentence, then the stats as a two-column grid with the next level's green deltas, traits, description, skins, the card-by-card counters and foils. The action bar holds Show odds (tertiary, the odds panel), Craft copy and Use (secondary; "In army" as a flat pill, Use has an Undo toast) and the two-tap Upgrade (UI-1's `useConfirmSpend` and MR-38b / MR-39 kept, now on the lg card). `route.upgrade` opens it already armed (Army's Upgrade). ## Owner request 2026-09-29: more capsule tiers

Owner: "Til kapslerne mangler der flere niveauer, tilføj f.eks. guld, platinium, så de øverste kapsler bliver virkeligt sjældne og eftertragtede." The lead designer scored three proposals (economy 21.0, desire 18.5, reveal 17.5 of 25, on the owner goal, economy stability, honesty, phone clarity and build cost). The economy proposal is the base. The ladder order and exclusives come from desire; colours, rings, crests and sounds from reveal. A critic then reviewed the spec (6 major, 14 minor issues); the entries below are the revised decisions, and the review round at the end lists what changed. The spec lives in DESIGN A6.3, A6.4, A6.5, A6.9, A9, A10 (follow-ups in A6.2, A13, A15.4, A16.18, A17.13, B8, B13, B15, C5) and in the per-package requests `docs/requests/capsule-tiers-wp0.md`, `-wp1.md`, `-wp6.md`, `-wp7.md`, `-wp8.md`, `-wp9.md` (with the `docs/ui-plan.md` follow-ups), `-wp10.md`, `-wp12.md` and `-warpath.md`.

- 2026-09-29 (the ladder): 7 tiers, lowest first: Clay, Bronze, Silver, Jade, **Gold**, **Platinum**, **Aeon** (ids `gold` and `platinum` are new; indices 0-6).
  - The four existing tiers keep their places (Jade stays index 3), so nothing a player learned moves. The owner's two metals go on top in the order everyone knows, and Aeon stays the pinnacle (our name, the game's theme). The owner is asked whether Platinum should be on top instead.
  - Gold, Platinum and Aeon always hold 1, 2 and 3 Legendaries. That is the one rule a player needs, shown by 1-3 Legendary crests once the tier is shown.
  - Rejected: economy's Gold as a mid tier below Jade (7%, more common than Jade, so "gold" would not be a top capsule); reveal's 8th tier "Eternal" (not asked for); desire's 11 Crown items, Crown Shelf, titles and stats (too costly for the gain).
- 2026-09-29 (odds):
  - Win Capsules come from a **200-slot bag of exactly 60 / 80 / 40 / 13 / 4 / 2 / 1**. Clay, Bronze and Silver keep their shares, Jade or better stays exactly 10%, and Legendary capsules are 7 in 200 (3.5%; the old Aeon was 3%). Aeon is 1 in 200, 6x rarer than before.
  - Supply: Bronze 78 / Silver 15 / Jade 5 / Gold 1.5 / Platinum 0.35 / Aeon 0.15 % (the old 2% Aeon split).
  - The bag is the tier guarantee ("luck decides when, never whether"), so no tier pity counter is added.
  - Why 13/4 and not desire's 12/5: 12/5 made every rarity 3-4% faster and Amber +4%. 13/4 keeps every time-to-max median within 3.6% of today.
- 2026-09-29 (hidden until opened): a capsule that climbs (Win, Supply, Clay meter) shows only its start tier and kind name until it is opened, everywhere (tray, shelf, Result, aria labels, odds panel), with no crests and no sorting by the rolled tier. Showing the rolled tier and then "revealing" it with strikes would be a staged tease (A15.1 red line 7, A15.3). Fixed-tier capsules show their tier.
- 2026-09-29 (contents):
  - Gold = the old Aeon (6 stacks, 26/10/5/2, 1 Legendary, 30% skin, 2,640 Amber) + 100 Dust.
  - Platinum: 7 stacks, 26/12/5/2, 2 different Legendaries (the 2nd holds 1 copy), ≥ 2 Epic, a sure skin at Wardrobe odds, 2,800 Amber, 200 Dust.
  - Aeon: 8 stacks, 40/14/6/2, 3 different Legendaries (2nd and 3rd hold 1 copy), ≥ 3 Epic, a skin Epic or better, an Aeon Collection item, 3,600 Amber, 500 Dust.
  - Foils stay purely rolled on every stack. No tier has a foil floor: a guaranteed Holo per Aeon would be the foil pity that A15.22 and A16.28 rejected, and at A15.11 prices (80,000 Dust per Legendary Holo) it would dwarf the rest of the capsule and empty the foil tail within about a year.
  - Jade loses its 25% Rare-to-Legendary conversion: Legendaries now come from Gold up, the 1% stack roll and pity. The conversion funded the top without inflating Legendary copies, and Jade becomes "the Epic capsule".
  - Extra Legendary stacks hold 1 copy so Legendary copy inflow stays flat: the thrill of a 2nd or 3rd Legendary is the card, not the copy count.
  - Every column (stacks, Legendaries, Epics, Amber, Dust, skin) is non-decreasing up the ladder. Dust-equivalent at craft prices: Jade 7,143, Gold 10,728, Platinum 12,854, Aeon 18,520 (skin valued Epic-or-better, 1,196) plus the Aeon item; the mean bag capsule is 1,730.
- 2026-09-29 (top-tier exclusive): the **Aeon Collection**, 4 A18.9.4 items (Aeon Hourglass decoration, Eternal Dawn base flag, Frozen Moment emote `frozen_moment`, quote "Well met, across the ages!"), source kind `capsuleTier`.
  - Each Aeon holds one the player lacks until the set is complete; after that, a normal collection item and +500 Dust.
  - Craftable for 3,000 Dust each once the save has opened an Aeon (flag `capsule.first.aeon`). A15.1 engagement rule 2 says randomness appears only with a crafting path; every other random-pool cosmetic is craftable, so these must be too. Gating crafting on a first Aeon keeps the meaning "I have opened an Aeon".
  - Never rotated, time-limited, sold or tradable. Class Grey with the A6.4 safeguards.
  - Pace: about 2-3 months for an engaged player (mostly the skill Aeons); a casual player's first Aeon comes after about 6 months, then crafting.
  - Access is guaranteed by the bag and by three skill Aeons: the Trophy Road 4,000 summit, the War Path Cosmic finale, and Conquest's 27 stars until the War Path fold.
  - Kept at 4 items (not 11) for build cost; the items may ship one release after the drums.
  - After about a year (set done, the 8 Epic and Legendary crate skins owned), an engaged player's Aeon is "the biggest pile" (about 10x the mean capsule). Accepted and told to the owner; later Aeon items (added, never rotated) renew it.
- 2026-09-29 (roll rules; DESIGN A6.4 steps):
  - A 2nd or 3rd Legendary stack counts cards already picked in the capsule as owned, so it picks an owned Legendary instead of falling back to Epic. Today's `pickCard` would drop it to Epic.
  - **Legendary catch-up** (required): a Legendary pick uses it whenever every Legendary in the pool is owned, promised by an unopened capsule or already picked in this capsule (as the prototype did); each card then weighs 1 + the copies it still needs to reach L10. Without it, 1 of 100 simulated years had a Legendary that never maxed; with it, none, and the worst year's Legendary median fell from 163 to 132.5 days. Disclosed on the odds panel. (Prototype figures; the shipped code measures differently, see "Capsule ladder: review fixes" below.)
  - Capsule skins never read or advance the Wardrobe pity counters (as today's `bonusSkin`).
- 2026-09-29 (sources):
  - Gate 7: Gold (the old Aeon's contents under the new name). Gate 8: Platinum.
  - Trophy Road 4,000 and Conquest 27 stars: Aeon (now the new Aeon).
  - Script capsule 5 (Mammoth Matriarch): Gold. The first Platinum and Aeon are always earned, never scripted (A15: no early generosity that sets false expectations).
  - War Path Future boss: Jade → Gold, and the Cosmic boss is now the new Aeon (request `docs/requests/capsule-tiers-warpath.md`).
  - Net one-time change for a player who clears everything: about +21,400 Dust-equivalent and +4,300 Amber, about 2.5 days of income.
- 2026-09-29 (veterans at the update):
  - The bag in progress finishes its old mix and its Aeon slots are the new Aeon: the displayed promise "Aeon: 2 left" is kept. Windfall: at most 3 new Aeons per save, once (up to 3 × +7,892 Dust-equivalent and 3 Aeon items).
  - A save that claimed the Trophy Road 4,000 node, the Conquest 27-star milestone or the War Path Cosmic boss before the update got an old Aeon (a Gold) there. It gets one new Aeon per claimed source, once, so "every player can earn an Aeon by skill" is literally true (up to 3 × 18,520 Dust-equivalent and 3 × 3,600 Amber for the most advanced saves). This is a one-time grant through the normal pure grant path (flag `capsule.legacySkillAeon`, granted on the next `tickTimers`), not the "top-up" rejected below.
  - A veteran who claimed Gate 8 got an old Aeon (a Gold) where a new player gets a Platinum (+2,226). Accepted and stated; not topped up.
- 2026-09-29 (climb and show, A10):
  - 4 main strikes climb at most to Gold (`summitAbove: 'gold'`). Platinum gets 1 and Aeon 2 **summit strikes** ("crown" would clash with War Path crowns).
  - The drum keeps its 5 carved rings (Clay to Gold). Platinum and Aeon are summit gems that rise out of the cap only when their strike will climb; nothing above the result is ever drawn empty. The step after strike 4 starts at the same moment for every tier, so no Gold ever looks "almost Platinum".
  - Crests: the Legendary star on a dark enamel shield (10.2:1), on the upper brass band; the one rarity colour allowed on a capsule (a ui-plan 3.2 exception), never drawn empty.
  - Contract: `strikeClimbs` stays 4 booleans and `climbs` is the total. New `CapsuleReveal.firstOfTier` drives a skippable 1 s "Your first Aeon Capsule" step after the pop (meta flag `capsule.first.<tier>`).
  - In one opening (a show or an Open all batch) only the first NEW Legendary gets the full walkout; every other one is 3 s and skippable.
  - Colours: Gold `#EFE0B0` champagne (12.3 from the primary button light, 17.8 from Legendary; the rendered drum's mid-tone and highlight are tested too, so no saturated gold); Platinum `#C4F2EA` ice (16.8 from Silver); Aeon `#5D3DFF` electric indigo (13.2 from Epic, 13.1 from the high-contrast team blue; the first pick `#4F66E6` was only 5.9 from it). The ΔE ≥ 12 test covers rarity, team (all presets) and button colours. A parchment outline on every surface gives each icon at least 4.63:1. Silver vs Common (4.2) stays a known exception.
  - Staging escalates with the tier already shown: Gold small, Platinum medium, Aeon large; the Aeon's age-glyph halo reads the content age list within 900 ms.
  - Sounds: `cap_climb_5`, `cap_climb_6`, `cap_summit_rise`, `cap_burst_platinum`, `cap_burst_aeon`. No casino imagery or copy; "jackpot", "rarest", "almost", "so close" and "only N left" are banned in capsule copy (C5 copy scan).
- 2026-09-29 (save): the next save version after the newest (v6 when written; v7 if the War Path migration lands first). Re-read `src/save/migrations` before adding it.
  - `capsules.bag` remaps tier index 4 → 6 (0-3 do not move) and then sorts.
  - New `capsules.bagSize` (100 for a bag in progress, 0 when empty, 200 after a refill), so the odds panel says "N of 100 left" truthfully; the UI reads `bagSize || content bag size`.
  - Pending `aeon` capsules become `gold` (tier and startTier) with +100 Dust. Their pre-rolled contents are exactly the Gold table: names follow contents, nothing is re-rolled or taken.
  - `flags['notice.capsuleLadder']` drives a one-time, closable notice in the Capsules tab (not Home, A15.13), only for saves past the script (`pity.opened` > 5), with a relabelled Aeon or with a legacy Aeon due.
  - Tests use a named fixture outside the `fixtures/v*.json` glob (`capsule-ladder-pre.json`) plus the usual frozen `vN.json`.
  - Rejected: desire's post-load "top-up" of old Aeons to the new Aeon (it changes pre-rolled contents); economy's `table: 1` legacy label (an "Aeon" drum with 1 Legendary would break the crest rule).
- 2026-09-29 (economy, measured): a scratch copy of the repo with the final numbers ran `tools/economy.ts` over 100 seeds against an untouched copy. The review round changed only foils, skins and cosmetics, which do not move copies or Amber.
  - Medians (today → new): Common to max 110.3 → 107.5 days; Rare 101.0 → 99.5; Epic 69.0 → 66.5; Legendary 111.5 → 112.0 (p90 129 → 123); whole collection (copies done) 209.5 → 192.5 (p90 264 → 217), about 17 days (8%) sooner, 12 of them from catch-up; Amber done 146 → 143; per day 96.5 → 97.8 copies and 2,981 → 3,030 Amber. **Superseded:** these came from the prototype, which consumed the RNG differently; the shipped code measures a collection about 10 days (5%) sooner, see "Capsule ladder: review fixes" below.
  - Per bag capsule before pity: 15.7 → 16.1 copies, 399 → 411 Amber, 7 → 13 Dust.
  - The old Rare band (4.3 months ± 20%) is stale, not noise: today's own 100-seed median (101) is below it. ECONOMY_TARGETS' time-to-max targets are rebased on today's measured medians (110 / 101 / 69 / 112), which is what the owner's "keep today's time to max a card" means, and WP12 gates on a 30-seed median. A6.9 now shows target and measured values; the plan-L7, all-Legendaries, copies-done and finish-gap misses exist today and stay Phase 3 items.
  - `sim-cli drops --mode smoke`: 10 / 10 pass (400 / 400 bags of 200 exact, Supply χ² p = 0.58, 0 guarantee violations, every Platinum 2 and every Aeon 3 Legendaries).
- 2026-09-29 (review round): what the critic changed, in short.
  - Climbing capsules hide their rolled tier until opened (was shown in the tray and Result).
  - Foil floors (Platinum Silver foil, Aeon Holo) and the Holo preference and fallback Dust are removed.
  - The Aeon Collection is craftable after a first Aeon (was never craftable, breaking A15.1 rule 2).
  - The spec moved from the session scratchpad into DESIGN and `docs/requests/capsule-tiers-*.md`; ui-plan follow-ups are requested in `capsule-tiers-wp9.md`.
  - A16.18 (Mythic columns, Holo rate), A17.13 and A6.9 were brought in line.
  - The drum keeps 5 rings plus summit gems (7 rings did not fit and drew empty slots above the result); crests sit on a widened brass band; icons below 32 px use a silhouette with a "★n" badge.
  - "Crown strike" became "summit strike"; the emote "Time Stop" became "Frozen Moment"; the glyph halo reads the content age list.
  - Gold and Aeon colours moved (see climb and show); the crest has a dark shield.
  - The first-of-tier banner is its own skippable step; one full walkout per opening, not per capsule.
  - Platinum's skin is now certain; the Aeon skin is valued correctly.
  - The notice copy names the Jade change, drops "rarest", and moves to the Capsules tab.
  - Veterans get legacy skill Aeons; the asymmetries are stated.
  - Economy targets rebased, 30-seed gate.
  - The owner gets four yes/no questions in Danish.
- **Til ejeren (dansk):** Kapslerne får 7 niveauer (Ler, Bronze, Sølv, Jade, Guld, Platin, Aeon). Aeon er nu 1 ud af 200 sejrskapsler og har 3 Legendaries, et sikkert skin og et af 4 Aeon-samlerobjekter; de kan også laves med Dust efter din første Aeon. En sejrskapsel viser først sit niveau, når du åbner den. Tiden til at maksimere et kort er næsten uændret, og hele samlingen bliver færdig ca. 10 dage (5 %) tidligere (målt på den færdige kode; det første tal, 17 dage, kom fra en prototype). Spørgsmål: (1) Skal Aeon blive over Guld og Platin, eller vil du have Platin øverst? (2) Er det OK, at Aeon-samlerobjekterne kan laves med Dust (3.000 hver) efter din første Aeon? (3) Er det OK, at uåbnede gamle Aeon-kapsler bliver til Guld med +100 Dust, og at spillere, der allerede har hentet en "dygtigheds-Aeon", får en ny Aeon én gang? (4) Gate 7 Guld, Gate 8 Platin og War Path-bossen i Fremtiden Guld?

## Owner request 2026-09-29: powers cost gold, reload, more powers, own-half limits

Owner: "Speciallen burde koste penge og have reload time, der skal være mange flere specials og nogle af dem f.eks. begrænset til egen banehalvdel så man ikke bare kan vente på, at modstanderen har bygget en hær som man så bare ødelægger på en gang." Money means in-battle gold; there is no real money, ever. The lead designer scored three proposals (strategy 50, feel 50, content 47; owner goals weighted ×2, then depth, phone clarity, AI feasibility, balance risk, build cost and fit with the work in flight) and wrote one spec on the strategy base. Rules: DESIGN A2.9; roster: A5.7; requests: `docs/requests/powers-sources.md`, `docs/requests/powers-hud-army.md`. Decided, not built (build phases P1-P5, A2.9.13).

- 2026-09-29 (evidence, headless on `013d958`, `SIM_VERSION` 3.0.0, from the three proposals' runs):
  - Free powers are the main stall engine: Standard War tier V mirror Final Bell 37-39% with powers, 1-5% without (150-200 matches each).
  - A cast zone holds 71-82% of the enemy's on-lane army; power kills were 9% / 19% / 21% of all enemy value killed in Short / Standard / Full War.
  - Priced Home-only powers: 15-17% Bell and a 10:14-10:35 median (target 10:30); cost plus own half: 16%, 9:56.
  - Reach alone backfires: a Home clamp without a cap sent a hoarding turtle to the Bell in 60% of matches (19% today) and let the attacker's charge kill 9.9 units per cast of an army massed at home (2.5 today); "own half" alone raised the largest single cast from 675 to 810 gold killed.
  - Free vs no power 93%; 100 g Home powers vs no power 38% with the AI not adapted; one side paying against a free side won 1 of 80.
  - Full War shortened to 8:27-9:58 in both priced prototypes (target 15:00).
- 2026-09-29 (base): the strategy proposal's rule skeleton wins the tie with feel on the owner's central complaint: typed Home and Field slots, reach, and a hard target cap on every damaging or controlling power. It is the only proposal that measured why reach alone is not enough.
- 2026-09-29 (cost and reload): every power costs 75-150 gold, flat across ages, paid on acceptance and never refunded (the telegraph is a commitment, which keeps baiting real); each slot reloads on its own, 25-60 s by power.
  - Reload stays progress in ppm per slot (from feel), so Overcharge, Signal Fires, Power Hour, the HUD ring and the evolve carry keep one shape.
  - Every slot starts 25% reloaded (a 40 s power is ready at 0:30, after the first clash); the evolve carry is min(progress, 75%) per slot, so a new-age power is always at least a quarter of its reload away.
  - Overdrive and Siege no longer speed reloads (passive gold already doubles); Overcharge adds 25% to the less-reloaded slot.
  - Rejected: a gold cost on top of the charge meter (it still says "fire when full"), rising cost per cast (rewards one big cast), stored charges (a double wipe), power levels (a grind axis the loadout multiplier already covers).
- 2026-09-29 (slots and reach): a Home slot (reach `home`: the whole zone at p ≤ 1,000) and a Field slot (reach `front`: centre ≤ max(front, 480) + 150; `anywhere` only for one-target strikes and drops; `army` for buffs).
  - Why a fixed half: it never moves, is drawn once, matches the Hold flag range and Forage, and favours the side being pushed (the comeback direction). Why "front + 150" with a 480 floor: you can only reach where your army stands, and a Front power can always defend home.
  - The front counts only trained ground units (no air, no summons), so a flyer or a drop cannot carry reach forward; `frontRank` 2 is the lever if a lone runner is abused.
  - Typed slots replace content's "one Barrage per loadout" and command lock: a loadout can never hold two area nukes. The lock stays as a data lever at 0.
  - The Home slot is there from match 1 (the tutorial's Arrow Storm beat is already a Home cast); the Field slot unlocks at the War Path Stone L5 first clear or 150 trophies. Both sides always play the same slots (feel's match rule).
- 2026-09-29 (the cap): one cast affects at most `maxTargets` enemy units (1-6), nearest the caster's gate first, through one `hitIds` rule for every kind.
  - Chosen over feel's crowd damage share: a cap keeps kills clean and satisfying ("five go down"), shows as numbered pips before the cast, and makes formation a skill (cheap units in front screen the Heavies). A damage share makes every cast into a big army feel weak.
  - With the cap, a bigger army is worth no more than a small one, so waiting for the enemy to mass earns nothing: the owner's "wait, then destroy it all at once" is closed by reach, the cap and the price together.
- 2026-09-29 (roster): 48 powers, 6 per age (3 Home, 3 Field) on a role template (from content): Home = an area damage power, a second one of another family, a control; Field = an assault (charge or front barrage), a strike or Suppress, a support.
  - 16 built powers kept (retagged and priced; Tidal Wave 130 → 150 and Iron Horse 150 → 130 from feel's per-power value data; Arrow Storm 40 → 50 and Royal Decree speed 25% → 20% to sit in the band and the A18.2 cap; Smoke Screen 7 → 6 s; Paratroopers 4 → 3 Riflemen and Warp Strike 3 → 4 Legionnaires to price drops at 150 g).
  - 32 new: 8 starters (so every age has a Home and a Field starter) and 24 War Path powers.
  - Three new sim kinds only: `field` (snares, pulls, stuns), `strike` (one locked target), `suppress` (silences enemy turrets; one in the final age of every ladder format, as a tool against turret stalls); plus `barrage.hitsGround` for Flak. Everything else reuses `barrage`, `sweep`, `stampede`, `buffAll`, `cloud` and `paradrop`.
  - Benched: Barricade (waits for the fort entity), Tremor (waits for Underground), Minefield, Star Shells, a Trojan Horse drop, summon lifetimes and a summon cap (levers only), Legendary powers.
  - Why 48 and not 32 or 40: "mange flere" asks for a clear jump, and 3 options per slot per age makes each slot a real 1-of-3 pick; families and effect recipes keep the build cost per power small.
- 2026-09-29 (sources): starters, the existing Trophy Road 100-500 nodes, the War Path (each region's L5, L7 and L9 first clear) and a Trophy Road fallback on nodes 550-1,950; whichever comes first grants the power, the other pays 60 Amber.
  - Never capsules (strategy's capsule cards rejected): powers have no copies or levels, capsule odds and the pacing sims stay untouched, and the capsule-tier work in flight is not disturbed.
  - Bots use only powers a player at that point could own.
- 2026-09-29 (AI, from feel): per slot, value per gold (ROI) over the capped targets in the legal band against a tier bar (7,000 at tier 0 to 17,000 at X), a gold ledger, a Home reserve from tier V, ring reading from V, bait-then-wave from VII and counter-timing at X; tiers 0-II use the Home slot only.
- 2026-09-29 (HUD, from feel): a dock of two round buttons (64 px on phones) using the reserved Fort space until forts ship (714 of 750 px); a cost chip, reach glyph, reload ring and seconds; a reach band with a magnetic edge; numbered target pips ("Hits 5 of 9 · −100"); two enemy mini-rings with "?" until scouted; feel scaled by family inside the A12 freeze budget. Space = Home, X = Field.
- 2026-09-29 (contracts): one WP0 bump and `SIM_VERSION` 4.0.0 with one golden re-record on the frozen fixture (the 3.0.0 replay policy: old replays keep their result, no longer play); a new golden covers both slots, the cap, a field, a strike and Suppress. Save: the next version after the newest at build time moves each loadout's power into its slot, fills the other with the age's starter, grants the new starters and already-earned War Path powers; nothing is lost.
- 2026-09-29 (gates): power share of gold 8-16%, of enemy value killed 5-12%; one cast touches ≤ 40% (p50) of an army worth ≥ 750; p99 of card value killed per cast ≤ 350; 1.5-3.5 casts per side per age; `power_hoarder` ≤ 40% with ≤ 20% at the Bell; `bait_wave` beats `plain_wave` by 5-20 points; `power_spam`, `drop_spam`, `runner_reach` ≤ 45%; `no_power` loses 60-80%; `home_turtle` in the turtle band; per power ±3 against its slot's starter; Final Bell not above today's at P1 and Standard ≤ 20% before P2; Full War median ≥ 12:00. Levers in order: cap ±1, Home cost or reload, the Home line, Front reach or `frontRank`, the power-kill bounty, the Overdrive reload bonus, the slot lock, Suppress length.
- 2026-09-29 (risks accepted): Full War may shorten (tuned together with pacing in P5, Home 100 → 75 first); a capped power can feel stingy (per-unit damage stays at the top of the old band, the pips make the cap a visible promise); two slots crowd the phone tray (fits at 714 of 750 px, 748 once forts ship).

## UI review fixes (fixer, 2026-09-29)

- 2026-09-29 (overlays): the app's blocking overlays (the first forced upgrade, the Age Capsule dialog) register in `src/ui/components/overlay.ts`; while one is open Home drops its primary and pulse and holds its level ceremony, unlock moment and first-seen captions until it closes (U1, U11, U13).
- 2026-09-29 (one new thing): the Amber/Dust first-seen captions wait for a pending unlock moment and count as seen only after showing in full; the unlock pointer folds away on any tap outside it, so a locked tab's hint never stacks on it.
- 2026-09-29 (tabs): a locked tab keeps its label; the next one to unlock adds a "Lv N" tag on its icon (2.6). Army's ready badge is a dot without a count (a count read as a backlog), and only when an upgrade is affordable (copies and Amber) after upgrades have been taught (`upgradesTaught`: the forced upgrade, level 3, or a legacy save). The capsule summary hides "Upgrade ready" before that too.
- 2026-09-29 (Army, 2.6): the average level, the advisor's warnings and Auto-fill arrive with level 3 (errors always show); empty troop slots with no owned troop left say "More in capsules". Phones: the average level moves from the header into the strip above the slots, and grid cards keep their names. A grid card's action bar picks the side with room and the grid scrolls it fully into view.
- 2026-09-29 (formats): `formatName` (UI model) names generated age windows by their ages ("Stone to Bronze"); VS shows "Level N · name" for War Path levels. "AI" and "AI General" are one chip.
- 2026-09-29 (onboarding Result): the app's onboarding Result uses the shared level badge (`LevelBadgeView`), the icon recap rows and the shared reward icons; War Path stars are no longer listed as reward rows (the badge stamps them). The onboarding capsule summary's primary reads "Continue". A card equipped into an age the player has not reached reads "Equipped for Bronze", not "Equipped".
- 2026-09-29 (Capsules tab, 4.6): stage with the best capsule (display tier, name, source, Odds, Open) and a shelf where a tile tap opens that capsule or crate directly (kept one tap; no select step). Banks show only after their first progress: charges once the Ladder is open, Supply when banked, Clay when it has a pip. The odds panel shows the odds first, then the bank rules.
- 2026-09-29 (Customize): U10's "instant with Undo" instead of a try-on step: equipping a flag, base skin or decoration shows the new state on the tile and a toast with Undo. Equip pills are green, "Equipped" is a flat state pill, every category tab has its label, and the base mock-up shows the player's current War Path age.
- 2026-09-29 (Modes): the Modes panel opens on the last mode started, so a Ladder battle is 2 taps after the first.

## Power rework: review fixes (lead, 2026-09-29)

A critic reviewed the power rework spec ("Owner request 2026-09-29: powers cost gold, reload, more powers, own-half limits" above). The lead checked every issue against the code and DESIGN; the fixes below are in DESIGN A2.9 and A5.7 and supersede the entry above where they differ.

- 2026-09-29 (measurement): the combined rule set was never measured; the 15-17% Bell came from priced Home-only prototypes without a cap and with an unadapted AI (100 g Home powers lost to no powers, 38%). New build phase **P0**: a `--patch` or harness prototype of the full rules with pre-registered go/no-go (Standard tier V Bell ≤ 25%, Short not above today, `no_power` loses ≥ 55%, `power_hoarder` ≤ 45%, Home starters' value per gold ≥ 1.0) and a fixed fallback order (Home 75 g, caps +1, power bounty 50%) before any contract change. The owner summary now says the package is not measured yet.
- 2026-09-29 (P1 keeps `main` playable): the contract bump breaks today's HUD, render, app, War Plan and tutorial readers of `Loadout.power` and `powerPpm`, so P1 now ships the save migration and one-button compatibility adapters (meta sends `field: null` for both sides until P2; `render/hudModel.ts` maps the Home slot onto the single button), placeholder manifest entries and template sounds for the 8 new starters (the ids test), and starts only after the UI rebuild's in-flight HUD and War Plan edits are committed. Requests: `docs/requests/powers-p1-compat.md`.
- 2026-09-29 (the screen is real): cap order only ranked units inside the zone, so the zone could skip cheap front units. Now eligibility is decided over the whole reach area: the first `maxTargets` enemies nearest the caster's gate (plus units already hit); the zone only decides which of them are touched. The loss tip "Lead with cheap units" is now true, the pips mark the eligible units, and backline sniping with area powers is gone.
- 2026-09-29 (Home mask): blasts, jitter and sweep widths reached 29-60 lu past mid-lane at the band edge. The reach area is a hard mask: a Home effect touches only enemies with own-frame p ≤ 1,000 (inclusive); the reach test asserts on hit positions.
- 2026-09-29 (strikes): four strikes killed their age's Epic in one cast (about 3.5× their cost every 30 s). Epics take 50% from strikes (`strikeEpicBp`); the static check now asserts a strike never kills a full-HP same-age Heavy or non-Legendary Epic. A manual strike locks the unit nearest the aim (the aim is never overridden); auto-aim and bots rank by value, and bots' aim error is a choice among their best k targets.
- 2026-09-29 (buffs): buffs scaled with army size (mass, then press). Buffs now affect at most 8 own units (frontmost), the cloud's ally bonus too; buffs have a static budget (shields plus heals ≤ 70% of the age's Infantry per target) and are gated through the per-power ±3 row.
- 2026-09-29 (controls): slows only cut move speed, so controls did little to a wave already fighting. New status `snare` (move and attack speed); snares and pulls get chip damage of 30-40% of Infantry; stuns cost 75 g, cap 5, 2.0 s; all controls must reach ≥ 12 disabled unit-seconds per 100 gold (static check). Field pulse count is max(1, durationMs ÷ 500) (the old "≤" gave one pulse too many).
- 2026-09-29 (AI): damage was valued as cost × damage ÷ HP, so chip damage passed every bar. Value is now kill-weighted (a kill counts cost × 1.3, other damage 40%); starting bars 6,000-18,000, calibrated in P1 (tier V median 1.5-3.5 casts per age; tier VII casts on small groups at most half as often). Tier VII+ keep bait discipline (no Home cast on targets worth < 200 unless the base was hit), so a bait draws tier V and not tier VII.
- 2026-09-29 (gates): `power_hoarder` now casts at a covered value ≥ 150 (the bait size), so `bait_wave` can gain; bait is also run against tier V and VII bots. Flak and Suppress are gated in matchups, not on the baseline. The army-share row counts value, not units. `home_turtle` uses the age's Home damage starter plus its Field starter. One setup statement for all power gates (proxies in Short, Standard and Full War; P0 and P1 Bell on tier V mirrors, release on tier VII).
- 2026-09-29 (Suppress is not a Bell answer): Era of the Week and chosen start eras end windows in ages without Suppress, and all Suppress powers are War Path Epics that the starter-only Bell gates never measure. Removed from the Bell mitigations; Suppress stays a situational tool (Medieval, Future). Its silence now belongs to the mount (selling or rebuilding does not clear it).
- 2026-09-29 (roster template): Modern Home had two bombards and no control; Industrial Field had no support. The Modern Home starter is now **Strafing Run** (a sweep; replaces the never-built Rocket Artillery) and Flak fills the control role in Modern; Industrial's L9 power is now **Field Hospital** (a mend; replaces Saboteurs). "Typed slots mean no two area nukes" was false: one power per slot, the cap bounds both, `lockMs` is the lever. The schema checks the template.
- 2026-09-29 (save): eight built powers move to the Field slot, so the migration unlocks the Field slot for every save with a match played; it also grants the new item of every already claimed Trophy Road node ≥ 550 directly (claimed nodes are stored as whole values), 60 Amber for a double grant. New fixtures "Stampede equipped, 50 trophies" and "road claimed to 1,000".
- 2026-09-29 (locks and names): the sim knows no meta unlock; meta sends `field: null` while the Field slot is locked. The lockout lever's reject code is `powerLockout` (HUD `lockoutUntil`); the progression state is `slotLocked`. An auto-aimed damage or control cast with nothing to hit is rejected before payment.
- 2026-09-29 (smaller rules): empty slots accrue and carry; cost modifiers multiply and rate bonuses add; the observation and the HUD show effective cost and reload; the reload keeps a remainder so every reload is exact; the power bounty ignores Forage and Bounty Hunters (as built); `power_ready` plays when a slot first becomes castable; ring speed is public; the power-incoming badge counts down the power's own telegraph; F ignores burrowed, structure and mid-leap units, and forts are never eligible; bots may use any power the player could own by either source; the Daily always plays both slots; the phone HUD keeps ≥ 16 px slack with forts and the enemy research icon; Last Stand floats above the Home button.
- 2026-09-29 (rejected or partly taken): the critic's option (b) for the screen (keep zone-local order) was rejected in favour of a real screen; a "value per gold 1.2-2.0" gate for buffs was not added, because a buff's marginal value is not measurable per cast: buffs are gated by the ±3 row and a static budget, and their value per gold is reported.

## Capsule ladder: rules and data build (WP0, WP1, WP7, WP8, WP12 tools; 2026-09-29)

Built from "Owner request 2026-09-29: more capsule tiers" above and `docs/requests/capsule-tiers-*.md`.

- 2026-09-29 (save v6): the ladder migration is save v6 (the newest was v5 when it landed). Any later migration (the War Path or the power rework) takes v7 or later. `meta/rules.ts` `SAVE_VERSION` is 6.
- 2026-09-29 (tier order is data): meta reads the ladder from `content.capsules.tierOrder` (`tierOrder`, `tierIndex`, `topTier`, `guaranteedLegendaries` in `meta/tables.ts`); the old `TIER_ORDER` / `TIER_INDEX` constants are gone. A test checks that the capsule rule files name no tier id. `capsule/tiers.ts` keeps its own `TIER_ORDER` and `SUMMIT_ABOVE` (the capsule layer may not import content, B2); `capsule/test/metaReveals.test.ts` checks that they match the content and that meta's strikes agree with `resolveStrikes` for all 49 pairs.
- 2026-09-29 (catch-up input): `copiesStillNeeded` counts, per card, the copies to L10 after the collection and every unopened capsule. A card that is only promised (not owned yet) counts from L1. The weights apply only when no Legendary is left that is unowned, unpromised and unpicked in this capsule, as A6.4 step 4 says.
- 2026-09-29 (Aeon Collection not in content yet): the 4 Aeon Collection items need code-drawn art first (the visuals test requires art for every item), so they are not in `raw/cosmetics.ts` yet. The rules are built and tested with a content fixture: an Aeon rolls the normal pool without the +500 Dust until the items exist (as the request says). Their strings are ready.
- 2026-09-29 (strings file): the new UI, capsule and cosmetic strings live in `src/i18n/capsuleLadder.en.json`, because `ui.en.json` and `capsule.en.json` fail their tests on keys no code uses yet. The owning packages move each key into their own file when they use it. `ui.odds.bagLine` keeps its current text and params until WP9 switches to the list form.
- 2026-09-29 (legacy count): the legacy grant sets `capsule.legacySkillAeon.<kind>` for each Aeon it gave. `legacySkillAeonCount` then counts those, so a source claimed after the update never shows in the "You had already earned {n}" line.
- 2026-09-29 (economy gate): `tools/economy.ts` gates on the median of 30 seeds (`--seeds`, about 20 s) against the rebased targets. Measured medians over 100 seeds, before and after the ladder: Common to max 110 → 108.5 days, Rare 101 → 97.3, Epic 69 → 66, Legendary 112 → 111.5, whole collection 210 → 200, Amber done 146 → 143, focused plan at L7 78 → 82.5.
- 2026-09-29 (show placeholders): `capsule/plan.ts` gets the Gold, Platinum and Aeon burst builds (820 / 1,000 / 1,200 ms) and `SHOW_LIMITS.burst` 1,600. Until WP6 adds `cap_climb_5` / `cap_climb_6` and the new stingers, tiers above Gold reuse `cap_climb_4`. The summit steps, crests and first-of-tier step are WP10's.

## Capsule ladder: UI (WP9; 2026-09-29)

Built from `docs/requests/capsule-tiers-wp9.md` on top of the rules and data build above.

- 2026-09-29 (one helper for what may be shown): `src/ui/components/capsuleLook.ts` decides what an unopened capsule shows: `visibleTier` (start tier for kinds with a `climbFrom`), `pendingCrests` (0 while climbing), `pendingNameKey` ("Starter Capsule", the kind name, or the tier name), `byVisibleTier` (shelf and tray order) and `legendaryBagTiers` (the odds bag line). Every capsule surface uses it: Capsules tab, the Home tray, the Result reward row and summary chip. Crests come from `guaranteed`, never a tier id.
- 2026-09-29 (drum icon mirrors the ladder): the UI layer cannot read content inside a bare icon, so `icons.tsx` keeps `TIER_LADDER` and `DRUM_RINGS = 5` (the drum's carved rings; tiers past ring 5 are summit gems). A test keeps `TIER_LADDER` equal to `content.capsules.tierOrder` and `DRUM_RINGS - 1` equal to the index of `summitAbove`, as `src/capsule/tiers.ts` does. Per-tier materials (ramps, Gold's lapis grooves, Platinum's brushed streaks, the Aeon starfield) are art tables keyed by tier, like `TIER_COLOR`.
- 2026-09-29 (strings): new `ui.odds.extraLegendarySecond` ("Second Legendary: {n} copy.") because Platinum has only a second Legendary, and `ui.notice.capsuleLadder.legacyOne` for n = 1 (the file convention for singulars). `ui.capsules.from.daily` is now "From finishing matches": the Supply Capsule's name is now its kind name, so the old source line repeated it. Used keys moved out of `capsuleLadder.en.json` into `ui.en.json` and `cosmetics.en.json`; `ui.odds.bagMix`, `capsule.*` and `cosmetic.set.aeon.*` and the item names stay there until their owners use them.
- 2026-09-29 (notice services): `UiServices` gains `legacySkillAeons()` (the app calls `meta.legacySkillAeonCount`) and `dismissNotice('capsuleLadder')` (clears `flags['notice.capsuleLadder']`). `setUiFlags` stays limited to `ui-` keys.
- 2026-09-29 (tier colour never text): the odds table and extras no longer paint tier names in the tier colour (Aeon `#5D3DFF` fails text contrast); each tier name is normal text beside its drum. A test checks the sheet has no inline text colour.

## Capsule ladder: the show and its sounds (WP10, WP6; 2026-09-29)

Built from `docs/requests/capsule-tiers-wp10.md` and `-wp6.md` on top of the rules and data build above.

- 2026-09-29 (crests are data): `capsule/tiers.ts` mirrors the guaranteed Legendary count per tier as `LEGENDARY_CRESTS` (the capsule layer cannot import content, B2); `test/metaReveals.test.ts` checks it against `guaranteedLegendaries`. Staging levels (Gold 1, Platinum 2, Aeon 3) come from the crest and summit-gem counts (`stagingLevel`), and the stingers from `SUMMIT_ABOVE` (`cap_burst_<tier>` above it), so no show code keys on a tier name.
- 2026-09-29 (summit gem slot): `SummitRiseStep.index` / `SummitStrikeStep.index` is the gem's slot in the cap band (a drum shown above Gold already has gems), not the strike's position; `checkPlan` asserts summit strikes = summit gems of the final tier minus those of the start tier.
- 2026-09-29 (walkout rule): `revealCards` keeps `firstLegendary` only for the first first-time Legendary in reveal order of one opening (a single show or one Open all batch); `checkPlan` fails a plan with more than one unskippable step.
- 2026-09-29 (drum geometry): body -192..-40 with rings 29 px apart (-62 ... -178), a 22 px upper brass (crest) band, and the stone cap band. Summit gems sit on the front of the cap band below the top face's rim (the rim covers the band's upper half), 1.1x size so they read at phone size. Crests and gems animate in an overlay and are committed into the drum's shared context when they settle, so the flying halves carry them.
- 2026-09-29 (transmutation): every climb now wipes the new material down from the top (200 ms for a main strike, 300 ms for a summit strike) instead of switching at once; the new material's own crests stamp during the wipe.
- 2026-09-29 (staged tiers hold their light): for Gold and up the burst build starts with the crack light low (0.35, rising to 2.0) and the summit rise calms it to 0.35, so the new material is seen under its staging before the pop; tiers below Gold build as before.
- 2026-09-29 (first-of-tier banner): DOM text (`capsule.firstTier.<tier>`, parchment text, tier colour only as glow and gem line) during the `firstTier` step, or over the end of an Open all volley for the highest first tier. The capsule string test accepts keys built from a tier id through their literal prefix.
- 2026-09-29 (Lite): `ShowSettings.lite` (optional) halves particles, freezes the starfield and the god-ray sway; the app passes it once `docs/requests/capsule-tiers-app-lite.md` lands.
- 2026-09-29 (sounds): the five new ids are rendered with `tools/audio` (FluidSynth; glass-bell partials at x2.76, choir and space-voice pads lowpassed at 2.4 kHz, tubular-bell clock chime) and packed into a new `capsule` sheet. Only the capsule sheet and its manifest lines were rebuilt (the other sheets and the music are untouched); the old capsule sheet files stay in `public/audio/sfx` because `tools/audio/generated/assets.gen.a17.ts` still names them.

## Power rework: the dock, targeting and the Army slots (WP5, WP9; 2026-09-29)

Built on the P1 rules and content (`SIM_VERSION` 4.0.0, save v7), per A2.9.10 and `docs/requests/powers-hud-army.md`.

- 2026-09-29 (one reach resolver for the pointer): the HUD asks the view for the raw lane point (`laneRawP`) and the slot's band (`powerReach`), and resolves it with `resolveAim` in `src/ui/hud/powerAim.ts`: inside the band as it is, toward your gate clamped, up to 120 lu past the far edge stuck to it, beyond that out of reach (red, hatched, nothing sent on release). The view repeats the same rule (`src/render/powerPreview.ts`) while a drag edge-scrolls under a still finger. The band and pips come from `core/powerReach.ts`, the sim's own helpers, so the ghost's promise matches the cast.
- 2026-09-29 (the band's edge is the reach area): the lane wash runs from your gate to the reach area's far bound (the Home line; for Front powers the band maximum plus half the zone), not to the last legal centre, because that is where effects can land. It refreshes every frame, so a Front edge follows your front. Strikes and powers without an aim draw no band.
- 2026-09-29 (pips are the screen): the ghost numbers the first `maxTargets` hittable enemies in the reach area in cap order (1 = nearest your gate), gold when the zone covers them; enemies in the zone that the cap or the mask skip get a faint outline. The token reads "Hits {covered} of {eligible} · −cost". Clouds, buffs, drops and Suppress show no pips (they have no enemy cap); a buff rings your 8 frontmost units.
- 2026-09-29 (a strike dropped on nothing is refused in the HUD): when the ghost has no lock, the release denies with "No target there" and sends nothing, so no gold is at risk; auto-aim (X) is left to the sim.
- 2026-09-29 (empty and locked slots are gaps): a slot without a power (a Home-only match, the Field slot before its unlock, match 1) keeps its space as an empty gap and X does nothing, so nothing jumps when the slot arrives. On Army the locked Field slot shows its padlock even when a migrated save already stores a Field power there, since the sim plays it empty until the flag is set.
- 2026-09-29 (ready chime moved to the HUD): `power_ready` plays when a slot first becomes castable (reloaded and affordable) after its cast, at most once per 3 s; the render no longer plays it on the sim's `powerReady`, which fires on reload even when gold is short.
- 2026-09-29 (long-press, then drag): a long-press opens the tip, but moving past the drag threshold afterwards still picks the power up; only a release without moving keeps the tip and casts nothing.
- 2026-09-29 (string namespaces): the HUD strings live under `hud.power.*` and the Army and card detail strings under `ui.power.*` (each file's test allows only its own namespace), instead of the shared `power.*` keys the spec sketched. The Army's keys are whole literals in `src/ui/screens/model/powerText.ts` so the strings check can see them.
- 2026-09-29 (power tiles): powers carry their rarity on tiles (Common, Rare, Epic), the gold cost in the cost chip, the reach glyph where units show their class, and "⟳ 40 s" where units show their level (`CardTileData.power`).
- 2026-09-29 (one hint at a time): the Home button's "Drag onto the battlefield!" hint never shows while the Field power is being aimed, and picking up either power dismisses it; an out-of-reach ghost puts "Only in your half" just under its cross at the top of the hatched area, so the dragged token at the finger does not cover it.

## Power rework: AI v1 (WP3, 2026-09-29)

Built from A2.9.9 on the core build (SIM_VERSION 4.0.0). Numbers are from headless runs at L7 with the baseline plan (both starters), reported in the build notes.

- 2026-09-29 (score): a cast that clears its ROI bar scores 19,000 (`SCORE.power`), above every action but Last Stand. At 10,000 it lost to training under pressure (train scores reach 18,000), so a cast whose targets were in the zone waited a decision and missed them. `powerBeforeEvolve` is gone: the cast already outranks Evolve, and a slot at 100% keeps 75% across the ageUp, so a cast is only worth it on its own value.
- 2026-09-29 (drops): the drop value weights are 1.2 x the summoned card value on a soft target (an enemy ranged or support unit within 300 lu behind the enemy front) and 0.6 x otherwise, not the starting 0.8 / 0.4. With 0.8 the best drop reached ROI 12,000 (Paratroopers) or 10,700 (Warp Strike), so no tier from VI up ever cast one.
- 2026-09-29 (ranged): "ranged" for the cloud and the drop means an attack range of at least 100 lu (melee reach is 60 lu at most); the Pebbler, Fusilier and the other Ranged Commons have 200-240 lu, so the A7.2 banking range of 250 would have missed all of them.
- 2026-09-29 (strikes): the best-k pick only aims (`p`) at a target that is fighting (an own unit within its range + 40 lu), inside the power clamp and with another hittable enemy within 60 lu, so the delayed position still locks someone; otherwise the bot uses the auto-aim ranking. Targets under 25% HP are skipped, and a strike needs 2 hittable enemies on the lane. Before this the fuzz run had 3-7 `powerNoTarget` rejections per 60 matches (targets that died or moved during the observation delay); now 0.
- 2026-09-29 (Suppress): the bot also wants its second front unit past 1,370, so one runner dying during the delay cannot turn the cast into `powerOutOfReach`.
- 2026-09-29 (counter-timing): "the enemy is banking" reads as: the enemy army on the lane is under 300 and the foe gold estimate (A7.1) is at least 300. The A2.9.9 wording ("no enemy unit in the bot's half") can never hold when a Home power has a target.
- 2026-09-29 (bait bank): from tier VII (Tempest from V) the bank comes first: while a scouted enemy Home bombard or sweep is ready, a wave the push gate would send waits until 500 is banked, then the bait goes. Without this the 500 trigger was never reached (the bot spent at the gate's ~400) and the bait never ran. Measured on tier VII vs V Standard (200 mirrored): Final Bell 41% -> 31%, wins 39.5% -> 41.5%.
- 2026-09-29 (loadout multiplier): the bot estimates its loadout multiplier from the levels of its own trained units on the lane (the observation has no loadout levels); 1.0 when none are out.
- 2026-09-29 (bars kept): the A2.9.9 starting bars stay. A grid on tier VII vs V (Standard, 200 mirrored each: VII bar 8,000 / 10,000 / 12,000 / 15,000, with and without bait discipline; V at 8,000) moved tier VII between 37.5% and 46.5%, all inside the noise, and with no casts at all the two tiers split 49%. The spec's "tier VII beats tier V >= 60%" is not reachable through the power policy.

## Capsule ladder: review fixes (fixer, 2026-09-29)

A review of the built ladder (economy, honesty, visual and bug lenses) confirmed 21 findings; these are the fixes.

- 2026-09-29 (economy, re-measured on the shipped code): `tools/economy.ts` `simulateEconomy`, seeds 1-100, the real content, then the same seeds with `capsules.legendaryCatchUp` off, against the untouched pre-ladder copy.
  - Before the ladder → with the ladder (catch-up on) → with the ladder, catch-up off. Time to max Common 110.3 → 108.5 → 107; Rare 101 → 97.3 → 98.5; Epic 69 → 66 → 66; Legendary 111.5 (p10-p90 94-129, worst 163) → 111.5 (97-124, worst 138) → 113.3 (99-127, worst 138.5).
  - Whole collection (copies done): 209.5 (p90 260, worst 334) → 200 (p90 227, worst 310) → 208.5 (p90 248, worst 313). So the collection finishes about 10 days (5%) sooner, 8.5 of them from catch-up; the ladder alone mostly cuts the unlucky tail.
  - Focused War Plan at L7: 78 → 82.5 → 79. Catch-up aims Legendary copies at the least-maxed Legendaries once all are owned, and the plan's own Legendaries are upgraded first, so they get fewer. Accepted and stated in A6.9: the row misses its 6-week target by far either way and stays a Phase 3 item.
  - Per bag capsule 15.6 → 15.9 copies and 399 → 411 Amber; per day 96.5 → 97.6 copies and 2,981 → 3,036 Amber; Amber done 146 → 143. No year had a Legendary that never maxed in any of the three runs (the prototype's "1 of 100" does not reproduce on the shipped code).
  - DESIGN A6.4 and A6.9, the owner line above and the scratch spec now carry these figures.
- 2026-09-29 (release gate, `tools/drops.ts`): the chi-square checks of one run share the p > 0.01 false-alarm budget (Bonferroni: each passes at p > 0.01 / k, k = the number of chi-square checks in the run, 7 today). With 0.01 per check a correct build failed about k% of runs; the full run on seed 1 failed on the Supply odds at p = 0.0074 although 10^7 direct draws and seeds 2, 3, 101 and 201 showed no bias. A real bias still fails: a Supply Aeon at 20 bp instead of 15 (about 400 instead of 300 in the full run's 200,000 Supply Capsules) gives χ² ≈ 33 and p < 0.00001. Recorded in DESIGN C4.5.
- 2026-09-29 (skin rarity, `tools/drops.ts`): new chi-square per tier that can hold a skin (Gold, Platinum, Aeon) against the Wardrobe weights from `skinMinRarity` up, so a wrong weight or fallback in the capsule skin roll fails the gate.
- 2026-09-29 (exact skin odds on the odds panel): `oddsModel` carries each tier's skin rarity split (`skinRarityBp`, the renormalised Wardrobe weights by largest remainder) and the sheet prints it next to the skin line: Gold "30% chance of a skin: Rare 78%, Epic 18% and Legendary 4%", Aeon "A skin, Epic or better: Epic 81.82% and Legendary 18.18%". DESIGN A6.4 step 7 states the exact 9 : 2 split instead of the rounded 82 / 18.
- 2026-09-29 (catch-up copy): `ui.odds.catchUp` now says what the rule does: "Once every Legendary here is yours, waiting in an unopened capsule or already in this capsule, Legendary stacks favour the ones you are furthest from maxing."
- 2026-09-29 (crafting a promised item): `craftCosmetic` refuses (`pending`) an item that an unopened capsule or crate already holds, for every source, so an Aeon's pre-rolled "an item you don't have yet" never turns into a duplicate refund. The toast says the item is waiting in an unopened capsule or crate.
- 2026-09-29 (legacy notice): `grantLegacySkillAeons` sets `notice.capsuleLadder` whenever it grants at least one Aeon. The save package reads no content, so the v6 migration could not tell a War Path finale; a save whose only trigger was that finale now gets its notice too (v6 itself is frozen). The notice's legacy line is past tense ("was added to your shelf"), so it stays true after the Aeon is opened; the dev fixture holds the granted Aeon.
- 2026-09-29 (onboarding Result): the app's Result draws each earned capsule by its visible tier and crests (`rewardCapsuleLook`: `visibleTier` / `pendingCrests`), and passes that tier to `resultBar`, so a scripted Starter Capsule shows Clay on the reward row and the Open capsule button, as in the show.
- 2026-09-29 (Lite): the capsule host passes `lite: settings.graphics === 'lite'` to the show (the request `capsule-tiers-app-lite.md`), so the Lite preset gets half the particles and a static starfield and sheen. Auto-detected phones on `graphics: 'auto'` keep the full show (the battle renderer's auto rule is not part of the request).
- 2026-09-29 (ring gems): each lit ring gem's face is its tier's key colour, with a dark rim, cel facets and one small glint; the pulsing ring light runs along the groove and stops short of the gem, and a soft glow of the gem's own colour sits behind its bezel. Rendered faces now equal the keys (neighbours ΔE ≥ 11.4, Silver vs Gold 23.7) where the white core and additive glow had made Silver and Gold the same white.
- 2026-09-29 (Gold drum): the ramp's mid-tone and shadow move from #CDB887 / #8A7A5A to #BCA45A / #6B5A2A, and Gold gets its own body gradient (a narrow specular band over the champagne key, then the mid-tone and shadow), so it reads as polished gold and not as ivory. The key stays #EFE0B0 (12.3 from button light, 17.8 from Legendary); the rendered mid-tone samples 12.6-12.8 from the nearest button gold and about 20 from the UI parchment. The key's 8.6 from parchment stays a stated exception (tested).
- 2026-09-29 (phone legibility): crests and summit gems scale with `ornamentScaleFor(stage scale)` (up to 1.5×, a crest shield of at least 14 CSS px; 1.42× at 844 × 390, 1× at 1280 × 720), and a crest stamps once the strike's 220 ms white-out has faded.
- 2026-09-29 (reduce motion): the drum's white-outs (strike, summit impact, burst pop, explosion) go through the same limiter as screen flashes: at most 20%, at most 3 a second, a white-out and a flash in the same frame count as one.
- 2026-09-29 (small icons and chips): the odds sheet's small tier icons (What's inside, the summit rule, the Aeon set rule, the cosmetic tier chips) carry their "★n" crest badge; the cosmetic rarity chips get a lead-in ("Which item it is, by rarity:") and their rarity gems, so they no longer read as more tier chips.
- 2026-09-29 (theme tokens): `--ui-tier-aeon` is #5d3dff (was the pre-ladder violet, ΔE 5 from Epic) and `--ui-tier-gold` / `--ui-tier-platinum` exist; the token test pins all seven.
- 2026-09-29 (War Path Future boss): still Jade in `src/content/raw/warPath.ts`, which the capsule work may not edit. `docs/requests/capsule-tiers-warpath.md` now says it is not applied yet and gives the exact edit and the content test that pins the 8 boss tiers. Until it lands, the one-time totals in A6.4 ("Every source") overstate the War Path by +3,035 Dust-equivalent and +1,240 Amber.

## Power rework: effects and sounds (WP4, WP6, 2026-09-29)

- 2026-09-29 (sizes): power effects are drawn for the battle camera at phone landscape (about 0.65 px per lu at 844 × 390): sweeps, charges and strike impacts were scaled up 1.3-1.8× after the Playwright frame reviews (boulders, dust walls, muzzle flashes, dirt spurts, runners), so one big shape reads per effect.
- 2026-09-29 (buff glow): a capped buff glows only on the caster's 8 frontmost units (`fxUnits.max`), picked like the sim (own-frame p high to low, ties to the lower id), so the art never promises a buff a unit did not get.
- 2026-09-29 (field pulses): a field's first pulse plays the hit (flash, spark, sound); its later pulses only add to one running damage number per unit (`mergeMs` 700 over the 500 ms pulses). Before, a Caltrops cast replayed 16 hit flashes and 16 numbers on every unit.
- 2026-09-29 (jammed mounts): the jam mark stays the render overlay's (readable, team-tinted); the effect under it is the silencing power's own (`power.jammed.<power>`: Undermine's rubble and dust, else the shared `fx.turret_jammed` arcs, sparks and smoke). The jam glow pulses at 0.7 s, never a strobe.
- 2026-09-29 (sounds): the 32 new `pw_*` sounds and `power_cast`, `power_lock`, `turret_jammed` are rendered in `tools/audio/sfx/sounds_powers.py` (imported at the end of `sounds.py`). Each power sound starts on the power's first impact and runs about as long as its effect; the starters render two variants, the War Path powers one (powers reload in 25-60 s). Loudness targets -17 to -21 LUFS (loudest 200 ms), every one within its phone-speaker gap. The effect sheets grew by about 330 KB (1.44 → 1.77 MB Ogg Opus); the size test's budget moved from 1.5 to 2 MB (sheets load per group, lazily; B16's 8 MB total is untouched).
- 2026-09-29 (DESIGN ids): the built effect ids and their parts joined A14.1 and the three power cues joined A13; `fx.reach_band` and `fx.target_pip` stay planned (the render overlay draws the band and pips).

## Power rework: AI v1, second pass (WP3, 2026-09-29)

- 2026-09-29 (scripted casts): a scripted `power` line (Old Grogg, tutorials, tools) casts the first reloaded, affordable slot, Home first, only when that slot's AI option has something to act on, so an auto-aim never meets `powerNoTarget` or `powerOutOfReach`. A due cast waits until then.
- 2026-09-29 (bars kept, larger grid): tier VII vs V, mirrored, draws half: bar 15,000 46%, 12,000 45-46%, 10,000 45-53%, 9,000 50-52% (240 per format), 8,000 51-56%. A 9,000 bar at VII and X gained 2-5 points but raised the tier VII mirror Bell in Short and Standard by 3-5 points and made tier VII cast on targets worth < 200 as often as tier V (the A2.9.9 calibration line asks for at most half), so the A2.9.9 starting bars stay. Engaged chip damage at 1.0 instead of 0.4 and ring reading off were also tried against the exploit proxies: no row moved beyond the noise except the flag ball (about 10 points better without ring reading); both stay as specified.
- 2026-09-29 (measured): tier V mirror Final Bell 42.7 / 27.3 / 15.3% (Short / Standard / Full, 150 each; today 52 / 38.5 / 20.5%), medians 8:08 / 11:03 / 15:12. Hoarder vs tier VII wins 0-1.7% (Full War Bell 80%). The turtle, flag ball and Mono Heavy exploit rows regressed against the pre-rework build (the bot can no longer wipe a defending army from anywhere); they are not moved by the power policy. Numbers and content proposals are in the scratchpad `powers/tuning.md` for the lead; the measured one is Home area starters' `maxTargets` − 1 (Standard Bell 27.3 → 24.7%, turtles −5 to −10 points).

## Capsule show: the timed hammer (WP10, WP6 sounds, owner request 2026-09-29)

- 2026-09-29 (beat, not wait): a strike no longer waits for a tap (1.5 s idle) and no longer lands 90 ms after one. Each strike is one bar of four 200 ms beats: three rising count-in ticks (`cap_strike_tick` at +0, +2, +4 semitones), the hit on the fourth beat (600 ms), 800 ms per strike; summit strikes use the same count-in (hit at 600 ms, 1,020 ms step). A tap can only be timed if the hit is predictable, and a hit that follows the tap is never "in sync". The strike lands on its beat whether or not the player taps, so the idle auto-strike is built in; mashing no longer hurries strikes (hold still fast-forwards, Skip still skips). The untouched show is faster than before (4 × 0.8 s instead of 4 × 2.1 s); a prompt tapper loses 0.8 s over 4 strikes. The Aeon from Clay reaches the pop in 9.6 s (was 8.56 s with prompt taps), all skippable.
- 2026-09-29 (window, `src/capsule/strikeTiming.ts`): Perfect ±60 ms, Good ±140 ms around the hit, centred +30 ms after it. Reasoning: a casual player extrapolating a steady 200 ms count-in taps with about ±50 ms spread (1 SD) on a phone; ±60 is then hit about 2 times in 3 (4 in a row about 1 in 5, so ×4 stays special), ±140 almost always (anyone who tries gets at least Good), and a pure reaction to the hit (≥ 170 ms measured) is outside both, so it is timing, not reflex. Started from the suggested ±70 / ±140: ±70 gave about 84% Perfect (half of all players would get ×4), too easy. The +30 ms centre covers the frame reaching the screen a frame or two late, audio output latency and touch-to-event delay, minus the habit of tapping slightly ahead of a beat. The tap is judged at its event time stamp, sub-frame (`ShowRunner.tap(lateMs)`, at most one frame after the last update). One judged tap per strike from 300 ms before the hit, so mashing cannot farm Perfects (an early tap there is a silent miss).
- 2026-09-29 (honesty): the grade reaches only the view (`ShowView.strikeHit`) and one runner-played sound; it never touches the plan. A test plays three capsules (Silver, Jade from Bronze, Aeon) under six tap patterns (none, all Perfect, all Good, early, too late, mashing, mixed) and asserts identical step traces, tiers, plan cues with their frame times, step start times and total time, and an untouched plan. The flourish is the same size on a climb and a non-climb and uses only the colour the drum already shows (white-hot on summit strikes), the timing ring is neutral warm white, and a miss has no text or sound, so nothing reads as a result or a near miss. The honesty line stays true.
- 2026-09-29 (feel): Perfect = 110 ms hit-stop with the head pinned on the drum (effects creep at 12% through a hit-stop instead of freezing, so a burst born on the frozen frame reads as a burst), a star and glow at the hit, a 30% flash, crack light, two rings and a floor shockwave, sparks, stars and shell chips, a punch, haptics [30, 20, 45], `cap_strike_perfect` (anvil, punch, C7 bell; up a major scale with the combo) and a "Perfect!" pop that springs up during the freeze. Good = about half, "Good!". Combos (cosmetic, this show only) add "×N", a star ring and, from ×4, a back-ray flare. The drum's white-out now fades on real time, so a hit-stop no longer hides a climb's colour for longer. The hammer's pose is a pure function of step time (`src/capsule/blow.ts`): it climbs a notch per tick (the hand lifts it), glints on the third tick, pulls back, swings in 100 ms with a smear and squash, rests 70-120 ms, bounces into the next count-in. A late tap only extends the pin while the head is still on the drum. Reduce motion keeps the ring, beads, ticks and notches; no flash, shake, punch, smear or squash; the pop fades in.
- 2026-09-29 (prompt): "Tap as the hammer lands!" replaces "Tap!", left of the drum (it covered the pips on a phone), shown late in the charge and through the strikes until the first tap on a strike; its looping 700 ms pulse is gone (it fought the 800 ms beat).
- 2026-09-29 (sounds): `cap_strike_tick`, `cap_strike_perfect` and `cap_strike_good` are rendered in `tools/audio/sfx/sounds.py` with ZzFX fallbacks in `src/audio/sounds.ts`; only the capsule sheet changed (`capsule.d5b3e7ce.ogg` / `.m4a`).
- 2026-09-29 (bench): `?autotap=perfect|good|early` and the Auto-tap buttons tap every blow at that timing; the log lists every graded tap; `__capsuleBench.step()` returns the step and its time for scripts.
- 2026-09-30 (review fixes): (1) Tapping along with the count-in always missed: the 300 ms lock made the tap on the third tick the judged tap (a miss). The lock is now 180 ms (just outside Good, after the third tick) and mashing is stopped by a tap-rate rule instead: a judged tap less than 150 ms after the previous tap is a miss (`STRIKE_WINDOW.minGapMs`, show time). Tested: taps at 0, 200, 400 and 600 ms give Perfect on every strike; mashing still gives a miss on every strike. (2) A graded hit on a non-climb borrowed a climb's signals and read as a near miss: it no longer pops the pip, flashes the screen (a local additive bloom at the hit point instead), leaves extra crack light (a pulse below a climb's +0.35 that fades within about 250 ms, `CapsuleDrum.pulseLeak`) or raises the drum's energy. (3) Most Perfects landed after the hammer had left: the ungraded pin is now 100 ms (`latencyMs + perfectMs + 10`), climb 110, Good 110, Perfect 130. (4) No full-screen flash for a grade at all. (5) The pop sits high above the drum, clear of its silhouette and crest; the graded rings stay at the hit point (no floor shockwave); the ×4 back-ray flare is gone (the rays are the climb's). (6) The prompt is "Tap on the beat!" (a reaction to the landing is outside the window). (7) The hammer tips about 0.16 rad further over the drum on each tick (0.34, 0.18, 0.02, -0.13), so its notches read on a phone. (8) The window centre moves later by half the AudioContext's `outputLatency` (or `baseLatency`), at most +25 ms so the window stays inside the strike; read at show start from the service's context when it exposes one. (9) The climb's colour wipe runs on real time through a graded hit-stop; every tap on a blow gets a neutral tick on the timing ring (no text, no penalty). The bench's `autotap=early` taps at -160 ms (inside the new lock).

## Power rework: review fixes (fixer, 2026-09-30)

- 2026-09-30 (mask at every hit, `SIM_VERSION` 4.1.0): a unit already in a cast's `hitIds` could still be hit after it was pushed or walked past the home line (barrage blasts, sweeps, charges and fields picked by the eligible list alone). `inCastArea()` (`sim/systems/powers.ts`) now checks the reach area at every hit (a charge checks body overlap). The cast hash (`sim/hashState.ts`) now covers every cast field (side, power, start, zone, level, telegraph end, end, cost, hit counts, runner hits). Golden replays re-recorded deliberately: results and commands unchanged, only hashes.
- 2026-09-30 (anti-stall AI): the stall exploits came from tier VII banking forever against a Home power turtle. A bot now banks only what its free population can spend (25 gold per free pop) and a wave launched at the pop cap ends only at half its peak value. Tried and dropped: counter-goal changes and a reserve gate on the approach (Mono Heavy 95-100%), faster float growth (mirror Bell 41-53%), charge cap 4 (flag ball worse), Iron Horse and Comet Run damage cuts (Standard mirror Bell 20 → 32%).
- 2026-09-30 (AI powers): buffs are valued over the 8 frontmost units in contact or within 600 lu behind one (the wave about to join), with `aiValueBp` 7,000 (Nanite Surge 8,000); controls 4,500 (snare) / 5,000 (pull, stun). Before, buffs almost never passed the bar and snares and pulls were rare. Suppress needs 80 lu of slack past the reach line and skips a charging Last Stand (a knockback no longer turns it into `powerOutOfReach`). Bait bank 500 → 300 and an unscouted full Home ring counts as loaded: the bait never fired before; it now fires in every test match.
- 2026-09-30 (caps and numbers): the per-cast army share and the largest cast were high, so Home caps came down: bombards 4, sweeps 5 (Solar Flare 4, Boiling Oil 3), Stasis Field 6. Weak Home sweeps got damage back: Rockslide 150, Tidal Wave 170, Strafing Run 360, Arrow Storm 55, Point Defense Grid 125; Solar Flare 560. Ion Cannon and Sniper Team reload 25 s (were below their band). DESIGN A5.7 updated.
- 2026-09-30 (UI): the HUD denial names the slot tapped; a Home drag no longer edge-scrolls past the reach band and the camera returns after the drag; the power tip and the token label stay on screen; clumped target pips fan into rows above the mount buttons; the War Plan Army panel treats the Field slot as "Coming soon" while `FIELD_SLOT_IN_BATTLE` is false (now in `core/powerReach.ts`, re-exported by `meta/rules.ts`) and its slot chip shows the slot glyph and short name.
- 2026-09-30 (War Path grants): `grantWarPathPower` and `warPathPowerOf` in `meta/powers.ts` (power, or 60 Amber when owned; Stone L5 opens the Field slot); the call in `meta/warPath.ts` is requested in `docs/requests/powers-warpath-grants.md`. Until then a ladder bot uses a War Path power only when the player cleared the level and owns it.
- 2026-09-30 (tools): the A2.9.12 proxies (`power_hoarder`, `home_turtle`, `power_spam`, `drop_spam`, `runner_reach`, `no_power`, `plain_wave`, `bait_wave`, `gate_sniper`) and power metrics (share of gold and kills, army share per cast, largest cast, casts per stay, value per gold), static family budgets and the Flak and Suppress situational setups. `save_counter` reads the covered zone through the reach, cap and screen (`coveredZone`), not the raw best zone.
- 2026-09-30 (measured, tier V mirror 200 per format): Final Bell 39.0 / 25.5 / 14.5% (Short / Standard / Full), medians 8:03 / 11:11 / 15:24; power share of gold 13.5 / 13.3 / 14.3%; of kills 17.9 / 23.5 / 18.8% (target 5-12%); army share per cast p50 48 / 58 / 44% (target ≤ 40%); largest cast p99 300 / 350 / 363; casts per stay median 1 / 2 / 2. Tier VII vs V (200 pairs): 56.8% Short, 58.0% Standard (was 46-49%; target 60%).
