Proxies vs tier VII (100 each, Short / Standard / Full): power_hoarder 0 / 0 / 14% (Bell 0 / 2 / 53%), home_turtle 3.5 / 13.5 / 34% (Bell 15 / 48 / 80%), power_spam, drop_spam, runner_reach ≤ 2.5%, no_power 31.5 / 19.5 / 40%, fall-back turtle 12.5 / 47 / 50.5% (Bell 47 / 96 / 100%), tech turtle 1.5 / 29.5 / 48.5%, flag ball 51.5 / 52.5 / 66%, Mono Heavy 71-73.5% (the last two failed before the rework too). Bait minus plain wave vs the hoarder +1 / -9.5 / +0.5 points: still not a skill edge.

## Owner decision 2026-09-30: back to the cartoon style; 1v1 first

- **Art:** the owner prefers the earlier cartoon style over the realistic restyle. The realistic Stone Age sheets are reverted to the cartoon sheets (public/art and unitSheets.gen.ts from 304040b), KO stars and cartoon dust return. The realistic Blender pipeline stays in art/blender/styles/realistic/ but is parked. New direction: keep the cartoon style and raise its quality: better animation (anticipation, squash and stretch, follow-through), more detail per figure, and distinct attack styles per unit.
- **Home:** the new Home layout is liked, but the War Path looks too simple and uninteresting. The 1v1 battle (the future online 2-player mode, ladder today) must be the first and primary thing on Home; the War Path against bots becomes a secondary, offline way to play and earn cards and currency, and its map needs a much richer look.
- The realistic restyle workflow is cancelled.

## Owner direction 2026-09-30: natural attacks and movement

Every figure gets the attack and movement a viewer expects from its body and weapon. The Mammoth Matriarch gores with its tusks, and the riders in its howdah throw light projectiles (the existing small rider attack, visual only). Recorded as an addendum to the cartoon upgrade plan.

## Owner request 2026-09-30: stance as three buttons

The stance control is three direct buttons, left to right Fall back ("Back"), Hold and Charge, instead of one button with a flyout. The current stance is lit; the 3 s change cooldown and its "Ready in" reason are unchanged; keys S and Shift+S are unchanged. HUD budget e2e passes at 844x390, 844x340, 800x360 and 1280x720.

## Home: the 1v1 Battle hub and a richer War Path map (owner decision 2026-09-30, WP9)

Built from the owner decision above ("the new Home layout is okay; the path looks far too simple; the 1v1 screen must be the first thing you see; the path against bots is a side thing").

- **Home is the Battle hub** (`src/ui/screens/home/HomeScreen.tsx`, `hub.tsx`, `Diorama.tsx`, `hub.css`). It keeps the liked frame (transparent top bar, full-bleed scene, a plate over the big button bottom-right, five tabs) and puts the 1v1 battle in it: the arena diorama in the centre (your base and the AI's as the real base pictures through the ArtProvider, `BaseLook`; the island, landmark and props are code-drawn chrome), the arena ribbon and the trophy bar under it, the match plate (opponent portrait with the AI badge and tier, the format picker from Arena 2) over **Battle** (the only primary), Modes beside it, and on the left the Campaign card and four capsule slots.
- **Battle** starts a Ladder match in the picked format in one tap (`ui-ladderFormat.<id>` flags remember the format). While the onboarding runs it starts the onboarding matches instead (step 0: match 1 vs Old Grogg, step 2: match 2 vs Pip), which stay War Path Stone L1 and L2 as before; the plate says "Training match" and names their General with the AI badge.
- **The online slot.** The match plate is where the online opponent will show once online play exists (A18.10), so that mode needs no redesign. No "Online (coming soon)" control is shown: a disabled control for something that does not exist is noise (U8) and a promise (U15). Revisit when M2 (Friend Duel) is close.
- **Tabs:** `TabId` `warPath` became `battle` (Home, centre, crossed swords). The War Path map is the sub-screen route `warPath` (S2c, A9 #22; `src/ui/screens/warPath/WarPathScreen.tsx`) with Back, reached from the Campaign card and the Progress stars row. A War Path result's Continue (and the capsule summary after it) returns to the map, so the level ceremony (MR-41/42) still plays; Home resets stay Home.
- **Unlocks by wins, not levels** (ui-plan 2.6): features open with wins in any mode, `max(beaten War Path levels, stats.wins)`, so the optional path never gates tabs. The Ladder opens when the onboarding ends (step 4; content `unlocks.ladder` is now 2, the two onboarding matches); `daily` moved from 7 to 6. A UI-only `campaign` unlock (the Campaign card) also opens at the end of the onboarding and has its own MR-40 moment. Order: army, ladder, capsules, campaign, modes, customize, progress, daily; at most one unlock moment per visit to Home. `meta/warPath.ts featureUnlocked` mirrors the rule (held equal by `warPathModel.test.ts`). `reachedAges` opens the Ladder format's ages with the Ladder, so a new player's Army shows the Short War ages after the onboarding.
- **Modes panel:** the Ladder card is gone (Battle is the Ladder); Quick Battle, Daily, Skirmish and Conquest stay.
- **Copy:** Pillar 4 forbids the word "offline" in strings (`tests/integrity/copyReview.test.ts`), so the Campaign card says "Solo battles vs AI, earn cards" and its unlock line "Campaign: solo battles, earn cards".
- **Richer map** (`regionScenery.tsx`, `mapDeco.tsx`, `WarPathMap.tsx`, `regionArt.tsx`): per region hills, water (lake with reeds, shore with boats, river) and two landmark set pieces; lanterns between nodes (lit on the way walked); a near layer (parallax 1.3) of dark plant and rock silhouettes; bird flocks. Node kinds from the data (`nodeKind`): boss (portrait in a lair with spiked banners and torches), elite (Hard shield with crossed swords), treasure (a chest; L3's named card), story (a scroll; `teaches`), battle. The current node has a bouncing marker; a newly opened node bursts its padlock (shards, ring, dust) as it drops (MR-41). Everything code-drawn and still under reduce motion.
- **Fixes on the way:** the map measures its layout size (`clientWidth/Height`), not the painted box, so a screen's enter transform no longer shrinks it; SVG groups that carry both a `transform` attribute and a CSS animation now nest the animation one level down (the CSS transform was replacing the placement, which threw the Gunpowder skyline ship to the corner).
- **Checks:** unit tests updated (interactions, screens, router, strings, War Path model, army, foundations); e2e boot, onboarding, flows, modes and ux pass (19/19); the ui-budget spec passes for home, home-new, home-maxed, home-first, home-campaign, warPath, warPath-new, level-preview, level-locked and modes at all four viewports.

## Cartoon art pass v2, Stone Age (owner decision 2026-09-30, art)

Built from the art director plan (`art/blender` cartoon kit v2). Stone Age first: all 8 units, the 4 turrets and the Cave Hold.

- **Shared kit, opt-in.** `ageborn_art/moves.py` (timing tables that keep every shipped `durationMs`, attack `impactAt` and die `fx` time; walk v2 with a down and an up frame and a foot lift that reads at 1x; idle v2 with a weight shift and a blink; hit variants by mass; death styles D1 fling-and-spin, D3 dizzy sit, D4 legs-up flop with a `roll` for a heavy side topple), `ageborn_art/face.py` (camera-facing decals found by ray casts: lids, squeeze, X and spiral eyes, grit, yell, O and KO-tongue mouths), `ageborn_art/smear2.py` (2D smears painted over the finished frame: arc, ring, claw, streak, dust, impact lines, rings). A unit opts in with `NO_RETIME = True` and `moves.clip(..., overlays=...)`; units that do not opt in render exactly as before. `world/common.turret_module` got optional `idle_frames=6` and `overlays`.
- **Every Stone unit attacks its own way:** Bonker hop-up smash with a club bounce, Pebbler sling windmill (ring smear) and side-arm release, Tuskback hoof scrape and gore toss, Spear Hunter leaping fish-stab, Drum Shaman double beat that bounces the stone, Sabertooth rear-up claw rake and bite, Mammoth trumpet rear and double stomp with a dust crescent, Training Dummy spring-back bonk.
- **Timing is frozen:** `node art/blender/check_timing.mjs [ref]` compares `unitSheets.gen.ts` durations and `impactAt` with a git ref; it passes against HEAD and 2a3b637. The static muzzle anchors of Pebbler, Drum Shaman and Mammoth moved a few lu because they follow the new release poses (visual only).
- **Budget:** Stone unit sheets 4.29 MB -> 4.81 MB (+11.9%); every unit stays within +25% (Pebbler +23% is the largest). Team coverage >= 18% and the colour rule hold on every frame.
- **Runtime:** `atlas.ts` still applies the realistic rigid-body rules to these sheets; filed `docs/requests/wp4-cartoon-runtime-motion.md`.

## Cartoon art pass v2, Stone Age: art director review (2026-09-30, art)

Review of the Stone pilot against the plan and the owner direction; fixed and re-rendered.

- **Mammoth Matriarch attacks with a tusk gore** (owner direction above; the pilot had shipped the plan table's trumpet rear and double stomp): a paw and stomp as the anticipation, the head held low with the tusks near the ground, then a lunge that hooks the tusks up and forward (ivory smear, impact lines, trunk flung up, bellow). The front rider winds his rock up behind his head and throws it on the impact beat with a throw smear; the back rider ducks and cheers. The riders are drawn 1.22x so they read at phone size. Timing is unchanged (heavy melee table, impactAt 0.4634).
- **Mammoth death:** the kept D4 steps skip the edge-on roll frame (it rendered as a flat pill); the riders leap clear in the first two frames.
- **Bonker's held extreme** is the club cocked high behind his head (it hung behind his heels, which read as dragging, not smashing); the dip no longer covers his face.
- **Whirl smears are hollow rings** (`smear2` arc `band`): Pebbler's sling whirl painted a solid grey disc that read as a shield at game size.
- **D3 dizzy sit** now changes the silhouette (lower, legs out, leaning back, arms wide, head lolling) for the Drum Shaman and the Training Dummy; before it looked like the idle pose with spiral eyes.
- Rules for the next ages are in the art director's style notes (scratchpad `cartoon/STYLE_NOTES.md`); the durable ones are folded into `art/blender/README.md`.

## Army per age, the Card Album and battle backdrop skins (owner request 2026-09-30, WP9, WP4, WP7, WP8, WP1)

Owner (Danish, verbatim in the task): skins for the battle background; per age, easier to see the soldiers in battle, the ones not picked and, below them, the ones not unlocked; a simpler card picker that shows what is missing; a long-scroll page like a Pokedex per age.

- **Army per age, three sections top to bottom** (ui-plan 4.2, DESIGN A18.9.3): a fixed **In battle** band (the ten slots in one row, grouped Troops | Turrets | Powers with a count each, "Troops 5/6"; the advisor and the class lines at its ends), then one scrolling pool with **Available** (owned, not in battle) and **Locked** (not found, greyed silhouettes with a short source line and "You own 13 of 17 Bronze cards"). The band never scrolls, so every slot stays a tap or drop target. Model: `ui/screens/model/armyAge.ts` (`ageSections`, `cardSourceShort`, `capsuleArenaFor`).
- **Removed from Army:** the "This age / All cards" switch, the class, rarity, owned and sort filters and their chips (the filter sheet), and the "In your army" copy of the loadout inside the grid. Every age's cards live in the Card Album; the owner asked for a simpler picker, and an age has at most 17 cards, which fit on one screen without filters. The strings they used left `ui.en.json` (the strings test forbids unused keys).
- **Slot-to-slot moves** replace "pick a card that is already in the army from the grid": select a slot, tap another slot of the same kind to swap (the existing gesture); in-battle cards are no longer in the pool.
- **Touch drag from the pool up to the band** keeps `axis: 'x'`: a vertical swipe scrolls the pool (the e2e check), a drag starts sideways and then goes anywhere. Tap and Use stay the primary path on phones.
- **Phone budget at 844 x 390:** band slots 58 px (pool cards stay 62), the band's title shares the Troops label row, the advisor sits at the band's right end, so the first pool row is fully visible under the band. Text stays at 12 px or more outside `[data-tag]`.
- **Honest sources:** a troop or turret of an age the current arena does not drop yet says "Capsules · Arena N" (the first arena whose `dropAges` hold it), never plain "Time Capsules".
- **The Card Album** replaces the Collection route's Cards grid (ui-plan 4.3, DESIGN A9 #10): one long scroll grouped by age with sticky age headers (the offset under the sticky bar is measured, because the bar wraps on phones), numbered tiles (album order: ages, then troops, turrets, powers; numbers never change with filters; `ui/screens/model/dex.ts`), missing cards as a dark silhouette with "?" and the full source line, Have / Missing / All, a Filters sheet (rarity, class), age jump chips that light up for the age in view (rAF-throttled passive scroll listener), and `content-visibility: auto` per age for smooth scrolling. The sticky bar uses opaque colours (a 98% alpha bar showed the scrolled tiles through it in Chromium). The Collection header's "62/88 cards" pill left (it counted troops and turrets only and contradicted the album's 110/136). The album and the Backdrops tab are strict in the UI budget e2e.
- **Backdrop skins: a new cosmetic collection `backdrop`** (DESIGN A18.9.4): 10 themes (Golden Dusk and Harvest Moon Common; Winterfall, Blossom Spring, Starry Night Rare; Lantern Festival, Thunderstorm, Ember Sky Epic; Northern Lights and Eclipse Legendary). Sources follow the other collections: the Time Capsule pool (Common to Epic; its Legendary odds are 0), the Wardrobe Crate pool (Rare to Legendary), Trophy Road nodes 2,100 (Ember Sky) and 3,500 (Eclipse); pool items are craftable with Dust. No starter: the classic skies are the default (`defaults.backdrop: null`). Adding pool items changes which item a given seed rolls, not the disclosed odds.
- **One backdrop for every age**, not one per age like base skins: a theme restyles whatever age your half shows (a Winterfall castle, a Winterfall city), so one pick reads through every evolve and the collection stays small enough to finish.
- **Your half only.** `SideLook.backdrop` per side; the battle view passes `skins: { left, right }` to `createBackdrop`. AI bots keep the classic sky (`botLook` sets null), so a theme always marks the player's half; the seam blends a skinned and a classic half exactly like two ages (a piece now carries age and skin, and two looks of the same age cross-fade).
- **Rendering (B5):** manifest entries `backdrop.<age>@<id>` (like unit skins); an unknown skin warns once and draws the classic sky. The procedural tier re-grades the age's own layer textures once per layer, age and theme (sky gradient, glow, sun, moon, harvest moon or eclipse, stars, aurora; a colour grade on the far and mid layers; props along the silhouette rims: snow, blossom, autumn speckles, festival lights), keyed by the base texture so a pre-rendered layer that arrives later gets its own themed copy. The themed sky is repainted at 2x its base resolution so stars and moons stay crisp. Sky features sit low (y about -250 lu) because phones show only about 290 lu of sky over the lane.
- **Weather** (`backdropWeather.ts`) lives in the backdrop root over the mid-ground and under the ground, so it never covers a unit, bar or effect; it spawns only over the visible part of its own half and fades across the seam; at most 90 particles (40 on Lite, half the rate); Reduce motion slows it and turns lightning off. Lightning is two soft pulses (a 16% sky veil), never a strobe, every 5.5-11.5 s.
- **Colour rule for themes:** in-band team hues in a theme's large areas (sky, grade, glow, clouds) stay at or under 40% HSV saturation unless the colour is dark (value at or under 40%, night skies); a test checks every theme. Weather and rim colours are small accents.
- **Customize → Backdrops** (between Bases and Flags; ui-plan 4.5): the preview is the lane's own painting (`cosmeticImageUrl(key, { age })` paints sky, far, mid and ground at preview scale with the same painters, a PNG cached per age and theme; `{ layer: 'fx' }` is the weather as an animated SVG, still with Reduce motion) with your base in front. Locked backdrops can be tried on (the only collection where a locked tile reacts, via `ItemTile.onPreview`; it is never equipped). A headless emblem SVG (`backdropIconSvg`) serves tests and any context without a canvas.
- **Save v8:** `cosmetics.equipped.backdrop` (null); the migration never invents a look and keeps a value already there; the replay schema accepts `look.backdrop` (presentation only, hashes skip it). New frozen fixture `v8.json`. `meta/rules.ts` SAVE_VERSION 8.
- **Dev:** `?dev=1#gallery/section=world&bdl=<id>&bdr=<id>` and the sandbox's `&backdrop=<id>` show a skin in the lane; `#screens/customize-backdrops`.
- **Open:** the arena ground is shared by both halves and stays unthemed (a snowy lane would need a per-half ground split); the VS screen does not show the backdrop yet; the pre-rendered 3D layers (Future) are re-graded like the painted ones but the Customize preview always paints the code-drawn layers.

## Home 1v1 hub: review fixes (fixer, 2026-09-30, WP9)

- **The plate shows the opponent Battle fights.** `UiServices.previewOpponent(format)` picks with the format (the ladder seed is `ladder:<format>`), memoised per save and format and built like `prepareMatch` (base look included). A test checks preview = VS for every format.
- **The AI's base no longer flips**: the mirror sits on the picture inside `.hd__base--foe`, and one wrapper (`.hd__bob`) floats the island, bases and troops together.
- **Unlock moments wait for the forced first upgrade** (`firstUpgradePending`); the Ladder and the War Path, which open together, share one moment (two rings, one line). The start hint hides while a moment shows, and the line lifts off the trophy bar (`data-unlock-avoid`).
- **One name: War Path.** The Home card is "War Path · Solo campaign vs AI" (slate coin, stars only, about 30% shorter, no gold). "Army: more ages" says "Climb arenas or win War Path levels". Presets open with the first boss or with 10 wins in any mode.
- **Format line**: the picker names the format, so the line is only "5 ages, about 10 min." (two lines allowed, never clipped). Long AI names wrap to two lines on the plate.
- **Arena scene**: each side's first two War Plan troops stand in the lane (ArtProvider portraits, `usePortrait(..., { side })`; the AI's in its team colour, mirrored). They breathe, the back pair taunts with a hop, and the front pair makes a near-clash with a spark every 6.4 s. The AI's base is painted in its team colour (`BaseLook side={1}`), and the diorama shows the furthest reached age of the chosen format. On large screens the island may reach 180 px into the side columns' empty upper space. First launch centres the stage on the screen.
- **Map**: the plate hides fully while panning or while the current level is off the view. The "Back to level N" chip sits in the plate's row. Region names ride along the left edge under the nodes. Far landmarks shrink to stay under the top bar. The road winds through more of the band (amplitude up to 2.2 nodes) and comes in from the left past a start camp. The ground gets fields (the farming ages), rock patches, worn earth and trodden road shoulders, with fewer repeated props.
- **Open (request filed):** some card portraits are framed as busts (the Footman), so a bust in the lane fades into the dust at its lower edge. A full-body pose for portraits is requested in `docs/requests/wp4-fullbody-portrait.md`.

## Bronze Age cartoon rollout (art, 2026-09-30)

Owner direction: keep the cartoon style; better animation, more detail and a natural attack per figure. Scope: `art/blender/units/{hoplite,javelineer,phalangite,standard_bearer,war_chariot,scorpion,bronze_colossus}.py`, `ageborn_art/rigs_bronze.py`, `world/{turrets,base}_bronze.py`, the installed sheets in `public/art/{units,turrets}/bronze/` and `public/art/bases/bronze.*`, and `src/visuals/unitSheets.gen.ts` (regenerated). Every clip `durationMs`, attack `impactAt` and die FX time is unchanged (`check_timing.mjs` passes against HEAD and 2a3b637).

- **Natural attacks:** Hoplite shoves with the shield and stabs over the rim; Javelineer skips, leans back and whips the javelin, then draws a new one from his case; Phalangite feints, coils and drives the sarissa; Standard Bearer swings the eagle standard forward while flicking a dart underhand and shouting; War Chariot gathers, lunges and slashes as it passes, the scythed hubs spinning; Scorpion cranks three times, aims (held), releases with a hop and reloads from a bolt rack; Bronze Colossus lifts a foot to its chest (shield high, sword out) and quake-stomps (its card ability is Stomp).
- **Shared bronze helpers** in `rigs_bronze.py`: `face_kit` (face.py expressions on the bronze head), `die_d2` (stiff backwards topple, the KO face stays visible; face-first was rejected because it hides the X eyes), `hit_armoured` (dip behind the shield, the helmet clanks down), `helmet(joint=..., crest_secondary=...)` so a helmet can sit on its own joint and pop off. `moves.py` was not changed; Medieval can reuse `die_d2`/`hit_armoured`.
- **Muzzle anchors moved (visual only):** Javelineer [51.9, 7.9] to [58.2, 14.1], Standard Bearer [40.6, 13.7] to [41.1, 30], Scorpion [64.8, 51] to [66.2, 48.5]; the Archer Tower's archer is drawn 1.3x and its arrow tip (the turret muzzle) moved with him; the Gorgon's face turns 50 degrees toward the camera and its gaze muzzle moved with it.
- **Team coverage fixes:** the Javelineer's case and cloak tail and a caparison on the chariot horse are team-coloured, so every frame keeps at least 18%.
- **Base crumble:** a hole in the lowest tier (75%), a burning breach in the second tier (50%), a big burning breach high on the third tier and the summit shrine toppled (25%).
- **Sizes:** unit sheets +10% for the age (per unit +4% to +14%), within the +25% budget.

## Cartoon art pass v2, Medieval Age (2026-09-30, art)

- **Every Medieval unit authors its own clips** (`NO_RETIME`, `moves.py` timing tables); all durations and attack `impactAt` are unchanged (`check_timing.mjs` passes against HEAD and 2a3b637). Shared Medieval helpers live in a new `art/blender/ageborn_art/kit_medieval.py` so the `rigs_medieval.py` API that the Bronze rigs import stays as it is.
- **Attacks, picked by what the weapon and body do naturally** (owner addendum): Footman a guard and a flat horizontal cleave from behind the shield (the arm is swung about the vertical axis); Pikeman a plant-and-drive two-handed thrust from a deep brace, the pike quivering after; Longbowman a high-arc volley (drawn and released 28 degrees up, string twang, reload from the quiver); Friar an underhand pendulum lob with the sling and a belly-bump hop (it had the same overhead whirl as the Pebbler); Destrier Knight a couched lance charge (the horse gathers on its haunches, bursts forward with the forelegs out); Battering Ram a crew heave-ho, the log now swinging level on two parallel chains; Ursa Paladin a bear rear-and-rake with a wide flat hammer sweep toward the viewer, so the cleave reads.
- **Deaths:** D2 plank topple for the armoured infantry (Footman face first, Pikeman backwards), the helmet rolling off; D1 for the Longbowman; D3 dizzy sit for the Friar; D5 unhorsed for the Destrier Knight and the Ursa Paladin (the rider is thrown off the back and lands behind; the mount flops on its belly; the knight's lance clatters to the ground); D7 wreck for the Ram (the crewman pops out of the roof hatch).
- **Detail:** the face kit on every unit (the horse and the bear get the eye marks; knights keep their great helms), a parchment bear paw on shields, tabards, the pennon, the caparison, the barding, the ram's roof and the keep banner; shaped horse and bear legs (knees, hocks, fetlocks, paws with claws); a visible Ram crew (legs between the wheels, a crewman in a roof hatch).
- **Projectile start points moved (visual only):** the Longbowman's `muzzle` is [25.6, 54.5] (was [34, 35.3]; the arrow leaves the raised bow) and the Friar's is [31.1, 47.7] (was [42.3, 28.7]; the stone leaves the top of the lob). The Trebuchet's per-frame muzzle follows its sling (it whips over the top on the release).
- **Turrets and base:** a Medieval masonry plinth (mortar lines, merlons, a team banner) for all four turrets, 6-frame idle loops and 2D accents; the Keep's crumble stages now read at a glance (lost merlons; fire, soot, a torn banner; a breach, askew roofs, the banner hanging by one corner). Portraits for the seven units were regenerated from the new frames (`gen_portraits.py`).

## Army per age, Card Album and backdrop skins: review fixes (fixer, 2026-09-30, WP9, WP4, WP1)

- **Locked is always in view on Army.** Both section heads are sticky inside the pool. Locked's head is a direct child of the pool (its section is `display: contents`), so it pins to the pool's bottom while its grid is below the fold and to its top while the grid scrolls. On phones (`max-height: 480px`) Locked starts as one 44 px row (count, owned line, small silhouettes, chevron, Album); a tap opens the grid and scrolls to it. When every card of the age is found, the row is not pinned. The pool is `isolation: isolate`, so a slot's action bar from the band stays above the sticky heads.
- **Card Album entry points.** The collection route takes `age` and `own`. Army's header has an album button with the found count that opens the album at the selected age. Locked's Album button opens it at that age on Missing. The Progress tab has a Card Album row. The album jumps to the age on open and repeats the jump for a few frames while lazily rendered sections get their real heights.
- **In battle mark.** A check shows only when every open slot is filled and the age tab is clean. "!" (amber or red) matches the age tab, and neutral crossed swords show while slots are open. The advisor's first warning is a short amber chip (`ui.armyAge.issue.*`, without the age, because the tab names it) on the band's header row. The full sentence is the chip's label and stays in the advice sheet. The chip is one line on desktop and at most two on phones, with "+N" on its corner. Army slots stay 88 px on large screens, so the chip has room.
- **Names never overlap.** The phone Army grid has a 16 px column gap, and names get at most half of it on each side. Album columns are the card plus 12 px each side, and names use that room.
- **Age tabs that do not fit** fade at that edge under a 44 px arrow button that pages the strip.
- **Album fixes.** A jump lands exactly under the sticky bar (`scroll-margin-top` adds back the bar's `--ui-s3` sticky offset). A Legendary's number moves left of its crown.
- **Fixed album numbers** (`src/content/album.ts`). No. is a card's place in a frozen list, and the list keeps today's numbers. A new card is appended, and a retired id keeps its entry. A content test checks that every card has exactly one number.
- **Backdrop stills are painted lazily.** `BackdropLook` paints at most one still per animation frame through a shared queue, and the big preview goes first. Tiles show a shimmer until painted. The painter themes only the preview crop, keeps the themed layers of the last four age and theme pairs, and paints tiles as a smaller, sky-heavy crop (`thumb`). An age switch in Customize now blocks for about 10-40 ms instead of about 300 ms.
- **Backdrop tiles.** Locked tiles keep their colour (slightly dimmed) under the padlock. The one tried on gets a gold ring and a "Trying on" tag.
- **Themed skies on phones.** A themed sky slides down by `themedSkyLift(above)`, up to 120 lu, so its sun, moon, eclipse or aurora sits at about 58% of the visible sky, under the HUD. Desktop views do not move. Classic skies never move. Northern Lights also lights the far hilltops with soft mint curtains (source-atop), because on phones the hills fill most of the sky.
- **Themed ground.** The arena ground is re-graded per theme (`themeGround`: 42% of the hills' grade, so units keep their contrast, plus frost and soft drifts for snow themes and scattered petals or leaves). The lane lays it over the classic ground on the themed half only and fades it across the seam in strips, including the mirrored ground copy.
- **VS** shows your equipped, owned backdrop behind your half under a wash of your team colour (dev: `#screens/vs-backdrop`). The AI's half keeps its colours.

## Cartoon art pass v2, Industrial Age (2026-09-30, art)

- **Every Industrial unit authors its own clips** (`NO_RETIME`, `moves.py` timing tables, new `ageborn_art/kit_industrial.py`). All clip durations and attack `impactAt` are unchanged (`check_timing.mjs` passes against HEAD and 2f31a96); the Flare Spotter keeps its 1200 ms idle and the Land Dreadnought its custom 1100 ms "crew bails out" death.
- **Attacks, picked by what the weapon and body do naturally** (owner addendum): Riveter a rising golf swing with the wrench (coiled low, swung under and up in a tilted plane, CLANG); Carbineer a snap shot, then flips the carbine up and throws the lever (casing arcs away); Steam Golem cocks the ram (gauge needle into the red), jabs the piston forward and down with a streak smear, steam jet and dust; Harpoon Gunner braces, fires (the cable snakes out after the harpoon), kicks back and cranks a reel under the barrel twice; Flare Spotter fires the flare overhead, then shouts (rings) with the pistol lowered; Sapper strikes a match on his helmet, lights the bundle, slams it on the target (blast), ducks with his hands over his ears and pulls a fresh charge; Land Dreadnought squats on its springs, fires, rocks back and forward, crew squint on the shot and cheer after.
- **Deaths and hits:** D1 fling and spin with the hat and the wrench flying off (Riveter, Carbineer, Harpoon Gunner, Sapper with a sooty face); D3 dizzy sit for the Flare Spotter; D6 fall-apart for the Steam Golem (the dome pops off, the knees sag, the ram arm drops); light hits with squeezed eyes; mech jolt with a popped rivet and a spark for the Golem; the Dreadnought crew ducks on a hit.
- **Detail:** the face kit on every biped, a cream cog emblem on team slabs (bib, coat skirt, turret signs), a tool belt with a swinging hammer and a rag, a neckerchief and spurs, a rubber apron and a reel, a map case and a whistle, a live pressure gauge on the Golem, hazard stripes, a team lifebuoy, a shovel and soot on the Dreadnought. The Steam Golem is turned less toward the camera (yaw -24, was -41) so the punch reads sideways; its furnace face moved on the boiler to stay in view.
- **Projectile start points moved (visual only):** Carbineer `muzzle` [41.1, 23.5] (was [40.8, 25.4]), Harpoon Gunner [25.2, 24.7] (was [24.9, 26.3]), Flare Spotter [3.7, 64.2] (was [13.8, 59]; the flare leaves the raised pistol overhead), Land Dreadnought [86.3, 119] (was [89.1, 115.9]).
- **Turrets and base:** an Industrial works plinth (brick footing, team-enamelled cap with a hazard edge, a team sign with a cog) for the Gatling Gun, Boiler Mortar and Tesla Tower; 6-frame idle loops and 2D accents on all four; the Gatling shows one flash per shot (it showed two), the Mortar Pit gets a loader who drops the shell and ducks, the Boiler Mortar a gauge needle that swings into the red and a safety-valve jet, the Tesla Tower a charge ring and a zap burst. The Foundry's crumble stages now read at a glance: a bitten cornice and a steam leak (75%), fires in the hall, dark windows and soot (50%), a breach with a hanging girder, a fire at the tower, the roof lantern caved in and the chimney snapped (25%).
- **Sizes:** unit sheets +9% for the age (per unit +2% to +19%), within the +25% budget; turret sheets grow 28-47% (47-59 KB to 50-77 KB each) for the 6-frame idles, the base sheet 42 to 59 KB.


## Gunpowder Age cartoon rollout (art, 2026-09-30)

Owner direction: keep the cartoon style; better animation, more detail and a natural attack per figure. Scope: `art/blender/units/{corsair,fusilier,cuirassier,grenadier,field_surgeon,bronze_cannon,balloon_admiral}.py`, a new `ageborn_art/kit_gunpowder.py`, `world/{turrets,base}_gunpowder.py`, the installed sheets in `public/art/{units,turrets}/gunpowder/` and `public/art/bases/gunpowder.*`, the seven portraits, and `src/visuals/unitSheets.gen.ts` (regenerated). Every clip `durationMs`, attack `impactAt` and die FX time is unchanged (`check_timing.mjs` passes against HEAD and 2a3b637). `rigs_gunpowder.py` is untouched (Bronze and Industrial units import it); `turrets_gunpowder.py` keeps `recoil_idle`/`recoil_fire` for `turrets_modern.py`.

- **Natural attacks:** Corsair flicks his grapnel at the target, yanks it back, rears with the cutlass cocked behind his head and cuts down in a deep fencing lunge; Fusilier drops to one knee, aims (held), fires with a big powder cloud, then bites a cartridge and primes the pan (a ramrod stroke cannot be reached with the oversized musket); Grenadier lights the fuse from a match cord, rears back like a pitcher and lobs the bomb high, then ducks with his hands over his ears; Field Surgeon spins his pistol round his finger, fires from the hip and blows the smoke off the barrel; Cuirassier's horse gathers and leaps while the rider rises in the stirrups and cuts the sabre down through the target; Bronze Cannon: the gunner touches the match to the vent, cringes with his eyes squeezed shut while it fizzes, BOOM, the gun hops and rolls back with the chocks bouncing and a big smoke cloud; Balloon Admiral spots the target, salutes, the bomb-bay doors open and the bomb drops, the lightened balloon lurches up and bobs.
- **Deaths:** D1 fling for the Corsair and Grenadier (the hat pops off and lands apart), D2 face-first topple for the Fusilier (the shako rolls), D3 dizzy sit for the Field Surgeon, D5 unhorsed for the Cuirassier, D7 wreck and bail for the cannon (the near wheel rolls off, the gunner leaps back), D8 for the balloon (the envelope tears and deflates over the gondola; no full spin, which showed the crown from above).
- **Detail:** the face kit on every figure (the horse gets a lid), a cream anchor emblem on team slabs (coat tails, the shako plate, the mitre, the shabraque, the gondola band, the turret plinth banners, the base banner), a tricorne with an eyepatch strap and a coin gag for the Corsair, a team shako, a match cord and bomb bag for the Grenadier, spectacles and a doctor's bag with a cream cross, shaped horse legs and a saddle roll, a gunner in a team knitted cap with ear flaps, dolphin handles and a ball pyramid, wicker weave, a belly band, a patch and bay doors on the balloon.
- **Projectile start points moved (visual only):** Fusilier `muzzle` [52.3, 22.9] to [52.8, 10.1] (fired kneeling), Grenadier [33.2, 22.4] to [20.2, 37.8] (released high), Field Surgeon [33, 37.2] to [28, 21.3] (hip shot), Bronze Cannon [35.1, 47.6] to [34.1, 57.2], Balloon Admiral [0.9, -3.5] to [0.7, -3].
- **Turrets and base:** a Gunpowder fort plinth (sandstone, a timber deck, a team banner with an anchor, a powder keg, a shot pyramid) for all four turrets, 6-frame idle loops and 2D accents; the Star Fort's crumble stages now read (a bitten corner; a breach, fire, soot, dark windows, a torn banner; the cavalier's parapet sheared off, the dome askew, a second fire, the banner hanging).
- **Sizes:** unit sheets +18% for the age (Corsair +24%, Fusilier +23%, Balloon +21%, Cuirassier +21%, Grenadier +16%, Cannon +11%, Surgeon +9%), within the +25% budget. Heavies keep 10 attack poses; the Corsair's idle is a 4-pose ping-pong and the Fusilier holds its kick pose, to stay in budget.

## Owner feedback 2026-09-29: Heavy too strong; the counter class

Owner (2026-09-29): "Der er også brug for en enhedskategori, der counter heavy, da den er meget stærk." Owner (2026-09-30): "Der skal også være nogle krigere i skyde-klassen som kan skyde en del længere men f.eks. angriber langsommere, f.eks. longbow mænd der skyder i en parabel. Der skal måske også være nogle specials, som kan angribe på hele banen, men som ikke skader så meget." Balance lead, measured headless on content `9f427999`, `SIM_VERSION` 4.1.0, prototypes as `--patch` overlays and a scratch copy of `src/ai`; nothing in `src/` changed. Full plan, scripts and raw numbers: the session scratchpad `heavy-counter/PLAN.md`. DESIGN A2.6, A2.9.4, A2.14, A3, A5.1, A5.2-A5.7, A7.2, A8, A9.2, A14.4, A17.9-A17.11, A18.9.1 and B4 updated in place. Decided, not built.

- 2026-09-30 (diagnosis): the counter class existed and failed on every count. **Too weak where the lane fights:** equal-gold duels on the real sim (L7, M = 50 + half the HP-left difference) gave Anti-armor vs Heavy 41-80 at 6 v 4 (Modern 41), 36-78 at 3 v 2 and **7-28 one on one**, while Heavy beat Infantry 66-84, Ranged 66-79 and Support; in a lane where units arrive one at a time a counter that only wins in groups of six does not work: mono Anti-armor lost to mono Heavy in 100% of matches (A2.14 wants ≥ 70% the other way) and mono Heavy beat the tier VII bot 73.5% Short and 73.0% Standard (gate ≤ 35%). **Too hidden:** `counters.json` (the compact engine behind the bots' `f_counter` and every Strong vs line) rated each age's Anti-armor against its Heavy at 38-78, a coin flip in six ages; no Anti-armor card listed its own Heavy under Strong vs. **Too late:** only Spear Hunter and Phalangite were equipped for the player (capsule 1); Pikeman and Grenadier arrived unequipped in capsule 2, the rest at the Arena 2 and 3 gates, so a new player's Medieval had no answer while every bot plan held one. **Unused by the bots:** against mono Heavy the tier VII bot spent 46% on Infantry and trickled one unit at a time into Heavies camped 40 lu from its gate (units lost 40.7 vs 14.3). In bot mirrors the Heavy share of unit gold rose with tier (Standard 14% at II to 47% at X).
- 2026-09-30 (no new category): the measurements show the existing role can do the job with numbers, so no breacher or tank-hunter class. The class is shown as **Anti-heavy** (ids stay `antiArmor`), with the Heavy class's shield split by a spear as its glyph, and the class legend becomes a floor for Strong vs / Weak vs.
- 2026-09-30 (numbers, data only): Brace for every Anti-heavy card; armored and mech ×3.0 (melee cards, Bazooka Trooper), ×2.5 (Grenadier, was 1.5; Harpoon Gunner), ×2.0 kept (Rail Gunner); a first `legendary` mod at the old multiplier so no Legendary matchup moves; HP +10% except the Harpoon Gunner. Heavies unchanged. Result: 6 v 4 72-89, 3 v 2 68-83, swapped into an Infantry mix 59-78, one on one 49-52 in the melee ages (ranged ones 25-42), Heavy vs Infantry unchanged 66-84, Infantry vs Anti-heavy 73-84; mono vs mono Anti-heavy > Heavy 95% in Short.
- 2026-09-30 (variants measured, tier V Standard mirror Bell, 200 matches; today 23.0%): the real-sim counter table alone 22.0% (and Short 47.0 → 21.5%, medians −1:00); ×3.0 only 29.5%; ×2.5 with Brace 30.0%; ×3.0 with Brace 41.0%; plus HP +10% 41.5%; plus HP +15% 45.0%; plus HP +20% 46.0%; the chosen table 36.5% (Short 37.5%); the same with Gunpowder and Industrial left at ×2.0 and Brace 33.5%; with a stronger Grenadier and Harpoon (×3.0, HP 300 and 290) 41.5%. With the Harpoon Gunner left at today's numbers the Standard Bell fell 12.5 points: Standard ends in Industrial, and a strong Industrial answer makes Standard grind. Mono Heavy vs tier VII with the Heavy answer rule: ×2.5 and Brace 59%, the chosen table 36 / 44%, HP +20% 27%. Levers tried against the Standard grind: Siege base damage ×3 −4.5 points, kill bounty 60% +1, Heavy ×1.5 against bases −2.
- 2026-09-30 (the tension, for the owner): a Heavy that can be answered stops ending Standard Wars: when both sides answer Heavies, Standard games grind at mid-lane until Siege decay takes both bases to about 20% (in the Bell matches units took only 5-9% of the base). The chosen package makes Short War better on every row (tier V Bell 47.0 → 37.5%, tier VII 57.0 → 45.0%, shorter medians) and Standard's Bell worse (tier V 23.0 → 36.5%, tier VII 40.0 → 48.0%; the tier VII pair measured with Gunpowder and Industrial at ×2.0 and Brace). Rule: the numbers ship with a Standard Bell check; the tuning phase closes it with the A16.4 levers or the Supply Cache (A18.3.5), else the Gunpowder and Industrial lines step back first, and the owner hears the result in plain words.
- 2026-09-30 (AI, A7.2): answer Heavy with Anti-heavy from tier III: with Heavies ≥ 50% of a visible army worth ≥ 300, the counter goal survives enemies near the gate and cards scoring < 0.40 against that army are skipped while one at ≥ 0.65 is in the tray. With the numbers: the bot's Anti-heavy gold share vs mono Heavy 41 → 74-77%, mono Heavy wins 36% Short, 44% Standard (47% with the numbers alone). On today's numbers the rule alone drops the bot to 8%: it ships only with the numbers. Standard losses left are age races (21 of 25 with the Heavy side one age ahead); next lever: evolve as soon as XP allows when one age behind a Heavy-dominant enemy.
- 2026-09-30 (availability): every age's Anti-heavy Rare joins the starter kit and slot 4 of the starter loadouts; the scripted capsules and Age Unlock Capsules bring the Support Rares; an additive, idempotent save migration grants and slots them. Match 1 teaches it at the Tuskback beat ("Spear Hunters beat Heavies"); the battle HUD shows a "Beats Heavy" chip and the hint "Heavies! Send {card}." when the enemy fields Heavies. Schema check "every age's Anti-armor Rare arrives by script or Age Unlock Capsule" becomes "is in the starter kit".
- 2026-09-30 (Long range, owner feedback 2): a Ranged-class trait, one Rare per age (Atlatl Thrower, Cretan Archer, Yeoman Archer for the owner's longbowmen, Coehorn Crew, Trench Mortar, Mortar Team, Arc Lobber, Star Mortar): cost 75 (flat with the Ranged group), HP 85×P, 34×P splash r35 every 2.6 s, range the Ranged Common + 120 (320-390, min 90), arcs at 300 lu/s aimed at the fire-time position, ground only, half damage to bases. No sim change: `projectile.arc`, `minRange` and fire-time splash exist; the landing marker is view only (from `projectileFired.toX` and `travelTicks`). Stays ≥ 90 lu inside every Rare arc turret. Prototype rows (200 matches, replacing the Ranged Common): Yeoman Archer −1.8, Atlatl Thrower −8.5 (the first draft at 26 per 3.0 s: −7.8 and −18.5). Counters: air, arc turrets, anything that reaches it; divers only with a longer leap search (an option gated by the Epics' own rows).
- 2026-09-30 (lane powers, owner feedback 2): new reach `lane` (no aim; the cap nearest your gate anywhere on the lane), one Field power per age: volleys (25% of the age's Infantry per unit) and two signals (Sandstorm snare, Target Painter mark), 50 gold, 25 s, cap 8. One cast deals at most 8 quarter-Infantries, never kills a full-HP unit and always hits the enemy front, so the wait-and-wipe fix holds and area powers stay the damage tool. Prototype (`anywhere` plus a 4,000 lu one-pulse field, identical semantics): Longbow Volley +1.0, Pebble Hail −11.5 against the Field starter, value per gold 0.66-0.77; the first draft (75 gold, 20%) was ignored by the bots (0.07-0.32). `PowerReach` gains `'lane'`: contract change, `SIM_VERSION` 4.1.0 → 4.2.0, goldens re-verified and re-recorded deliberately, new golden `12-lane-and-long-range.json`.
- 2026-09-30 (build phases, all behind the headless gates): H1 visibility (label, glyph, Strong/Weak floor, tray chip, hint; WP9, WP5, WP4); H2 counter table on the sim (WP1, WP12); H3 starter kit, capsules, save migration, match 1 beat (WP7, WP8, WP1, WP11); H4 numbers (WP1) and H5 the AI rule (WP3), shipped together; H6 Long range (data, arc marker, cartoon art and sounds, proxies `mono_longrange` and `longrange_turtle`); H7 lane powers (`lane` reach, 8 powers, HUD, AI value, `SIM_VERSION` 4.2.0). H1-H3 carry no balance risk and can ship first.

## Modern Age cartoon rollout (art, 2026-09-30)

Owner direction: keep the cartoon style; better animation, more detail and a natural attack per figure. Scope: `art/blender/units/{trench_raider,rifleman,bazooka_trooper,radio_operator,tankette,gyrocopter,behemoth_tank}.py`, a new `ageborn_art/kit_modern.py`, `world/turrets_modern.py`, `world/base_modern.py`, the sheets in `public/art/{units,turrets}/modern/`, `public/art/bases/modern.*`, the Modern portraits and the regenerated `src/visuals/unitSheets.gen.ts`. Every clip duration, attack `impactAt` and die fx time is unchanged (`check_timing.mjs` passes against HEAD and 2a3b637).

- **Natural attacks:** Trench Raider stabs his spade into the dirt, flicks dirt at the enemy, cocks the spade over his back shoulder (held) and lunges into a downward whack; Rifleman snaps the rifle up, leans into the aim with one eye squeezed, fires once and works the bolt (the casing flips out); Bazooka Trooper braces, fires with a front flash and a back-blast, and the kick knocks him back a step into a wobble while a smoke cloud billows behind; Radio Operator keeps talking into the handset while he pops one pistol shot and then shouts the fire mission (the antenna whips); Tankette's commander ducks and the lid clanks shut, the gun fires and the whole tankette hops back, lands with dust and the commander pops up waving; Gyrocopter dips its nose and fires both guns together (one flash per gun on the shot frame, no fake burst), two casings fall; Behemoth Tank's commander ducks, the main gun fires and the hull rears up on its rear wheels and slams down. The Behemoth's machine gun stays an independent attack (`mgMuzzle` per frame); no MG flash is baked into the main-gun clip.
- **Deaths:** D1 fling for the Trench Raider and Bazooka Trooper (the helmet and the weapon fly off), D2 face-first plank topple for the Rifleman (the rifle flies back, the helmet rolls), D3 dizzy sit for the Radio Operator (the handset dangles on its cord), D7 wreck and bail for the Tankette and Behemoth (a standalone crewman with legs leaps out and runs away), D8 spiral down for the Gyrocopter (nose-over, wobble, crash squash, the rotor flies off).
- **Detail:** the face kit on every figure and crew head, cream rank chevrons as the Modern emblem on sleeves, skirts, turrets, the pod and plates, team scarf and team helmet cover as team cues, netting, grenades, canteens, pouches, mud, hazard stripes, camo patches, tools, a spare wheel, sandbags, a bolt that moves, a searchlight and whip antenna on the Bunker.
- **Projectile start points moved (visual only):** Trench Raider unchanged (melee), Rifleman `muzzle` [48.4, 23.9] to [56.2, 25.6], Bazooka Trooper [29.8, 26.1] to [32, 28.4], Radio Operator [35, 25.3] to [30.4, 35.2] (a pistol now, not a carbine), Tankette [38.1, 61.9] to [37.2, 65.7], Gyrocopter [28.5, 3.6] to [28, 0.2], Behemoth Tank [117.8, 116.9] to [117.5, 119.4].
- **Turrets and base:** a Modern mount (a concrete pad with a hazard edge and a team sign with a chevron, sandbags), a crew member with a face on every turret (on the rotating head, so crews tilt with the aim), 6-frame idles, one flash per shot (the old MG Nest showed a second flash on frame 3 through a merge OR; fixed), a belt that feeds, a breech that opens and ejects the casing, a scope glint. The Bunker's crumble stages now read: 1 bitten chunks and a lost sandbag, 2 fire in the slits, lights out, dish askew, searchlight smashed, 3 a breach with rebar, a second fire, the mast bent with the dish hanging, the antenna snapped.
- **Sizes:** unit sheets +14.5% for the age (Tankette +22%, Behemoth +20%, Gyrocopter +18%, Bazooka +16%, Trench Raider +16%, Rifleman +8%, Radio Operator -6%), within the +25% budget; turret sheets about double (25-28 KB to 45-62 KB each: crews and 6-frame idles), the base sheet 33 to 42 KB.

## Future Age cartoon rollout (art, 2026-09-30)

Owner direction: keep the cartoon style; better animation, more detail and a natural attack per figure. Scope: `art/blender/units/{photon_knight,pulse_trooper,rail_gunner,emp_saboteur,repair_drone,walker_mech,chrono_titan}.py`, a new `ageborn_art/kit_future.py`, a `pose_from` option in `ageborn_art/smear2.py`, `world/{turrets,base}_future.py`, the installed sheets in `public/art/{units,turrets}/future/` and `public/art/bases/future.*`, the Future portraits, and the regenerated `src/visuals/unitSheets.gen.ts`. Every clip duration, attack `impactAt`, die `fx` time and `hideUnitAtMs` is unchanged (`check_timing.mjs HEAD` passes).

- **Natural attacks:** Photon Knight coils with the blade trailing back, hops into a full pirouette (two mint ring smears) and lands in a lunge with the blade level; Pulse Trooper shoulders the rifle, the coils heat ring by ring while a charge ball grows, fires once, kicks, pops the spent cell out of the top hatch and slaps a fresh one in; Rail Gunner drops to one knee, the rails spread with growing arcs, one snap, the recoil slides him back on his knee, the rails clack shut and vent; EMP Saboteur crouches, cocks the baton low and lunge-jabs (a mint streak and crackle), then the shock kicks back through him (his hair spikes out of the hood); Repair Drone reaches out and spins its wrench, charges the emitter and heals with a happy eye; Walker Mech rears up with both arms overhead and hammers both fists into the ground in front (legs buckle, dust, vents flare); Chrono Titan winds the blade back to nine o'clock and sweeps it 180 degrees over the top like a clock hand (the clock hands and the gear halo spin) into a level cleave.
- **Faces:** `kit_future.visor_face` lays emissive robot-eye glyphs on every visor (troopers, knight, mech hull, titan, drone lens, the Pulse Laser pod): angry on the wind-up, slits on the strike, > < when hit, wide or spirals when dazed, X on KO, a blink in the idles.
- **Hits and deaths:** light hits for the troopers and the Saboteur, an armoured dip behind the buckler for the knight, a flyer tilt and drop for the drone, a hard mech jolt with a popping panel and sparks for the Walker and the Titan. Deaths: D1 fling (Pulse Trooper, Rail Gunner; the weapon ends along the body), D2 timber onto the back for the knight (the photon blade powers down to the hilt; the visor faces up so the X eyes read), D3 dizzy sit for the Saboteur, D8 spiral down for the drone (no full turn), D6 fall-apart for the Walker (the antenna and the ram forearm drop off, it kneels, smoke) and the Titan (the clock hands spin wild, the halo drops off, it kneels and topples forward).
- **Detail:** the pale Future hex emblem on the team chest plates, the hull band and the Pulse Laser panel; mint light strips on shoulder pads and thighs; rivets; belt pouches and glowing buckles; a crest tail, antenna and power cables as follow-through secondaries; a team stripe on the backpacks so team reads during the knight's spin; team gear guard on the Titan's blade (18% team share on the smear frame).
- **Projectile start points moved (visual only):** Pulse Trooper `muzzle` [53.4, 27.8] to [61.9, 26.1] (shouldered), Rail Gunner [62.4, 16.6] to [62, 9.4] (kneeling), Repair Drone beam [18.5, 15.1] to [17.7, 16.9].
- **Turrets and base:** a Future hover plinth (charcoal hex foot, mint hover gap, white-rimmed team hex deck, corner running lights, a holo pennant) for all four turrets, 6-frame idles and a readable anticipation before the one-frame flash (the Pulse Laser's iris opens and its eye turns angry, the Arc Coil's rings flare, the Plasma Mortar squashes, the Gravity Well sucks its orbiting debris in). The Spire gets lit window slits and crumble stages that read at a glance (a bitten chunk with sparking wires; a magenta plasma fire, dark windows and a dead light ring, the tip askew; a breach with a hanging cable and a second fire, the tip snapped to a burning stump and lying in the rubble).
- **Sizes:** unit sheets +11.6% for the age (Walker +17.9%, Titan +14.9%, Pulse Trooper +13.2%, Knight +12.3%, Drone +6.0%, Saboteur +5.3%, Rail Gunner +3.2%), within the +25% budget. Turret sheets 33-36 KB to 46-52 KB each (6-frame idles), the base sheet 38 to 52 KB.

## Heavy counter build, phases H1-H5 (2026-09-30)

Built from the plan above on content `9f427999` → `fe6b1c0c` (`SIM_VERSION` 4.1.0 unchanged: no sim rule moved; the golden replays run on the frozen fixture content). Measured headless on the working tree (scratchpad `heavy-counter/build/final`): mirrors 200 per cell, exploit proxies 100 per format vs tier VII, rules-sanity 40, duels L7.

- **H1 visibility.** The class label reads "Anti-heavy" (i18n only; ids stay `antiArmor`); the glyph is the Heavy kite shield split by a spear (`core/cardClass.ts`, and the two older role glyphs in `ui/hud/icons.tsx` and `ui/components/icons.tsx`). `counterClasses(..., floor)` lists the counter triangle first for Common and Rare cards (every Anti-heavy card: Strong vs Heavy; every Heavy card: Weak vs Anti-heavy); Epics and Legendaries keep their measured lists (`takesCounterFloor`). Battle: `core/cardClass.ts` `heavyThreat` (2+ Heavy-group units, or 40%+ of the enemy value on the lane; Legendary heavies do not count) feeds `HudModel.foe.heavyThreat` and `HudCard.beatsHeavy` (both optional, additive contract fields); the tray card wears a warm ring, lifts once and carries a "Beats Heavy" tab with the glyph (reduced motion keeps ring and tab). The adaptive hint `heaviesStopInfantry` (id kept for saved counts) now reads "Heavies! Send {card}." on the same trigger, names and points at the tray's Anti-heavy card (`AdaptiveHintDef.varKeys`, `TutorialPrompt.varKeys`). The tutorial bubble treats a card's tab as part of its target, so it never covers it. A loss where Heavies came while the age had no Anti-heavy card shows "Add {card} to {age}: it beats Heavies." (`app/heavyGap.ts`, `lossTip`, router `tipCard`/`tipAge`), ahead of the wave tip.
- **H2 counter table on the sim.** `tools/counters.ts` duels on `src/sim/duel.ts`; the file keeps its input-hash check, now with `SIM_VERSION` in the hash and engine id 102 (`SIM_DUEL_ENGINE`), and the test re-duels its sample on the sim. We did not switch CI to regenerating the file because `.github/**` is out of scope for this change. On the sim, bombers never trade with melee (they fly to the gate), so "air punishes melee" now reads as even for the Balloon Admiral; the gunship still wins (test relaxed accordingly).
- **H3 availability.** `meta/tables.ts` `antiHeavyCard` / `isStarterCard`: the starter collection holds the 8 Anti-heavy Rares at L1 and every starter loadout has it in slot 4 (meta and the tutorial's match loadouts). Scripted capsules 1 and 2 bring Drum Shaman + Standard Bearer and Friar + Field Surgeon; Age Unlock Capsules bring the age's Support Rare. Schema: a scripted card may not be a starter Anti-heavy card; each age's Support Rare must arrive by script or Age Unlock. Save v9 (`save/migrations/v9.ts`, fixture `v9.json`): grants each missing Anti-heavy Rare at L1 (NEW flag, so the Collection points at it) and puts it in the first empty unit slot of its age in every plan, never replacing a card; idempotent. Match 1: the Spear Hunter unlocks at 0:37 as the Tuskback walks on and its beat follows the Rock Tosser beat (~0:39). Deviation from the plan: **no 100 gold grant** (with it the stronger army toppled Grogg before the Future beat); retimed pins Gunpowder 1:34, Future 2:28, Grogg falls 2:30.
- **H4 numbers** exactly as the plan's table (Rail Gunner ×2.0 kept; Grenadier `legendary` ×1.5). `damageMods` gains the `legendary` entries; the Harpoon and Rail mods live in `antiHeavyMods` so the frozen raw fixture keeps its shape.
- **H5 AI** (`ai/brain.ts`, tiers III+ via `counterDepth > 0`): the plan's (a) and (b), plus (c) a turret goal when Heavies camp within 600 lu of the gate and no turret is up, and (d) the Anti-heavy Troops line only (or non-Troops picks) while the tray holds an Anti-heavy card. Trace that led to (c) and (d) (tier VII vs mono Heavy, Standard, seed 1002): the opening died by 0:29, the bot bought Heavy Weapons at 0:30, then sent single Spear Hunters into Heavies parked at its gate; no turret in two minutes; 32 of 44 losses were the Heavy side reaching Gunpowder first (Heavy evolves at 1:05 / 2:19 vs the bot's 1:27 / 2:58). With (a)-(d) the age race mostly disappears (the plan's evolve-race lever is not needed).
- **Results vs targets.** Duels: Anti-heavy vs Heavy 72-89 at 6 v 4 and 68-83 at 3 v 2 (target 65-85: met, Grenadier 89 in a clump); swapped into an Infantry mix 59-78 (Medieval 59, one point under 60); one on one 49-52 in the melee ages (ranged 25-42, as planned); Heavy vs Infantry 66-84 and Infantry vs Anti-heavy 73-84 (met). Mono Heavy vs tier VII 14.0% Short, 26.5% Standard (target ≤ 35%: met; was 73.5 / 73.0%). Rules sanity mono Anti-heavy > mono Heavy 95% Short, 0% Standard (Standard not met: the scripted mono side trickles Grenadiers and Harpoon Gunners, which lose one on one). Other exploits inside their gates: rush 14 / 3%, cheapest 0 / 0%, random 3 / 1%, Heavy plus mass Ranged 7.5 / 15.5%; the turtle stays below its band as before (5.5 / 11%). Final Bell, tier V: Short 47.0 → 45.0%, Standard 23.0 → 35.5%; tier VII: Short 57.0 → 50.0%, Standard 40.0 → 53.0% (Standard not met).
- **Standard Bell, levers measured on the built package** (tier V / VII, 200 each; mono Heavy Standard in brackets): Gunpowder and Industrial back to ×2.0 with Brace, Grenadier HP 230: 31.0 / 52.0% (36.5%); Harpoon Gunner alone at ×2.0: 34.0 / 46.0% (35.0%); Siege base damage ×3: 33.0 / 49.0% (27.5%); ×4: 31.0 / 45.5% (25.0%). None reaches 23 / 40%, and the step back costs the mono Heavy gate, so the numbers ship as planned and the Standard grind goes to the owner (next step: the Supply Cache, A18.3.5, a rule change outside this package).
- **Onboarding side effects found by the e2e run.** (1) Capsule 1 no longer auto-equips its NEW cards: they are Support Rares now, and with the Drum Shaman auto-equipped the onboarding autopilot lost match 2 to Pip in 2 of 4 seeds (17 and 7936, the e2e's); without it 3 of 4, and seeds 17 and 7936 win (autopilot vs Pip over seeds 1-20: 17 of 20 without the Shaman, 14 of 20 with it). The summary still offers Equip now. (2) Two-word names ("Spear Hunter", now in every starter tray) clipped in the battle tray: the name box was one pixel line short of two lines (max-height 36 → 39 px, phones 32 → 37 px). (3) Pip's War Plan (`content/generals.ts`; the onboarding General, tiers 0-II) drops its Anti-heavy card in every age: with the new numbers his Spear Hunters hard-countered a new player's Tuskbacks, and the onboarding autopilot won match 2 in 84% of 80 seeds (90% on the old tree); without them 94%. Pip now leans on Heavies with no answer to the player's, as A8 describes him.

## Cosmic Age cartoon rollout (art, 2026-09-30)

Owner direction: keep the cartoon style; better animation, more detail and a natural attack per figure. Scope: `art/blender/units/{star_legionnaire,ion_ranger,hover_tank,graviton_halberdier,starwarden,warp_stalker,mothership}.py`, a new `ageborn_art/kit_cosmic.py`, `world/{turrets,base}_cosmic.py`, the installed sheets in `public/art/{units,turrets}/cosmic/` and `public/art/bases/cosmic.*`, the Cosmic portraits, and the regenerated `src/visuals/unitSheets.gen.ts`. Every clip duration, attack `impactAt`, die `fx` time, `hideUnitAtMs` and the Warp Stalker's 480 ms blink-out are unchanged (`check_timing.mjs HEAD` passes).

- **Natural attacks:** Star Legionnaire jabs twice past the rim of his deflector, cocks the gladius back at the shoulder behind the disc and lunges straight through (streak smears); Ion Ranger shoulders the rifle while a ring of light runs down the coils to a charge ball, fires once and the kick knocks him into a back-hop; Hover Tank dips its nose (front pads flare), the coils light in turn, fires, rears and slides back on its pads; Graviton Halberdier braces low, heaves the halberd up in a big arc, holds it high while the gravity orb flares, and chops down diagonally (crescent smear, the orb bursts); Starwarden lifts his staff overhead while the beacon swells, slams the butt into the ground and the bolt leaves the beacon; Warp Stalker squeezes thin and blinks out in a violet star, reappears a stride forward in the air with both blades crossed high, and slashes down into a low X; Mothership opens the drone bay, charges the belly emitter (rings, squashed hull), fires the beam flare and drops the drone.
- **Faces:** `kit_cosmic.visor` lays the `kit_future` glyph eyes (light violet, or mint for the hooded and piloted units) on dark visors: the Legionnaire and Halberdier visor slots, the Ion Ranger's single cyclops lens, the Starwarden's hood (the opening is cut back toward the camera so the face reads at the -10 degree yaw), the Warp Stalker's mask (slanted), the Hover Tank's pilot in an open hatch, a pilot silhouette seen through the Mothership's glass dome, and the Ion Turret and Tachyon Lance pods.
- **Hits and deaths:** armoured dips (Legionnaire with the disc flaring, Halberdier), light hits (Ion Ranger, Starwarden, Warp Stalker), a vehicle bounce with the pilot ducking (Hover Tank), the flyer tilt and drop (Mothership). Deaths: D2 timber onto the back (Legionnaire: the blade powers down and the disc collapses; Halberdier: the halberd falls away), D1 fling (Ion Ranger), D3 dizzy sit with the robe hem tucked (Starwarden), D7 wreck and bail (Hover Tank: the pads cut out and the pilot ejects on a mint jet), the Warp Stalker's blink-out and the Mothership crash are kept (KO glyphs added).
- **Detail:** the pale Cosmic star on chest plates, the Hover Tank's side panels, the robe and the plinths; a team plume, cape hem, pteruges and pouches (Legionnaire), a team streamer on the halberd and star-white shoulder rivets, a swinging lantern and robe specks (Starwarden), a hood tip and power cable on follow-through (Ion Ranger, Warp Stalker), mint panel seams (Hover Tank), violet chevrons on the drone bay door.
- **Projectile start points moved (visual only):** Ion Ranger `muzzle` [59.6, 27.5] to [61.6, 26] (shouldered), Starwarden [55.3, 48.5] to [40.4, 45.6] (the beacon over the planted staff), Hover Tank [59.1, 66.4] to [58, 61.6] (nose dipped), Mothership [40.7, 27] to [39.9, 26.7].
- **Turrets and base:** a Cosmic star plinth (a void five-point star foot, a mint hover gap, a star-white deck with a team ring and a pale star, lights on the star points, the team pennant), 6-frame idles and an anticipation before the one-frame flash for all four turrets (Ion Turret coils and charge ball with an angry eye, Starburst Gun petals spread wide with lit cores, Starfall Battery dish squash and a hot shard, Tachyon Lance rails spread and the lights run to the tip). The Star Ark's crumble stages now read: 1 a chunk bitten out of the upper team band with sparking wires, 2 a violet plasma fire, scorch, dark windows, the nose askew, 3 a breach with a hanging cable and a second fire, the nose snapped to a burning stump and lying in the rubble.
- **Sizes:** unit sheets +10.9% for the age (Hover Tank +20.5%, Halberdier +16.1%, Legionnaire +12.7%, Warp Stalker +9.3%, Mothership +7.4%, Ion Ranger +4.8%, Starwarden +3.6%), within the +25% budget. Turret sheets 39-48 KB to 50-62 KB each (6-frame idles), the base sheet 41 to 56 KB. Every frame keeps at least 18% team colour (worst: Mothership die 18.3%, Warp Stalker walk 18.8%); worst colour-rule frame 1.05% (limit 10%).

## Cartoon rollout: final art-direction pass (art, 2026-09-30)

- **Scope:** all 57 unit sheets, 32 turrets and 8 bases reviewed side by side (a cross-age lineup at game size and at 1x, per-age clip grids) and in sandbox battles for every age plus four mixed-age pairs, at 844x390 and 1280x720. Shape language, outline weight, cel shading, team zones, face kit and scale bands read as one family from Stone to Cosmic; no console errors in any battle.
- **Rule reinforced: nothing may cover the weapon on the impact frame.** Two impact frames broke it and were re-rendered (timing unchanged, `check_timing.mjs HEAD` passes for 57 units):
  - Chrono Titan: the swing smear painted on the impact frame hid the level blade behind a flat magenta wedge. The impact frame now shows the blade with the tip burst and dust; the two smear frames before it carry the sweep.
  - Graviton Halberdier: the orb's hot shell on the impact frame covered the crescent blade with a pale disc (read as a frying pan). The shell now shows only on the held charge; sparks and the burst carry the hit.
- **Art size:** `public/art` is 42.6 MB in the tree (units 39.0 MB, HD plus SD), against 37.7 MB at 2a3b637 (+13%). A match loads one sheet set (HD or SD) per age in play, so a phone loads well under that.
- **Accepted as is:** bases keep low team share (a team banner and trims only; bases have never met the unit 18% rule), turret sheets grew 30-60% in bytes for 6-frame idles and crews (each still 45-77 KB), and the known small weak spots listed in the Cosmic entry.

## Heavy counter review fixes (2026-09-30)

The review of H1-H5 (content `fe6b1c0c`) found the counter still failing in two ages, a match 1 beat that taught nothing and a hidden loss tip. Content `fe6b1c0c` → `af593517`; `SIM_VERSION` 4.1.0 unchanged (no sim rule moved; the golden replays use the frozen fixture). Checked by re-running each finding first.

- **Industrial: two causes, both fixed.** (1) Numbers: the Harpoon Gunner gets the ×3.0 and the HP +10% (260 → 286) the other Anti-heavy cards got; its Reel In pull stays (removing it changed nothing in the lane). (2) AI (A7.2 (c)): the camp-turret goal picked the Mortar Pit (min range 60 lu) against Steam Golems 24 lu from the gate, and `urgent` kept spending the goal on single Harpoon Gunners that spawned into the campers. The goal now skips a turret whose minimum range lies beyond the nearest camper (`TurretCard.minRange`, `Brain.campReach`) and the trains wait while it banks. Each fix alone was not enough: numbers only 42.5%, AI only 72.5%, both 2.5-5% (mono Heavy vs tier VII in `w1.industrial`).
- **Modern:** Bazooka Trooper HP 330 → 363. Mono Anti-heavy vs mono Heavy went 0% → 100% (a knife edge: one more Tankette/Rifleman hit survived). **Gunpowder:** mono Anti-heavy vs mono Heavy was 60% with 40% at the Bell; Grenadier damage 50 → 55 gives 75% and no Bell (HP 278 gave 90% but 60% at the Bell).
- **Per-age lane gate** in `tools/exploits.ts` (`lane.*`, on by default, `--no-lane`, `--lane-matches`): in every `w1.<age>`, `mono_heavy` ≤ 35% vs tier VII and the new proxy `mono_antiheavy` ≥ 70% vs `mono_heavy`. Smoke: mono Heavy 0 / 0 / 0 / 2.5 / 5.0 / 0 / 6.3 / 0%; mono Anti-heavy 100% everywhere but Gunpowder 75%.
- **Exploit smoke vs the committed package** (same seeds): mono Heavy vs tier VII 14.0 → 0% Short, 26.5 → 0% Standard; no new failure. The 11 remaining failures all failed before (turtle band, `flag_ball` Short 47%, fall-back turtle Bell 92%, bait rows).
- **Standard Final Bell not fixed** (Balanced mirror, 200 per cell): tier V 35.5 → 41.5%, VII 53.0 → 58.0%, X 54.0 → 51.5%; Short V 45.5%, VII 52.0%, X 51.0%. The review's hypothesis (a weak Industrial counter causes it) does not hold: restoring the old Harpoon numbers gave 36.5% at tier V but reopened Industrial (72.5%); Harpoon ×2.5 with HP 286 gave 44.5 / 53.0% and 17.5% in Industrial. The cell noise is about ±7 points. Stronger counters make the Standard grind more defensive; it goes to the owner with the Supply Cache (A18.3.5) as the next lever.
- **Heavy still wins more bot mirrors** (larger Heavy share wins 74-87%, larger Anti-heavy share 25-43%): correlation (the winner banks for Heavies), reported to the owner. The review's "Anti-heavy plus a screen (2:1)" script is a proxy artefact: the weighted picker re-rolls until something is affordable, so it trained about 46 Infantry per 2 Anti-heavy. A true 2:1 sequence beats mono Heavy 100% in six ages and loses in Gunpowder and Modern (0%, while 1:1 wins there), a scripted knife edge rather than a rule.
- **Balloon Admiral:** the first `legendary` mod gives it ×2.0 from the Harpoon Gunner and the Bazooka Trooper (×1.0 before; it has no armored or mech tag). Kept and documented in A2.6: it only meets them across ages; on the sim they score 52.7 and 60.2 against it at equal gold, and its own age's answers do not move. Restructuring would need a two-tag mod (a sim rule).
- **Match 1 Spear Hunter beat** (A8): the script grants the Spear Hunter's 100 gold with its unlock; the beat triggers only while Grogg's Heavy is within 500 lu of the player's front (`foeHeavy`, the follow camera's frame) and ends when the Spear Hunter walks on (`spawned`), 20 s timeout. The first Tuskback walks into the Bonkers camped at Grogg's gate and dies there before any Spear Hunter can cross the lane (28 s), so Grogg's Tuskbacks are level 10 (`MATCH1_GROGG_LEVELS`) and a second one walks on at ~0:59, as the Spear Hunter arrives; the Spear Hunter kills it at ~1:07. Retimed: Medieval 0:57, Gunpowder 1:27, Modern 1:46, Future 2:19, Grogg falls 2:25 (was 2:30; 6 s after the Future beat, so the extra gold no longer topples him first).
- **Loss tip:** out of the collapsed "Also earned" row, at the top of the Rewards column, with an "Open Army" button that jumps to that age's War Plan (Army tab). Visible at 844x390 and 1280x720 by default.
- **Save v9** also rewrites an unopened onboarding capsule 1 or 2 rolled before v9: its old Anti-heavy stacks become the new script's Support Rares (NEW when not owned). v9 is part of this unreleased package, so the step was amended rather than a v10 added.
- **Beats Heavy ring:** the affordable glow (`.state-ready`, later in `hud.css`) overrode the counter ring on every affordable card, which is why it read as the gold border; the ring now wins and is two-tone (light inner line, deep orange band).
- DESIGN fixed where it contradicted the code: 0.2 starter-kit note, A6.3 Age Unlock Capsule, A6.5 script table, A17.13, C5 #5-6, A8 match 1 row.

## Owner request 2026-09-29: free capsule every 5 hours (built as a banked, play-gated Field Cache)

Owner: "Der skal også være sådan en 'kom tilbage hver 5 time for at åbne en gratis kapsel'." Orchestrator decision (owner delegated): build it in the healthy form (banked, play-gated, honest capsule show and odds, a timer in the Capsules tab only, no notifications, economy-neutral within about 3%). Designed 2026-09-30; spec in the session scratchpad `timed-capsule/SPEC.md`, rules in DESIGN A6.3 and A15.4.

- **Name: the Sundial, not "Field Cache".** A18.3.5 already has the in-battle Supply Cache and the Expedition has caches. A sundial fits Time Capsules and names the clock the player sees. The bag capsule is now the Sundial Capsule (kind id `win` kept for saves; it was the Win Capsule).
- **The rule.** One capsule ready every 5 h (18,000,000 ms, epoch time, integer), bank 34. Any finished match except the tutorial and a Retreat claims one, win or lose: a Sundial Capsule from the unchanged 200-slot bag. One match claims at most one; free capsules (the first 10 of a save) come first. A new save starts with 12 ready. Clock tampering accepted as before (backwards restarts the period and never removes one).
- **Cap 34, not small.** A 2-3 slot free chest fills in 10-15 h and then stops. That is appointment pressure, and A15.1 rule 3, the walk-away test and A15.22 forbid it. 34 × 5 h = 170 h is the smallest bank of 5-hour steps that holds 7 days.
- **It replaces capsule charges and the Supply allowance instead of adding a capsule.** This is the measurement behind it: the `tools/economy.ts` player and the real meta rules, the timed capsule as a harness, 100 seeds, days to max a median card Common / Rare / Epic / Legendary; today 108.75 / 99.25 / 67 / 112.5.
  - An extra Clay capsule every 5 h with nothing removed gave 72.5 / 92 / 61.5 / 80.25, copies a day +30%.
  - Funded by retiring the Supply Capsule and slowing charges to 8 h (bank 21), it gave 96.5 / 145 / 91 / 110. Copies a day were only −4%, but Clay is nearly all Commons while the removed bag capsules carry the Rares and Epics (Rare +46%, Epic +36%). Outside pity it was 95 / 144 / 92.75 / 120.
  - With a 7-day bank and one capsule per match, an engaged player claims 4.8 a day. Only a capsule from the bag keeps the rarity mix, and 4.8 bag capsules a day is the charge budget. So the Sundial becomes that budget.
- **Any match, not only wins.** Keeping charges for Ladder wins at 5 h with the Supply Capsule retired was neutral for the engaged player. But a player of 3 matches a day fell from 55.2 to 39.9 copies a day (−28%), because the Supply Capsule was theirs; and it is not the owner's "free capsule". With any finished match that player gets 57.1 (+3%). Wins keep trophies, Amber (20 with a capsule, 40 without), the War Chest, quests and peak rank. A Retreat claims nothing, so leaving a match at once never pays.
- **Clay meter 3 → 2 pips**, from every Ladder match that brings no capsule. Without it the 7-match player lost 5.3% of copies (the Supply Capsule's value is larger than the extra 0.7 bag capsule); with it the change is within about 3%. A meter already at 2 or more turns into a Clay capsule at the first tick after the update.
- **Result, same player before and after** (7 finished ladder matches a day, 60% wins, medians of seeds 1-100 and 101-200): Common 106.0 → 106.5 days (+0.5%), Rare 96.1 → 98.1 (+2.1%), Epic 64.4 → 62.75 (−2.5%), Legendary 108.6 → 105.1 (−3.2%); copies a day 100.4 → 97.4 (−3.0%), Amber 3,102 → 3,055 (−1.5%); whole collection 188.5 → 193 days (+2.4%). Against A6.9's published model (whose 12 starting charges are never spent) Epic is −7% and Legendary −5.5%. That gap is the unspent charges, and all A6.9 ±20% gates pass. `tools/drops.ts` smoke: 17 of 17 pass (bag, tiers, Supply odds and pity unchanged).
- **Supply Capsule retired, promise kept.** `supply.accrues` false: no new allowance. An allowance banked before the update still turns into a Supply Capsule every 3rd finished match until it is used, and the one-time capsule after capsule 2 (scripted capsule 3, the A8 beat) stays.
- **Display.** Home shows only "Sundial n/34", lit while one is ready, with no time. The Capsules tab has the dial, "n of 34 ready" and "Next one at 17:40", a local clock time that never ticks and has no seconds. A15.3 rule 4 now says a clock time there is not a countdown. There are no notifications (red line 4). The Supply tray line goes, so the counter budget drops by one.
- **Save v10** (the newest was v9): no shape change. It sets `flags['notice.sundial']` for saves that have played, for a one-time notice. Banked charges carry over one-for-one as ready capsules (the cap rises, and each now needs any finished match instead of a win).
- **Owner check.** Wins no longer bring a capsule by themselves. The win-only alternative is measured above and not recommended.
- **Build (2026-09-30).** Content `capsules.charges` { start 12, max 34, regenMs 18,000,000, freeCapsules 10 }, `clayMeterPips` 2, `supply.accrues` false; kind `win` named "Sundial Capsule". Meta: `claimSundial` (free capsules first, then one ready capsule; a loss by Retreat never claims), `nextSundialAt`, `sundialReady`, `settleClayMeter` (a meter at 2+ pips becomes one Clay capsule at the first tick, exactly once); every non-tutorial mode claims after its own rewards, only the Ladder adds pips. Save v10 sets `flags['notice.sundial']` when `matchesPlayed > 0` (no shape change). UI: Home chip "Sundial n/34" in the top bar (only once the Ladder is open; lit while one is ready, no time), the Capsules tab's Sundial card (stone dial whose hour marks light as the 5 h period runs, the shadow eases in once, re-renders only at minute boundaries, local clock time via `Intl`), the one-time notice card, the Supply row only while an old allowance is left. Wrap card: the match that claimed the last ready capsule, any mode, win or lose. The mode rules copy lost "no charges".
- **Measured on the built code** (`tools/economy.ts`, 7 finished ladder matches a day, medians of seeds 1-100 and 101-200, against the prototype's same-player baseline 105.75/106.25, 96.5/95.75, 63.75/65, 107.25/110): Common 106 / 106.75 (+0.4%), Rare 99.5 / 99.5 (+3.5%), Epic 63.25 / 62.5 (−2.3%), Legendary 105 / 104.5 (−3.5%); copies a day 97.7 / 97.2 (−2.9%), Amber 3,052 / 3,059 (−1.5%); Sundial 4.80 and Clay 1.10 capsules a day. The built rule gives free capsules to losses too, which moves Rare and Legendary about 1 point further than the prototype. Casual player (3 matches a day): 56.9 copies a day (+3.0% against 55.2). Against the shipped tool's old player (4 charged wins): Common −2.8%, Rare +0.5%, Epic −5.6%, Legendary −7%. The same 5 A6.9 design targets fail before and after (all Legendaries owned, plan at L7, copies done, collection maxed, finish gap; Phase 3 items). `tools/drops.ts`: 17 of 17 pass.
- **Review fixes (2026-09-30).**
  - **A Retreat adds no Clay pip.** The build let a Ladder Retreat add a pip when it claimed nothing; with 2 pips that paid a Clay capsule every two Retreats (about 24 an hour from 60 s Retreats below 400 trophies, where no trophies are lost). A15.4 says every reward needs play, never a Retreat, so a Retreat now pays only the 15 loss Amber. It still counts as a loss (trophies, MMR, loss streak, the Supply match count for an old allowance).
  - **Home: a glyph, not "Sundial n/34".** Correction to the Display bullet above: HEAD's Home had no charges counter (only Amber, Dust and the gear), so the chip was an added counter, and a full "34/34" reads as a backlog and a pull cue (A15.13, A15.4 "no count of anything waiting"). The owner's brief asked for a calm "ready" badge, so Home keeps only the Sundial glyph: in colour while a capsule is ready, grey when none is, no number, no glow, no time, no motion; tapping opens the Capsules tab. A15.13 lists it as the one Home addition. A badge on the Capsules nav tab was rejected: that badge counts capsules that can be opened now, and a Sundial capsule cannot be opened, only claimed by playing.
  - **Honesty copy.** Quick Battle help no longer says "no capsules" ("No trophies. {n} Amber per win. Claims a ready Sundial Capsule."); the Result's Clay chip reads "Clay meter {meter}/{max}" from content (it said "/3"); the Retreat dialog says "Retreating counts as a loss and claims no Sundial Capsule."; a full Sundial says "Full: it has stopped filling." (A15.3); the "Next one Tue 03:10" weekday uses the game's language, the time the device's clock format; the one-time notice drops its Supply sentence for saves with no allowance left, and sits under the Sundial card so the dial stays above the fold at 844x390.
  - **One-for-one carry-over for full saves.** The old rule never moved `chargesUpdatedAt` while the bank was full, so a save sitting at 28 would have jumped to 34 on its first tick. Save v10 now sets `flags['sundial.restart']` for a save at 28 or more, and meta's next timer tick restarts the period at that moment and deletes the flag (a migration has no clock).
  - **Economy deviation accepted.** On the built code Rare is +3.5%, Legendary −3.6% and the whole collection +3.4% (188.5 → 195 days), slightly past "about 3%". Rare and Legendary move in opposite directions because the rarity mix changed (bag capsules for the Bronze-heavy Supply Capsule); any income lever moves all rarities the same way, so no lever brings both within 3%. Kept and stated in A6.9 and A15.4.
