The casual player now runs over 730 days in `sim:economy` so its per-rarity figures are real: Common −2.3%, Rare −6.3%, Epic −11.8%, Legendary −12.6% (faster), collection +3.2%, copies +3.6%; the casual gain is intended. A 28-day test checks the weekly player against the daily one (within 15%; measured within 1%).
  - **Owner sign-off still needed before `main`:** the Sundial replaces capsule charges and the Supply Capsule rather than adding a funded extra cache, the bank is 34 rather than a small cap, and Ladder wins no longer bring a capsule by themselves.

## Owner request 2026-09-29: stationary class (walls, spawners, towers)

"Der skal også være en klasse af "krigere" som er faste strukturer, som man kan stille på banen, f.eks. et palisadehegn, en militærbase der spawner gratis meget svage tropper hver x sekund, tårne der kan skyde men som står stille." (A class of fixed structures you place on the lane: a palisade, a military camp that spawns free, very weak troops every x seconds, towers that shoot but stand still.) Lead design, 2026-09-30; rules in DESIGN A16.14.1-A16.14.9, with follow-ups in A18.5.3, A18.7.5, A18.7.8, A18.9, A18.9.3, A18.11, A18.12 and A18.13 phase 6.

- **One Fort class, four kinds, one slot.** Wall, Tower, Camp (the spawner) and Trap (A16.14's traps stay). Each age loadout has one Fort slot, so the kinds compete per age: a real deck choice, and one tray button (the HUD budget of A2.9.10 has room for exactly one). 32 cards: walls Common, camps and traps Rare, towers Epic; 8 hidden levies.
- **Pads, not free placement.** Home pads 240, 360, 460 for every kind; camps may also use Field pads 640 and 820 while the own front is 100 lu past them. (Revision 2: Home pads 160, 230, 300.) Pads are readable for both sides, snap targets for the drag and simple for the AI.
- **Towers stay home (measured).** On the emulation, towers allowed on Field pads raised the tier VII mirror Final Bell from 58% to 78% in Standard War (Short 52 → 56.5%). Weaker towers (one alive, 40% HP, ×1.0 damage) 68%, no placing from Overdrive 75.5%, decay from completion 82.5%, pads reaching into the enemy half 74.5-79.5%: forward towers are a second defensive line at mid-lane whatever their size. On Home pads, placed only against a real wave, the Bell fell to 49% (Short 43%, Full 44% vs 40% at 100 matches). (Revision 2: towers stay home by the cover invariant; this row was confounded by placement volume.)
- **Towers are not turrets:** pads not mounts; HP, 6 pop and decay; range = the age's Ranged Common (hard cap 270); never bases or forts; no mount research; Suppress silences them; ×0.5 in Siege.
- **The spawner cannot farm, stall or screen:** levies are summons (no pop, bounty or XP either way), 40% of the age's Infantry HP and damage, one per 8 s, 2 alive per camp, 1 camp per side, always march (they ignore Hold and Fall back), last in the power cap order, and stop in Siege.
- **Bounty at the unit rates (50% gold, 70% XP), no loss XP to the owner.** Measured: 30% gold and 42% XP raised the wall mirror Bell by 5 points (Short 34.5 → 40%, Standard 48 → 53%) and added no value, so a fallen fort keeps paying the attacker like a unit. The A16.14 60% / 100% predated A18's bounty cut. (Revision 2: the 5-point reading is within noise, so a lower bounty is unmeasured, not rejected.)
- **Siege: forts crumble** (no placing, decay and damage taken ×2, camps stop, towers ×0.5), so forts never extend an end-game. Pop 6 (traps 3), 25 s recharge, 5 s scaffold at 50% HP, decay after 60 s, ×2 from Heavy, siege and Legendary, ×0.5 from range ≥ 100, powers and Last Stand never touch forts (A16.14).
- **Sources: starter walls, War Path L4 / L6 / L8 for camps, traps and towers, a Trophy Road fallback on 2,100-4,000, never capsules.** Forts have no copies, levels or Dust (they scale with the loadout multiplier, like powers), so the capsule tables, the bag and the economy targets stay exactly as tested. Unlock at War Path Bronze L4 or 400 trophies; both sides always play the same slots. (Revision 2: the Stone set comes with the unlock, and the Road fallback is one fort set per region on 7 plain nodes.)
- **Research:** the wall pick is Engineers (scaffold 5 → 3 s), already in A18.5.3; no fort line in phase 6.
- **Save and replays:** the next save version at build time (v11 today; v10 is the Sundial's), additive with fixtures; one WP0 bump and `SIM_VERSION` 5.0.0 with a deliberate golden re-record and a new `13-forts.json`.
- **Measured (emulation of the final rules on `SIM_VERSION` 4.1.0; about 24,000 headless matches; scratch tools, no repo code).** Tier VII mirror Bell (Short / Standard / Full): no forts 52.0 / 58.0 / 40%, walls 41.0 / 48.0 / 28%, camps 38.5 / 52.5 / 26%, Home towers 43.0 / 49.0 / 44%; medians equal or up to 72 s shorter. Turtles vs tier VII (win, Bell): `turret_turtle` 0-3.5% with 20-71% at the Bell; with walls, camps or towers re-placed on every recharge 0-4% with 4-20%; `home_turtle` 0-7% with 22.5-42% and with forts 0-4% with 9-21%. Each fallen wall paid the bot 62 gold and 87 XP, which is why no kind helps a turtle. The emulation's gaps (no contact rule, ranged ×0.65, ranged enemies target forts at unit priority, loss XP still paid) all favour the fort user. (Revision 2: the numbers are indicative only, and some gates already fail on them; see below.)
- **Not settled by the emulation: the fort AI value.** Tier VII with forts beat tier VII without only 26.5-43% (many draws): the bolt-on driver spent the bot's gold behind its back and the bot counted its forts as army. F1's AI plans forts inside its gold ledger and the 50-62% row is re-measured; "too weak" levers are listed in A16.14.9 (never a lower bounty, never forward towers).
- **Rejected:** free placement; towers on Field pads; forts in capsules; more than one Fort slot; a fort research line in phase 6; levies that obey Hold; stunning traps; the Defences turret picks on towers; the A16.14 names Caltrops and Barbed Wire (power ids since the power rework; the traps are Wolf Pits and Tripwire Charge).
- **For the owner:** forts are a fourth card per age that you drag onto marked spots in front of your base. They cost gold and army space, take 5 s to build, crumble after a minute or two and Heavy units break them fast, so they help in a fight but cannot be used to hide until the clock runs out. The owner check after the art phase asks: fun, or turtle-y?

### Revision 2 (2026-09-30, lead design after a critic pass)

Every critic issue was checked against the code and the raw runs; this block supersedes the bullets above where they differ. Rules in DESIGN A16.14.1-A16.14.9 and the A2/A17/A18 follow-ups.

- **Home pads 160, 230, 300 (were 240, 360, 460).** Turret range is an edge distance from the own gate (`sim/systems/targeting.ts` `turretCandidate`). A large wall on 460 or 360 stopped the wave at p 484 or 384, outside every Common turret (130-380; Flak 420) and, on 460, every Stone turret, so the wall could not do its job and a tower there was a lone forward post. On 300 the blocked front stands at 324, inside every age's 150-gold Common (340-380). A static test keeps it so. Sizes: wall and camp large, tower medium.
- **Tower cover never past p 560.** A tower's range is clamped to 560 − pad − 16 (at most 244 on pad 300), so the A2.8 880 lu gap between covers holds; Fog, Night and every turret-range rule apply to towers; towers take no research, doctrine, relic, aura or side modifier. Towers stay on Home pads by this invariant; the Field-tower rows were confounded (4.3-10.4 placements per match against 0.47-0.94 at home).
- **Siege starts every fort's decay at once (2%/s),** so a fort lives at most 55 s into Siege, less than the shortest Siege (90 s). Before, a wall placed at 5:29 in a 1- or 2-age window still stood at the Bell. A static test covers every format, window and Siege-moving modifier.
- **Contracts complete in one bump.** Walls, towers and camps compile into hidden twin `UnitDef`s with the same id, because `content.units[u.card]` is read about 75 times in about 40 files; traps get `SimState.traps` (a unit without HP would read as dead); `RoleGroup` `'fort'`; `createFort`/`FortView` in the art contract; tower silence state and `towerSilenced`; `CapCandidate.capRank`; and the WP8 mirrors in `replaySchema.ts` and `save/schema.ts` (Valibot `v.object` strips unknown keys, so fort commands would silently vanish from stored replays) with a round-trip test. `SideMods.preFort` and a fort `VictoryRule` are not added: the boss's pre-placed fort and the fort variant of "Take the tower" leave phase 6.
- **Levies cost 0 and rank last.** Cost 0 (no bounty, no auto-aim value) with an AI-only `aiValue` of 8. `capRank` puts levies last in enemy power caps and own buffs (A2.9.5 amended); drops keep soaking Home powers as A2.9.12 measured. The AI never lets summons or forts set `pastMidTick` or `foeOnMyHalfTick` (`ai/memory.ts`), so a camp cannot switch off the attack clock or quiet-lane research; forts never count as army and `gateUnits` excludes them. Levy kills and base damage pay their owner; Miracle skips summons; levies spawn at the camp's p.
- **Contact capped at 5 attackers** (the blocked front rank first, then the nearest within 60 lu). Under the A2.7 single file the uncapped rule let about 7 Infantry hit a wall at once (a Stone L7 wall in about 4 s); capped, a wall holds about 5.6 s against a same-level Infantry blob in every age. The cap (5 → 3) is the first "too weak" lever.
- **Targeting order:** range < 100 treats forts like units (the blocking fort); range ≥ 100 takes units, then the base, then forts, so a fort never shields the base from fire. A special-attacks rule covers bomber drops (the Admiral is a Legendary: ×2), death explosions, call-ins and chain, pierce and cleave hops (skip forts). The `artillery` role joins the ×2 list.
- **Safe pads for the AI and Key D:** a legal pad the nearest enemy cannot reach before the scaffold completes (+1 s), inside own cover. The HUD rings unsafe legal pads amber ("Builds under fire"). The 120 lu legality rule alone let the AI build scaffolds into a wave and pay the bounty.
- **Formations:** forts are outside the front rank, spacing, `followSupport`, stance targets, the camera front, the open gate test and the Supply Cache claim; summons cannot claim a Supply Cache.
- **Sim readings settled** (camp timer, trap primary, decay grid and step order with a 3 s bounty credit, overlap at completion, attack index 0, compiled base range, regen only after completion) so golden replays have one answer.
- **Bots use only forts a player could own** (the A2.9.8 rule); a General's preferred kind overrides the tier's kind list but never the source filter.
- **Sources and onboarding:** the Stone Camp, Trap and Tower come with the unlock (Stone L4 is the Home power lesson and cannot grant an unusable card); no fort reward shows before the unlock; Bronze L4 teaches forts only (its lane feature moves to L7); a trophy unlock gets a one-time hint; every preset's empty Fort slot is filled; `fixedPlan` has an explicit `fort`; the Road fallback is one fort set per region on 7 plain nodes (2,200-3,200; 2,100 already holds a backdrop).
- **Phone UI:** the Army band budget comes from the built CSS (736 of 794 px, the advisor moves to the band's title row below 480 px height); the Fort button sits in a placement dock with the powers and a stone frame, so a tap beside the unit cards never means "aim"; minimap taps snap to the nearest legal pad; the enemy's fort recharge is public like power rings.
- **Evidence downgraded.** The emulation is indicative: few placements (0.47-1.95 per match in the AI mirrors), confounded tower rows, noise (±7 points on a share, ±10 on a difference at n = 200; the same config read 34.5% and 41.0%), empty checks (`fort_spam` on a script that loses 97-100%; turtles that win 0-7%), and missing rules. Gates that already fail on it are stated: old pads `camp_turtle` 20% and `wall_turtle` 17% in Full War, Home towers +4 in the Full War mirror; new pads (`run9a`-`run9c`, 200 per row) `tower_turtle` 18.5% and `camp_turtle` 16.5-17% in Standard War, with `maxTowers` 1 no help (20.0%). The gates now use paired seeds, 1,000 per mirror format, a placebo row, forced-placement mirrors, a one-sided median (not more than 30 s longer), a tier VII `fort_spam`, `camp_hold_mirror`, `flag_ball` + towers and `runner_camp`. F0 models every missing rule before the go/no-go.
- **Rejected:** keeping 240/360/460 with a runtime cover limit (legal pads that change with turret builds are harder to read); a scaffold that pays reduced bounty; placing bans in the last 120 s before the Bell (the Siege switch is simpler and covers every window); hiding the enemy fort recharge; changing `ui-plan.md` here (F2 owns it).

## Forts: AI v1 (WP3, 2026-09-30)

Built on the forts F1 core (`SIM_VERSION` 5.0.0) from spec section 9 and DESIGN A16.14.7. The numbers
below come from headless runs with tier VII at L7 on the baseline plan, with paired seeds. They are in
`scratchpad/forts/tuning.md` of the forts build.

- 2026-09-30 (when a wall, tower or trap goes up): the wave is read from 300 lu before mid-lane, not only
  once it is in the bot's half.
  - Stone Infantry walks 87 lu/s, so "scaffold + 1 s" is 522 lu. A Home pad is only safe while the
    enemy front is about 560 lu or more away. A wave that has fully crossed mid-lane stands on or near the
    pads.
  - Before this change, fewer than a third of the decisions that met the value rule (≥ 300, ≥ 1.2 × own
    army, under half breakers) had a safe pad.
  - The value and breaker rules are unchanged.
- 2026-09-30 (ledger): the defensive saving goal starts when a qualifying wave is 700 lu short of
  mid-lane and the slot is ready within 8 s (it was 300 lu and 3 s). With the late goal the bot held
  0-50 gold in most qualifying decisions.
  - Wall placements per side per match (Short / Standard / Full) went from 0.10 / 0.32 / 0.42 to
    0.47 / 1.01 / 1.31.
  - The fort AI value moved from 47.5 / 47.7% to 51.0 / 52.8% (walls, Short / Standard; n = 200, ±7).
- 2026-09-30 (cover fallback): with only a short-range turret built (the Pitch Cauldron, 130 lu), the
  core cover limit falls below pad 160, so no Home pad is ever safe.
  - The bot then reads pad 160 as it would with no turret: legal, and every visible enemy ground unit
    needs scaffold + 1 s to reach it at its card speed (public numbers only).
  - The core fix is requested in `docs/requests/wp0-fort-cover-short-turret.md`. The fallback goes
    once it lands.
- 2026-09-30 (measured, indicative sizes):
  - The placebo mirror is identical to the no-fort mirror.
  - In the fort-AI and forced mirrors, walls, towers and camps stay within ±3 Bell points in Short and
    Standard.
  - Traps read +4 to +9 in both mirror kinds, with a Full median of +45 to +50 s: traps are the stall
    risk.
  - `fort_spam` scores 11-44%.
  - Every fort turtle reaches the Bell less often than the same proxy without forts (the worst is towers
    in Short, +1.0 ±11.0). The fort-free `turret_turtle` is itself at 17 / 32 / 71%, so the absolute
    "≤ 15% at the Bell" gate cannot pass for any kind in Full War.
  - Proposals (trap price 100 with one charge fewer; a paired turtle gate) are in the tuning file.
    Nothing in content was changed.

## WP4/WP6 forts F3: art and audio (2026-10-01)

- **All 32 forts are rendered in the realistic Blender pipeline**, not only Stone: a static fort renders in about a minute (`art/blender/styles/realistic/forts/`: `kit.py` sheet contract, `parts.py` breakable masonry, sandbags, gabions, tents, pits; one module per age; `run.py` renders, installs and writes the card still). Sheets go to `public/art/forts/<age>/<slug>(.hd).json` (2.8 MB for all 32 at both densities).
- **Sheet contract:** `body` (crumble 0-3), `front` (crewed towers: the parapet drawn over the crew), `scaffold`, `rubble`, `flag` (loop), `door` (camps), `trap` (unarmed, armed, sprung, spent). One canvas and feet anchor for every clip. Crumble by construction: each piece falls off at a stage (top and edge pieces first) and lies at the foot from then on.
- **Tower crews are the age's Ranged Common drawn from its own unit sheet** (scale 0.6, standing on the platform, warped so its throw releases on the sim's shot: the 200 ms wind-up reads `nextAttackInMs`). Crewless towers (Pillbox, Sentry Pylon, Ion Spire) charge a glow at their muzzle. So a restyled Ranged Common restyles its tower crew for free.
- **Levies draw from their Infantry Common's sheet at 0.85** (`VisualDef.heightLu` below the sheet's scales the sprite and its walk), not from a new outfit: a new levy outfit means re-rendering eight units and is left for the restyle pass.
- **Everything that moves on a fort is code** (`src/visuals/fortViews/atlasFortView.ts`): the placing drop, the ground-up reveal behind the scaffold, the build pop, flag unfurl, hit shake, flash and chips by material, crumble bursts, decay cracks and flakes, the camp door, trap pops, and the collapse into rubble. Presentation only; the sim owns every timing.
- **Fort sounds pre-render in the `match` group** (no fort is up before 0:20), keeping the boot ZzFX budget under 300 ms; `fort_denied` is a UI sound. Material per card for hit sounds and debris lives in `src/render/fortFeel.ts` (render-side presentation data).
- **In battle the trap art draws no charge pips** (the HUD's chip shows them); requested the HUD drop its DOM hazard patch (`docs/requests/forts-f3-art-hud-content.md`).

## Forts: review fixes (fixer, 2026-10-01)

All 30 confirmed findings of the forts review, fixed against DESIGN A16.14. Measured with the new repo
tool `npx tsx tools/sim-cli.ts forts` (tier VII at L7, paired seeds 5001+); raw results and reports in the
session scratchpad (`forts/fix/r3a`, `r3b`).

- **Sim (`SIM_VERSION` 5.1.0, goldens re-recorded deliberately).** The contact cap is a hard limit: a
  ground unit's attack with compiled range < 100 may target a completed fort only while the unit is in
  that fort's contact set, so reach units (Spear Hunter, Pikeman, Hover Tank) no longer put 8-9 attackers
  on a wall. The safe-pad test reads a moving enemy's current speed (research, Charge with War Horns,
  speed buffs, slows); standing units keep card speed. The observation computes the safe-pad inputs once
  per observation, and the contact scan reuses its lists. Two new fort goldens, `13-forts-cases` and
  `14-forts-scaffold` (scripted fort directors), plus `12-forts` cover every listed case, asserted by one
  test: credited and uncredited decay, a silenced tower, a Heavy breaking a fort, a destroyed scaffold,
  a contact-capped blob and the Siege decay switch.
- **Content (data only, the A16.14.9 levers).** `maxAlive` 2 → 1 (one fort or trap up at a time: forced
  walls stopped feeding two bounties per recharge, which is what made every other kind look strong next to
  the wall); camps every 10 s with 1 levy alive (was 8 s, 2); levies 30% of the Infantry Common (was 40%);
  traps 100 gold (was 75) with fewer splash charges (Powder Keg 1 × 230, Tripwire 1 × 200, Minefield
  2 × 130, Void Mine 1 × 430; budget 0.61-0.79; the Stone to Medieval pits keep 3 charges, P1 option b).
  Wall price unchanged (100 measured worse on the per-card rows).
- **Per card vs the wall** (n = 200 per window): the worst camp moved from +34.3 to −8.8..+5.0; traps
  −7.8..+6.8; towers −3.5..+2.8 (was up to +16 for Powder Keg and Void Mine). CIs are ±6-8 at this size.
- **Mirrors** (n = 400): forced trap mirror Bell Δ 0.0 (Short) and −1.3 (Standard), was +9.0; fort-AI
  walls Standard −6.3 (was +3.6), towers Standard −5.5; Short fort-AI rows still read +1.8 to +6.8 with
  ±6.7 noise (towers Short +6.8 [0.3, 13.2] is the one to re-measure at 1,000). Mean length Δ within
  ±21 s everywhere. Placebo exactly 0, camp_hold_mirror 0, runner_camp −8.8 / −21.3.
- **Median gate → mean.** The Standard no-fort mirror reaches the Bell in ~59% of matches, so its median
  is pinned at 12:30 and a median gate can never fail; DESIGN A16.14.9 now gates the mean length.
- **Turtle gate: not passable as written; owner or lead decision needed.** Turtles vs tier VII, Bell %
  Short / Standard / Full (n = 200): no forts 12.5 / 23.5 / 63.0; walls 5.0 / 18.0 / 25.0; towers
  17.5 / 16.0 / 43.0; camps 10.5 / 14.5 / 32.0; traps 8.0 / 18.5 / 43.0. Every paired Δ is ≤ 0 except
  towers Short (+5.0 ±7.2), wins never above the fort-free turtle, and every home_turtle row passes (Bell
  ≤ 47%, was 61%). Forts lower the turtle's Bell share; the fort-free proxy alone fails the absolute
  15%, so no fort lever can pass it (weaker forts move the Bell toward 63%). Proposed: restate as "fort
  turtle Bell ≤ the same proxy without forts + 2, paired, upper bound + 5", with 15% kept as a target for
  the Bell work. Until decided, `FORT_SLOT_IN_BATTLE` stays off.
- **Fort AI value still below 50%** (42.8-52.4%; camps in Standard 42.8%). The planning window moved from
  300 to 500 lu before mid-lane (goal 700 → 900): wall placements rose a little (Short 0.51 → 0.59). The
  "too weak" levers fight the per-card and turtle rows; next step is an AI policy pass (when a camp pays).
- **B3:** sim-only Full War (recorded commands, bots excluded, 6 seeds): none 150 ms, camp 197, wall 191,
  tower 164, trap 155 against the 850 ms budget. With both bots and an observation every tick: 761 / 907
  / 809 / 697 / 750; the extra is AI time on levies. tuning.md's "897 ms over budget" note is corrected.
- **AI and meta:** bots bring their own Fort card (`withBotForts` in `app/matchSetup.ts`, before the source
  filter; scripted Generals and Echo keep theirs); summons (not only levies) never feed `pastMidTick`,
  `foeOnMyHalfTick`, the composition memory or counter targets; the once-per-age camp is spent only when
  the bot sees its camp stand (a rejected command no longer spends it).
- **Tools:** `cloneLoadout` keeps the Fort slot; `tools/forts.ts` (`sim:forts`) runs placebo, fort-AI and
  forced mirrors, value, fort_spam, turret/home turtles and flag_ball + towers, camp_hold_mirror,
  runner_camp (forced camps, Field pads first) and the per-card rows, with paired deltas and gates;
  `FortTurtleDriver` and `HoldDriver` live in `tools/proxies.ts`.
- **HUD and Army:** a drop on a blocked pad says why and shakes; the card in hand rides above the pad labels
  and names a hovered pad's reason ("No clear pad" when none is legal); the trap hazard sticker is gone
  (the art shows; the pips chip stays); the card, card detail, tray button and drag ghost use the fort's
  own rendered still; the Palisade wears a team banner with an ink outline in battle and on its card (the
  sheet's team layer read as a blob); the locked Fort slot names both routes ("Bronze 4", "or 🏆 400");
  fort cards stay out of Locked and the age totals until the save's slot opens; the fort tip is solid and
  hides the power hint; the tower reach band and the card's diagram start at the tower's edge; the fort
  cap reads "Max {n} up at once" and counts `maxTowers`.
- **Render:** no crumble sound or broken frames while a scaffold rises; an Engineers scaffold rises from
  0%; decay cracks survive the Siege switch; a wind-up toward a dead target no longer eats the next shot's
  crew animation.

## Lead decision 2026-10-01: forts go live; turtle gate restated

The absolute turtle gate (Bell ≤ 15%) cannot pass: the turtle proxy without any forts already reaches the Bell in 12.5 / 23.5 / 63% (Short / Standard / Full). Forts lower that share in every row except towers in Short (+5.0 ±7.2, inside noise). Adopted the proposed paired restatement: a fort turtle's Bell rate may be at most the same proxy without forts + 2 points (paired seeds, upper bound + 5); 15% stays a target for the general Bell work. `FORT_SLOT_IN_BATTLE` is switched on. Open: the fort AI's value is still below its 50% floor (bots use forts weakly), a few per-card rows sit outside ±3 at n=200, and towers in Short need an n=1,000 re-measure.

## Owner direction 2026-10-01: online = strangers and friends

Battle must offer both online matchmaking against strangers and friend battles via a code (or similar). The online milestone plan: friend battles first (a room code; peer-to-peer lockstep if a free, account-less signalling path works, otherwise the Cloudflare relay in server/), then public matchmaking for strangers on the Cloudflare Durable Objects relay, which needs the owner's free Cloudflare account. Until then, Battle plays AI opponents, always labelled AI.

## Owner decision 2026-10-01: MVP first, online after

Build the game up to a sensible offline MVP first; online (friend codes and public matchmaking) comes after the MVP. Home keeps the online-ready layout, with the friend entry shown as coming later.

## Online-first Battle hub and four battle lengths (lead design, 2026-10-01)

Owner request (Danish, verbatim in the task): set Home up so its layout is made for online battles, take inspiration from Clash Royale, the goal is 2 users playing each other, and offer a short, a medium and a long battle plus one with no time limit that ends only when a base is destroyed. Research and the full spec are in the session scratchpad (`online-home/RESEARCH.md`, `online-home/SPEC.md`). DESIGN A2.10, A2.10.1 (new), A6.3, A9 (#2-#4, #20-#21, A9.2), A15.8, A18.3.4, A18.9.5, A18.10, A18.11 and A18.13 were updated in place.

**Home**
- **Principles only.** From Clash Royale we take one Battle verb with the mode chosen beside it, the arena before numbers, the next reward in sight, one deck for all modes, and escalation instead of a buzzer. We take none of its names, art, positions or wording.
- **The liked frame stays:** diorama, trophy bar, War Path card, four capsule slots, plate over Battle, five tabs.
- **The Modes tile becomes the mode switcher.** It shows the selected mode ("Ladder · vs AI"). The panel *selects* a mode (UI flag `ui-homeMode.<id>`, so no save change) and never starts one. Battle plays what the switcher shows. The Ladder returns as a card and stays the default, so the Ladder is still 1 tap. ui-principles-short rule 2 ("Home's Play starts the next War Path level", out of date since 2026-09-30) is to be reworded in step H1.
- **The plate is the lobby card.** It shows exactly what Battle will do, in one state per situation (SPEC 1.4): the labelled AI General; online, a neutral silhouette until a player is found (never a face or name in advance); and the one choice the mode needs (the length, or a difficulty).
- **Online is designed now and shown only when it works.** The flows are: search with elapsed time counting up; after 25 s, a labelled AI General offered as a choice while the search continues; VS with a Player or AI chip; the Friend room by 6-character code; Rematch. The 2026-09-30 rule stands: no "coming soon" control. The states exist only behind `?dev=1` so the owner can see screenshots. An AI match never shows a search, and no player counts are shown anywhere.
- **Friend Duel pays nothing** (no Amber, Sundial claim, quest or War Chest progress), which rules out collusion farming and keeps red line 9. Casual online pays like the Ladder vs AI with no trophies; ranked pays trophies and the rating. Trophy sharing with the AI Ladder is decided at M5 (recommendation: one count, with a cap on AI-Ladder trophies).

**Lengths**
- **Short, Medium and Long are the built Short, Standard and Full War, renamed for players.** The ids and clocks are unchanged. The plate quotes the upper bound ("up to 8½ / 12½ / 17½ min"), never a median that the Bell share makes untrue. "Standard" is left free for "Standard levels".
- **Last Base Standing** (`last`, kind `untimed`, 7 ages; DA v1.1 "Til sidste base") has no Final Bell, no countdown and no win on HP. The steps are Overdrive 12:00, Siege I 14:30 (today's Siege without base decay), Siege II 17:00 (base ×3, turrets ×0.35), Siege III 19:30 (×4, ×0.25), Crumble 22:00 and Crumble II 24:30.
  - **The rope (Crumble):** the side whose own half holds the fight loses 0.5%, then 1%, of its base max HP per second; within a 40 lu dead band both sides crumble.
  - There is no evolve heal in Crumble.
  - A base therefore falls by 26:35 (`endByMs`, derived by a content test).
  - It is unranked: Full War Amber, no trophies either way.
  - Offered from Arena 3, in Skirmish, and in Friend Duel; never in online queues (capacity) or on the War Path.
- **Measured on a scratch emulation** (tier VII Echo mirrors, baseline plans at L7, 7-age window; indicative):
  - Removing only the Bell gave a median 16:56, a maximum of 17:50 and **20% draws** (both bases decayed to 0 on one tick).
  - Siege without decay gave a median 22:07 and p90 37:05, with 1 of 40 still running at 60:00. In the 5-age window **16 of 40** were still running at 60:00.
  - The chosen steps (n = 80) gave a median 19:35, p90 25:20, longest 26:14, 0 draws, 30% ending in Crumble and a first-mover split of 41/39. With 3:30 steps the turret turtle won 0 of 40 and cheap spam 0 of 40.
- **Sim:** one contract bump, `SIM_VERSION` 6.0.0.
  - The step is a pure function of the tick, so there is no new hashed state and goldens 01-14 must stay bit-identical.
  - New golden `15-last-base`.
  - AI: research and forts stay allowed in Siege I-III when there is no Bell; push value uses the step's base damage; the bot is aware of Crumble.
  - Gates and levers are in DESIGN A2.10.1.
- **Owner point to raise plainly:** this is "no clock", not "no limit". A war with no pressure was measured to run past an hour, and the free server cannot hold such rooms. The recommendation, taken, is the guaranteed escalation. The owner may still ask for a later Crumble.

**Online carry-over**
- Both players always play the same length: one casual queue per length, one ranked length (Short once its Bell rows pass, else Standard), and the host's pick in a Friend Duel.
- The relay cap becomes `(finalBellMs ?? endByMs) + 2 min`.
- Capacity: Last Base Standing at about 148 GB-s per war (at most about 201), about 88 a day (about 65 at the worst case), so Friend Duel only.

**Build order:** H1 (Home, now, M) in parallel with L1 (contracts and sim, M), then L2 AI (S), L3 HUD, art and sound (M), L4 meta and the fourth segment (S), L5 gates (S), and the owner check. Then M2 Friend Duel (owner's Cloudflare steps), M4 search, M5 ranked, M6 Friends button.

## Last Base Standing built: sim, content, meta, AI and gates (L1, L2, L4 meta, L5 smoke; 2026-10-01)

Built to the lead spec above (DESIGN A2.10.1). Decisions taken while building:
- **Unranked as a row flag.** `LadderWin.unranked: true` on `arenas.ladder.winByFormat.formats.last` (0 trophies, 35 / 70 Amber) instead of a separate `lossTrophies` override: an unranked row applies at every trophy count, and `trophyDelta` returns 0 for a win, loss or draw (`meta/trophies.ts isUnranked`). Everything else stays a Ladder match (Sundial or Clay pip, MMR, loss streak, counting win).
- **The observation carries the public schedule.** `Observation.escalation` is optional (absent in timed formats, so bots there see exactly what they saw before) and holds the step, the schedule in ticks with each step's values, and `crumbling` indexed by Side. Bots need it because `last` shares its 7 ages with the Full War, so the window alone reported the Full War's Bell (`ai/book.ts observedClock`).
- **Steps follow Siege.** A Siege-moving modifier shifts every step by the same ticks, so the rope stays on the Siege decay beat. `escalated` is also emitted for step 1, on the same tick as the Siege `phaseChanged`.
- **Forts keep today's Siege rule.** The sim still refuses fort placement in every Siege step (Siege I is "today's Siege, with the forts' Siege switch"); SPEC 2.6 point 2 (forts allowed in Siege I-III) would need a sim rule change, so the bot keeps its Siege check. Raise with the lead if forts in Siege are wanted.
- **No push-value change.** Bots already always push in Siege (`pushOk = siege`), so scaling the push value by the step's base damage would change nothing; not built. Research now continues through Siege when there is no Bell, and a bot whose base crumbles alone goes all-in (the Kettle all-in branch).
- **Feats, titles and quests:** feats naming the Full War also list `last`; the `finalAgeBefore` titles and the `fastFinalAge` quest count Last Base Standing as a Full War (same ages).
- **Goldens:** 01-14 re-recorded for SIM_VERSION 6.0.0 only; every final hash and per-second hash is identical (only `simVersion` changed). `15-last-base` runs on `fixtureLast` (the fixture plus a compressed `last` format, its own content hash).
- **Tools:** `sim-cli lbs` (new), with two new proxies: `idle` (never trains, builds, researches, casts or evolves) and `rope_runner` (4 turrets at home, one cheap runner at a time on Charge). Headless matches of a format with `endByMs` are capped at `endByMs` + 2 min, as the relay is (`server/shared/match.ts maxTicksFor` now reads `endByMs`). The fort-turtle row is not in `lbs` (forts cannot be placed in Siege anyway).
- **UI touch:** the Mode select's default ladder length and `nextFormat` (War Plan averages) skip untimed formats, so Last Base Standing is only ever chosen on purpose.

## Online-first Home and the battle-length UI built (H1, L3 HUD, L4 picker; WP9, 2026-10-01)

Built to the lead spec above and the owner's "MVP first" decision (the friend entry shown as coming later).
- **Battle plays the mode on the switcher.** The Modes tile is the mode switcher (`home/switcher.tsx`): icon, mode name and a tag ("vs AI"), with a caret. The Modes panel (`warPath/ModesSheet.tsx`) is its chooser: one card per row with a check on the selected one; a tap selects the mode (`ui-homeMode.<id>`), closes the panel and flies the card's icon into the tile (MR-120). It never starts a match, so it has no primary. Ladder is a card again and the default. Skirmish's "Set up" opens the setup; its Play also selects Skirmish and remembers the setup (`ui-skirmish.*` flags); the plate then shows it with Change. Conquest stays a card that opens its board until A18.7.10.
- **Friend Duel is shown now as a locked card** under "vs players", with the line "Arrives with online play" and, on tap, what it will be (a room code, any of the four lengths, every card level 8, no trophies). This follows the owner's 2026-10-01 MVP decision; it is a locked card (like "Opens at 6 wins"), never a button that pretends to work, and it has no timer. Online Battle and Ranked are not shown before they work.
- **The plate** (`home/plate.tsx`) has a state per mode: Training, Ladder (one length at Arena 1; the four-length picker from Arena 2; Last Base Standing with an info panel and a once-only caption), Quick Battle (a difficulty stepper), Daily (Recruit / Veteran / Warlord on the plate, `ui-dailyDiff.<d>`), Skirmish (summary and Change). Every bot carries the AI chip. The line quotes the upper bound and the win trophies as a pill.
- **The length picker** shows all four lengths from Arena 2; a locked one keeps its place with a padlock and says on tap which arena opens it. Under 300 px plate width the fourth segment is the cracked-tower glyph alone (aria-label "Last Base Standing, no clock"). Home keeps the shortest open timed length as its default; Last Base Standing is only ever picked on purpose. The Mode select uses the same short labels.
- **Online is a dev-only mock** (`home/online.tsx`, `onlineMock`; `?dev=1#screens/home-online*`, `home-friend`, `home-room*`, `home-join`, `vs-online`): plate states P8-P14, the search (count-up clock in the over row, tips, "Play an AI General" after 25 s as a choice, Battle becomes a slate Cancel, the tab bar dims, Esc cancels), Found (Player chip, the diorama's "?" base clears with a squash, MR-122/123), the room panel (code flip-in MR-124, Copy/Share, the host's length picker, Ready once the friend has joined, Leave), the code entry (no look-alike characters), and the online VS (Player chips, length, "All cards at level 8", connection bars, Block and Report, 3 s, no skip). Nothing sets the mock in the shipped game.
- **HUD (L3, part):** `HudModel.escalation` is filled from the observation (`crumbling` stays indexed by Side). A war with escalation shows a counting-up clock with a 6-pip meter (gold Overdrive, red Siege I-III, cracked stone Crumble I-II) and the step's name; a tap drops the public schedule down (folds after 3 s, never pauses). Each step plays a 1.2 s banner (MR-125); Siege I replaces the timed "Siege! Bases crumble" banner, which would be wrong without decay. The crumbling side's panel gets a "Crumbling" chip and a cracked HP bar. `escalated` plays the Siege horn rule and lifts the music's intensity layer; `crumbled` drops stones from the base top (MR-126). VS shows "No clock · Siege rises every 2½ min from 14:30 · Crumble from 22:00".
- **Fixes:** `progress.ts ladderWin` honours the unranked row (as meta `ladderWinFor`), so no plate ever promises trophies for Last Base Standing. Strings: Medium War and Long War; feat hints name the Long War or Last Base Standing.
- **Not built yet:** the rope marker on the minimap and the mid-lane ground decal, dedicated `sfx.escalate`/`sfx.crumble` sounds (the existing Siege horn and debris stand in), the Result reason line ("Their walls crumbled"), the P15 night state's pulse change on the new plate states (unchanged behaviour), and the picker's collapse-to-chip fallback for a clipping locale (not needed in English).

## Online-first Home and Last Base Standing: review fixes (fixer, 2026-10-01)

The review found CI red, a Crumble gate that only passed on lucky seeds, and a Home that did not read as an online game. Fixed:
- **CI determinism.** Golden 15 was recorded on the fixture plus a `last` format, but the cross-engine spec replayed every golden on the plain fixture. The `last` fixture moved to `tests/fixtures/lastBase.ts` (shared by the sim helpers and `tests/e2e/determinism/entry.ts`), and the spec now picks each replay's content by its `contentHash`. The fixture is frozen: tuning `UNTIMED` never touches golden 15.
- **The Crumble gate at gate size.** `sim-cli lbs --mode full` on the first values ended 41.3% of 400 tier VII mirrors in Crumble (target ≤ 35%). Single levers moved it 1-5 points (table in DESIGN A2.10.1): the mirror stalls mid-lane, as the Full War does at its Bell. **Chosen:** Siege II ×3.5 base / turrets ×0.3, Siege III ×5 / ×0.2, Crumble from **23:00** at 1%/s and Crumble II 1.5%/s. Measured: median 19:53, p90 24:44, 32.5% in Crumble (35.0% on a second seed set), first-mover 49.3%, every exploit row at 0%. The guarantee moves earlier, from 26:35 to **25:44** (relay cap 27:44). The B3 time of the longest war stays at the noise floor of this machine (1.17-1.37 s against 1,300 ms; the Full War at its Bell is at its own budget here too): open for the lead. `sim-cli lbs` now re-times the longest war alone after the batch (best of 3) instead of trusting a time taken while 3 workers share the CPU.
- **Home reads as online-first.** The Modes panel always lists "vs players" first. Home gains one honest friend entry, per the owner's MVP decision ("the friend entry shown as coming later"): a quiet, locked chip left of the switcher, "Friend Duel · Later" on phones and "Arrives with online play" on desktop; a tap opens the panel on the Friend Duel note and never selects or starts anything. The online switcher tags read "vs players" and "vs a friend" (no more "Online / Online").
- **The No clock length on phones** shows the glyph over "No clock" in 11 px type, and its line is "7 ages · no clock" (one line, no jump; "no trophies" moved to the info panel). Its first-time caption waits behind a currency caption already on screen, and now says "at most about 25½ min" (from `endByMs`) instead of "usually 15-25 min", which was untrue for tier III mirrors.
- **Retreat in an unranked length pays 0 Amber** (`meta/rewards.ts`): it costs no trophies, so the 15 loss Amber made a Retreat at 1:00 a free farm at every trophy count. A ranked Retreat is unchanged.
- **Result for Last Base Standing:** a reason line ("Their walls crumbled at 23:41", "Your base fell at 15:00", "Both bases fell together", "You retreated") and the trophy row "Unranked · stays at N".
- **The Siege horn plays once at 14:30:** `escalated` step 1 lands on the tick of the Siege `phaseChanged`, which already plays it.
- **Spec and build agree on the AI.** DESIGN A2.10.1 now says what is built: research through Siege with no Bell, forts keep today's Siege switch (so no fort-turtle row), no per-step push value or pre-Crumble hold (bots already Charge in Siege), all-in when crumbling alone.
- **One rule for the ladder win row.** `formatKind`, `rewardFormat`, `ladderWinFor` and `isUnranked` live in `src/content/ladder.ts`; meta re-exports them and the UI's `ladderWin` calls the same function (the UI may not import meta).
- **The online VS mock** got equal, aligned plates (a spacer row where the opponent has Block and Report), team-coloured frames with an avatar halo, a clash glow and a light sweep.

## Lead decision 2026-10-01: Last Base Standing timing row (B3)

The headless sim of the longest no-clock war takes about 1.2-1.4 s best-of-3 against a 1.3 s budget; a Full War at its Bell already sits at 0.81-1.02 s against its own 850 ms budget on this shared machine, so the row is at the machine's edge rather than a player-facing problem (it is the cost of simulating a whole match headless, used by balance tools, replay checks and a future online relay). Accepted for now; it moves into the MVP performance pass, which profiles late Siege before re-baselining any target.

## MVP pass: bug hunt and first-30-minutes fixes (fixer, 2026-10-01)

From the two MVP audits (bug hunt on phone and desktop; four new-player runs of the first 30 minutes). Decisions taken while fixing:

- **Match 1 cannot stall.** The stance beat points at Charge (new target `stanceCharge`), never at the middle Hold button, and reads "Charge attacks. Hold guards your base." A new re-arming guard beat `m1.charge` ("Tap Charge to attack their base!", hand on Charge) shows whenever the army has been off Charge for 10 s in match 1, which has no clock; it ends on Charge and arms again. Adaptive hints start in match 2 (`STAGES.hintsFromMatch`) and show at most 2 per match (`ADAPTIVE.maxPerMatch`).
- **One prompt at a time** in match 2 too: a free-order beat waits while another prompt shows ("Drag the field" used to replace the Council beat mid-tap).
- **The first power use lands.** The drag hand aims at a legal point (the spot tap-to-aim would start at, inside the reach band; `BattleView.powerAimScreen`), and the camera brings it into view while the beat shows. The line says "Drag arrows onto them. Powers cost gold."
- **The Council beat follows the player**: Council button, then the Economy track, then the first ready pick, then the selected pick ("tap again"). Copy: "Open the Council: research more gold"; timeout 30 s.
- **Match 2 is winnable for a new player.** The Rookie AI bonus (onboarding matches only, owner feedback 2026-09-28 unchanged) rises from 1,000 to 5,000 bp. Measured in a scratch lab (starter plan L1 vs Pip tier 0, Short War, 30-40 seeds): random spam won 43% at 1,000, 87-90% at 3,000-4,000 and 100% at 5,000 (median 5:02); power spam and "a few, then evolve" 100%. A 2-age window was tried and dropped: it was not shorter. The Ladder keeps its matchmaking (Kettle tier I beat random spam 43% in the same lab: open for the balance track).
- **The Field power slot ships** (`FIELD_SLOT_IN_BATTLE = true`): the dock, Army slot, AI and the balance tools (which always played both slots) were built; an earned slot that said "Coming soon" broke U15. War Path first clears now pay their power (`applyWarPath` calls `grantWarPathPower`; Stone L5 opens the Field slot), closing `docs/requests/powers-warpath-grants.md`.
- **No fort or Field power before it is taught**: the Daily plays the Fort and Field slots only once the save has opened them (it still fills an open, empty slot).
- **A Ladder battle left by a reload counts as the Retreat it could have been** (`src/app/abandon.ts`): once Retreat is open (1:00) the app keeps a small local record; any end clears it; a boot that finds it applies a Retreat through meta and shows one toast. Before 1:00 nothing is recorded. Unranked lengths cost nothing, as a Retreat there does.
- **Capsule summary from a Result**: Home is always offered (secondary). While a Home unlock moment or the first upgrade waits, Home is the primary and the Result's path ("Next battle") the secondary, so following the primary returns to Home and the unlocks play.
- **Early capsules play in a Short War.** Capsule 2 brings Friar and the Bronze Onager (was the Gunpowder Field Surgeon, now in capsule 4 so every age's Support Rare still arrives, A3). A newly found card takes an *empty* troop or turret slot of its age in the active plan; nothing is replaced.
- **Starter Capsule show**: no guarantees panel or Odds button during the scripted show (they stay on later capsules and in the Capsules tab); its prompt is "Tap to crack it!". The A15.3 honesty line stays word for word (the copy test pins it).
- **The welcome Wardrobe Crate** holds a skin for an owned first-age card (in practice the Bonker), not a random card the player has never seen.
- **Same opponent in every length**: the Ladder's opponent draw uses one seed (`ladder`), so switching Short, Medium or Long changes the war, not who you fight. The Daily plate names today's General (`UiServices.previewDaily`).
- **Names beside an AI chip drop the "AI · " prefix** (HUD nameplate, Result line, Profile history); the chip is the label there. The prefix stays in the data and everywhere without a chip. The HUD foe name condenses to fit instead of an ellipsis.
- **Layout and copy**: blank Bronze, Industrial and Cosmic age glyphs drawn; Quick Battle's VS chip says Quick Battle and the pause button "Quit battle"; the meta Pause is a compact card under the minimap with no scrim (closes `docs/requests/wp9-a17-pause-scout.md`) and paints scouted cards in the enemy's colours with a red mark; the emote picker closes on Esc (without pausing), any tap outside it and pause; the replay viewer leaves on Esc and browser or Android Back, its controls dock at the bottom and its badges are 12 px; Army on phones moves the collapsed Locked row beside Available; the Age Capsule picker fits phones (3 columns, no capsule art); the Capsules tab tiles drop their source line on phones so the Sundial stays above the tab bar; the Sundial card says only that the next capsules are free while free ones remain; Settings balances its two phone columns and "For parents" is a secondary; the unlock pointer also avoids the arena ribbon; the locked-length toast sits over the tapped length; Profile says "Rookie" for tier 0; "1 foil"; "Wars that reached the Cosmic Age"; onboarding Results do not announce titles; the "New slot" offer waits for match 2; the first screen shows the game's name.
- **Not changed, for other tracks or the owner**: the Amber flood of early capsules (balance), the renderer memory in long headless sessions (performance), a reload right after a lost match 2 still opens capsule 2 instead of offering "Try again" (rare now), the War Path's teaching order and the L2 title, a guided second upgrade, the editable auto name.

## MVP pass: sounds, effects and load performance (audio/FX and perf fixer, 2026-10-01)

From the audio/effects and performance audits. Gameplay timing unchanged (render, UI, audio and boot only).

- **31 new sounds** (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_mvp.py`, rendered and in the sheets; DESIGN A13 "MVP pass" row): the 12 UI ids that played stand-ins (`UI_SOUND_FALLBACK` is now empty), Council open, pick stamp and `research_done`, one cue per stance (the enemy's stance is heard 9 dB softer and seen on its troops), `escalate_horn` raised 2, 4, 7, 9 semitones per Last Base Standing step, `crumble_pulse`, `alert_heavy` (an enemy Heavy, at most every 8 s), `hit_armor_crack` (Anti-heavy ×2.5+ on a Heavy), `brace_clank`, `thunder` after Thunderstorm lightning, `vs_slam`, `sundial_claim`, `glyph_light`, and energy fort sounds (`fort_build_energy` for energy walls, `camp_warp`/`levy_warp` for Clone Bay and Warp Barracks, `trap_blast_energy` for Void Mine and Grav Mire, which now also implode in light instead of `fx.blast`). Power casts play `power_cast`, the fort denial `fort_denied`, equips and crafts `ui_stamp`/`ui_deny`, album and Customize taps their UI sounds; stars stamp a step higher each (`kit.sound(id, { pitchBp })`).
- **Groups:** the new battle cues sit in the `match` sheet (it loads right after unlock) and the UI fallbacks borrow `ui_hover`, so the no-files ZzFX boot render stays under its 300 ms budget (299.6). The effect sheets total 2.2 MB (size test 2.5 MB).
- **Effects** (`src/visuals/effects/mvpRecipes.ts`, DESIGN A14.1): stance cues on a side's frontmost units; slow and snare marks that last as long as the status (one mark per unit, renewed only as it runs out, so a field's pulses do not stack them); Brace plant; a research glint at the base for Defences, Economy and Command; a team pennant over every levy; a stronger `fx.fort_build_pop`.
- **AAC copies** re-encoded at 56 kbps music, 48 sheets, 40 stems (the 96 s age tracks are 684 KB, inside B16's 700 KB; all audio 25.2 → 19.9 MB).
- **Load:** Home no longer waits for battle art; a match started from the meta screens waits for the Stone and Bronze art (the VS screen stays up meanwhile). The first launch's capsule title, the autopilot and `?quick=` still wait at boot. Ages load per match (the side's age and the next one, from the battle view), never all in idle time. The boot service chunks are modulepreloaded, the stylesheets no longer block the first paint (the app waits for them before its first screen), and `index.html` paints a static splash. With recorded sheets the ZzFX boot render is skipped. The audio prefetch follows the eight-age order.
- **Battle hitches:** puppets bake ahead only where the procedural tier draws them; every effect particle bakes once in idle slices after boot; parts baked one at a time in battle go to small 512 pages (`PartBaker.seal()`), so a first effect uploads about 1 MB instead of a 16 MB page.
- **HUD:** the fort lane re-reads its nodes only when they change, its width on resize, and writes a style only when it changed; the minimap reads its size and team colours on resize (and once a second), not every frame.
- **Measured** (scratch build, the audit's throttled server): phone 4G CPU 4x Home 9.2 → 3.7-4.3 s, first paint 4.7 → 0.4-0.8 s; slow 4G Home 31.6 → 10.3 s; desktop 4G 7.4 → 2.65 s; warm 3.9 → 2.3 s. No other age downloads after Home (was about 9 MB). Heavy battle, 60 s: texture uploads 62 → 34, full-page uploads 32 → 5, upload time 700 → 198 ms. Frame-time numbers are not comparable on this shared, GPU-less machine.
- **Not done (open):** unloading ages after a match, streaming music instead of decoding it, splitting screens out of the main chunk, a service worker, smaller texture formats, the Home idle animations, fewer display objects per unit, one shared HUD frame loop, a quiet loop under lingering field powers and more War Path power variants, the realistic-pipeline forts (they still do not match the cartoon units), the minimap rope marker, the VS backdrop, ambience for the other backdrop skins.

## Owner request 2026-10-01: many more collectibles

After the MVP pass, keep expanding the collection (troops, powers, turrets, forts, skins) so there is something to collect for a long time. Plan: roughly double the roster, about 20 troops, 6 turrets, 8 powers and 6 forts per age plus at least 3 troop skins per age, built age by age (Stone first) with cartoon art, sound, AI support and per-card balance, then an economy re-tune so time-to-max stays on target.

## MVP pass: balance (balance lead, 2026-10-01)

From the balance audit (`scratchpad/mvp/audit-balance.md`): most even matches ended at the Final Bell, powers did too much of the killing, `flag_ball` broke its gate, six Legendaries and ten late powers sat far outside ±5, and bots used forts badly. Data first (content numbers), AI policy where a bot misused something. Work log, before/after tables and every lever tried: `scratchpad/mvp/balance.md`; raw runs in `scratchpad/mvp/lead/` (`final/` holds the gate runs on the shipped content). Content `9d1ccf1e` → `d20e8eb9`; `SIM_VERSION` and the goldens are untouched (the goldens play the frozen fixture).

**What changed (content)**
- **The power trim** (audit path A): every Home bombard and sweep costs 25 gold more (125) and hits one enemy fewer; every Field charge and front barrage hits one enemy fewer. Exceptions found by the gates: Strafing Run keeps 100 gold (at 125 the Modern lane row, mono Anti-heavy vs mono Heavy in `w1.modern`, fell from 100% to 0%), and Broadside and Point Defense Grid stay at 100 gold and cap 4 with 135 a hit (both lost 8-10 points to their starter).
- **Base HP 8,000 × P** (was 10,000 × P). The schema rule is now "the same multiple of P in every age"; the frozen fixture keeps 10,000. Base time to kill 126 s → 63-103 s (target 40-60 s, still open: ×0.6 cut the Bell further but shortened the Full War median to 13:43 and match 1 more).
- **The falling gate at 300 lu** (`economy.gateFall.lu`, was 120): in Overdrive and Siege a unit killed within 300 lu of its own gate costs its base its max HP. The strongest single Bell lever measured (tier V Short 25 → 9%, Standard 19 → 12.5% on top of the trim and base HP ×0.6); it needs no new rule, HUD or AI clock, unlike the rope.
- **Legendaries** (per card vs baseline, tier V, n = 400): Bronze Colossus 1,700 HP, Molten Heart 100 (+14.2 → +1.2); Ursa Paladin 2,150 HP, a 2.0 s swing, a 35 shield (+8.8 → +1.6); Balloon Admiral 1,200 HP, bombs 85, crash 200 (+5.2 → +0.1); Land Dreadnought 1,800 HP, gun 85, riders 6 (+8.5 → +0.8); Behemoth Tank 2,500 HP, gun 130 every 3.5 s, MG 14 (+19.0 → +1.6); Chrono Titan 4,500 HP, 190 a swing, Time Stop every 20 s (+12.3 → +4.0); Mothership 2,100 HP, beam 40, Drone Strike 150 every 10 s (+26.0 → +1.9 at n = 800). HP cuts alone barely moved Ursa, the Behemoth and the Dreadnought (a useless 800 HP Behemoth reads −5, so the slot itself is worth about −4); attack speed and damage did.
- **Powers**: the five War Path strikes (Hunter's Spear, Apollo's Arrow, Railway Gun, Sniper Team, Ion Cannon) cost 50 and reload in 15 s, and Sniper Team hits for 895 (65% of the Tankette) after a 1.0 s telegraph (Ion Cannon −20.5 → −2.6, Sniper Team −8.5 → −3.7); their damage is pinned at 55-65% of the Heavy (A2.9.6), so price and reload are the levers, and the schema allows strikes 50-150 gold and 15-60 s. Field Hospital 75 gold, 30 s, a 110 shield for 6 s on top of its 35% heal (a pure heal lost 9-15 points to Iron Horse whatever its numbers, −3.0 now); Royal Decree 75 gold, 30 s, +35% damage, +25% attack speed and +20% speed for 15 s (−9.3 → 0.0); Aegis 75 gold, 30 s, a 120 shield and +25% damage for 8 s; Medusa's Gaze 350 lu; Stasis Field 300 lu and 3 s; Horse Artillery 115 a shell (cap 4 from the trim; −7.0 → −1.4); Singularity 100 gold, pull 40%, 27 a pulse, snare 50% (it beat Starfall by 7; at cap 4 or 45 s it failed its control budget or stayed +6). Every static family budget passes.
- **Camps**: levies 35% of the age's Infantry Common (was 30%: camps sat 6-7 points under the wall in Bronze and Gunpowder; mean camp delta −2.2 → +0.3).
- **Match 1** ends 14 s sooner (Grogg falls at 2:10, was 2:24; the Future evolve at 2:06): `MATCH1_TIMING` is retimed as its test prescribes, and the A8 order holds.
- **Strings**: the eight camp cards and the camp tip said "every 8 s, two at most" (data: every 10 s, one at a time since the fort review fixes); the power and Legendary descriptions follow the new caps, timers and buffs.

**What changed (AI)**
- **A set ball** (`brain.ts`): a held enemy line that has held for 20 s counts ×1.5 in the push gate's D. `flag_ball` 45 / 47% → **29.3 / 13.0%** (Short / Standard, n = 200, gate ≤ 45%); mirrors unchanged (a bot never holds 20 s between waves). Rejected: ×1.3-×1.5 on every held line (the mirror's Short Bell +6 points), reading the enemy's guard research (a Short War flag ball owns none), the 700 flag cap and smaller guard auras (the audit: 59-63%).
- **Fort placement** (`forts.ts`, measured earlier in the pass): walls, towers and traps go up once the wave is in the bot's half (`FORT_APPROACH` 0, was 500 lu: half the walls decayed unhit), and a camp only joins a push already worth 1.5× the enemy army (`CAMP_AHEAD_BP`; 1.2× read 43.8% in Standard War on the final content).
- **Last Stand margin** (`brain.ts`): in Overdrive and Siege the margin adds the falling-gate risk (own units within 300 lu of the gate, at the Siege multiplier), so a manual Last Stand never meets an automatic one (`lastStandNotArmed` once in 100 tier VII-III Full Wars).

**Measured on the shipped content** (`lead/final`, `lead/final2`; bots carry their Fort card; n = 400 per format unless noted)

| Row (Short / Standard / Full) | Before | After |
|---|---|---|
| Final Bell, tier V mirror | 38.7 / 42.7 / 21.3% | **14.8 / 14.0 / 1.8%** (target ≤ 20%) |
| Final Bell, tier VII mirror (release row; target 10 / 8 / 5%) | 48.0 / 57.3 / 32.3% | **23.8 / 8.8 / 3.8%** |
| Draws, tier VII mirror | 15.3 / 32.3 / 20.0% | 9.0 / 2.8 / 1.5% |
| Medians, tier VII mirror (targets 7:00 / 10:30 / 15:00) | 8:27 / 12:30 / 16:28 | 7:49 / 10:52 / 15:14 |
| Medians, tier V mirror | 8:09 / 12:09 / 16:07 | 7:27 / 10:26 / 14:53 |
| Balanced-mirror tool (tier V, starters, no forts, n = 200): Bell; 80% bands | 46 / 42 / 16.5%; 97.5 / 78 / 46% | 15.0 / 17.5 / 3.5%; 95.5 / 68 / 55% |
| Power share of enemy value killed (target 5-12%) | 19.9 / 22.4 / 21.1% | 17.9 / 19.7 / 17.4% |
| One cast's share of a big army (p50, target ≤ 40%) | 46.7 / 46.9 / 45.0% | 36.7 / 40.0 / 33.3% |
| Base time to kill (target 40-60 s) | 126 s | 63-103 s |
| `flag_ball` vs tier VII (≤ 45%, n = 200) | 45.0 / 47.0% | 29.3 / 13.0% |
| `fallback_turtle` vs tier VII (n = 200) | 7.0 / 51.0% (35 / 90% at the Bell) | 0 / 0% (6 / 10% at the Bell) |
| `no_power` vs Balanced (20-40%) | 32.0 / 27.0% | 39.5 / 38.0% |
| Last Base Standing, tier VII mirror (n = 80) | median 19:53, 32.5-35% in Crumble | median 16:13, p90 19:33, longest 23:54, 0 past 25:44, 3.8% in Crumble, 0 draws |
| Per card (n = 400; reruns at n = 800 where shipped numbers changed) | 7 Legendaries +5.2 to +26, 10 powers −5 to −20.5 | every unit and power within ±5 except the Mammoth's empty-slot row (−5.0) |
| Fort AI value, camps (Standard) | 40.0% | 46.6% (walls, towers, traps 50.3-53.3% in Standard, 47.4-49.8% in Short) |


**Still open (for the lead and the owner)**
 - **Tier VII Short War Bell 23.8%** (target 10%). Its Siege lasts 2:00. The audit's rope (`FormatDef.escalation` in the timed formats: the side pushed back into its own half loses base HP) measured 0-14% but needs HUD, AI-clock and Result work and the owner's yes. Next data levers: the Short War's Siege 30 s earlier, the falling gate at 400 lu (tier V 9 / 9.5%).
 - **Power share of kills 17-20%** (target 5-12%) while damaging casts return 0.97-1.04 per gold (target 1.2-2): the two A2.9.12 rows pull opposite ways and want a design look, not another trim.
 - **Base time to kill 63-103 s** (target 40-60 s); **age stays 1:56-2:23** after the first (A18.3.1: 1:30-2:05; the audit's XP ×0.85 lever); Standard and Full 80% bands 68 / 55% at tier V (the tier VII mirror's spread is narrower).
 - **`no_research` wins 41% in Short War** (gate ≤ 30%; 26.5% before): shorter Short Wars pay research back later than they last. Standard 23.5% passes. `few_then_evolve` vs Normal 11.5% in Short (≤ 10%; 11% before).
 - **Turtles win 0-5%** against a 35-45% band: the audit's proposal stands, make the band a ceiling (A2.14, A18.12).
 - **Last Base Standing median 16:13** against 17:00-23:00: every war still ends (longest 23:54, 0 past 25:44), now mostly in Siege I-II instead of Crumble; re-baseline the band or move Siege II-III later.
 - **Mammoth Matriarch**: never trained in the standard per-card test (350 gold in a 62-79 s Stone stay), so its −5.0 is the missing Drum Shaman; in a Stone-only war it reads **+46** (n = 400). Needs a design call (a cheaper Stone Legendary, or Legendaries out of one-age windows), and the per-card tool should test first-age cards where they are played.
 - **Turret per-card rows measure nothing** (0.0 ± 2): the bot never builds the second mount. A WP12 tool change (force the second mount in the test plan).
 - **Cosmic forts** vs the wall: camp +7.2, trap +6.5, tower −5.1; **camp AI value** 46.6% in Standard (floor 50%).
 - Two levers need the renderer before they can ship: the siege crowd (`gateCrowdLu` 600: tier V 17 / 17.5% on top of the trim and base ×0.6; it piles a whole army on one spot where `render/depth.ts` spreads only the last 90 lu) and front width 4 (no fourth front row).
 - First-mover at tier VII reads 55% in the lab mirrors (Short and Standard, n = 400, ±5); the gate row (tier V tool) passes at 53.0 / 51.8%.


## MVP pass: release check (release checker, 2026-10-01)

Re-ran the new-player (match 1 with the Hold trap, capsule 1, match 2, capsule 2, first upgrade, first Ladder match) and returning-player (Arena 2 save: Home, Modes, Army, Capsules, a Ladder battle with emote picker, Pause and a reload after 1:00, a full Ladder win, replay, Daily plate) playthroughs on 844x390 and 1280x720 after all three MVP tracks. Fixes:

- **Arrow Storm beat pays its new price.** The balance pass raised Arrow Storm to 125 gold; the match 1 script still granted 100 (`MATCH1_POWER_GOLD`), so the "free" first cast cost the player 25. Now 125, and a test pins it to the content price. Match 1 retiming test still passes.
- **"AI · " beside an AI chip** is also dropped on the Home opponent plate (`PlateFrame`, whenever it shows the chip) and in the replay badge; procedural Commanders read "AI · Tessric Boneshield" next to the chip there.
- **HUD foe name on phones**: the condense-to-fit ran once, before the research rings took their space, and a 125 px name had 53 px (below the 62% floor), so it was cut mid-word. `useFitLabel` now refits when its box changes size, and when even the condensed name does not fit, the nameplate shows the given name only (whole words, never cut). The full name stays on the VS screen, Pause and Result.
- **DESIGN A6.3 "Void matches"** now names the exception added by bug hunt #9 (a Ladder battle left after 1:00 counts as a Retreat).

Measured: match 2 headless on the shipped content (starter plan L1 vs Pip tier 0, Rookie 5,000, 40 seeds): random spam 100% (median 3:33), power spam 100%, "a few, then evolve" 93% (was 100% before the balance pass). In the browser a slow scripted tapper (13-16 units in 7 minutes) lost match 2 twice; an active random tapper (37 units) won in 6:08. Not changed: replay "Back" and Esc go Home, not back to the Result (existing behaviour); the "Drag the flag" bubble can sit half off the left edge when the flag is at the screen edge.

## Owner decision 2026-10-02: the Siege rope in Short, Medium and Long War (built and tuned, 2026-10-02)

The owner said yes to the MVP balance pass's open proposal: in the timed formats "the side pushed back into its own half slowly loses base HP, like the No clock crumble rule", to bring the Short War Final Bell near its 10% target. Spec in DESIGN A2.10.2; scratch runs in the session scratchpad `anim/rope/` (`lab-results.tsv`, `ex-*`, `fb-*`).
- **Data, not a new sim rule.** Every Short, Medium and Long window (any start era) carries `FormatDef.escalation` built from `TIMED_ROPE` (`raw/economy.ts`): a rope step at Siege and a tighter one 45 s (Short) or 60 s (Medium, Long) later; the Final Bell stays. The sim already ran A2.10.1's steps in any format (no symmetric decay, the rope on the 20-tick beat, base and turret damage from the step, no evolve heal while a rope step runs). War Path and custom windows (1, 2, 4 ages) keep the symmetric decay. The schema now allows steps in `short`/`standard`/`full` formats (first step at `siegeMs` with a rope, every step before the Bell, no `endByMs`).
- **Values:** Short 0.6%/s, then 1.1%/s with base damage ×3 and turret damage ×0.35; Medium 0.55%/s, then 1.05%/s; Long 0.4%/s, then 0.85%/s.
- **No `SIM_VERSION` bump, no golden change.** The only sim-side change is an additive, unhashed observation field (`ObservedEscalation.finalBellTick`); goldens 01-15 play the frozen fixture. The content hash moves (`d20e8eb9` → `95a7cf2a`; the value also includes the paused content expansion's partial data in the tree), so older timed replays show their result card only, as after every tuning pass.
- **Bots know the rope** (`src/ai`): `observedClock` keeps the Bell when a timed format has steps (it used to drop the Bell whenever steps existed); 30 s before a rope that runs from Siege, a Holding bot stages its flag short of mid-lane instead of inside its turret cover; Last Base Standing's "crumbling alone, go all-in" rule is not used where a Bell exists. Why: with the rope, `flag_ball` won 52% of Short Wars at every rope rate (it used to stall at the bot's gate to a 40/40 draw at the Bell; the rope handed it those wars). A wider dead band (120 or 200 lu) did nothing; the staging gave 47%, both rules 41%.
- **UI:** the timed clock keeps its countdown and timeline, gains a cracked-stone mark where the rope tightens, names "Siege"/"Siege II", and drops the schedule down on tap; the Siege banner says "The side in its own half crumbles", Siege II has its own banner; the crumbling side gets the Last Base Standing chip and cracked HP bar (`ropeView` beside `escalationView`). VS shows "Push or crumble · Siege from 6:30: the side fighting in its own half crumbles" (`vs-rope`; the Last Base Standing row now needs a format with no Bell). The Result's reason line now also covers timed rope wars ("Your walls crumbled at 7:41", "You led at the Final Bell", "Even at the Final Bell"). A new adaptive hint, `crumbling` ("Your base crumbles. Push past the middle!", 4 rope beats in 20 s, points at the stance control). The search tip and Last Base Standing's "Crumbling doubles" (it rises 1 → 1.5%/s) were corrected.
- **Measured** (tier VII mirrors with bot Fort cards, n = 400 per format, seeds 1-400; before = the same seeds with the rope patched out):

  | Row (Short / Medium / Long) | Before | After |
  |---|---|---|
  | Final Bell, tier VII (target 10 / 8 / 5%) | 23.8 / 8.8 / 3.8% | **8.0 / 5.0 / 4.0%** |
  | Draws, tier VII | 9.0 / 2.8 / 1.5% | 0 / 0 / 0% |
  | Medians, tier VII (7:00 / 10:30 / 15:00) | 7:49 / 10:52 / 15:14 | 7:39 / 10:58 / 15:21 |
  | Final Bell, tier V (n = 200) | 14.8 / 14.0 / 1.8% | 8.5 / 9.0 / 5.0% |
  | `flag_ball` vs tier VII, Short / Medium (≤ 45%, n = 200) | 29.3 / 13.0% | 41.0 / 16.5% |
  | `fallback_turtle` / `turret_turtle` / `tech_turtle`, Short / Medium | 0 / 0, 0.3 / 2.0, 0.5 / 3.3% | 0 / 0% each |

- **Open:** the medians are set by age pacing (A18.3.1), not by the rope (Short 7:39 against 7:00); the turtle band in A2.14 should become a ceiling (turtles now win 0%, as the audit proposed); the rope marker on the minimap and in-battle screenshots of the rope (checked in the HUD state gallery, VS and Result at 844x390 and 1280x720); `src/content/generated/counters.json` is stale from the paused content expansion (not regenerated here).
- **Smallest fix to the paused content work:** `validateContent` failed on the structural schema because some Generals' Stone War Plans now list 6 troops (`LoadoutSchema.units` required exactly 5), which hid every later content check; the loadout schema now accepts 5 or 6 troop slots (A18.9).

## Unit animation: Stone pilot review fixes (pilot fixer, 2026-10-02)

The review of the Stone animation pilot (scratchpad `anim/review/`) found that real matches still floated. Every point was verified and fixed; the standard for the other ages is in `art/blender/README.md` ("Recipe: bring another age to the standard") and ANIM_SPEC section 8.
- **Glide in real matches (pre-existing):** the sim's `unitSpawned` plays `spawn`; no sheet has spawn frames, so the view held the idle fallback as a one-shot that looped forever and drew it over the walk. Now a missing spawn is code motion only (pop and dust), one-shots never loop, and `atlasVisualDef` no longer copies the idle's `loop` onto one-shot fallbacks. In an AI vs autoplayer match the share of moving unit-frames that show the walk went from about 10% to 96.5%. New e2e `tests/e2e/unitMotion.spec.ts` (real spawns, not dev spawns).
- **Variant impact timing:** the pipeline writes `impactFrame` as a unique-frame index; the runtime read it as a step, so 13 of 15 B/C variants showed their impact up to 130 ms late. The runtime now finds the impact step from `impactAt` (then `impactStep`, then `sequence.indexOf(impactFrame)`); the pipeline also writes `impactStep`; `check_timing.mjs` now fails any sheet whose impact step does not start at `impactAt`.
- **One-shots against movement (view only):** an attack gives way to the walk after its impact hold once the sim walks the unit; a hit while walking keeps the walk (squash and flash only); while a one-shot or the idle shows and the sim already moves the unit, the planted body holds and the walk melts the offset away (no 4-6 px pop); walk switches cross-dissolve for 110 ms (the carry pose no longer pops to the guard); a rider shot started during the gore plays `attack_alt` afterwards when 250 ms or more remain; a Fall back retreat longer than 700 ms turns the unit to walk home forward; hold loops start after 200 ms; a legacy rider with wheels (War Chariot) is not frame-locked.
- **Art:** Pebbler B, Drum Shaman B, Tuskback C and Sabertooth C re-posed so their held extremes differ from A at 62 px (hip ring behind the body / upright release; drum high overhead / kneeling slam; reared forehand; low stalking crouch). Variant muzzles may be up to 25 lu from A's when the release is deliberately different (Pebbler B 23 lu); the projectile still leaves the hand.
- No sim change, no `SIM_VERSION` bump, goldens untouched; A clips and every `durationMs` / `impactAt` unchanged (`check_timing.mjs`).

## Unit animation: Bronze Age to the standard (anim Bronze agent, 2026-10-02)

The seven shipped Bronze sheets (Hoplite, Javelineer, Phalangite, Standard Bearer, Scorpion, War Chariot, Bronze Colossus) were re-rendered to ANIM_SPEC with the Stone recipe. Captures in the session scratchpad `anim/bronze/`.
- **Bipeds (G1/G2):** `rigs_bronze.skeleton_v3` / `sandal_legs_v3` (thigh pivots at 19.5 lu, foot joints with sole trackers, the upper body 2 lu higher, the far leg 20% darker, 8.8 lu sandals, hems lifted); walk v3 at card speed x 1.25 with a carry pose that differs from the guard. A shouldered or sloped-back spear is hidden by the Bronze crest, so the Hoplite and Javelineer trail the weapon low with the point forward; the Phalangite carries the sarissa at the port (40 degrees up); the Standard Bearer (G2 brisk walk) holds the standard high and tipped forward. Attack and variant feet are placed by IK (`rigs_bronze.plant`): planted feet stay on the ground, a wide stance lowers the body by at most 4 lu, beyond that the back foot drags.
- **War Chariot:** the horse trots at ground speed with IK-planted hooves; the wheels became 4-spoked so 2 spoke spacings per cycle (22.5 degrees a frame) match the ground speed at a 576 ms trot (the 6-spoke wheel needed a 386 ms cycle). Its sheet writes `gait: "rider"` (frame-locked like a mount) instead of ANIM_SPEC G6's `wheeled`: with planted hooves the lock keeps the hooves on their pixel and the wheels turn exactly one frame's advance per frame, so nothing slides; unlocked, the hooves would sawtooth about 6 px.
- **Scorpion:** `wheeled` (no lock). The crewman moved to the near side so his legs show while he pushes (his soles give the natural speed); three team spokes per wheel (the pattern repeats every 120 degrees, the wheel turns exactly that far per 688 ms cycle).
- **Bronze Colossus:** G3 heavy walk, 10 frames over 1.25 s, IK feet with a heel-toe roll; the stride is authored in character space at 50 / cos 46 degrees because the statue is turned 46 degrees to the camera.
- **Variants:** B for all seven, C for the melee units (Hoplite, Phalangite, War Chariot, Colossus). Hold loops on the Javelineer, Standard Bearer and Scorpion (warp 2.4x, 2.1x, 4.1x): their step lists gained a hold-loop partner frame (javelin pump, dart twirl, aim jiggle) right after the hold, with `durationMs`, `impactAt` and the pre-impact sum unchanged (`check_timing.mjs` passes against HEAD). Variant muzzles are within 8 lu of A's (4.6-7.7 lu).
- **Checks:** `check_walk.py` energy now counts luma changes as well as the silhouette (ANIM_SPEC section 1 defines motion energy as alpha or luma > 30); a rolling spoked wheel keeps its silhouette, so the alpha-only measure read the new Scorpion at 0.05 (the old sheet reads 0.19, the new 0.43 with luma). Hoplite and Javelineer show the feet apart in 3 of 8 frames (gate 4), the same as every Stone G1 pilot unit.
- **Not done:** the paused content-expansion Bronze sheets (Rhodian Slingers, Shield Bearer, Thracian Raider): their wave owns them (ANIM_SPEC 8.2) and their cards are not in the compiled content.
- No sim change, no `SIM_VERSION` bump, goldens untouched.

## Unit animation: the Medieval Age to the standard (animation pass, 2026-10-02)

All seven shipped Medieval sheets were re-rigged, re-animated and re-rendered to ANIM_SPEC (walk at ground speed with planted feet, 2-3 attack variants, idle, hit and die kept); manifest regenerated, portraits regenerated, raw frames deleted.
- **Bipeds (Footman, Longbowman, Pikeman; Friar G2):** new `kit_medieval.skeleton_v3` / `legs_v3` / `legs_ik` / `jog_gait` / `ArmChain` (the `rigs_medieval` API the Bronze rigs import is unchanged): thighs at 19.5 lu, foot joints with 8.8 lu boots, far leg 20% darker, upper body lifted 2 lu. G1 bounce jog 616 ms with a carry pose (sword sloped back on the shoulder, pike sloped back over the shoulder, bow low and diagonal); the Friar's habit is shortened to 11 lu above the soles and he waddles a G2 brisk walk (560 ms).
- **Riders (Destrier Knight, Ursa Paladin):** G5 trot with IK hooves/paws on every leg (`_foot_<leg>` trackers), 740 ms horse / 900 ms bear (10 frames, Legendary), mount bob 6-10 px, the rider posting a frame late, lance, banner, plume and tail trailing.
- **Battering Ram (G6):** six-spoke wheels turning exactly 2 spoke spacings per 570 ms cycle (56 lu/s ground speed, 15 degrees per frame), hull bump and pitch, the crew jogging, a new rear pusher whose legs drive behind the shed, rear-wheel dust.
- **Variants** (held extremes differ by body level, weapon angle or hit direction; same `durationMs`, `impactAt` and pre-impact sum as A): Footman B overhead chop over the rim, C shield bash then a stab; Pikeman B overhead downward push, C double jab rising; Longbowman B kneel and loose (muzzle 24.5 lu from A's: a different release); Friar B overhand sling (2.3 lu); Ram B double pump with the shed rearing; Destrier B horse rears and the lance drives down, C lance sweep from over the rump; Ursa B bite and overhead hammer smash, C reared double-paw slam with an underhand hammer spin. The Friar's A and B get `holdLoop` [2, 3] (sim wind-up 2.07x the authored pre-impact time).
- **Measured:** `check_walk.py` passes for six units (step 20.5-28.4 px, natural speed 0.98-1.00 x ground speed, drift ≤ 0.21 lu); the Ram's silhouette-only vehicle energy is 0.25 against the 0.30 gate (0.20 before; its wheels now turn at the right speed but a turning wheel does not change the silhouette). Real AI vs autoplayer match with both sides set to Medieval: moving units show `walk` on 98.3% of frames at 844 x 390 and 1280 x 720, no console errors. `check_timing.mjs` unchanged against HEAD. Budget (PNG, HD+SD, core plus extras): +21% to +48% per unit (limit +50%); Ursa 1.86 MB (Legendary cap 2.0), Destrier 1.35 MB and Ram 1.17 MB (cap 1.4), small units 0.38-0.70 MB (cap 0.75).
- No sim change, no `SIM_VERSION` bump, goldens untouched; render code unchanged.

## Unit animation: the Industrial Age to the standard (animation pass, 2026-10-02)

All seven shipped Industrial sheets (Riveter, Carbineer, Harpoon Gunner, Flare Spotter, Sapper, Steam Golem, Land Dreadnought) were re-rigged, re-animated and re-rendered to ANIM_SPEC; manifest and portraits regenerated, raw frames deleted. Captures, lane GIFs, `walk_before_after_industrial.png` and `extremes_ABC.png` in the session scratchpad `anim/industrial/`.
- **Bipeds (G1):** the Medieval walk-v3 body (`kit_industrial` re-exports `kit_medieval.skeleton_v3` / `legs_ik` / `jog_gait`) with new `kit_industrial.legs_v3` boots and slim shins; hems raised to 13 lu (`hem_z` on the shared clothes), the Carbineer's duster tails and the Riveter's hammer and rag shortened so nothing hangs into the sole band. Bounce jog at card speed x 1.25: Riveter 90 lu/s in 600 ms (wrench on the shoulder, far fist pumping), Carbineer, Harpoon Gunner and Flare Spotter 81.25 lu/s in 616 ms (carbine at port, harpoon gun on the shoulder, binoculars at the chest with the pistol pumping), Sapper a 540 ms sprint at 106 lu/s with an 18 degree lean. The shipped FK attack tables are kept; `ground_feet` re-plants their feet by IK on the longer legs.
- **Steam Golem (G7):** IK walker legs on `gait.Gait`, 10 frames in 900 ms at 68.75 lu/s, an 11 lu foot lift, a contact jolt, foot pads squashing, knee steam per step, no hull squash. Its standing crouch went from 6 to 3 lu so the planted feet reach the ground (the old idle floated 1-3 lu).
- **Land Dreadnought (G6 tracked):** the step time went from 100 to 80 ms so 2 grouser spacings per 640 ms cycle match 43.75 lu/s; a 4.4 lu heave, 1.8 degree pitch and rear dust. A new `mgMuzzle` tracker gives the R3 accents and the `attack_alt` bullets (both sponson gunners fire, the body idles; `noMuzzle`, so the runtime uses the frame's `mgMuzzle` instead of the main gun's muzzle).
- **Variants:** Riveter B overhead wrench slam, C straight jab with a ratchet twist; Carbineer B kneeling aimed shot; Harpoon Gunner B kneeling braced shot (the appendix's standing shoulder shot reads like A at 62 px, so B changes the body level); Flare Spotter B pointed flat shot; Sapper B underhand bundle toss and duck; Steam Golem B rising uppercut piston, C shoulder ram with a steam blast; Land Dreadnought B main gun angled up with the hull reared, funnels belching. Hold loops (sim wind-up 2.06-3.14x the authored pre-impact time) on Carbineer, Harpoon Gunner, Flare Spotter, Sapper and Land Dreadnought, A and B: the shipped hold is split into the hold and a partner frame (aim wobble, jiggle, fuse fizz) with `durationMs`, `impactAt` and the pre-impact sum unchanged. Optional C clips (Carbineer hip snap, Sapper plunger) were not made.
- **Muzzles:** Harpoon B 4.5 lu from A (kneeling, the gun tipped up), Carbineer B 12.5 lu (kneeling), Dreadnought B about 23 lu (elevated barrel); the Flare Spotter's B muzzle is 47 lu from A's on purpose (A fires straight up overhead, B flat at arm's length). The runtime starts every projectile at the playing variant's own muzzle, so it always leaves the barrel.
- **Measured:** `check_walk.py`: natural speed 0.98-1.00 x ground speed, drift ≤ 0.12 lu, step 22.9-27.9 px, bob 5.4-7.9 px on all seven; the Golem passes G7; the bipeds show two feet in the sole band on 2-3 of 8 frames (gate 4, the same as the Stone and Bronze pilots); the Dreadnought's vehicle energy rose from 0.16 to 0.23 (gate 0.30; the big riveted side frame is static).
