Team coverage ≥ 18% and the colour rule hold on every frame. Budget (PNG HD+SD, core plus extras, against HEAD): +18% to +47% per unit; Riveter 0.65 MB, Steam Golem 1.07 MB, Land Dreadnought 1.84 MB (caps 0.75 / 1.4 / 2.0). `check_timing.mjs` unchanged against HEAD.
- **Portraits:** Riveter and Sapper stay in `UNITS_WITHOUT_STILLS` (WP4 `atlasPortrait.ts`); new stills for them were generated by the pass but removed again so the card art stays as the code expects.
- No sim change, no `SIM_VERSION` bump, goldens untouched; no render code changed.

- **Measured:** `check_walk.py`: natural speed 0.99-1.00 x ground speed, drift ≤ 0.21 lu, step 24.3-26.7 px, bob 6.4-7.6 px on the bipeds; Grenadier and Cuirassier pass, Balloon passes G8; Fusilier and Surgeon show two feet in the sole band on 2-3 of 8 frames (gate 4, the same as the Stone, Bronze and Industrial bipeds), the Corsair's contact gap is 3 px (gate 5, tall cuffed boots); the Cannon's silhouette-only vehicle energy is 0.26 (gate 0.30; 0.22 before the bigger hull bump, its turning wheels do not change the silhouette). Team ≥ 18% and the colour rule hold on every frame, no clipped frames. Real AI vs autoplayer match with both sides set to Gunpowder: moving units show `walk` on 97.3% of frames at 844 x 390 and 1280 x 720, no console errors (the bots field Corsair, Cuirassier and Fusilier; all seven checked with dev spawns). `check_timing.mjs` unchanged against HEAD. Budget (PNG HD+SD, core plus extras, against HEAD): +16% to +45% per unit; Corsair 0.69 MB (cap 0.75), Cuirassier 1.28 MB and Cannon 0.95 MB (cap 1.4), Balloon 1.08 MB (cap 2.0).
- No sim change, no `SIM_VERSION` bump, goldens untouched; no render code changed.

## Unit animation: the Modern Age to the standard (animation pass, 2026-10-02)

All seven shipped Modern sheets (Trench Raider, Rifleman, Bazooka Trooper, Radio Operator, Tankette, Gyrocopter, Behemoth Tank) were re-rigged, re-animated and re-rendered to ANIM_SPEC (walk at ground speed, 2-3 attack variants, idle, hit and die kept); manifest and portraits regenerated, raw frames deleted. Captures, lane GIFs, `walk_before_after_modern.png` and `extremes_ABC.png` in the session scratchpad `anim/modern/`.
- **Bipeds (G1):** new `kit_modern.legs_v3` (olive trousers, khaki puttees with slanted wraps on slim shins, 8.8 lu boots on foot joints, the far leg 20% darker, sole trackers, optional mud) on `kit_modern.skeleton_v3` (= `kit_industrial`'s); `rigs_modern.tunic` takes an optional `hem_z` (default unchanged, so the Industrial re-exports and the other callers are untouched) and the Modern tunics end at 13 lu. Bounce jog at card speed x 1.25 with a carry pose that differs from the idle guard: Trench Raider 93.75 lu/s in 584 ms, hunched, the spade sloped back on his shoulder and the far arm pumping (his sleeves are no longer rolled, so every walk frame keeps 18% team); Rifleman, Bazooka Trooper and Radio Operator 81.25 lu/s in 616 ms (rifle high at port, the tube sloped up on the shoulder, the handset kept at the ear with the pistol arm pumping). The shipped FK attack tables are kept; `ground_feet` re-plants their feet on the longer legs.
- **Tankette and Behemoth (G6 tracked, the track pilot):** the grouser phase per step is unchanged; only the step time changed so 2 grouser spacings per cycle match the ground speed of the scaled tracks (Tankette 57.5 ms steps, 460 ms cycle, 62.5 lu/s; Behemoth 92 ms steps, 736 ms cycle, 43.75 lu/s). Bigger hull heave and pitch, the crew a beat late, exhaust puffs and rear dust (the Tankette every step). `gait: tracked`.
- **Gyrocopter (G8, `gait: fly`):** the rotor is never static: one blade shape steps 45 degrees a frame over three alternating pale blur arcs fore and aft of the mast (they do not cover the pilot); nose down 7 degrees in forward flight, odometer at 100 lu/s; the hover bob stays in code (R8).
- **Variants** (held extremes differ by body level, weapon angle or hit direction; same `durationMs`, `impactAt` and pre-impact sum as A): Trench Raider B flat-side backhand swat (one-handed, from low behind the hip up to chest height), C spade-edge stab from a low crouch; Rifleman B kneeling shot; Bazooka Trooper B kneeling braced fire; Radio Operator B two-handed aimed shot in a low lunge, the handset dropped onto its cord (the appendix's standing two-handed pose read like A at 62 px); Tankette B turret traverse toward the camera and a quick shot, the hull rocking side to side; Gyrocopter B banked shot (rolled toward the camera); Behemoth B turret swing-in shot with a smaller, rolling rock. The Behemoth's machine gun has no body clip (R3 accent at the per-frame `mgMuzzle`). Hold loops (sim wind-up 2.06-5.0x the authored pre-impact time) on Bazooka, Radio, Tankette, Gyrocopter and Behemoth, A and B: the shipped hold is split into the hold and a wobble partner frame. Optional C clips (Rifleman hip snap, the vehicles and the flyer) were not made.
- **Muzzles (B against A):** Rifleman 8.7, Radio 8.4, Bazooka 9.7, Gyrocopter 10.5, Tankette 15.5 lu (kneeling, banked or traversed releases, within 25 lu); the Behemoth's B muzzle is 36.8 lu from A's on purpose (the turret is swung toward the camera and the long gun lifted). The runtime starts every projectile at the playing variant's own muzzle.
- **Measured:** `check_walk.py`: natural speed 0.98-1.00 x ground speed, drift 0.05 lu, step 22.9-25.0 px, bob 5.5-7.4 px on the bipeds; two feet in the sole band on 2-3 of 8 frames (gate 4, the same as the earlier ages); vehicle energy Tankette 0.29 and Behemoth 0.25 (gate 0.30). Team ≥ 18% and the colour rule hold on every frame, no clipped frames. Real AI vs autoplayer match with both sides set to Modern: moving units show `walk` on 97.8% (844 x 390) and 98.0% (1280 x 720) of frames, no console errors (the bots field Trench Raider, Rifleman, Bazooka and Tankette; the others checked with dev spawns). `check_timing.mjs` unchanged against HEAD. Budget (PNG HD+SD, core plus extras, against HEAD): core +0% to +4%, whole unit +20% to +39%; Trench Raider 0.68 MB (cap 0.75), Tankette 0.97 MB (cap 1.4), Behemoth 1.55 MB (cap 2.0).
- No sim change, no `SIM_VERSION` bump, goldens untouched; no render code changed.

## Unit animation: the Future Age to the standard (animation pass, 2026-10-02)

All seven shipped Future sheets (Photon Knight, Pulse Trooper, Rail Gunner, EMP Saboteur, Repair Drone, Walker Mech, Chrono Titan) were re-rigged, re-animated and re-rendered to ANIM_SPEC (walk at ground speed, 2-3 attack variants, idle, hit and die kept); manifest and portraits regenerated, raw frames deleted.
- **Bipeds (G1):** new `kit_future.skeleton_v3` / `legs_v3` (charcoal undersuit legs on slim shins, white knee pads, 8.8 lu glossy white armoured boots with a dark sole on foot joints, the far leg 20% darker, sole trackers, optional team thigh or greaves) on `kit_medieval`'s walk-v3 skeleton (the Future biped has the same joint layout; `rigs_future.py`'s API is unchanged because the Cosmic rigs and the Bronze Colossus use it). Photon Knight's tabard hem was lifted to 9 lu above the soles. Jogs at card speed x 1.25: Pulse Trooper and Rail Gunner 81.25 lu/s (616 and 640 ms), Photon Knight 93.75 lu/s (616 ms), EMP Saboteur sprint 106.25 lu/s (540 ms, 18 degree lean). Carry poses: rifle at port, rail cannon on the shoulder tipped up, sword sloped back over the shoulder, baton trailing level behind the swept-back arm. Kept A attacks re-plant their feet by IK (`kit_industrial.ground_feet`); the Rail Gunner's A kneel is now `kit_industrial.kneel`.
- **Walker Mech (G7):** reverse-knee IK legs on `gait.Gait`, 10 frames in 1000 ms at 62.5 lu/s, 12 lu foot lift, contact jolt with the foot pad squashing, visor squint and a vent puff per step, no hull squash.
- **Chrono Titan (G3):** IK legs on `gait.Gait`, 12 frames in 1560 ms (130 ms each) at 43.75 lu/s: the spec's 850-1300 ms cycle gives this slow Legendary a step too short for its boots to read apart, so the cycle is longer at the spec's per-frame limit; the boots were shortened from 34 to 26 lu and the far leg darkened 20%. Torso jolt and shoulder roll instead of squash (a machine).
- **Repair Drone (G8, `gait: fly`):** nose down 7 degrees, jets pulsing on a 2-frame beat (0.85-1.3x), arm and wrench trailing; odometer at 87.5 lu/s; the hover bob stays in code (R8).
- **Variants** (held extremes differ by body level, weapon angle or hit direction; same `durationMs`, `impactAt` and pre-impact sum as A): Photon Knight B overhead chop (blade straight up), C lunge thrust from a crouch; EMP Saboteur B overhead baton slam, C low backhand (baton cocked back low behind the hip); Pulse Trooper B hip-fire snap from a low crouch, C kneeling shot; Rail Gunner B standing shoulder shot (A kneels); Walker Mech B straight piston punch (ram drawn straight back), C low backhand sweep from a deep crouch; Chrono Titan B overhead chop from 12 to 6, C thrust from a wide crouch; Repair Drone B dive-and-spray (nose up with the arm cocked back, then nose down through the flare; Appendix B lists no B for the drone, added for variety, it never changes the heal).
- **Muzzles (B/C against A):** Pulse Trooper B 10.8 lu (hip), C 23.1 lu (kneeling); Rail Gunner B 16.5 lu (standing against kneeling), within the 25 lu allowance.
- **Measured:** `check_walk.py`: natural speed 0.95-1.00 x ground speed, drift ≤ 0.28 lu, step 22.9-31.2 px; bipeds bob 5.6-7.5 px and show two feet in the sole band on 3 of 8 frames (gate 4, the same as the earlier ages); Walker Mech passes G7, Titan G3 (bob 10.6 px; its wide boots never split in the 2 px sole band), Drone G8. Colour rule ≤ 0.2%, team ≥ 18% on every frame, no clipped frames. Budget: every unit under its hard cap; the extras sheets are +23-48% of the old sheet (Photon Knight, EMP Saboteur, Walker Mech, Titan and Pulse Trooper over the spec's +35%, like the earlier ages), the Titan's whole unit +53% (1.73 MB, cap 2.0 MB).
- **Real match** (AI vs autoplayer, both sides Future, `sides[i].ageIndex = 6`): moving units show `walk` on 98.5% of frames at 844 x 390 and 98.4% at 1280 x 720; A, B and C all played; no console errors.
- No sim change, no `SIM_VERSION` bump, goldens untouched; no render code changed.

Budget (PNG HD+SD, against HEAD): core +0%, whole unit +17% to +50%; Halberdier 0.89 MB is over the 0.75 MB small-unit cap (its sheet was already 0.61 MB: the 104 lu wide halberd sweeps), Legionnaire 0.70 MB, Hover Tank 0.89 MB (cap 1.4), Mothership 1.19 MB (cap 2.0).

## Release check: unit animations in every age and the Siege rope (release checker, 2026-10-02)

- **Animation in battle, every age, 844 x 390 and 1280 x 720:** one 36 s battle per age (real AI vs autoplayer forced into the age, plus all seven units of the age dev-spawned on both sides twice). Moving units show `walk` on 94.4-96.9% of moving unit-frames per run; each unit 87-99%, except the Balloon Admiral (72-73%) and the Mothership (88%), which keep bombing while the sim drifts them forward (flyers attack on the move; not a glide in the walk sense). Every unit played A and at least B (all C clips of melee units played; the Dreadnought and Mammoth also `attack_alt`). The Repair Drone never plays its clips: its heal is an ability (`healed` names only the healed unit), so the view has no trigger; pre-existing, a render request if the drone's beam should show. No console errors, no failed requests (scratchpad `anim/final/ages/`, `ages_summary.txt`).
- **Siege rope live:** Ladder battles started from Home on an Arena 3 profile (Short, Medium, Long; both sizes), fast-forwarded with both bases topped up to Siege and Siege II: VS row, clock tag "Siege" / "Siege II", schedule drop-down, Crumbling chip and cracked bar on the side in its own half, and the Result reason ("Your walls crumbled at 7:18", "They led at the Final Bell") all show; no console errors (`anim/final/rope/`). The dev route `?quick=` ends on the onboarding Result, which has no reason line by design (A8).
- **Gates rerun:** the rope lab (tier VII n = 400, tier V n = 200) and the exploit proxies reproduce the rope report exactly; flag ball in Long War 39.5% (limit 45%, new row). Last Base Standing smoke is unchanged from the MVP balance pass (median 16:13, open).
- **Frame cost and draw calls:** compared with a build of `ec813b9` (before the animation work) on the same sandbox battle: JS per frame and draw calls match within noise. Big Stone fights reach 100-290 draw calls in both builds (B16 budget 80): pre-existing, open.
- **Download:** phone unit sheets 11.2 → 15.4 MB (extras sheets 3.5 MB), HD 26.0 → 35.7 MB; initial chunk 964 KB.
- **Test fix:** `tests/integrity/hard-rules.test.ts` "Age of War appears nowhere" scans about 6.5 MB of sheet JSON and timed out at 5 s under a full parallel run; it gets the 30 s allowance its sibling scan has.
- **Blocker for publishing:** the paused Stone wave is compiled in without art or sounds, Generals' Stone plans use its cards (placeholder shapes in Ladder battles), and its cards drop from Arena 2. Not on `origin/main`. Gate it or ship its art before `main` moves.

## Release gate for unfinished content (release gate, 2026-10-03)

The paused Stone wave (W1) is compiled in without art or sounds. To publish the unit animations and the Siege rope now, content cards get a data-driven release gate instead of being deleted.
- **The flag:** `released?: boolean` (default true) on `UnitDef`, `TurretDef`, `PowerDef`, `FortDef` (and `FortSpec`) and `SkinDef`. `released: false` keeps the card in the compiled records, so the sim, tests (`tests/fixtures/x0.ts`, golden 16) and dev tools still use it; a skin of an unreleased card is unreleased too. `isReleased` / `unreleasedIds` live in `src/content/release.ts`.
- **Gated now (23 ids, exactly `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts`):** troops `hunting_wolves`, `hide_shield`, `torch_runner`, `bolas_thrower`, `woolly_rhino`, `atlatl_thrower`, `boulder_hurler`, `herbalist`, `pelt_rager`, `beast_caller`, `cave_bear`, `rockfall_shaman`, `elk_chieftain` and the summon `cave_pup`; turrets `quill_porcupine`, `sapling_sling`; forts `thorn_hedge`, `bone_watchtower`; powers `pebble_hail`, `tangle_vines`; skins `snowball_pebbler`, `fossil_sabertooth`, `aurora_elk`. (`rock_tosser` and `angry_beehive` are original cards with art; the wave only flagged them `starter`, so they stay released.)
- **Where it bites:** compile (`compile.ts`, `gate.ts`) leaves unreleased ids out of every `order` list (album, Army, collection, pools, wardrobe, forts, powers, skins) and lists them in `order.unreleased`; strips them from General plans (refilled to five troops and both turrets from released starters first, an unreleased power becomes the age's starter, a fort the wall) and signatures, Trophy Road rewards (4,100 keeps its Amber; 2,100 loses Pebble Hail), War Path side nodes whose reward is unreleased (Stone s1 and s2 are removed with their levels) and level reward cards; counter hints name released cards only (forts too, in `core/forts.ts`). Meta checks `isReleased` where it reads records directly: `isCollectable` (pools, Age and Sundial capsules, pity, starter kit, crafting), skin crafting and crate pools, `botMayUsePower` / `warPathPowerOf`, `botMayUseFort`, matchmaking's `allowedPlan` and `fullPlan` (Ladder, Daily, Conquest, Skirmish, Echo, War Path). AI: `botFortCard` and the next-age prediction pool skip unreleased cards (before, the sorted id list made `bone_watchtower` the Stone tower pick). App/UI: the capsule "unowned in pool" check, the heavy-gap tip, power yardsticks, War Path side rewards and the tutorial starter loadout.
- **Saves:** `origin/main` never had these cards, but `meta/release.ts` `withoutUnreleased` runs first in `tickTimers` (every load): unreleased cards leave the collection, owned powers, forts and skins, equipped skins and War Plans (troop and turret slots refilled from owned cards, powers to starters, forts to the wall). Same object back for a clean save.
- **Hash:** `released` is in `PRESENTATION_KEYS`: it never changes a battle, so the content hash and the counter-matrix input hash are unchanged (95a7cf2a), and flipping the flag later keeps replays and `counters.json` valid. The counter matrix still covers the wave.
- **Dev tools:** `?dev=1` content page lists the unreleased ids and marks unreleased units "UNRELEASED: no art yet".
- **Tests:** `src/content/test/release.test.ts`, `src/meta/test/release.test.ts` (bots in every mode, capsules of every tier and kind in every arena, crates, crafting, road, War Path, starter set, save loading), `src/ui/screens/test/release.test.ts`, `src/ai/test/release.test.ts`. Tests of the wave's own rules (roster shape, side nodes, arena pools, wave skins) run on `tests/fixtures/allReleased.ts`, the live content with the gate open.
- **When the wave resumes:** flip each card to `released: true` (or delete the line) in `src/content/raw/stone.ts`, `raw/powers.ts` and `skins.ts` once its sheet, puppet, effects and sounds ship. Everything above follows automatically (side nodes reappear once both their power and fort are released). Then drop the ids from `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts` and update `GATED` expectations in the release tests and the counts in `src/ui/screens/test/model.test.ts` / `interactions.test.tsx`.

## X0 Stone wave shipped: art, sounds, numbers, release (content wave, 2026-10-03)

The W1 Stone wave (CONTENT_PLAN 5.1) is released: 13 troops (Hunting Wolves, Hide Shield, Torch Runner, Bolas Thrower, Woolly Rhino, Atlatl Thrower, Boulder Hurler, Herbalist, Pelt Rager, Beast Caller, Cave Bear, Rockfall Shaman, Elk Chieftain) and the Cave Pup summon, 2 turrets (Quill Porcupine, Sapling Sling), 2 powers (Pebble Hail, Tangle Vines), 2 forts (Thorn Hedge, Bone Watchtower) and 3 skins (Snowball Pebbler, Fossil Sabertooth, Aurora Elk). `released: false` is gone from all 23 ids; `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts` now lists only the gated Bronze wave.
- **Sheets (cartoon v3, ANIM_SPEC):** 14 unit modules in `art/blender/units/` on the new `ageborn_art/kit_stone.py`; every unit has idle, a gait walk, 2-3 attack variants (A/B/C), hit and a KO die; team coverage ≥ 18% on every frame (bolas cord, boulder head band and belt, bear cape enlarged to get there), colour rule ≤ 2.2% except Rockfall Shaman 6.5%. `check_walk.py`: all quadrupeds and riders pass (wolves and pup bob raised, Elk scaled into the A11 Legendary band, 172 lu, with a uniform root scale); Herbalist and Rockfall Shaman are G2 (robed waddle, added to `CLASS_OVERRIDE`); the G1 "two feet apart" gate fails for most bipeds exactly as for the shipped Stone units (accepted per age). Portraits regenerated from the final frames; `unitSheets.gen.ts` regenerated; `check_timing.mjs` agrees.
- **Turrets and forts:** porcupine and sapling turret sheets (`world/turrets_stone.py`, listed in `WORLD_TURRET_SHEETS`); all six Stone forts redrawn in the cartoon kit (`world/forts_stone.py`, new `world/render_forts.py`), so the palisade's code-drawn dressing (`FORT_DRESS`/`PORTRAIT_DRESS`) is retired.
- **Effects:** `fx.tangle_vines` (root bed, tendrils, leaves), `fx.pebble_hail` with `fx.pebble_pop`, medallions for both powers, projectile sprites `proj.bolas`, `proj.dart`, `proj.herb`, `proj.quill`, `proj.snowball`. New render hook: a `power.fx.<id>.hit` feel rule plays on every unit a power hits (Pebble Hail's pebbles land on the 8 units it screens anywhere on the lane).
- **Skins:** procedural puppets like the 12 v1 skins (Snowball Pebbler: knit cap and snowballs; Fossil Sabertooth: bone-pale hide, rib bands, amber eyes; Aurora Elk: night-violet pelt, star speckles, mint and violet antlers). Like every skin today, an equipped skin draws its procedural puppet, not the base unit's 3D sheet.
- **Sounds:** 14 designed sounds (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_stone_wave.py`), rendered and packed into the `stone` sheet, ZzFX fallbacks in `src/audio/sounds.ts`, listed in DESIGN A13. The boot ZzFX budget test was already at 299.6 of 300 ms before the waves; request in `docs/requests/audio-boot-budget.md`.
- **Numbers (win delta vs the baseline plan, n = 400, tier V Balanced, L7; one-age window w1.stone / two-age window w2.stone):** Hunting Wolves 100 HP, 12 dmg: -2.4 / +4.9. Hide Shield (unchanged): -0.1 / +4.1. Torch Runner 166 HP, 20 dmg: +7.0 / -3.0. Bolas Thrower 100 HP, 10 dmg, range 165, slow 20%: +12.0 / -7.8. Woolly Rhino 580 HP, 33 dmg: +1.7 / -0.8. Atlatl Thrower 92 HP, 39 dmg: +1.1 / -3.9. Boulder Hurler 175 HP, 1.4 s: -4.5 / 0.0. Pelt Rager 176 HP, 17 dmg: -4.4 / +2.7. Cave Bear 800 HP: +3.6 / -4.2. Elk Chieftain 900 HP, 40 dmg: +4.9 / +1.9. Herbalist, Beast Caller, Rockfall Shaman, Sapling Sling: ±0.5 (the support slot and the second mount are almost never used by the Balanced bot, so these rows measure little). Quill Porcupine +0.9 / +0.2. Tangle Vines -0.4 / -0.3. Pebble Hail 48 per unit on 8, 50 gold, 25 s (the strongest the A2.9.4 lane budget and the schema allow): -30.6 / -10.1 against Stampede. Forts (paired, 400 matches, w1.stone): Thorn Hedge vs Palisade +1.3, Bone Watchtower vs Sling Perch 0.0.
- **Tools:** `balance --card-format` (the window a card test plays in); `tools/economy.ts` and `tools/drops.ts` count only released cards (gated cards never drop; before this the economy reported "not reached" and the drops pity row failed because of the gated Bronze cards). Economy with the wave (30 seeds): Common / Rare / Epic / Legendary to max 119 / 121 / 76 / 114 days (targets 110 / 101 / 69 / 112 ±20%: pass); whole collection 222 days (target 160; 194 before the wave, already failing); War Plan at L7 99 days (80 before). Drops smoke: 17 of 17 pass. The capsule re-tune of CONTENT_PLAN 8 (per-arena copy scaling) is left for when the Bronze wave lands.
- **AI:** `f_role` counts a squad as one card (brain.ts); counters regenerated with the final numbers.

## X0 Medieval wave shipped: art, sounds, numbers, release (content wave, 2026-10-03)

The W3 Medieval wave (CONTENT_PLAN 5.3) is released: 13 troops (Squires, Flailman, Brigand, Crossbowman, Greatsword Knight, Yeoman Archer, Warhammer Sergeant, Herald, Kennel Master, Mangonel, Siege Belfry, Alchemist, Lindworm) and the Kennel Master's War Hound summon, 2 turrets (Springald, Grapple Crane), 2 powers (Longbow Volley, Great Bell), 2 forts (Bear Snares, Crossbow Keep) and 3 skins (Greenwood Archer, Chess Knight, Bone Wyrm). `released: false` is gone from all 23 ids; `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts` lists only the gated Bronze wave. Mechanics: only existing ones (M1 squad for the Squires, M3 summoner for the Kennel Master, riders on the Belfry, `periodicShieldAura`, `callStrike`, `followBehind`); no sim change.

- **Sheets (cartoon v3, ANIM_SPEC):** 14 unit modules in `art/blender/units/` (`squire_pair`, `flailman`, `brigand`, `crossbowman`, `greatsword_knight`, `yeoman_archer`, `warhammer_sergeant`, `herald`, `kennel_master`, `mangonel_cart`, `siege_belfry`, `alchemist`, `lindworm`, `war_hound`). Every unit has idle, a gait walk, 2-3 attack variants, hit and a KO die; the Belfry's archers have `attack_alt`. Team coverage ≥ 18% and the colour rule ≤ 10% on every frame (worst 6.6%, Flailman); no clipped frames. Two-handed weapons are their own joints with IK'd arms; the Mangonel and Belfry reuse the Battering Ram's wheels. The Lindworm is drawn at body scale 1.25 (130 lu): at 1.4 its 74 frames did not fit a 4096 sheet. Card stills from `gen_portraits.py`; `check_timing` agrees. `check_walk.py`: the jog-gait bipeds miss the feet-apart gate like the shipped Stone wave bipeds; Alchemist step 19.2 < 20; Kennel Master gap 0; Mangonel and Belfry vehicle energy 0.29 / 0.18 (gate 0.30; the Ram is 0.25).
- **Turrets and forts:** springald and grapple-crane sheets (`world/turrets_medieval.py`, `WORLD_TURRET_SHEETS`); all six Medieval forts in the cartoon kit (`world/forts_medieval.py`: Shield Barricade, Longbow Tower, Levy Camp, Wolf Pits redrawn; Bear Snares and Crossbow Keep new). The two towers break top-down on fixed stages, so their crew platform sinks with the broken top instead of floating.
- **Procedural fallbacks:** every new unit and turret has a puppet in `src/visuals/puppets/medieval.ts` (size, group, muzzle; the sheets draw them). The Lindworm and the War Hound join the art test's small-by-design set (a long, low dragon; a summon), the Greatsword Knight and the Lindworm the heavy-pennant set.
- **Effects:** `fx.lindworm_breath`, `fx.grapple_hook` (instant attacks), `fx.longbow_volley` with `fx.arrow_thud` (on each unit hit, like Pebble Hail), `fx.great_bell`; medallions `power.longbow_volley` and `power.great_bell`; projectiles `proj.longarrow`, `proj.note`, `proj.vial`, `proj.spear_bolt`.
- **Skins:** palette puppets (Greenwood Archer: moss and leaf greens; Chess Knight: ebony horse, ivory plate; Bone Wyrm: bleached bone hide).
- **Sounds:** 18 designed sounds (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_medieval_wave.py`, musical ones in D), packed into the `medieval` sheet, ZzFX fallbacks in `src/audio/sounds.ts`, DESIGN A13 row. The long hits keep 2 variants and 0.6 s to hold the sheet at 239 KB. The effect-sheet byte test rises from 2.5 to 3 MiB: the three content waves add about 140 KB each, all in lazily loaded age sheets. The boot pre-render test still fails (476 ms of 300; boot groups are ui, battle, stone, bronze, so not this wave; request `docs/requests/audio-boot-budget.md`).
- **Numbers (win delta vs the baseline plan, n = 400, tier V Balanced, L7; w1.medieval / w2.bronze, final sweep):** Squires 2 × 131 HP, 16: +0.9 / +0.3. Flailman 216 HP, 22 cleave: -2.6 / -0.4. Brigand 228 HP, 27, base 52: +0.9 / +4.8 (r6 at base 56). Crossbowman 43 (armored ×1.25): +0.3 / 0.0. Greatsword Knight 760 HP, 42: +3.6 / +4.4. Yeoman Archer 62: -3.0 / -4.6. Warhammer Sergeant 350 HP, 35: +4.0 / +4.9. Herald: -0.5 / -2.0. Kennel Master (hounds 160 HP, 16, every 6.5 s, max 3): +1.0 / -4.3 (r7). Mangonel: +4.0 / -3.1. Siege Belfry 900 HP: +4.1 / +1.4. Alchemist: +0.1 / -0.4. Lindworm 1,170 HP: -3.6 / +5.8 (the two windows disagree by about 9 points at every HP from 1,120 to 1,200; 1,170 is the best measured). Springald +0.1 / -1.3. Grapple Crane +0.4 / +2.3. Great Bell (zone 350): +0.1 / -1.8. Longbow Volley -16.8 / -17.5: it already sits at every limit (64 per unit = the 30% lane cap, 50 gold, 25 s, cap 8), like Pebble Hail. Forts (paired, 400 matches, w1.medieval): Bear Snares vs Wolf Pits 0.0, Crossbow Keep vs Longbow Tower -0.3 (the bot rarely builds forts, so these measure little).
- **Economy (30 seeds, 365 days):** with both waves released the median card to max is Common 140 / Rare 146 / Epic 88 / Legendary 133 days (targets 110 / 101 / 69 / 112 ± 20%): Common, Rare and Epic now miss. Measured fix: Common / Rare / Epic copies per stack ×1.3 / ×1.42 / ×1.38 (the pool grew C 40 → 52, R 24 → 34, E 16 → 22) gives 100 / 97 / 61 / 131 days and a copy-Amber gap of 20 days, but it moves the A6.4 table, the A6.9 copies-per-capsule and daily-copies targets and their tests, so it is filed as `docs/requests/content-economy-retune.md` for one re-tune after the Bronze wave (CONTENT_PLAN 8) instead of changing the capsule table mid-wave.
- **Counters** regenerated; contentHash `8377e0da`.

## X0 Gunpowder wave shipped: art, sounds, numbers, release (content wave, 2026-10-03)

The W4 Gunpowder wave (CONTENT_PLAN 5.4) is released: 13 troops (Highlander, Powder Monkey, Voltigeurs, Blunderbuss, Dragoon, Coehorn Crew, Wall Gunner, Drummer Boy, Bagpiper, Rocket Cart, Hussar, Mesmerist, Grand Marshal), 2 turrets (Carronade, Sea Mortar), 2 powers (Rocket Volley, Cannon Salute), 2 forts (Cavalry Picket with its Picket Rider levy, Fougasse) and 3 skins (Parade Cuirassier, Fireworks Grenadier, Pufferfish Balloon). `released: false` is gone from all 22 ids; `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts` lists only the gated Bronze wave. DESIGN A5.4 has the wave table.
- **Mechanics:** existing X0 ones (M1 squad: Voltigeurs; M4 Dread aura: Bagpiper; M5 `timeStop` with `frozen: false`: Mesmerist; H7 lane reach: Rocket Volley; H6 Long range: Coehorn Crew; `callStrike` and a `damageBuff` aura: Grand Marshal; `followBehind` cone: Blunderbuss). One new sim rule: a unit volley with `scatter` lands each projectile within ±scatter lu of the aim, drawn from the seeded sim RNG (the Congreve Rack's turret rule, now for units: the Rocket Cart). SIM_VERSION 7.2.0; all 17 goldens re-recorded with unchanged hashes (no golden uses a scattering unit); `src/sim/test/gunpowderWave.test.ts` covers scatter bounds, determinism per seed, the Marshal's aura and strike.
- **Sheets (cartoon v3, ANIM_SPEC):** 13 unit modules in `art/blender/units/`; every unit has idle, a gait walk, 2-3 attack variants, hit and a KO die. Colour rule ≤ 8.6% (Voltigeurs) and team coverage ≥ 18.6% on every frame; no clipped frames. The Coehorn Crew is a two-man crew on one root (gunner, mortar in front, mate with IK legs); its gunner topples from the hips in the die clip because the whole-rig D1 flip carried the mortar and mate with it. The Grand Marshal is drawn at body scale 1.5 (171 lu) to sit in the A11 Legendary band. `check_walk.py`: riders pass; the jog-gait bipeds miss the feet-apart gate like the shipped bipeds (Drummer Boy and Mesmerist also gap 0: drum and long coat hide the legs); the Rocket Cart vehicle energy is 0.27 (gate 0.30; the Mangonel is 0.29). Portraits from `gen_portraits.py`; `unitSheets.gen.ts` regenerated; `check_timing` agrees.
- **Turrets and forts:** carronade and sea-mortar sheets (`world/turrets_gunpowder.py`, `WORLD_TURRET_SHEETS`); all six Gunpowder forts in the cartoon kit (`world/forts_gunpowder.py`: Gabion Wall, Musket Redoubt, Militia Muster, Powder Keg redrawn; Cavalry Picket and Fougasse new).
- **Levy visuals:** a camp variant's levy placeholder is now drawn from its own Common (`LEVY_VISUALS.infantry`) with group `infantry`, not the age's Infantry puppet, so the Picket Rider (a Cuirassier at 0.85) and the Bronze Slinger Levy get the right size class and muzzle.
- **Procedural fallbacks and effects:** puppets for every new unit and turret in `src/visuals/puppets/gunpowder.ts` (Dragoon and Grand Marshal join the heavy-pennant set); effects `fx.blunderbuss_spray`, `fx.drum_boom`, `fx.pipe_drone`, `fx.mesmer_spiral`, `fx.rocket_volley` with `fx.rocket_pop`, `fx.cannon_salute`; projectile `proj.mortar_shell`; medallions for both powers; palette skins.
- **Sounds:** 15 designed sounds (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_gunpowder_wave.py`, musical ones in G), packed into the `gunpowder` sheet (313 KB Opus), ZzFX fallbacks, DESIGN A13 row. The Dragoon's shot is `shot_dragoon`: `shot_carbine` already belongs to the Industrial Carbineer, and a second registration had overwritten its render (re-rendered; the industrial sheet is unchanged).
- **Numbers (win delta vs the baseline plan, n = 400, tier V Balanced, L7; w1.gunpowder / w2.medieval; counters regenerated before each round):** Highlander 380 HP, 25: +1.4 / -3.0. Powder Monkey 262 HP, 34: -1.6 / +2.2. Voltigeurs 3 × 69 HP, 19: +3.0 / +1.4. Blunderbuss 57: -2.9 / +4.7. Dragoon 820 HP, 52: -0.5 / +4.2. Coehorn Crew 80: +3.9 / -0.1. Drummer Boy (aura +25% attack speed): -0.9 / -2.9. Bagpiper 420 HP, slow 35%: +3.6 / -1.9. Rocket Cart 1,300 HP, 4 × 70 / 3.5 s: +2.4 / +3.7. Hussar 1,000 HP, 78: -2.9 / +0.4. Mesmerist 1,050 HP, 58, every 12 s: -3.0 / -1.4. Grand Marshal: -2.5 / +0.2. Carronade -0.8 / +3.0. Sea Mortar -0.5 / -0.1. Cannon Salute (130 and a 2 s stun on 6): +0.7 / +2.0. Misses: **Wall Gunner** +16.2 / +15.2, and the row barely moves with its own numbers (damage 70 → 30 gave +21.8 → +15.9): it replaces the Grenadier, and the plan without the Grenadier simply wins more, so the row measures the Grenadier, not the Wall Gunner; kept at 210 HP, 40. **Rocket Volley** -4.7 / +9.5 at 80 per unit (87: -2.6 / +11.9): the windows disagree by about 14 points. Forts (paired, forced fort AI, vs the age's wall, n = 400, w1.gunpowder): Cavalry Picket 53.3% (+7.0 against the Militia Muster on the same seeds; its levy every 18 s), Fougasse 53.8% (+1.8 against the Powder Keg).
- **Measurement note:** the Epics and supports replace the Field Surgeon, which the Balanced bot trains about 3 times a match in Gunpowder; an Epic the bot rarely affords (the Rocket Cart was trained 0.3 times a match at first) measured -14 to -20 until its duel strength (HP, damage) lifted its counter score enough to be trained.
- **Economy (30 seeds, 365 days):** Common 153 / Rare 169 / Epic 103 / Legendary 147 days to max; per-capsule and per-day targets pass; drops smoke 17 of 17. Added to `docs/requests/content-economy-retune.md`.
- **Counters** regenerated; contentHash `95cac302` at the time of writing (it includes the gated Bronze data, which the Bronze wave is still tuning).

## Owner requests 2026-10-03: rating and War Path hover (queued)

- **Skill rating (queued after the content expansion).** A chess.com-style rating (Elo or Glicko-2) that later drives matchmaking so players meet others at the same level, and that the player can follow over time from Home. It must stay secondary on Home (a small chip or row with a trend, a history graph one tap away), never the main focus. Offline it rates results against AI opponents with honest, labelled AI ratings; online it becomes the matchmaking key. Trophies and arenas stay as the progression; the rating is the skill measure. Needs a design pass (rating model, provisional period, AI ratings, display, honesty rules A15) before building.
- **War Path hover animation (queued, small).** The hover/press motion on the War Path today just moves the image straight down. Replace it with a satisfying, correct interaction following docs/ui-plan.md motion rules: lift and scale with a soft shadow on hover, a squash on press, eased return, reduced-motion fallback, checked with Playwright on 844x390 and 1280x720.

## X0 Bronze wave shipped: Dread aura, art, sounds, numbers, release (content wave, 2026-10-03)

The W2 Bronze wave (CONTENT_PLAN 5.2) is released: 13 troops (Shield Bearer, Thracian Raider, Rhodian Slingers, Discus Thrower, War Elephant, Cretan Archer, Belly Bowman, Aulos Piper, Tragic Chorus, Wooden Horse, Amazon Rider, Minotaur, Hydra), 2 turrets (Net Caster, Polybolos), 2 powers (Sandstorm, Charybdis), 2 forts (Hoplon Line, Slinger Camp with its levy) and 3 skins (Marble Hoplite, Sun Chariot, Obsidian Colossus). `released: false` is gone from all 22 ids and the Bronze block left `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts`. DESIGN A17.9 has the wave table; A5.7, A16.14.4, A13 and A14.1 list its powers, forts, sounds and effects.
- **Sim (SIM_VERSION 7.1.0):** M4 Dread aura (`aura.foe: true`) and an ally `speedBuff` aura. Both are per-tick unit fields (`auraSlowBp`, `auraMarkBp`, `auraSpeedBp`), cleared and recomputed in the status system, so they end the tick a victim leaves the radius or the source dies, never stack, and only a stronger timed status of the same kind wins (it keeps its own timer). A Dread aura touches enemy ground non-fort units only and re-emits `statusApplied` on each heal-grid pulse so the slow mark shows. Goldens 01-16 re-recorded with unchanged hashes (only the version string); golden 17 (`17-dread-speed-siege-riders`, fixture `tests/fixtures/bronzeWave.ts`) plays the Dread and speed auras, the Wooden Horse (`siegeOnly` + `riders`, a first-time combination) and the Minotaur (`firstHitBonus` + `frenzy`); `src/sim/test/bronzeWave.test.ts` covers them and Sandstorm's lane snare.
- **Design calls:** the Hydra is one attack with cleave 3 and an on-hit slow (the plan's three independent heads would need a multi-attack rule per target; cleave reads the same in play). The Slinger Camp's levy is drawn from the Bronze ranged Common, which is the Javelineer, so the camp and levy are shown as Skirmisher Camp and Skirmisher Levy (ids unchanged); `LEVY_VISUALS` now allows a ranged or brute Common for an X0 camp variant. Bots keep bringing the first wall and camp by id (`botFortCard`), so Hoplon Line and Slinger Camp are player picks. Wave skins are palette puppets like the other waves' skins.
- **Sheets (cartoon v3, ANIM_SPEC):** 13 unit modules in `art/blender/units/` with idle, a gait walk (G1 jog, G2 strut for the Piper and Chorus, G3 Minotaur on v3 legs at 1.38, G4 four-beat War Elephant at 1,020 ms and trotting Hydra, G5 Amazon gallop, G6 Wooden Horse on 4-spoke wheels with a rear pusher), 2-3 attack variants with holdLoop timing where the sim windup is long, hit and a KO die; the Wooden Horse's riders have `attack_alt`. Team coverage ≥ 18% and the colour rule ≤ 10% on every frame (worst 9.7%, Thracian Raider idle); no clipped frames. Fixes on the way: aged bronze or leather instead of polished bronze on large areas (Minotaur, Raider's flying rhomphaia), team bands on the Belly Bowman, War Elephant and Hydra, the Polybolos crank turning about its own axle. `check_walk.py`: bipeds miss the feet-apart gate like every shipped jog biped (3/8); the Wooden Horse's vehicle energy is 0.22 (gate 0.30; the shipped Battering Ram is 0.25). Turrets (Net Caster, Polybolos) in `world/turrets_bronze.py`; the four Bronze forts restyled and the two new ones drawn in `world/forts_bronze.py` (`world/forts_toon_render.py`), all installed with card stills; portraits from `gen_portraits.py`; procedural fallback puppets for every new unit and turret (`src/visuals/puppets/bronze.ts`; the Hydra's is long and low like the Lindworm's).
- **Effects and sounds:** `proj.discus`, `proj.net`, `proj.arrow_arc`, `fx.note_pop`, `fx.wail_ring`, `fx.sandstorm`, `fx.whirlpool`, power icons; 15 designed sounds (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_bronze_wave.py`, musical ones in D) packed into the `bronze` sheet, ZzFX fallbacks, `analyze.py` flags none.
- **Numbers (win delta vs the baseline plan, n = 400, tier V Balanced, L7; w1.bronze / w2.stone):** Shield Bearer 240 HP, 16: -1.0 / -1.5. Thracian Raider 190 HP, 22, speed 90: -4.5 / +5.6 (a cliff: 170 HP read -38, 200 HP +28; the Hoplite's Shield Bash is worth a lot in the mirror). Rhodian Slingers 3 × 36 HP, 6 / 1.6 s: +4.6 / +12.4. Discus Thrower 90 HP, 12 / 1.6 s: +3.4 / +10.9 (after the last counter regeneration; -0.1 / +8.9 before). War Elephant 690 HP, 40 / 1.8 s: -1.4 / -0.8. Cretan Archer 38 / 2.4 s: -2.1 / +2.2. Belly Bowman 220 HP, 1.2 s: -2.5 / +1.9. Aulos Piper +0.1 / -1.0, Tragic Chorus +0.7 / 0.0 (the support slot is rarely trained). Wooden Horse -0.9 / +1.6. Amazon Rider 500 HP, 40: -1.1 / +2.4 (trained rarely). Minotaur 45 / 1.6 s: +4.2 / +1.6. Hydra 1,230 HP, 48 / 2.2 s: -0.4 / +6.4. Net Caster -2.9 / +1.4. Polybolos -0.9 / -0.5. Sandstorm 55 per unit on 8 and a 30% snare for 3 s (both the A2.9.4 lane caps): -6.6 / -1.1 (63 per unit read -3.4 / +1.1, over the cap). Charybdis 9 per pulse, snare 45%: -6.4 / +4.4. Forts head to head on the same seeds (n = 400): Hoplon Line vs Cyclopean Wall -1.3 / +1.8, Slinger Camp vs Muster Tents -1.8 / +2.3.
- **Misses and why:** the ranged Commons replace the Javelineer; the plan without it trains fewer ranged cards and wins more in the two-age window whatever the replacement's numbers (a useless discus reads -6.5, every playable one +9 to +12 in w2.stone), like the Gunpowder wave's Wall Gunner. The Hydra's and Charybdis's two windows disagree by about 7 and 11 points at every value tried. Counters regenerated after tuning.
- **Economy:** with four waves released, `economy --seeds 30`: Common / Rare / Epic / Legendary to max 176 / 194 / 116 / 163 days (targets 110 / 101 / 69 / 112 ±20%: fail), whole collection 335 days; copies and Amber per day pass; `drops --mode smoke` 17 of 17. The CONTENT_PLAN 8 capsule re-tune is now due; numbers appended to `docs/requests/content-economy-retune.md`.

## X0 Industrial wave shipped: art, sounds, numbers, release (content wave, 2026-10-03)

The W5 Industrial wave (CONTENT_PLAN 5.5) is released: 13 troops (Coal Miners, Iron Mantlet, Dispatch Rider, Bomb Bowler, Steam Tractor, Trench Mortar, Steam Driller, Bandmaster, Clockwork Tinker with its hidden Clockwork Soldier summon, Armoured Car, Alpine Climber, Spark Scientist, Armoured Train), 2 turrets (Rivet Spitter, Steam Hammer), 2 powers (Shrapnel Shells, Great Magnet), 2 forts (Rail Barricade, Tesla Pylon) and 3 skins (Chimney Sweep, Teapot Golem, Circus Train). `released: false` is gone from all 23 ids; `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts` lists only the gated Modern wave. DESIGN A5.4 has the wave table, A13 and A14.1 the sound and effect rows.
- **Mechanics:** existing X0 ones only (M1 pair: Coal Miners; M2 frenzy: the Armoured Train's Overpressure, the first frenzy on a two-attack unit; M3 summon: Clockwork Tinker; M5 `timeStop` with `frozen: false`: Spark Scientist; H6 Long range: Trench Mortar; H7 lane reach: Shrapnel Shells; Home pull and snare: Great Magnet; chain tower: Tesla Pylon; turret volley + pierce: Rivet Spitter; gate zone with slow: Steam Hammer). No sim rule changed: SIM_VERSION and the goldens are untouched. `src/sim/test/industrialWave.test.ts` pins frenzy on a two-attack Legendary (no bonus above the HP line, +20% damage and +25% attack speed at or below it, both attacks speed up on their own clocks).

The Armoured Train (body scale 1.3, 172 lu) and the Steam Tractor (1.12, 103 lu) are scaled into the A11 Legendary and Heavy bands; the Dispatch Rider and the Armoured Car are wheeled (odometer foot, wheel strobe rule). `check_walk.py`: the jog bipeds miss the feet-apart gate like every shipped biped (Iron Mantlet 0/8 and Clockwork Tinker gap 0: the wheeled shield and the long apron hide the legs); vehicle energy 0.24-0.26 for the Armoured Car, Steam Tractor and Armoured Train (gate 0.30; the shipped Rocket Cart is 0.27); Dispatch Rider and Trench Mortar pass. Portraits from `gen_portraits.py`; `unitSheets.gen.ts` regenerated; `check_timing` agrees. Procedural fallbacks for every new unit and turret in `src/visuals/puppets/industrial.ts` (a `machine()` vehicle and a `worker2()` biped helper).
- **Turrets and forts:** rivet-spitter and steam-hammer sheets (`world/turrets_industrial.py`, `WORLD_TURRET_SHEETS`); all six Industrial forts in the cartoon kit (`world/forts_industrial.py`: Trench Parapet, Sniper Nest, Recruiting Depot, Tripwire Charge redrawn; Rail Barricade and the crewless Tesla Pylon, muzzle on its copper sphere, new), `FORT_VISUALS`, `FORT_SHEETS` and `FORT_MATERIAL` (both new forts metal).
- **Effects:** `fx.coil_arc` (chain arc), `fx.hammer_shock`, `fx.shrapnel_shells` with `fx.shrapnel_burst`, `fx.great_magnet` (a horseshoe magnet part swings down over the zone), projectiles `proj.bowl_bomb` and `proj.rivet`, medallions for both powers, feel cues; palette skins (the Circus Train uses raspberry, outside the team hue bands).
- **Sounds:** 18 designed sounds (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_industrial_wave.py`; the Bandmaster's cornet in F#), packed into the `industrial` sheet, ZzFX fallbacks, DESIGN A13 row. The Armoured Train's roof gun reuses `shot_gatling`.
- **Numbers (win delta vs the baseline plan, n = 400, tier V Balanced, L7; w1.industrial / w2.gunpowder; counters regenerated before each round; final sweep):** Coal Miners 2 × 194 HP, 24: -0.9 / +4.2. Iron Mantlet 420 HP, 28: +1.1 / +5.1. Dispatch Rider 325 HP, 42, base 84: -2.4 / +8.4. Bomb Bowler 191 HP, 24: +2.9 / +11.4. Steam Tractor 1,200 HP, 66: +3.1 / +5.0. Trench Mortar 266 HP, 118: -0.4 / +8.6. Steam Driller 490 HP, 50: +0.6 / +1.1. Bandmaster: +0.7 / +1.5. Clockwork Tinker (soldier 168 HP, 16, every 8 s, max 3): -1.5 / -3.7. Armoured Car: -3.5 / +1.4. Alpine Climber 980 HP, 80: +1.5 / +4.4. Spark Scientist: 0.0 / +3.0. Armoured Train 1,520 HP: +0.9 / +1.4. Rivet Spitter +1.5 / +1.1. Steam Hammer +1.0 / +1.9. Great Magnet +3.7 / -0.9. Forts (paired seeds, both sides forcing their fort, vs the age's classic card of the same kind, n = 400, w1.industrial): Rail Barricade 53.0% (±4.0) vs the Trench Parapet, Tesla Pylon 50.1% (±2.3) vs the Sniper Nest.
- **Misses and why:** the steep cards: the Commons and the Trench Mortar move 20-40 points for a 5-10% stat change in the one-age window (Dispatch Rider 320 / 41 read -14.1, 330 / 42 read +0.1; Trench Mortar 260 / 115 read -26.1, 270 / 120 read +1.4; Clockwork Tinker 6.5 s / max 3 read +43, 9 s / max 2 read -46), so the final numbers favour the home window. The two-age window reads higher for every card that replaces the Riveter or Carbineer (Dispatch Rider +8.4, Trench Mortar +8.6). **Bomb Bowler** +11.4 in the two-age window does not follow its own numbers (weaker bowlers read higher: 160 HP, 21 gave +18.9): it replaces the Carbineer and the bot trains it little, so the row measures the plan without the Carbineer (like the Wall Gunner); kept at 191 HP, 24. **Shrapnel Shells** -25.3 / -24.1 at every limit (99 per unit = the 30% lane cap, 50 gold, 25 s, cap 8), like Pebble Hail and Longbow Volley. A `mark` status tried on it made the AI book read the volley as a control power (valued only by `aiValueBp`, 0), so the bot never cast it; the status is gone.

## X0 Modern wave shipped: bomber riders rule, art, sounds, numbers, release (content wave, 2026-10-03)

The W6 Modern wave (CONTENT_PLAN 5.6) is released: 13 troops (Commando, Sandbag Carrier, SMG Squad, Rifle Grenadier, Assault Gun, Mortar Team, Sticky Bomber, Combat Medic, Bulldog Sergeant, Dive Bomber, Bulldozer, Ghillie Sniper, Sky Fortress), 2 turrets (Anti-Tank Gun, Rocket Battery), 2 powers (Creeping Barrage, Concussion Shells), 2 forts (Rifle Depot with its Rifle Levy, Wire Snare) and 3 skins (Desert Raider, Tin Tankette, Origami Fortress). `released: false` is gone from all 22 ids and the Modern block left `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts`. DESIGN A5.5 has the wave table; A5.7, A13 and A14.1 list its powers, sounds and effects.
- **Sim (SIM_VERSION 7.3.0):** a bomber's riders shoot their own targets. The bomber rules (keep flying, attack only inside the drop window) now apply to attack 0 only (`bombs(r, ai)` in `systems/targeting.ts`), so the Sky Fortress's waist gunners pick, keep and hit targets in range like any rider while the plane flies on. Goldens re-recorded with unchanged hashes (only the version string); `src/sim/test/modernWave.test.ts` pins the gunners hitting an air unit 120 lu away, bombs only inside the window, the bomber never stopping, and the crash spawning two Riflemen.
- **Design calls:** the Combat Medic wears a plain cream helmet disc and a white armband, never a red cross (a protected emblem). The Rifle Depot's levy is drawn from the Modern ranged Common (the Rifleman, `LEVY_VISUALS`). Concussion Shells sit at the control damage cap (177 = 45% of the Trench Raider's EHP) with a 2.5 s stun (15 disabled unit-seconds per cast) instead of the planned 175 / 2.0 s, which read -4 / -9. Bots: every new card is in at least one General's Modern plan (`generals.ts`); Rifle Depot and Wire Snare are player picks like the other X0 forts.
- **Sheets (cartoon v3, ANIM_SPEC):** 13 unit modules in `art/blender/units/` on the shared `kit_modern_wave.py` (a `trooper()` body with hat, mud, chevrons and puttee options, the D1 die with a flying prop, leaf tufts for the ghillie suit); each has idle, a gait walk (G1 jog for the troopers and the two-man Mortar Team, G6 tracks for the Assault Gun and Bulldozer, G8 flight for both bombers), 2-3 attack variants (holdLoop timing where the windup is long), hit and a KO die; the Sky Fortress's gunners have `attack_alt`. Team coverage ≥ 18% and the colour rule ≤ 10% on every frame (worst 2.4%, Commando; team coverage at least 18.8%, Rifle Grenadier); no clipped frames (the long rifles of the Ghillie Sniper and the Rifle Grenadier now land flat, `RIFLE_PATH`). Fixes on the way: the Sandbag Carrier's bag joint (it rendered inside the body), the Mortar Team's tube moved clear of the gunner, team tufts on the ghillie suit, a team stripe on the sandbag, the Dive Bomber's muzzle ahead of the bomb. `check_walk.py`: the jog bipeds miss the feet-apart gate like every shipped biped (3/8; the Ghillie Sniper 0/8, its suit hides the legs); vehicle energy 0.28 / 0.29 for the Assault Gun and Bulldozer (gate 0.30; shipped tracked units 0.24-0.27); Mortar Team, Dive Bomber and Sky Fortress pass. Portraits from `gen_portraits.py`; `unitSheets.gen.ts` regenerated; `check_timing` agrees. Procedural fallbacks for every new unit and turret in `src/visuals/puppets/modern.ts`.
- **Turrets and forts:** anti-tank gun and rocket battery sheets (`world/turrets_modern.py`, `WORLD_TURRET_SHEETS`); all six Modern forts in the cartoon kit (`world/forts_modern.py`: Sandbag Bunker, crewless Pillbox with its muzzle on the MG slit, Forward Base and Minefield redrawn; Rifle Depot and Wire Snare new; cream chevron emblems on team tarps, bands and plates), `FORT_VISUALS`, `FORT_SHEETS` and `FORT_MATERIAL` (Rifle Depot wood, Wire Snare metal).
- **Effects:** `fx.creeping_barrage` with `fx.barrage_burst`, `fx.concussion_shells`, `proj.rifle_grenade`, medallions for both powers, feel cues; palette skins.
- **Sounds:** 17 designed sounds (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_modern_wave.py`), packed into the `modern` sheet, ZzFX fallbacks, DESIGN A13 row; the sfx budget test rose to 3.5 MiB. The Sky Fortress's gunners reuse `shot_mg`.
- **Numbers (win delta vs the baseline plan, n = 400, tier V Balanced, L7; w1.modern / w2.industrial; counters regenerated twice during tuning and after the last change; final runs after the release flip):** Commando 368 HP, 47, speed 110: +1.6 / -0.6. Sandbag Carrier 455 HP, guard 20%: -0.1 / -2.0. SMG Squad 3 × 74 HP, 11 / 1.0 s: +2.7 / +10.3. Rifle Grenadier 205 HP, 39 splash: -5.0 / -1.8. Assault Gun 1,350 HP, 88: -4.1 / -2.3. Mortar Team 266 HP, 124: -3.4 / -1.3. Sticky Bomber 580 HP, 124: -4.1 / -5.2. Combat Medic heal 65: +2.9 / +5.1. Bulldog Sergeant 415 HP: +2.9 / -0.9. Dive Bomber +1.2 / +0.9. Bulldozer +1.2 / +1.6. Ghillie Sniper +1.5 / +2.1. Sky Fortress 1,270 HP, bombs 70, gunners 6 / 0.6 s: +4.4 / -1.1 (it opened at +32 / +32 with 12 / 0.4 s gunners). Anti-Tank Gun +0.7 / -0.4. Rocket Battery +0.2 / +1.0. Concussion Shells -2.3 / -6.0. Creeping Barrage -24.6 / -16.9.
- **Misses and why:** **SMG Squad** reads +10 in the two-age window at every value tried (15 → 11 damage and 94 → 74 HP moved the one-age window from +10.5 to +2.7 but left the two-age window at +10): it replaces the Rifleman, and the plan without the Rifleman wins more in w2.industrial, like the Bronze ranged Commons and the Industrial Bomb Bowler. **Combat Medic** (+5.1) and the three Epics read the same at every value: they replace the Radio Operator in the support slot, which the bot trains rarely, so the row measures the plan more than the card. **Sticky Bomber** -5.2 and **Concussion Shells** -6.0 in the two-age window sit just past the band (the stun power is at the damage cap). **Creeping Barrage** sits at the lane caps (110 per unit = 28% of the Trench Raider, 50 gold, 25 s, cap 8) like every lane volley before it. The Commons are steep and noisy (the Commando read -12 at 370 HP / 46, +9 at 395 / 48 after a counter regeneration, then +2 at 368 / 47).

## X0 Future wave shipped: art, sounds, numbers, release (content wave, 2026-10-04)

The W7 Future wave (CONTENT_PLAN 5.7) is released: 13 troops (Android Pair, Barrier Trooper, Hover Biker, Needle Gunner, Crab Mech, Arc Lobber, Plasma Lancer, Overclock Engineer, Holo Projector with its hidden Holo Decoy, Jetpack Trooper, Particle Cannon, Overload Android, Drone Carrier with its hidden flying Attack Drone), 2 turrets (Cryo Pod, Tractor Beam), 2 powers (Target Painter, Nano Mesh), 2 forts (Skyguard Pylon, Mech Bay with its Mini Mech levy) and 3 skins (Space Cadet, Chrome Rail, Grandfather Clock). `released: false` is gone from all 24 ids and the Future block left `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts`. DESIGN A5.6 has the wave table; A5.7, A13 and A14.1 list its powers, sounds and effects. Sources: Commons from Arena 2 (the Future age first drops in Arena 3), Rares 3, Epics 4, the Legendary 5 (`cardArena`); Target Painter War Path Future L3 / Road 3,900; Nano Mesh, Skyguard Pylon and Mech Bay on the War Path side nodes and the Road 4,700 region set.
- **Mechanics:** existing X0 ones only (M1 pair: Android Pair; M2 frenzy: Overload Android; M3 summon: Holo Projector and Drone Carrier, whose summon flies; H6 Long range: Arc Lobber; lane signal: Target Painter; Home snare that hits air: Nano Mesh; slow and drag turrets). No sim rule changed: SIM_VERSION 7.3.0 and the goldens are untouched. `src/sim/test/futureWave.test.ts` pins a ground summoner with an air summon (the drone takes off at once, ground-only melee cannot reach it, anti-air shoots it down).
- **Sheets (cartoon v3, ANIM_SPEC):** 15 unit modules in `art/blender/units/` on `kit_future` (visor glyph faces) plus the shared `kit_future_wave.py`; each has idle, a gait walk (G1 jog for the eight bipeds and the Holo Decoy, 560-640 ms; a trotting four-leg walker for the Crab Mech; G6 tracks for the Particle Cannon and the Drone Carrier; hover for the Hover Biker; G8 flight for the Jetpack Trooper and the Attack Drone), 2-3 attack variants, hit and a KO die. Portraits from `gen_portraits.py`; `unitSheets.gen.ts` regenerated; `check_timing.mjs` agrees (153 units, 612 sheets). `check_walk.py` (re-run 2026-10-04): the jog bipeds miss the feet-apart gate at 3/8 like every shipped biped (the Barrier Trooper passes 8/8; the Arc Lobber's contact gap 4.0 vs 5.0); Crab Mech, Hover Biker, Jetpack Trooper, Attack Drone, Particle Cannon and Drone Carrier pass. Procedural fallbacks in `src/visuals/puppets/future.ts`; skins are palette puppets (`src/visuals/skins.ts`) like the earlier waves'.
- **Turrets and forts:** cryo-pod and tractor-beam sheets (`world/turrets_future.py`); all six Future forts in the cartoon kit (`world/forts_future.py`: Hardlight Barrier, Sentry Pylon, Clone Bay and Grav Mire redrawn from the old realistic style; Skyguard Pylon and Mech Bay new), colour rule 0% on every frame, team share on the flag layer as for the shipped forts.
- **Effects and sounds:** `fx.zap_beam`, `fx.particle_beam`, `fx.tractor_beam`, `fx.target_paint`, `fx.nano_mesh`, `proj.needle`, `proj.arc_shell`, `proj.frost`, medallions for both powers; 19 designed sounds (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_future_wave.py`) packed into the `future` sheet with ZzFX fallbacks.
- **Final tuning (2026-10-04, after a container restart):** Needle Gunner 20 → 13 damage per 0.5 s (it read +12 / +15.5); Hover Biker 68 → 66; Plasma Lancer 96 → 100 (it read -6.0 in the two-age window); Nano Mesh 19 → 15 a pulse and a 40% → 50% snare: 19 × 12 pulses was 49% of the Photon Knight, past the snare family's 30-40% damage band (`cards.test.ts`), and 50% is the Singularity's snare.
- **Numbers (win delta vs the baseline plan, n = 400, tier V Balanced, L7; w1.future / w2.modern; counters regenerated before the final sweep):** Android Pair 2 × 328 HP, 40: +2.5 / +1.6. Barrier Trooper 650 HP, 47, guard 25%: -4.1 / -2.6. Hover Biker 545 HP, 66, base 100: -0.3 / +4.6. Needle Gunner 250 HP, 13 / 0.5 s: +4.5 / +14.4. Crab Mech 2,000 HP, 112 cleave 2: -2.5 / -0.1. Arc Lobber 360 HP, 172: -3.9 / -2.0. Plasma Lancer 800 HP, 100: -2.8 / -4.9. Overclock Engineer: +0.6 / -3.4. Holo Projector (decoy every 6 s, max 3): +1.8 / -3.6. Jetpack Trooper: +3.0 / +0.8. Particle Cannon: +3.0 / -0.8. Overload Android: +3.0 / -0.4. Drone Carrier 1,900 HP, drones every 7 s, max 2: +2.3 / +0.1. Cryo Pod -2.5 / +1.3. Tractor Beam 0.0 / 0.0. Target Painter -0.3 / +3.6. Nano Mesh -2.4 / -4.0.
- **Misses and why:** **Needle Gunner** reads +14 in the two-age window at every value: a near-null Needle Gunner (2 damage) still reads +8.6 there while it reads -19.8 in the one-age window, so the row measures the plan without the Pulse Trooper in w2.modern (like the SMG Squad, the Bronze ranged Commons and the Bomb Bowler); the one-age window sets its numbers (12 → -2.4, 13 → +2.9, 14 → +6.1, 16 → +8.8, 20 → +12.0; HP 210-250 changed nothing). **Tractor Beam** reads exactly 0.0: the Balanced bot never builds the rare-turret mount in these windows, so the row says nothing (as for the earlier waves' second turrets). The Epics and the Legendary replace the Repair Drone, which the bot trains rarely, so their small deltas measure the plan as much as the card. The n = 2,000 runs for |delta| > 3 and the per-fort rows were not run (the fort tool measures fort kinds, not the wave's extra forts).
- **Economy (30 seeds, 365 days, measured at the release flip):** Common and Rare to max are not reached in 365 days, Epic 165 / Legendary 206 days (targets 110 / 101 / 69 / 112 ± 20%: fail); per-capsule and per-day targets pass; `docs/requests/content-economy-retune.md` has the update. The CONTENT_PLAN 8 re-tune is overdue and should land before the Cosmic wave's 15 cards.
- **contentHash** snapshot `7ec0568f` (it includes the gated Cosmic data in the tree as it stands).

## X0 Cosmic wave shipped: art, sounds, numbers, release (content wave, 2026-10-04)

The W8 Cosmic wave (CONTENT_PLAN 5.8), the last of the eight, is released: 13 troops (Crystal Guard, Void Skimmer, Moonlings, Nova Thrower, Asteroid Golem, Star Mortar, Antimatter Rifler, Bio-Weaver, Void Whisperer, Star Fighter, Swarm Matron with its hidden Swarmling, Gravity Sage, Star Leviathan), 2 turrets (Shard Spitter, Event Horizon), 2 powers (Meteor Drizzle, Pulsar Pulse), 2 forts (Star Bulwark, Stardust Snare) and 3 skins (Starlight Legionnaire, Shadow Stalker, Classic Saucer). `released: false` is gone from all 23 ids and `tests/fixtures/pausedWave.ts` is empty again. DESIGN A5.6 has the wave table; A5.7, A13 and A14.1 list its powers, sounds and effects. Sources: Commons from Arena 3, Rares 3, Epics 4, the Legendary 5 (`cardArena`); Meteor Drizzle War Path Cosmic L3 / Road 4,900; Pulsar Pulse (s1, "Pulsar Night"), Star Bulwark (s2) and the Stardust Snare (the 20-star milestone) on the Road 4,800 region set; album entries 302-320; Cosmic plans for seven Generals (every new troop, turret and power except the Legendary; the Warden keeps the originals).

- **No new mechanic, no SIM_VERSION change:** the trio is an M1 squad, the Matron an M3 summoner, the Whisperer an M4 foe aura, the Sage's Collapse an M5 `frozen: false` stun, the Leviathan uses riders with `onDeathSpawn: 'moonlings'` (the two riders hop off as single Moonlings) and `followBehind` for its song cone.
- **Ion Cannon renamed "Ion Strike"** in the strings after an IP check of the wave's names (id `ion_cannon` unchanged).
- **Art:** 14 unit sheets in the cartoon pipeline (`art/blender/units/<slug>.py`, shared helpers `ageborn_art/kit_cosmic_wave.py`), each with 2-3 attack variants, a KO death and the ANIM_SPEC gaits: v3 jog for the troopers (stance 0.52, which gives four feet-apart frames on the shared body; the robed Bio-Weaver and Void Whisperer are G2 brisk walkers in `check_walk.py`, like the Starwarden), a six-legged tripod IK gait for the Matron and the Swarmling, hover swims with an odometer for the Skimmer, the Sage, the Fighter and the Leviathan, attack_alt for the Leviathan's riders. Team share at least 18% on every frame and colour rule at most 1.3% on every sheet; `check_timing` and `check_walk` pass. The Star Leviathan is modelled at 96 lu and scaled 1.8x at the body joint (172 lu, Legendary pixel scale, interior lines kept at their lu width) so it reads as a Legendary next to the Mothership and the Chrono Titan. Card portraits for all 14. Procedural fallbacks in `src/visuals/puppets/cosmic.ts` (the Asteroid Golem reuses the Steam Golem's walker build in rock colours; the Leviathan fallback carries the riders' tall team pennant); skins are palette puppets (`src/visuals/skins.ts`), the Shadow Stalker at 72% opacity with the ghost aura.
- **Turrets and forts:** Shard Spitter and Event Horizon sheets (`world/turrets_cosmic.py`); all six Cosmic forts in the cartoon kit (`world/forts_cosmic.py`: Void Rampart, Ion Spire, Warp Barracks and Void Mine redrawn from the realistic style with the same heights, hit points, flag points and the Ion Spire's muzzle; Star Bulwark and Stardust Snare new), colour rule at most 0.33% (the flag).
- **Effects and sounds:** instant `fx.tendril_lash`, `fx.void_ripple`, `fx.song_wave`, `fx.horizon_pulse`; eight projectiles; `fx.meteor_drizzle` (+ `fx.meteor_pop`) and `fx.pulsar_pulse`; medallions for both powers; 18 designed sounds (`tools/audio/sfx/sounds_cosmic_wave.py`) packed into the `cosmic` sheet with ZzFX fallbacks.
- **Dev sandbox:** `&format=w1.cosmic` (also `last`, `w2.future`) and loadouts for all eight ages, so the late ages can be screenshotted.
- **Numbers (win delta vs the baseline plan, n = 400, tier V Balanced, L7; w1.cosmic / w2.future; counters regenerated before each of the last three sweeps; 95% intervals are about ±4.7):** Crystal Guard 890 HP, 67, guard 25%: +6.0 / +3.4. Void Skimmer 670 HP, 94, base 90, speed 110: -2.8 / +5.3. Moonlings 3 × 172 HP, 21, range 230: -5.0 / +4.5. Nova Thrower 440 HP, 62 splash 30: -3.4 / -0.6. Asteroid Golem 2,950 HP, 150 cleave 2: +0.1 / -3.5. Star Mortar 470 HP, 190: -6.0 / +4.5. Antimatter Rifler 700 HP, 140: -2.4 / +1.4. Bio-Weaver (135 HP/s): +1.5 / +1.0. Void Whisperer: 0.0 / +0.5. Star Fighter: +2.4 / +1.4. Swarm Matron: +1.5 / -0.8. Gravity Sage: +1.5 / +0.3. Star Leviathan 2,800 HP, song 118: +3.5 / -0.3. Shard Spitter +0.6 / +1.0. Event Horizon +0.5 / +0.3. Meteor Drizzle -26.1 / -15.4. Pulsar Pulse (165, 2 s stun): +4.5 / +3.0.
- **Misses and why:** **Meteor Drizzle** sits at the lane cap (210 = 30% of the Star Legionnaire) and the price and reload floors (50 gold, 25 s; the schema rejects less), so it cannot be tuned up; at 35 gold / 18 s it read -4.8 / -1.5. Like the earlier lane volleys it is weak by rule. **Crystal Guard, Star Mortar, Void Skimmer and Moonlings** end 0.3-1.0 points outside ±5 in one window each, inside their 95% intervals: each counter regeneration moved the Crystal Guard by up to 20 points at the same numbers (830/64 read -11 after one regeneration and +2 before it), so further steps chase the counter matrix rather than the card; the Skimmer and the Moonlings read about 8-10 points apart between the two windows at every value tried (as the earlier waves' Raiders and squads). The Epics and the Legendary replace cards the bot trains rarely, so their small deltas measure the plan as much as the card. The n = 2,000 runs and the per-fort rows were not run.
- **Economy (30 seeds, 365 days, at the release flip):** Common and Rare to max not reached in 365 days, Epic 188 / Legendary 223 days (targets 110 / 101 / 69 / 112 ± 20%: fail, as since W6); per-capsule and per-day targets pass. `docs/requests/content-economy-retune.md` has the update: with all eight waves in, the CONTENT_PLAN 8 re-tune can be measured on the final pool.
